"""Bridge completed response inputs into the selected checkout's sole plant.

Capture runs in a separate process against the historical input builder and
stops BEFORE its rollout/scoring. Prediction imports only the selected checkout.
Prediction commands do not start native runs or fit coefficients. The explicit
prepare-seed53 command freezes a finite new response test for the existing runner.
"""
import copy
import csv
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import pickle
import pickletools
import io
import math
import sys
import time

HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT=HERE.parents[2]
OLD=ROOT.parent/'control-full-review'
OUT=HERE/'congested_replay'


def load(p): return json.loads(Path(p).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
def sha(p): return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def save(p,x): Path(p).write_text(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False),encoding='utf-8')


def read_primitive_capture(path, expected_sha):
    raw=Path(path).read_bytes()
    assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==expected_sha
    allowed={'PROTO','FRAME','STOP','MARK','MEMOIZE','BINGET','LONG_BINGET',
        'NONE','NEWTRUE','NEWFALSE','BININT','BININT1','BININT2','LONG1','LONG4','BINFLOAT',
        'SHORT_BINUNICODE','BINUNICODE','BINUNICODE8','EMPTY_DICT','EMPTY_LIST','EMPTY_TUPLE',
        'APPEND','APPENDS','SETITEM','SETITEMS','TUPLE','TUPLE1','TUPLE2','TUPLE3'}
    assert all(op.name in allowed for op,arg,pos in pickletools.genops(raw)), 'Non-data capture opcode'
    class DataOnly(pickle.Unpickler):
        def find_class(self,*args):raise ValueError('Class/function resolution forbidden')
        def persistent_load(self,*args):raise ValueError('External reference forbidden')
    return DataOnly(io.BytesIO(raw)).load()


def capture():
    sys.path.insert(0,str(OLD))
    from diagnostics.metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924 import extract as e
    c=e.c
    data,ctx,_,_=c.inputs()
    prior=e.HERE/'legal_release2670'
    assert load(prior/'status.json')['stage']=='complete_response_validation'
    protocol=load(prior/'protocol.json')
    assert protocol['source_sha']==load(HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json')['sources']['network']['sha256']
    ctx=list(copy.deepcopy(ctx));ctx[3]=protocol['port_profile']
    model=c.prior.one.base.load_base_model(data['rm'].geometry,e.HERE/'local_fd/candidate.json')
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    class Captured(BaseException): pass
    records=[]
    for arm in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90'):
        commands={int(k):v for k,v in load(prior/f'{arm}_commands.json').items()}
        def intercept(*args,**kwargs):
            # Native future truth is scored only after model.rollout, which
            # this capture deliberately never enters.
            json.dumps([args,kwargs],allow_nan=False)
            payload=pickle.dumps((args,kwargs),protocol=5)
            path=OUT/(arm+'_input.pickle');path.write_bytes(payload)
            records.append(dict(arm=arm,input=path.name,sha256=sha(path),
                truth=str(prior/'observations'/arm),previous_native_result=str(prior/f'{arm}_result.json'),
                previous_prediction=str(prior/f'{arm}_prediction.json.gz'),
                command_sha256=sha(prior/f'{arm}_commands.json')))
            raise Captured()
        model.rollout=intercept
        try:
            c.prior.forecast(model,protocol['parameters'],data['rm'],data['rm'],2670.1,450,
                             *ctx[:-1],commands,True)
            raise AssertionError('Rollout input not captured')
        except Captured: pass
    save(OUT/'capture.json',dict(source_network=protocol['source_sha'],cutoff=2670.1,horizon=450,
        geometry_sha256=sha(data['rm'].folder/'geometry.json'),records=records,
        parameter_source=str(prior/'protocol.json'),parameter_sha256=sha(prior/'protocol.json'),
        builder_sources={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(c.prior.__file__),Path(c.prior.one.__file__),Path(__file__))},
        cutoff_truncation_assertion_passed=True,native_started=False,future_truth_read_for_prediction=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(captured=len(records),folder=str(OUT))),flush=True)


def capture_response2220():
    """Reuse two completed common-state interventions; no native or fitting."""
    sys.path.insert(0,str(OLD))
    from diagnostics.metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924 import extract as e
    c=e.c
    data,ctx,_,_=c.inputs()
    ctx=list(copy.deepcopy(ctx))
    ctx[3]=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/port_profile.json')
    parameters=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/parameters.json')['parameters']
    network=load(HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json')['sources']['network']['sha256']
    assert sha(c.prior.REF/'source/mtfc_s29_none.inpx')==network
    for seed in (29,43):
        destination=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929'/f'component2220_s{seed}_inputs_v2'
        destination.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
        initial=data['none'] if seed==29 else c.prior.ObservationData(e.r.HERE/'gain_response/seed43/none')
        native_path=e.HERE/f'source_cost_native{seed}/summary.json'
        native=load(native_path)
        assert all(r['trajectory_exact'] and r['native_signals_exact'] for r in native['rows'])
        observations=e.r.HERE/'observations' if seed==29 else e.HERE/'heldout43/observations'
        model=c.prior.one.base.load_base_model(initial.geometry,e.HERE/'local_fd/candidate.json')
        records=[]
        class Captured(BaseException):pass
        for arm in ('none','vsl','rm','both'):
            commands=c.exp.commands(arm,ctx[0],ctx[-1])
            command_path=destination/(arm+'_commands.json');save(command_path,commands)
            truth=initial.folder if arm=='none' else observations/arm
            measurement=next(r for r in native['rows'] if r['arm']==arm)
            def intercept(*args,**kwargs):
                json.dumps([args,kwargs],allow_nan=False)
                path=destination/(arm+'_input.pickle')
                path.write_bytes(pickle.dumps((args,kwargs),protocol=5))
                records.append(dict(arm=arm,input=path.name,sha256=sha(path),truth=str(truth),
                    command_sha256=sha(command_path),native_total_cost=dict(
                        internal_veh_h=measurement['native_network_ttt_veh_h'],
                        external_wait_veh_h=measurement['external_wait_veh_h'],
                        total_veh_h=measurement['network_tts_including_external_veh_h'])))
                raise Captured()
            model.rollout=intercept
            try:
                c.prior.forecast(model,parameters,initial,initial,2220.1,450,*ctx[:-1],commands,True)
                raise AssertionError('Rollout input not captured')
            except Captured:pass
        initials=[read_primitive_capture(destination/r['input'],r['sha256']) for r in records]
        assert all(a[0][0]==initials[0][0][0] and a[0][3]==initials[0][0][3]
                   and a[1]==initials[0][1] for a in initials)
        save(destination/'capture.json',dict(source_network=network,seed=seed,cutoff=2220.1,horizon=450,
            geometry_sha256=sha(initial.folder/'geometry.json'),records=records,
            native_cost_receipt=str(native_path),native_cost_receipt_sha256=sha(native_path),
            builder_sources={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(c.prior.__file__),Path(c.prior.one.__file__),Path(c.exp.__file__),Path(__file__))},
            common_initial_inputs_exact=True,cutoff_truncation_assertion_passed=True,
            native_started=False,future_truth_read_for_prediction=False,
            qualification='Previously inspected common-state response; different seeds, not blind holdout. Fixed g8/6/4 and bottleneck90 from2250, not full9000 ALINEA policy.'))
        print(json.dumps(dict(captured=len(records),seed=seed,folder=str(destination))),flush=True)


def capture_extended_nc_windows():
    """Capture seven clean, broader NC states with the existing causal builder."""
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    sys.path.insert(0,str(OLD))
    from diagnostics.metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924 import extract as e
    c=e.c;data,ctx,_,_=c.inputs();ctx=list(copy.deepcopy(ctx))
    ctx[3]=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/port_profile.json')
    params=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/parameters.json')['parameters']
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929';cw=parent/'cellwise_calibration'
    out=cw/'expanded_nc_windows';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    assert not (out/'capture.json').exists(), 'Reuse captured inputs'
    (out/'inputs').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    assert not any((out/'inputs').iterdir()), 'Do not overwrite a partial capture'
    coverage=load(cw/'full_run_coverage_review.json');records=[];evidence=[];pins={}
    network=load(HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json')['sources']['network']['sha256']
    canonical=Path(c.prior.REF/'source/mtfc_s29_none.inpx');assert sha(canonical)==network
    root=ET.parse(canonical).getroot()
    def tree(z):return (z.tag,tuple(sorted(z.attrib.items())),(z.text or '').strip(),tuple(tree(x) for x in z))
    class Captured(BaseException):pass
    for dataset in coverage['datasets']:
        seed=dataset['seed'];initial=c.prior.ObservationData(dataset['folder'])
        for name,digest in dataset['cache_sha256'].items():
            path=initial.folder/name;assert sha(path)==digest;pins[str(path)]=digest
        source=Path(f'D:/VISSIM_runs/20260924_bottleneck90_full_s{seed}/source/mtfc_s{seed}_none.inpx')
        receipt_path=source.parent.parent/'none/run/run.json';receipt=load(receipt_path)
        assert receipt['completed'] and receipt['finished'] and not receipt['owned_native_alive']
        assert receipt['terminal_sec']==9000 and receipt['exit_code']==receipt['fixed_profile_validation_exit_code']==0
        proof=receipt['fixed_profile_proof'];assert proof['event_rows']==0 and proof['native_resolution_probe']==10
        assert proof['network_sha256']==sha(source) and proof['seed']==seed
        prepared=Path(receipt['network']);prepared_receipt=Path(receipt['prepared'])/'prepared.json'
        assert sha(prepared_receipt)==receipt['prepared_sha256']
        assert sha(prepared)==proof['network_sha256']
        native=ET.parse(source).getroot();assert tree(native)==tree(ET.parse(prepared).getroot())
        seed_only=copy.deepcopy(native);seed_only.find('simulation').set('randSeed','29')
        assert tree(seed_only)==tree(root), 'NC differs beyond seed'
        legacy=Path(initial.geometry['source_network']['path']);oldroot=ET.parse(legacy).getroot()
        changed=sorted(k for k in {z.tag for z in root}|{z.tag for z in oldroot}
            if tree(root.find(k))!=tree(oldroot.find(k)))
        assert changed==['dataCollectionMeasurements','dataCollectionPoints','desSpeedDecisions']
        for p in (canonical,source,prepared,prepared_receipt,receipt_path,legacy):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        evidence.append(dict(seed=seed,network_sha256=sha(source),seed_only_difference=True,
            prepared_tree_exact=True,completed_native_sec=9000,no_control_events=True,
            legacy_geometry_source_differences=changed,legacy_geometry_physical_links_exact=True))
        model=c.prior.one.base.load_base_model(initial.geometry,e.HERE/'local_fd/candidate.json')
        commands=c.exp.commands('none',ctx[0],ctx[-1])
        assert all(set(h['greens'].values())=={10} and h['vsl']==ctx[-1] for h in commands.values())
        assert all(ctx[-1][i]==110 for b in ctx[-2] for i in b['dsd_ids'])
        for window in dataset['candidate_windows']:
            if not window['complete_with_no_recorded_accounting_issue']:continue
            t0=window['cutoff'];name=f's{seed}_t{int(t0)}';path=out/'inputs'/(name+'.pickle')
            def intercept(*args,**kwargs):
                assert kwargs['horizon_sec']==450 and kwargs['roads']==['FW_E']
                assert len(args[1])==450 and args[2]==params
                assert all(set(s['vsl_commands'].values())=={110} and
                    all(h['mode']=='OFF' for h in s['ramp_head_service'].values()) for s in args[1])
                json.dumps([args,kwargs],allow_nan=False)
                path.write_bytes(pickle.dumps((args,kwargs),protocol=5))
                raise Captured()
            model.rollout=intercept
            try:
                c.prior.forecast(model,params,initial,initial,t0,450,*ctx[:-1],commands,True)
                raise AssertionError('Capture must precede rollout/scoring')
            except Captured:pass
            records.append(dict(case=name,seed=seed,arm='none',cutoff=t0,horizon=450,input=str(path),
                sha256=sha(path),truth=str(initial.folder),source_network=network,
                actual_seed_network_sha256=sha(source),role='train_coverage' if seed==29 else 'inspected_check',
                future_truncation_exact=True,recorded_accounting_issue_veh=0))
    assert len(records)==7
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest
    pins[str(Path(__file__))]=sha(__file__)
    save(out/'capture.json',dict(records=records,network_evidence=evidence,pins=pins,
        builder_sources={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(c.prior.__file__),Path(c.prior.one.__file__))},
        excluded_windows=[dict(seed=d['seed'],**w) for d in coverage['datasets'] for w in d['candidate_windows']
            if not w['complete_with_no_recorded_accounting_issue']],
        scope='Seven NC states only, not paired intervention evidence. No future realized boundary input.',
        native_started=False,new_forecasts=0))
    print(json.dumps(dict(captured=len(records),future_truncation_exact=True)),flush=True)


def capture_baseline_policy_windows():
    """Reuse original four-policy windows; capture data only before rollout."""
    sys.path.insert(0,str(OLD))
    from diagnostics.metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924 import extract as e
    c=e.c;data,ctx,commands,_=c.inputs();ctx=list(copy.deepcopy(ctx))
    ctx[3]=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/port_profile.json')
    params=load(HERE/'selected/port_gain/parameters.json')['parameters']
    out=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/original_policy_windows_20261001'
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);(out/'inputs').mkdir()
    network=load(HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json')['sources']['network']['sha256']
    sources={arm:(c.prior.REF if arm in ('none','bottleneck90') else c.prior.Q)/'source'/f'mtfc_s29_{arm}.inpx' for arm in data}
    assert all(sha(p)==network for p in sources.values()), 'Original policy source must match selected traffic network'
    manifest=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/coupled_expanded_joint/candidate_manifest.json'
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources.values()}
    for arm,obs in data.items():
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv','boundaries_30s.csv','geometry.json','port_cohorts_30s.json'):
            p=obs.folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        save(out/(arm+'_commands.json'),commands[arm])
    pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
    # This is an absolute/state check of the existing policy, not a new fit.
    protocol=dict(source_network=network,manifest=str(manifest),own_state_cutoffs=list(c.prior.CUTOFFS),
        own_state_horizon=150,common_cutoff=870.1,common_horizon=450,maximum_rollouts=32,
        calibration=False,scope='East31+4on+4off connectors; not wholeOmega or urban approaches.',
        distinction='Late own-state differences include inherited policy states. Common870 commands are execution-conditioned, not a forecast of ALINEA decisions.',
        native_runs=0,live_poll=0,source_pins=pins)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol)
    records=[]
    class Captured(BaseException):pass
    for mode,cutoffs,horizon in [('own_state150',c.prior.CUTOFFS,150),('common870_450',(870.1,),450)]:
        for arm,truth in data.items():
            initial=truth if mode=='own_state150' else data['none']
            model=c.prior.one.base.load_base_model(initial.geometry,e.HERE/'local_fd/candidate.json')
            for t0 in cutoffs:
                name=f'{mode}_{arm}_{int(t0)}';path=out/'inputs'/(name+'.pickle')
                def intercept(*args,**kwargs):
                    assert kwargs['horizon_sec']==horizon and kwargs['roads']==['FW_E']
                    assert len(args[1])==horizon and args[2]==params
                    json.dumps([args,kwargs],allow_nan=False)
                    path.write_bytes(pickle.dumps((args,kwargs),protocol=5))
                    raise Captured()
                model.rollout=intercept
                try:
                    c.prior.forecast(model,params,initial,truth,t0,horizon,*ctx[:-1],commands[arm],True)
                    raise AssertionError('Must stop before legacy rollout/scoring')
                except Captured:pass
                records.append(dict(case=name,arm=arm,mode=mode,cutoff=t0,horizon=horizon,input=str(path),
                    sha256=sha(path),truth=str(truth.folder),initial_observations=str(initial.folder),
                    commands_sha256=sha(out/(arm+'_commands.json')),future_truncation_exact=True))
    assert len(records)==32
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h
    save(out/'capture.json',dict(records=records,source_pins=pins,
        builder_sources={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(c.prior.__file__),Path(c.prior.one.__file__),Path(__file__))},
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,future_traffic_input=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(captured=len(records),native=0)),flush=True)


def baseline_policy_vsl_history(model, observations, records, out, network):
    """Past-only cohort estimate from existing 30s balances, never future traffic."""
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import VSLExposure
    from evaluation.controllers.vsl_exposure_history import assimilate, cell_fluxes
    spec=model.base.network.component_vsl_transport['FW_E'];size=31
    dsds=ET.parse(network).findall('./desSpeedDecisions/desSpeedDecision')
    snapshots={};audit=[]
    for folder,obs in observations.items():
        own=[r for r in records if r['initial_observations']==folder]
        arm=next(r['arm'] for r in own if r['mode']=='own_state150')
        commands={float(t):v for t,v in load(out/(arm+'_commands.json')).items()}
        chains={str(c['link']):c['offset_m'] for c in obs.geometry['chains']['FW_E']}
        stations={}
        for d in dsds:
            link=d.get('lane').split()[0]
            if link in chains:stations.setdefault(round(chains[link]+float(d.get('pos')),5),[]).append(d.get('no'))
        bindings=[]
        for cell in sorted((c for c in obs.geometry['cells'] if c['road']=='FW_E'),key=lambda c:c['cell']):
            center=(cell['start_m']+cell['end_m'])/2
            bindings.append(stations[max(x for x in stations if x<=center)])
        def vector(t):
            applied=commands[max(s for s in commands if s<=t)]['vsl'];result=[]
            for ids in bindings:
                values={float(applied[i]) for i in ids};assert len(values)==1
                result.append(values.pop())
            return result
        cutoffs={r['cutoff'] for r in own};endmax=max(cutoffs)
        ends=sorted({t for t,road,cell in obs.flows if road=='FW_E' and t<=endmax})
        exposure=None;unresolved=0.;removed=0.;last=None
        for end in ends:
            rows=[obs.flows[end,'FW_E',i] for i in range(size)]
            start=float(rows[0]['window_start_s']);duration=end-start
            assert last is None or abs(start-last)<1e-6
            values=lambda key:[float(z[key]) for z in rows]
            old=values('start_n_veh');new=values('end_n_veh')
            assert not any(values('unexplained_entries'))
            assert not any(values('source_admissions')[1:])
            losses=values('unexplained_losses');deletions=values('native_removals')
            # Unresolved past disappearance is a separate sink, NOT normal exit.
            sinks=[a+b for a,b in zip(losses,deletions)]
            unresolved+=sum(losses);removed+=sum(deletions)
            entry=sum(values('source_admissions'));terminal=sum(values('terminal_exits_inferred'))
            merges=values('ramp_merges');exits=values('off_departures')
            inferred=cell_fluxes(old,new,entry,merges,exits,sinks,terminal)
            assert all(abs(a-b)<1e-7 for a,b in zip(inferred[:-1],values('downstream_crossings')[:-1]))
            if exposure is None:
                assert not any(old), 'History must start before vehicles exist'
                exposure=VSLExposure(old,spec,110)
            changes={t-start:vector(t) for t in commands if start<t<end}
            exposure=assimilate(exposure,old,new,entry,merges,exits,sinks,terminal,vector(start),duration,1.,changes)
            last=end
            if end in cutoffs:
                observed=[z['n_veh'] for z in sorted((z for z in obs.cells[end] if z['road']=='FW_E'),key=lambda z:z['cell'])]
                assert new==observed
                snapshots[folder,end]=copy.deepcopy(exposure)
                audit.append(dict(arm=arm,cutoff=end,cohorts=exposure.cohorts,max_mass_residual=exposure.max_residual,
                    past_unresolved_disappearance=unresolved,past_native_removals=removed,
                    restricted_vehicles=sum(n for z in exposure.cohorts for cmd,n in z.items() if cmd<110)))
        assert all((folder,t) in snapshots for t in cutoffs)
    save(out/'history_audit.json',dict(rows=audit,source='Past cached30s balances and previously verified executed commands only.',
        limitation='Uniform within-window flux and well-mixed cells; no individual passage labels or partial-cell position correction.',
        future_traffic_used=False,unresolved_disappearance_is_normal_exit=False))
    return snapshots


def predict_baseline_policy_windows():
    """Frozen expanded036 check on the user's original four-policy data."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from evaluation.controllers import area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    out=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/original_policy_windows_20261001'
    assert not (out/'status.json').exists(), 'Preserve existing attempt; no implicit rerun'
    cap=load(out/'capture.json');protocol=load(out/'protocol.json')
    pins=dict(cap['source_pins'])
    for p in (out/'capture.json',out/'protocol.json',Path(__file__)):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    for p in (ROOT/'evaluation/controllers').glob('*.py'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    for p in out.glob('*_commands.json'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    for p,h in cap['source_pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h
    save(out/'prediction_pins.json',pins)
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(protocol['manifest']);model=ctx['component']
    assert model.base.simulation.T_f_sec==1
    records=cap['records'];obs={r['truth']:ObservationData(r['truth']) for r in records}
    payloads={r['case']:read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']) for r in records}
    history=baseline_policy_vsl_history(model,obs,records,out,ctx['paths']['network'])
    save(out/'history_protocol.json',dict(maximum_rollouts=33,calibration=False,
        reason='Old component captures omit past VSL cohorts. Estimate past history before own-state comparison; one NC parity rollout added.',
        core_modified=False,production_sdmpc_has_separate_history_initialization=True))
    # Require identical state/cohort/initial origin and boundaries in the common case;
    # only the already executed actuator commands may differ.
    common=[r for r in records if r['mode']=='common870_450']
    aa,kk=copy.deepcopy(payloads[common[0]['case']])
    for r in common:
        a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[r['case']]);assert a[0]==aa[0] and a[2:]==aa[2:] and k==kk
        def traffic(steps):return [{key:value for key,value in s.items() if key not in ('vsl_commands','ramp_head_service')} for s in steps]
        assert traffic(a[1])==traffic(aa[1])
    (out/'predictions').mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='running',completed=0,total=len(records)))
    started=time.perf_counter();compute=0.;rows=[]
    for r in records:
        args,kwargs=copy.deepcopy(payloads[r['case']]);assert args[2]==ctx['parameters']
        posterior=history[r['initial_observations'],r['cutoff']]
        counts=[z['n_veh'] for z in sorted((z for z in args[0] if z['road']=='FW_E'),key=lambda z:z['cell'])]
        assert all(abs(sum(z.values())-n)<1e-7 for z,n in zip(posterior.cohorts,counts))
        original=accounting._freeway_substep_events;injected=[]
        def with_history(state,*a,**k):
            if not injected:
                assert abs(state.time_sec-r['cutoff'])<1e-7
                assert not getattr(state,'_component_vsl_exposure',{})
                state._component_vsl_exposure={'FW_E':copy.deepcopy(posterior)};injected.append(True)
            return original(state,*a,**k)
        begin=time.perf_counter()
        accounting._freeway_substep_events=with_history
        try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
        finally:accounting._freeway_substep_events=original
        assert injected==[True]
        elapsed=time.perf_counter()-begin;compute+=elapsed
        if not rows:
            begin=time.perf_counter();parity=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs);compute+=time.perf_counter()-begin
            assert all(cmd==110 for z in posterior.cohorts for cmd,n in z.items() if n>1e-7)
            differences=[]
            for left,right in zip(pred['cells'],parity['cells']):
                assert left.keys()==right.keys()
                for key in left:
                    if isinstance(left[key],(int,float)):differences.append(abs(left[key]-right[key]))
                    else:assert left[key]==right[key]
            assert max(differences)<1e-7
            save(out/'nc_history_parity.json',dict(max_cell_difference=max(differences),extra_rollouts=1))
        path=out/'predictions'/(r['case']+'.json.gz')
        with gzip.open(path,'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,obs[r['truth']],horizon_sec=r['horizon'])
        row.update(mode=r['mode'],cutoff=r['cutoff'],horizon=r['horizon'],rollout_sec=elapsed)
        rows.append(row);save(out/'predictions'/(r['case']+'_result.json'),row)
        save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='running',completed=len(rows),total=len(records),last_case=r['case']))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for r in records:assert sha(r['input'])==r['sha256']
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(status='complete',rows=rows,rollouts=len(rows)+1,comparison_rollouts=len(rows),rollout_sec=compute,
        wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,source_pins=pins,common_initial_and_traffic_exact=True,
        scope=protocol['scope'],distinction=protocol['distinction'],adopted=False,calibration=False,
        gain_qualified=False,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,live_poll=0))
    save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='complete',completed=len(rows),total=len(records),new_native=0))
    print(json.dumps(dict(completed=len(rows),rollout_sec=compute,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started)),flush=True)


def predict_extended_nc_windows():
    """Check frozen prior/159-cellwise candidates on wider NC state coverage."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929';cw=parent/'cellwise_calibration';out=cw/'expanded_nc_windows'
    assert not (out/'protocol.json').exists(), 'Do not repeat a prepared comparison'
    cap=load(out/'capture.json');selected=load(cw/'jacobian_step/selection.json')
    manifests={'prior':HERE/'closedloop9000_d4e2_analysis/local_merge_cell23/candidate_manifest.json',
        'cellwise':cw/'jacobian_step'/selected['selected']/'manifest.json'}
    core={str(ROOT/p):sha(ROOT/p) for p in load(cw/'protocol.json')['core_pins']}
    for p,digest in cap['pins'].items():assert sha(p)==digest
    protocol=dict(manifests={k:dict(path=str(p),sha256=sha(p)) for k,p in manifests.items()},
        core_pins=core,capture_sha256=sha(out/'capture.json'),maximum_new_rollouts=14,
        models_frozen_before_new_states=True,calibration=False,production_adopted=False,
        purpose='Determine whether the previous cellwise coefficients generalize across time before expanded calibration.',
        qualification='NC wider-state check only; no new control-pair ranking/fullOmega/SDMPC qualification.')
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);contexts={k:lpr.load_sources(p) for k,p in manifests.items()}
    records=cap['records'];payload={r['case']:read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']) for r in records}
    observations={r['seed']:ObservationData(r['truth']) for r in records};results={};completed=0
    start=time.perf_counter()
    for version,context in contexts.items():
        folder=out/version;folder.mkdir(exist_ok=False);rows=[]
        for r in records:
            args,kwargs=copy.deepcopy(payload[r['case']]);assert args[2]==context['parameters']
            pred=context['component'].rollout(*args,**kwargs);completed+=1
            with gzip.open(folder/(r['case']+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            row=_cellwise_measure(context['component'],pred,r,observations[r['seed']]);rows.append(row)
            save(folder/(r['case']+'_result.json'),row)
            save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='running',completed=completed,last_case=r['case'],last_version=version))
            print(json.dumps(dict(version=version,case=r['case'],n_rmse=row['score']['cell_n']['rmse'],
                q_rmse=row['score']['flow_vph']['rmse'],v_rmse=row['score']['speed']['rmse'])),flush=True)
        results[version]=rows
    for p,digest in {**core,**cap['pins']}.items():assert sha(p)==digest
    comparisons=[]
    for base,new in zip(results['prior'],results['cellwise']):
        assert base['case']==new['case'] and base['actual']==new['actual']
        comparisons.append(dict(case=base['case'],metrics={k:{v:r['score'][k]['rmse'] for v,r in
            [('prior',base),('cellwise',new)]} for k in ('speed','cell_n','flow_vph','source_flow_vph')},
            ttt_actual=base['actual']['ttt'],ttt_prior=base['predicted']['ttt'],ttt_cellwise=new['predicted']['ttt']))
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(comparisons=comparisons,rows=results,completed=completed,
        wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-start,core_and_inputs_unchanged=True,fit=False,new_native=0,
        scope='East31+8connector,30s trapezoid; not fullOmega. NC does not establish intervention response.',
        production_adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
    save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='complete',completed=completed,new_native=0,calibration=False))


def predict(*, manifest_path=None, output=None, input_dir=None, inflow_diagnostic=False):
    sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.scoring import score_rollout
    manifest_path=Path(manifest_path) if manifest_path is not None else HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json'
    destination=Path(output) if output is not None else OUT
    if output is not None:destination.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest_path)
    source=Path(input_dir) if input_dir is not None else OUT
    model=context['component'];manifest=load(source/'capture.json')
    assert context['document']['sources']['network']['sha256']==manifest['source_network']
    results=[];conditional_pins={}
    for row in manifest['records']:
        p=source/row['input'];assert sha(p)==row['sha256']
        args,kwargs=read_primitive_capture(p,row['sha256'])
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'], 'Selected coefficients differ; do not silently replace them'
        assert kwargs['roads']==['FW_E'] and kwargs['horizon_sec']==450
        if inflow_diagnostic:
            # Cause isolation only, following the existing response_balance
            # diagnostic. Future inflows are NOT deployable prediction inputs.
            truth=ObservationData(row['truth']);t0=manifest['cutoff'];counts={}
            event_path=truth.folder/'port_events.csv'
            with event_path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                for event in csv.DictReader(f):
                    if event['kind']=='arrival' and t0<float(event['time_s'])<=t0+450+1e-7:
                        key=(round(float(event['time_s']),6),event['connector'],int(event['lane']))
                        counts[key]=counts.get(key,0)+1
            for step in args[1]:
                delta=step['window_start_s']-t0
                end=round(t0+(int(math.floor((delta+1e-7)/30))+1)*30,6)
                step['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']=float(truth.flows[end,'FW_E',0]['source_admissions'])*120
                arrival_end=round(t0+(int(math.floor((delta+1e-7)/5))+1)*5,6)
                step['ramp_arrival_lane_profile']={}
                for mid,port in model.ramps.items():
                    if port['road']!='FW_E':continue
                    lanes=[[counts.get((arrival_end,str(port['connector']),lane),0)/5.]
                           for lane in range(1,port['lanes']+1)]
                    step['ramp_arrival_lane_profile'][mid]=lanes
                    step['ramp_arrival_vph'][mid]=sum(x[0] for x in lanes)*3600
            for path in (event_path,truth.folder/'flows_30s.csv'):
                conditional_pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            assert abs(sum(s['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']/3600 for s in args[1])-
                sum(float(truth.flows[round(t0+d,6),'FW_E',0]['source_admissions']) for d in range(30,451,30)))<1e-7
        started=time.perf_counter();pred=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
        wall=time.perf_counter()-started
        with gzip.open(destination/(row['arm']+'_prediction.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        # Autonomous mode opens future observations only after its prediction.
        # Conditional diagnostic mode is explicitly excluded from qualification.
        truth=ObservationData(row['truth']);t0=manifest['cutoff']
        initial_n=sum(x['n_veh'] for x in args[0] if x['road']=='FW_E')
        times=[round(t0+d,6) for d in range(0,451,30)]
        port_ids={str(v['connector']) for v in model.ramps.values() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
        port_ids|={str(k) for k,v in model.offramps.items() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
        observed_main={t:sum(x['n_veh'] for x in truth.cells[t] if x['road']=='FW_E') for t in times}
        observed_port={t:sum(float(truth.ports[t,c]['end_n_veh']) for c in port_ids) for t in times}
        predicted_main={t:sum(x['n_veh'] for x in pred['cells'] if abs(x['time_s']-t)<1e-6) for t in times[1:]}
        predicted_port={t:sum(x['n_veh'] for x in pred['ports'] if abs(x['time_s']-t)<1e-6)+
            sum(x['end']['connector_veh'] for x in pred['ramps'] if abs(x['end_sec']-t)<1e-6) for t in times[1:]}
        assert observed_main[t0]==initial_n
        predicted_main[t0]=initial_n;predicted_port[t0]=observed_port[t0]
        integral=lambda values:sum((values[a]+values[b])*(b-a)/7200 for a,b in zip(times,times[1:]))
        flows={}
        for label,records in [('predicted',pred['flows']),('actual',[dict(v,window_end_s=t)
                for (t,r,c),v in truth.flows.items() if r=='FW_E' and t0<t<=times[-1]])]:
            flows[label]={key:sum(float(x[key]) for x in records) for key in
                          ('source_admissions','off_departures','terminal_exits' if label=='predicted' else 'terminal_exits_inferred')}
        merges={r:sum(x['accepted_merge_veh'] for x in pred['ramps'] if x['ramp']==r) for r,v in model.ramps.items() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
        actual_merges={r:sum(float(truth.ports[t,str(model.ramps[r]['connector'])]['departures_veh']) for t in times[1:]) for r in merges}
        score=score_rollout(truth,t0,pred,'FW_E',include_source_boundary=True)
        assert not score['invalid']
        residual=max([abs(x['conservation_residual_veh']) for x in pred['ports']]+[abs(x['conservation_residual_veh']) for x in pred['ramps']])
        assert residual<1e-7
        native_cost=load(row['previous_native_result'])['native'] if 'previous_native_result' in row else row['native_total_cost']
        item=dict(arm=row['arm'],wall_sec=wall,score=score,conservation_max=residual,
            predicted_mainline_ttt=integral(predicted_main),actual_mainline_ttt=integral(observed_main),
            predicted_port_ttt=integral(predicted_port),actual_port_ttt=integral(observed_port),
            predicted_merge=merges,actual_merge=actual_merges,flows=flows,
            native_total_cost=native_cost)
        item['predicted_component_ttt']=item['predicted_mainline_ttt']+item['predicted_port_ttt']
        item['actual_component_ttt']=item['actual_mainline_ttt']+item['actual_port_ttt']
        results.append(item);save(destination/(row['arm']+'_result.json'),item)
        print(json.dumps(dict(arm=row['arm'],wall_sec=wall,predicted=item['predicted_component_ttt'],actual=item['actual_component_ttt'])),flush=True)
    base=results[0]
    for row in results:
        row['deltas']={k:row[k]-base[k] for k in ('predicted_component_ttt','actual_component_ttt','predicted_mainline_ttt','actual_mainline_ttt','predicted_port_ttt','actual_port_ttt')}
        row['deltas']['predicted_merge']=sum(row['predicted_merge'].values())-sum(base['predicted_merge'].values())
        row['deltas']['actual_merge']=sum(row['actual_merge'].values())-sum(base['actual_merge'].values())
    save(destination/'summary.json',dict(results=results,gain_qualified=False,calibration=False,
        source_capture=str(source/'capture.json'),source_capture_sha256=sha(source/'capture.json'),
        manifest=str(manifest_path),manifest_sha256=sha(manifest_path),
        scope='East31 cells plus8 ramp connectors; not full urban/Omega prediction',
        total_native_cost_scope='All network plus latent waiting; independent scope, not compared numerically to component prediction',
        independent_holdout=False,future_observed_inflow_inputs=inflow_diagnostic,
        conditional_only=inflow_diagnostic,conditional_source_pins=conditional_pins,
        source_file_pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in
            (Path(lpr.__file__),Path(sys.modules[model.__class__.__module__].__file__),manifest_path,Path(__file__))}))


def diagnose_expanded_merge_boundary(resume=False, fresh_seed=None, trace_balance=False, recovery_speed=False):
    """External-merge cause isolation, never autonomous gain qualification."""
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.physical_ramp_boundary import gap_acceptance_supply_vph
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    assert fresh_seed in (None,67)
    fresh=fresh_seed is not None
    assert not trace_balance or (fresh_seed==67 and not resume)
    assert not recovery_speed or (fresh_seed==67 and not resume and not trace_balance)
    out=root/'freeway_first'/('recovery_speed67' if recovery_speed else 'b67' if trace_balance else 'conditional_boundary67' if fresh else 'conditional_merge_boundary')
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume)
    manifest=root/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__)}
    if fresh:
        case_dir=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930';prior=root/'freeway_first/choice67'
        frozen=load(case_dir/'protocol.json')
        assert load(case_dir/'postprocess_status.json')['stage']=='complete'
        assert load(prior/'verification.json')['frozen_model_sources_unchanged']
        assert load(Path(frozen['results_root'])/'task_status.json')['phase']=='native_complete'
        assert not (Path(frozen['results_root'])/'STOP').exists()
        # The historical helper is archived beside its completed predictions.
        # Preserve the original experiment pins when extending this diagnostic.
        for group in ('model_pins','comparison_pins'):
            for path,digest in frozen[group].items():
                resolved=prior/'helper_executed.txt' if Path(path).resolve()==Path(__file__).resolve() else Path(path)
                assert sha(resolved)==digest,path;pins[str(resolved)]=digest
        capture=load(case_dir/'capture.json')
        assert capture['cutoff']==2670.1 and capture['horizon']==450 and not capture['future_truth_used_as_input']
        records=[dict(r,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(case_dir/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in capture['records']]
        arms=['hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90']
        if trace_balance or recovery_speed:
            arms=['release','release_vsl90']
            records=[r for r in records if r['arm'] in arms]
        saved_rows={r['arm']:r for r in load(prior/'summary.json')['rows'] if r['model']=='route036'}
        for p in (case_dir/'capture.json',prior/'summary.json',prior/'verification.json'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    else:
        frozen=load(root/'protocol.json')
        for path,digest in frozen['core_pins'].items():
            assert sha(ROOT/path)==digest;pins[str(ROOT/path)]=digest
        records=[r for r in load(root/'data_catalog.json')['checked_records'] if r['case']=='s29_early']
        arms=['none','vsl','rm','both']
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP')
    pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    assert [r['arm'] for r in records]==arms
    if resume:
        expected=dict(stage='running',completed=1,last_mode='saved_merge_parity',last_arm='hold') if fresh else dict(stage='running',completed=4,last_mode='saved_merge_parity',last_arm='both')
        assert load(out/'status.json')==expected
        if fresh:assert load(out/'failure.json')['completed_forecasts']==2
        for p,digest in load(out/'protocol.json')['pins'].items():
            candidate=((out/'helper_executed.txt' if fresh else root/'freeway_first/merge_boundary_source/replay_executed.py.txt') if p==str(Path(__file__)) else Path(p))
            assert sha(candidate)==digest
    conditional_mode='observed_source_merge_conditional' if fresh else 'observed_merge_conditional'
    parity_arms=('release',) if recovery_speed else () if trace_balance else ('hold','release_vsl90') if fresh else arms
    save(out/('resume_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(rollout_limit=3 if recovery_speed else 2 if trace_balance else 6 if fresh else 8,arms=arms,fit=False,new_native=0,
        trace_balance=trace_balance,new_fzp_reads=0,
        recovery_speed_diagnostic=recovery_speed,
        recovery_speed_method='Only for conditional arms: replace next-step mean speed of cells24/25 with linear interpolation of native5s mean speeds. Do not replace N, accepted flows, route inventory, off storage, or VSL cohort state. Recompute stored q=rho*v*lanes. Future speed injection is cause isolation, NOT a deployable forecast or empirical causal effect.' if recovery_speed else None,
        hypothesis='Separate late67 VSL discharge/recovery errors from source admission and accepted merge errors.' if fresh else 'Missing early RM discharge response comes from merge-volume/timing prediction rather than only mainline dynamics.',
        parity='Replay saved accepted1s merges through the existing external-boundary mode; cells/flows/off stocks exact. Late67 checks both control extremes before4diagnostics.' if fresh else 'Replay each saved autonomous accepted1s merge through the existing external-boundary mode. Cells/flows/off stocks must match before any native-boundary probe.',
        diagnostic='Observed5s merges plus observed30s source admissions, each spread uniformly within its bin. Initial route inventory, VSL history, off storage/drain and coefficients frozen.' if fresh else 'Replace ONLY the accepted merge boundary with observed5s connector departures uniformly spread each5s bin. Source forecasts, VSL exposure, off storage/drain and coefficients unchanged.',
        exclusions='Ramp arrival/head/queue/gap dynamics are outside this conditional component; no ramp or Omega net gain is reported. Future inputs are diagnostic only. No autonomous qualification.',
        caveats=['Within5s departure time is unknown; uniform spreading is a timing approximation.',
                 'Imposed native merges can exceed the predicted-state receiving/gap envelope. Report rather than clip them.',
                 'Shared off storage/drain is still predicted, not fixed to its future observation.'],
        pins=pins,resume_completed_parity=resume,scope='31 east mainline cells;450s;same30s trapezoidal stock integration.'))
    if fresh:(out/('helper_resumed.txt' if resume else 'helper_executed.txt')).write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    payloads={r['arm']:read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']) for r in records}
    if fresh:
        ri=root/'freeway_first/route_inventory'
        inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json'))
        for p in (ri/'contract.json',ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        for args,kwargs in payloads.values():
            kwargs['offramp_inventory']=copy.deepcopy(inventory)
            kwargs['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
    first=payloads[arms[0]];t0=records[0]['cutoff'];times=[round(t0+30*j,6) for j in range(16)]
    for args,kwargs in payloads.values():
        assert args[0]==first[0][0] and kwargs['ramp_dynamics']==first[1]['ramp_dynamics']
        assert kwargs['port_dynamics']==first[1]['port_dynamics'] and kwargs['horizon_sec']==450
    initial={r['cell']:r['n_veh'] for r in first[0][0] if r['road']=='FW_E'}
    def measure(pred):
        ns={t0:sum(initial.values())}
        for row in pred['cells']:ns[row['time_s']]=ns.get(row['time_s'],0.)+row['n_veh']
        return dict(mainline_ttt=sum((ns[a]+ns[b])*(b-a)/7200 for a,b in zip(times,times[1:])),
            flows={k:sum(x[k] for x in pred['flows']) for k in ('source_admissions','ramp_merges','off_departures','terminal_exits')},
            end_n=ns[times[-1]],
            downstream_by_cell={str(c):sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==c) for c in range(31)})
    saved={};truths={};native={};results=[]
    for record in records:
        arm=record['arm'];p=Path(saved_rows[arm]['source']) if fresh else root/'expanded_joint/selected_predictions'/f's29_early_{arm}.json.gz'
        pins[str(p)]=sha(p);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        with gzip.open(p,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:saved[arm]=json.load(f)
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);truths[arm]=truth
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','port_events.csv'):
            p=truth.folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            if not fresh and name!='port_events.csv':assert pins[str(p)]==frozen['truth_file_pins'][str(p)]
        rows=[dict(v,cell=c,window_end_s=t,terminal_exits=float(v['terminal_exits_inferred']),
                   **{k:float(v[k]) for k in ('source_admissions','ramp_merges','off_departures','downstream_crossings')})
              for (t,road,c),v in truth.flows.items() if road=='FW_E' and t0<t<=times[-1]]
        if fresh:
            anomalies=[(r['window_end_s'],r['cell'],float(r['unexplained_losses'])) for r in rows if float(r['unexplained_losses'])]
            assert sorted(anomalies)==[(2730.1,30,1.),(2790.1,30,1.)]
            assert all(all(float(x[k])==0 for k in ('unexplained_entries','native_removals')) for x in rows)
        else:
            assert all(all(float(x[k])==0 for k in ('unexplained_entries','unexplained_losses','native_removals')) for x in rows)
        native[arm]=measure(dict(cells=[r for t in times[1:] for r in truth.cells[t] if r['road']=='FW_E'],flows=rows))
    for mode in ('saved_merge_parity',conditional_mode):
        for record in records:
            if mode=='saved_merge_parity' and record['arm'] not in parity_arms:continue
            arm=record['arm'];context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
            assert not model.aggregate_merge_speed and not model.lane_groups_enabled and not model.ramp_lane_coupling
            nodes=copy.deepcopy(model.ramp_receiving_nodes);model.ramp_receiving_nodes={}
            args,kwargs=copy.deepcopy(payloads[arm]);assert args[2]==context['parameters']
            if resume and mode=='saved_merge_parity':
                completed=out/f'{mode}_{arm}.json.gz';pins[str(completed)]=sha(completed)
                with gzip.open(completed,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:pred=json.load(f)
                exact={k:pred[k]==saved[arm][k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports')}
                assert all(exact.values())
                results.append(dict(mode=mode,arm=arm,reused=True,exact=exact,predicted=measure(pred),
                    actual=native[arm],receiving_envelope_exceedance=None,diagnostics=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]))
                continue
            kwargs.pop('ramp_dynamics')
            assert model.base.simulation.T_f_sec==1.
            original={(round(x['start_sec'],6),x['ramp']):x['applied_merge_vph'] for x in saved[arm]['ramps']}
            counts=Counter()
            if mode==conditional_mode:
                with (truths[arm].folder/'port_events.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                    for event in csv.DictReader(f):
                        if event['kind']=='departure' and t0<float(event['time_s'])<=times[-1]+1e-7:
                            counts[round(float(event['time_s']),6),event['connector']]+=1
                for ramp,spec in model.ramps.items():
                    if spec['road']!='FW_E':continue
                    c=model._config('FW_E',context['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']).network.ramp_merge_segment_index[ramp]
                    for end in times[1:]:
                        observed=sum(counts[round(end-d,6),str(spec['connector'])] for d in (0,5,10,15,20,25))
                        assert observed==float(truths[arm].flows[end,'FW_E',c]['ramp_merges'])
            requested_source={end:float(truths[arm].flows[end,'FW_E',0]['source_admissions']) for end in times[1:]} if fresh and mode==conditional_mode else None
            for index,step in enumerate(args[1]):
                start=round(step['window_start_s'],6)
                end=round(t0+(math.floor((start-t0+1e-7)/5)+1)*5,6)
                step['ramp_release_vph']={r:(original[start,r] if mode=='saved_merge_parity'
                    else counts[end,str(spec['connector'])]*720.) for r,spec in model.ramps.items() if spec['road']=='FW_E'}
                if requested_source is not None:
                    step['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']=requested_source[times[index//30+1]]*120.
            violations={r:dict(seconds=0,excess_veh=0.,maximum_excess_vph=0.) for r in nodes}
            recovery_values={};recovery_updates=[]
            if recovery_speed and mode==conditional_mode:
                frame_path=root/'freeway_first/flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz')
                expected=load(root/'freeway_first/flow67/verification.json')['artifacts'][frame_path.name]
                assert sha(frame_path)==expected;pins[str(frame_path)]=expected
                with gzip.open(frame_path,'rt') as f:frame_data=json.load(f)['frames']
                for stamp,frame in frame_data.items():
                    for cell in (24,25):
                        speeds=[z[1] for z in frame.values() if z[0]==cell];assert speeds
                        recovery_values[round(float(stamp),6),cell]=sum(speeds)/len(speeds)
            transition=accounting._freeway_substep_events
            traced=[];terms=[]
            def observe(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw):
                if trace_balance:
                    stamp=state.time_sec
                    before_n=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(state,cfg)['FW_E']
                    before_v=list(state.freeway_speed['FW_E'])
                    lengths=accounting.cell_lengths_km(cfg,'FW_E',31)
                scratch=copy.copy(state);scratch.ramp_queue=dict(state.ramp_queue);scratch._control_area_ledger=None
                command=copy.deepcopy(control)
                for r in cfg.network.ramps:
                    cap=cfg.network.ramp_capacity_veh_h[r]
                    scratch.ramp_queue[r]=math.nextafter(cap*cfg.simulation.T_f_h,math.inf)
                    command.ramp_metering[r]=cap
                supply,_=accounting._mn.compute_ramp_release_flows(scratch,command,demand,cfg,include_current_arrivals=False)
                for r in cfg.network.ramps:
                    upstream=cfg.network.ramp_merge_segment_index[r]-1
                    split=sum(cfg.network.off_ramp_split_ratio[o] for o in cfg.network.off_ramps
                        if cfg.network.off_ramp_segment_index[o]==upstream)
                    conflict=state.freeway_density['FW_E'][upstream]*state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][upstream]*(1-split)
                    spec=nodes[r]
                    gap=model.ramps[r]['lanes']*gap_acceptance_supply_vph(conflict,spec['critical_gap_sec'],spec['followup_sec'])
                    excess=max(0.,kw['ramp_release_veh_h'][r]-min(supply[r],gap))
                    z=violations[r];z['seconds']+=int(excess>1e-7)
                    z['excess_veh']+=excess*cfg.simulation.T_f_h
                    z['maximum_excess_vph']=max(z['maximum_excess_vph'],excess)
                result=transition(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw)
                if recovery_values:
                    end=round(state.time_sec+cfg.simulation.T_f_h*3600,6)
                    lo=round(t0+math.floor((end-t0+1e-7)/5)*5,6);hi=min(round(lo+5,6),times[-1])
                    weight=0. if hi==lo else (end-lo)/(hi-lo)
                    before_counts=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(state,cfg)['FW_E']
                    assert all(cfg.network.off_ramp_segment_index[o] not in (24,25) for o in cfg.network.off_ramps)
                    for cell in (24,25):
                        value=(1-weight)*recovery_values[lo,cell]+weight*recovery_values[hi,cell]
                        old_speed=state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][cell]
                        state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][cell]=value
                        state.freeway_flow['FW_E'][cell]=accounting._mn.segment_flow_veh_h(state.freeway_density['FW_E'][cell],value,state.freeway_effective_lanes['FW_E'][cell])
                        recovery_updates.append(dict(time_s=end,cell=cell,equation_speed=old_speed,injected_speed=value))
                    assert accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(state,cfg)['FW_E']==before_counts
                if trace_balance:
                    resources=state._control_area_ledger._response['resource_allocations']
                    get=lambda kind,resource:next(r for r in resources if r['kind']==kind and r['resource']==resource)
                    for cell in range(14,26):
                        send=get('freeway_mainline_sending',f'FW_E:cell:{cell}')
                        recv=get('freeway_mainline_receiving_after_ramps',f'FW_E:cell:{cell+1}')
                        offsend=[r for r in resources if r['kind']=='freeway_offramp_sending' and model.offramps[r['resource']]['from_cell']==cell]
                        assert abs(send['accepted_total_veh']-recv['accepted_total_veh'])<1e-10
                        traced.append(dict(time_s=stamp,cell=cell,n=before_n[cell],v=before_v[cell],
                            kinematic_veh=before_n[cell]*before_v[cell]/lengths[cell]*cfg.simulation.T_f_h,
                            send_veh=send['available_veh'],receive_veh=recv['available_veh'],
                            accepted_veh=send['accepted_total_veh'],
                            off_requested_veh=sum(r['available_veh'] for r in offsend),
                            off_accepted_veh=sum(r['accepted_total_veh'] for r in offsend),
                            effective_lanes=state.freeway_effective_lanes['FW_E'][cell]))
                return result
            started=time.perf_counter();accounting._freeway_substep_events=observe
            try:
                if trace_balance:
                    from diagnostics.sdmpc_n31_20260924.integration_20260926.probe_selected_arrival_path import observe_first_interval_speed_terms
                    probe=Path(sys.modules[observe_first_interval_speed_terms.__module__].__file__)
                    pins[str(probe)]=sha(probe)
                    with observe_first_interval_speed_terms(t0,terms,horizon_sec=450):
                        pred=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
                else:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
            finally:accounting._freeway_substep_events=transition
            if trace_balance:
                previous=root/'freeway_first/conditional_boundary67'/f'obs_{arm}.json.gz'
                pins[str(previous)]=sha(previous)
                with gzip.open(previous,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:reference=json.load(f)
                parity={k:pred[k]==reference[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports')}
                save(out/f'{arm}_parity.json',parity)
                assert all(parity.values()),parity
                assert len(traced)==450*12 and len(terms)==450*19
                with gzip.open(out/f'{arm}_trace.json.gz','wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(dict(allocations=traced,speed_terms=terms),f,allow_nan=False)
                blocks=[]
                term_map={(round(z['time_sec'],6),z['cell']):z for z in terms}
                for end in times[1:]:
                    for cell in range(14,26):
                        rows=[z for z in traced if z['cell']==cell and end-30-1e-7<=z['time_s']<end-1e-7]
                        assert len(rows)==30
                        ts=[term_map[round(z['time_s'],6),cell] for z in rows]
                        pf=next(z for z in pred['flows'] if z['cell']==cell and z['window_end_s']==end)
                        flow_error=abs(sum(z['accepted_veh'] for z in rows)-pf['downstream_crossings'])
                        assert flow_error<1e-8
                        obs=truths[arm].flows[end,'FW_E',cell]
                        blocks.append(dict(cell=cell,end=end,flow_error=flow_error,
                            actual_downstream=float(obs['downstream_crossings']),
                            actual_off=float(obs['off_departures']),
                            totals={k:sum(z[k] for z in rows) for k in ('kinematic_veh','send_veh','accepted_veh','off_requested_veh','off_accepted_veh')},
                            receiving_limited_seconds=sum(z['receive_veh']<z['send_veh']-1e-9 for z in rows),
                            off_limited_seconds=sum(z['off_accepted_veh']<z['off_requested_veh']-1e-9 for z in rows),
                            minimum_receiving_margin_veh=min(z['receive_veh']-z['send_veh'] for z in rows),
                            mean_terms={k:sum(z[k] for z in ts)/30 for k in ('speed_before','rho','desired','relaxation','convection','anticipation','lane_drop_raw','post_equation_change','delta_lanes')},
                            speed_change=ts[-1]['speed_final']-ts[0]['speed_before']))
                save(out/f'{arm}_balance.json',dict(blocks=blocks,parity=parity,conditional_only=True))
            filename=(('parity_' if mode=='saved_merge_parity' else 'obs_')+arm if fresh else
                      f'{mode}_{arm}' if mode=='saved_merge_parity' else f'merge_{arm}')
            with gzip.open(out/f'{filename}.json.gz','wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            exact={k:pred[k]==saved[arm][k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports')} if mode=='saved_merge_parity' else None
            if exact:assert all(exact.values()),exact
            d=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]
            assert d['continuity_residual_max_veh']<1e-7 and d['negative_density_count']==0 and d['jam_density_exceedance_count']==0
            row=dict(mode=mode,arm=arm,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,exact=exact,
                predicted=measure(pred),actual=native[arm],receiving_envelope_exceedance=violations,
                diagnostics=d)
            if recovery_speed:
                assert len(recovery_updates)==(900 if mode==conditional_mode else 0)
                row['future_recovery_speed_updates']=len(recovery_updates)
                save(out/(filename+'_speed_injections.json'),recovery_updates)
            if requested_source is not None:
                admissions={x['window_end_s']:x['source_admissions'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==0}
                row['source_max_error_veh']=max(abs(admissions[t]-n) for t,n in requested_source.items())
                row['source_admissions_match']=row['source_max_error_veh']<1e-8
                row['merge_max_error_veh']=max(abs(sum(x['ramp_merges'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['window_end_s']==end and x['cell']==spec['to_cell'])-
                    sum(counts[round(end-d,6),str(spec['connector'])] for d in (0,5,10,15,20,25))) for end in times[1:] for spec in model.ramps.values() if spec['road']=='FW_E')
                assert row['merge_max_error_veh']<1e-8
            save(out/f'{filename}_result.json',row)
            results.append(row);save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='running',completed=len(results),last_mode=mode,last_arm=arm))
            print(json.dumps(dict(mode=mode,arm=arm,mainline=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],exact=exact)),flush=True)
    for row in results:
        base=next(x for x in results if x['mode']==row['mode'] and x['arm']==arms[0])
        row['delta_mainline_ttt']={k:row[k]['mainline_ttt']-base[k]['mainline_ttt'] for k in ('predicted','actual')}
        row['delta_flows']={k:{f:row[k]['flows'][f]-base[k]['flows'][f] for f in row[k]['flows']} for k in ('predicted','actual')}
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=results,pins=pins,pins_unchanged=True,new_rollouts=sum(not r.get('reused',False) for r in results),
        reused_rollouts=sum(bool(r.get('reused',False)) for r in results),
        conditional_only=True,gain_qualified=False,fit=False,new_native=0,ramp_cost_not_modeled=True,
        source_conditioned=fresh,native_unknown_losses_per_arm=2 if fresh else 0,
        source_conditioning_exact=all(r.get('source_admissions_match',True) for r in results)))
    save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='complete_conditional_diagnostic',completed=len(results)))


def diagnose_expanded_flux_state(*, output_name='flux_state'):
    """Conditional allocation probes at5s snapshots; unchanged autonomous pair."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.control_area_objective import ModelAreaLedger
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    assert output_name in ('flux_state','flux_state_v2','flux_state_v3','flux_state_v4')
    out=root/'freeway_first'/output_name;out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';protocol=load(root/'protocol.json')
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__)}
    for path,digest in protocol['core_pins'].items():assert sha(ROOT/path)==digest;pins[str(ROOT/path)]=digest
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    records=[r for r in load(root/'data_catalog.json')['checked_records'] if r['case']=='s29_early' and r['arm'] in ('none','rm')]
    moment_dir=OLD/'diagnostics/metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924/mainline_moments'
    mm=load(moment_dir/'manifest.json');pins[str(moment_dir/'manifest.json')]=sha(moment_dir/'manifest.json')
    bases=('predicted','observed_n','observed_v','observed_nv','observed_nv_off')
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(bases=bases,arms=['none','rm'],cutoff_sec=2220.1,horizon_sec=450,
        autonomous_rollouts=2,conditional_steps=900,
        scope='Read-only canonical allocation at each5s sample; native N/v current snapshots substituted, not a continuous future-informed forecast.',
        common='Same current predicted source/merge rates, off split/proxy and VSL110; observed_nv_off additionally replaces off stock/free space, retaining its entry proxy.',
        limits=['One instantaneous sampled flow multiplied by5s is not exact native boundary count.',
            'Passive VSL cohorts are reset only in substituted copies; all commands110. Unchanged copy must reproduce the actual step.',
            'Current observed snapshots are future information relative to the original cutoff; diagnostic only.'],
        production_change=False,fit=False,new_native=0,pins=pins))
    all_rows=[];actual_rows=[];exact=[];sample_error=0.;mass_error=0.
    for record in records:
        arm=record['arm'];context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
        args,kwargs=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        assert kwargs['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']=='storage_and_proxy' and not model.lane_groups_enabled
        assert all(all(v==110 for v in step.get('vsl_commands',{}).values()) for step in args[1])
        t0=record['cutoff'];times=[round(t0+5*j,6) for j in range(91)];truth=Path(record['truth'])
        spec=next(r for r in mm['rows'] if r['seed']==29 and r['arm']==arm)
        mp=moment_dir/spec['output'];assert sha(mp)==spec['sha256'];pins[str(mp)]=sha(mp)
        moments=defaultdict(lambda:[0.,0.])
        with gzip.open(mp,'rt',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                if row['scope']=='cell':
                    z=moments[round(float(row['time_s']),6),int(row['bin'])]
                    z[0]+=float(row['n']);z[1]+=float(row['speed_sum'])
        assert sorted({t for t,c in moments})==times
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv','port_events.csv'):
            pins[str(truth/name)]=sha(truth/name)
            if name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):assert pins[str(truth/name)]==protocol['truth_file_pins'][str(truth/name)]
        with (truth/'cells_30s.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                t=round(float(row['time_s']),6);c=int(row['cell'])
                if row['road']!='FW_E' or t not in times:continue
                n,nv=moments[t,c];assert n==float(row['n_veh'])
                if n:assert abs(nv/n-float(row['v_kmh']))<1e-7
        off_ids={str(o) for o,spec in model.offramps.items() if spec['road']=='FW_E'}
        with (truth/'ports_30s.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            observed_ports={(round(float(r['window_end_s']),6),r['connector']):float(r['end_n_veh']) for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['connector'] in off_ids}
        changes=defaultdict(float)
        with (truth/'port_events.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for r in csv.DictReader(f):
                t=round(float(r['time_s']),6)
                if r['connector'] in off_ids and t0<t<=times[-1]:
                    changes[t,r['connector']]+=1 if r['kind']=='arrival' else -1
        off_stock={}
        for o in off_ids:
            n=observed_ports[t0,o];off_stock[t0,o]=n
            for t in times[1:]:
                n+=changes[t,o];assert n>=0;off_stock[t,o]=n
                if (t,o) in observed_ports:assert n==observed_ports[t,o]
        with (truth/'flows_30s.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            native=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['road']=='FW_E' and t0<float(r['window_end_s'])<=times[-1]]
        for c in range(31):
            actual_rows.append(dict(arm=arm,cell=c,downstream=sum(float(r['downstream_crossings']) for r in native if int(r['cell'])==c),
                off=sum(float(r['off_departures']) for r in native if int(r['cell'])==c),
                terminal=sum(float(r['terminal_exits_inferred']) for r in native if int(r['cell'])==c)))
        step_map={round(x['window_start_s'],6):x for x in args[1]}
        transition=accounting._freeway_substep_events;probed=[]
        def get_flow(ledger):
            q=[0.]*31;off=[0.]*31
            for r in ledger._response['resource_allocations']:
                if r['kind']=='freeway_mainline_sending':q[int(r['resource'].rsplit(':',1)[1])]=r['accepted_total_veh']
                elif r['kind']=='freeway_terminal_sending':q[-1]=r['accepted_total_veh']
                elif r['kind']=='freeway_offramp_sending':off[model.offramps[r['resource']]['from_cell']]+=r['accepted_total_veh']
            return q,off
        def observe(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw):
            nonlocal mass_error,sample_error
            t=round(state.time_sec,6);current=[]
            if t in times:
                lengths=accounting.cell_lengths_km(cfg,'FW_E',31)
                for basis in bases:
                    trial=copy.deepcopy(state);trial._control_area_ledger=None;opts=copy.deepcopy(kw)
                    if basis!='predicted' and hasattr(trial,'_component_vsl_exposure'):del trial._component_vsl_exposure
                    if basis in ('observed_n','observed_nv','observed_nv_off'):
                        trial.freeway_density['FW_E']=[moments[t,c][0]/(lengths[c]*trial.freeway_effective_lanes['FW_E'][c]) for c in range(31)]
                    if basis in ('observed_v','observed_nv','observed_nv_off'):
                        trial.freeway_speed['FW_E']=[moments[t,c][1]/moments[t,c][0] if moments[t,c][0] else cfg.network.v_free for c in range(31)]
                    if basis=='observed_nv_off':
                        for o in cfg.network.off_ramps:
                            capacity=model.offramps[o]['storage_capacity_veh']
                            free=max(0.,capacity-off_stock[t,o])
                            trial.urban_link_storage[cfg.network.off_ramp_storage_link[o]]=free
                            drained=step_map[t]['off_drain_vph'][o]*cfg.simulation.T_f_h
                            opts['offramp_capacity_veh_h'][o]=min(step_map[t]['off_capacity_vph'][o],(free+min(off_stock[t,o],drained))/cfg.simulation.T_f_h)
                    n0=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(trial,cfg)['FW_E']
                    stocks={'freeway:FW_E':{'inside':sum(n0)},'origin:FW_E':{'outside':trial.mainline_origin_queue['FW_E']},
                        **{'merge_pending:'+r:{'outside':q*cfg.simulation.T_f_h} for r,q in opts['ramp_release_veh_h'].items()}}
                    trace=ModelAreaLedger(stocks,capture_response=True);trace.begin_response_step('freeway',t,t+1)
                    trace.expect_constraint_coverage('freeway_allocator');trial._control_area_ledger=trace
                    # Nested probes must not add rows to the autonomous
                    # harness's live desired-speed observation list.
                    observed_fd=accounting._mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh
                    closure=dict(zip(observed_fd.__code__.co_freevars,
                        (x.cell_contents for x in observed_fd.__closure__ or ())))
                    assert observed_fd.__name__=='record_desired' and callable(closure['desired_speed'])
                    from evaluation.controllers import vissim_stackelberg_adapter as adapter
                    contexts=[(x,copy.deepcopy(x)) for x in (adapter._FW_SEG_CTX,adapter._FW_SEG_CTX_STATE)]
                    accounting._mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh=closure['desired_speed']
                    try:transition(trial,copy.deepcopy(control),copy.deepcopy(demand),copy.deepcopy(cfg),*a,**opts)
                    finally:
                        accounting._mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh=observed_fd
                        for target,previous in contexts:target.clear();target.update(previous)
                    q,off=get_flow(trace);n1=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(trial,cfg)['FW_E']
                    entry=next(r['accepted_total_veh'] for r in trace._response['resource_allocations'] if r['kind']=='freeway_entry_request')
                    merges=[0.]*31
                    for r,v in opts['ramp_release_veh_h'].items():merges[cfg.network.ramp_merge_segment_index[r]]+=v*cfg.simulation.T_f_h
                    for c in range(31):
                        mass_error=max(mass_error,abs(n1[c]-n0[c]-(entry if c==0 else q[c-1])-merges[c]+q[c]+off[c]))
                    current.append(dict(arm=arm,basis=basis,time_s=t,downstream_1s=q,off_1s=off))
            result=transition(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw)
            if current:
                q,off=get_flow(state._control_area_ledger);base=current[0]
                sample_error=max(sample_error,max(abs(x-y) for x,y in zip(q+off,base['downstream_1s']+base['off_1s'])))
                assert sample_error<1e-10
                probed.extend(current)
            return result
        accounting._freeway_substep_events=observe
        try:prediction=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
        finally:accounting._freeway_substep_events=transition
        oldpath=root/'expanded_joint/selected_predictions'/f's29_early_{arm}.json.gz';pins[str(oldpath)]=sha(oldpath)
        with gzip.open(oldpath,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:old=json.load(f)
        serialized=json.loads(json.dumps(prediction,allow_nan=False))
        exact.append(dict(arm=arm,**{k:old[k]==serialized[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}))
        save(out/(arm+'_parity.json'),exact[-1])
        if not all(v for k,v in exact[-1].items() if k!='arm'):
            with gzip.open(out/(arm+'_failed.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(prediction,f,allow_nan=False)
        assert all(v for k,v in exact[-1].items() if k!='arm')
        assert len(probed)==90*len(bases)
        all_rows.extend(probed)
        save(out/(arm+'.json'),dict(rows=probed,unchanged_autonomous_arrays=True))
        print(json.dumps(dict(arm=arm,conditional_steps=len(probed),parity=exact[-1])),flush=True)
    assert mass_error<1e-8
    totals=[]
    for arm in ('none','rm'):
        for basis in bases:
            samples=[r for r in all_rows if r['arm']==arm and r['basis']==basis]
            totals.extend(dict(arm=arm,basis=basis,cell=c,
                sampled_downstream=sum(r['downstream_1s'][c]*5 for r in samples),
                sampled_off=sum(r['off_1s'][c]*5 for r in samples)) for c in range(31))
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(totals=totals,actual=actual_rows,pins=pins,parity=exact,max_mass_error=mass_error,
        copied_step_flow_error=sample_error,conditional_steps=len(all_rows),autonomous_rollouts=2,
        future_snapshots_diagnostic_only=True,gain_qualified=False,new_native=0,fit=False))


def audit_saved_vsl_discharge():
    """Locate current VSL response errors using saved states/flows only."""
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929'
    out=parent/'spatial_vsl_response';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    assert not (out/'summary.json').exists(), 'Reuse completed spatial audit'
    results={};pins={};verification=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        capture_path=parent/f'component2220_s{seed}_inputs_v2/capture.json'
        manifest=load(capture_path);pins[str(capture_path)]=sha(capture_path)
        t0=manifest['cutoff'];times=[round(t0+30*j,6) for j in range(16)]
        data={};cost_checks={};geometry=None
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            record=next(r for r in manifest['records'] if r['arm']==arm)
            truth=Path(record['truth']);proof=load(truth/'manifest.json')
            native={}
            for name,time_key in [('cells_30s.csv','time_s'),('flows_30s.csv','window_end_s')]:
                path=truth/name;pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
                if 'files' in proof:
                    assert pins[str(path)]==proof['files'][name]
                    verification.append(dict(path=str(path),proof='native extraction manifest SHA'))
                else:
                    assert seed==43 and arm=='none' and proof['seed']==43
                    prior=load(parent/'component2220_s43_conditional_inflow/summary.json')['conditional_source_pins']
                    if name=='flows_30s.csv':
                        assert pins[str(path)]==prior[str(path)]
                        basis='previous conditional audit SHA'
                    else:
                        basis='No prior per-file SHA in native manifest; current hash and exact prior mainline TTT checked only'
                    verification.append(dict(path=str(path),proof=basis))
                with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                    native[name]={(round(float(r[time_key]),6),int(r['cell'])):r
                        for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['road']=='FW_E' and t0<=float(r[time_key])<=times[-1]}
            initial={c:native['cells_30s.csv'][t0,c] for c in range(31)}
            if geometry is None:
                geometry=load(truth/'geometry.json');pins[str(truth/'geometry.json')]=sha(truth/'geometry.json')
            data['actual',arm]=(native['cells_30s.csv'],native['flows_30s.csv'])
            folders={'current':parent/f'component2220_s{seed}_current_v2',
                     'observed_inflow_diagnostic':parent/f'component2220_s{seed}_conditional_inflow',
                     'rejected_E4':parent/'vsl_E4_countercheck'/f's{seed}_early'}
            for version,folder in folders.items():
                p=folder/(arm+'_prediction.json.gz');pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
                with gzip.open(p,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:pred=json.load(f)
                cells={(round(r['time_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in pred['cells']}
                cells.update({(t0,c):r for c,r in initial.items()})
                flows={(round(r['window_end_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in pred['flows']}
                data[version,arm]=(cells,flows)
                cost_checks[version,arm]=load(folder/(arm+'_result.json'))
        assert all(data['actual','none'][0][t0,c]==data['actual','vsl'][0][t0,c] for c in range(31))
        rows=[];blocks=[]
        for cell in range(31):
            row=dict(cell=cell,initial=data['actual','none'][0][t0,cell],
                     geometry=next(z for z in geometry['cells'] if z['road']=='FW_E' and z['cell']==cell))
            for version in ('actual',*folders):
                values={}
