        improves=bool(feasible) and next(s['score'] for s in paired if s['name']==selected)<baseline_score
        selection.update(selected=selected,paired_scores=paired,baseline_score=baseline_score,training_improved=improves,
            adopted=False,status='frozen_for_validation' if improves else 'rejected_training_diagnostic_only')
        comparison=['baseline','cell23','recovery_'+selected,'combined_'+selected]
    save(out/'frozen_selection.json',selection)
    choices=[]
    for name in comparison:
        for r in records:run(name,r)
        for case in sorted({r['case'] for r in records}):
            rows=[z for (v,c,a),z in results.items() if v==name and c==case and not (recovery_pair and c=='s47_late' and a=='hold_vsl90')]
            held=next(z for z in rows if z['arm']==z['reference'])
            for z in rows:
                z['predicted_delta']=z['costs']['predicted']['total']-held['costs']['predicted']['total']
                z['actual_delta']=z['costs']['actual']['total']-held['costs']['actual']['total']
            best=min(rows,key=lambda z:z['costs']['predicted']['total']);actual=min(rows,key=lambda z:z['costs']['actual']['total'])
            choices.append(dict(variant=name,case=case,selected=best['arm'],actual_best=actual['arm'],
                regret=best['costs']['actual']['total']-actual['costs']['actual']['total']))
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(selected=selected,training_scores=scores,rows=list(results.values()),choices=choices,
        rollouts=len(results),new_native=0,production_adopted=False,gain_qualified=False,optimizer_iterations=0,
        new_rollouts=new_count,reused_predictions=reused,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-overall_begin,
        comparison=comparison,selection=selection,
        scope='East31+8connector costs only; not full Omega or outside waiting.'))
    print(json.dumps(dict(selected=selected,choices=choices)),flush=True)


def local_speed_balance(*, recovery_pair=False, expanded_joint=False):
    """Conditional speed-term audit, no fitting or autonomous rollout.

    Existing 30s snapshots and only the preceding30s merge count are supplied
    to the canonical1s speed update. The observed next30s trend is a descriptive
    comparator, NOT a same-vehicle acceleration measurement or forecast input.
    """
    sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'vendor/NumSim-mine'))
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from evaluation.controllers import area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients, literature_desired_speed
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    out=HERE/'closedloop9000_d4e2_analysis'/('recovery_cells24_25/conditional_terms' if recovery_pair else 'recovery_response/seed47_audit/speed_balance')
    cw=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    if expanded_joint:out=cw/'freeway_first/local_terms53'
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    assert not (out/'summary.json').exists(), 'Preserve the completed speed audit'
    records={r['arm']:r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']=='s47_late'}
    if expanded_joint:
        admission=load(cw/'additional_admission_s53.json')
        for p,h in admission['pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h
        records={r['arm']:r for r in admission['records'] if r['arm'] in ('hold','release')}
        for record in records.values():
            a,k=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256'])
            assert all(all(v==110 for v in step.get('vsl_commands',{}).values()) for step in a[1])
    pins={};native={};rows=[]
    for arm in (('hold','release') if expanded_joint else ('hold','release_10484')):
        folder=Path(records[arm]['truth']);manifest=load(folder/'manifest.json')
        data={}
        for name,time_key in [('cells_30s.csv','time_s'),('flows_30s.csv','window_end_s')]:
            path=folder/name;assert sha(path)==manifest['files'][name];pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                data[name]={(round(float(z[time_key]),6),int(z['cell'])):z for z in csv.DictReader(f) if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        native[arm]=data
    manifests={'baseline':HERE/'heldout53_response_v2/candidate_manifest.json',
               'tau4':out.parents[1]/'candidate_manifest.json'}
    if recovery_pair:
        manifests={'baseline':HERE/'heldout53_response_v2/candidate_manifest.json',
                   'cell23':HERE/'closedloop9000_d4e2_analysis/local_merge_cell23/candidate_manifest.json'}
    if expanded_joint:manifests={'expanded':cw/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'}
    bases=('native','autonomous_prediction')
    if recovery_pair or expanded_joint:
        bases=tuple(f'local{a}_up{b}_down{c}' for a in (0,1) for b in (0,1) for c in (0,1))
    for variant,manifest in manifests.items():
        pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
        ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
        cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network
        maximum=max(cfg.freeway_follower.vsl_set);assert maximum==110
        control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':maximum})
        for arm in native:
            path=(HERE/'closedloop9000_d4e2_analysis/local_merge_cell23'/('nu0.25_delta2' if variant=='cell23' else 'baseline')/f's47_late_{arm}.json.gz'
                  if recovery_pair else out.parents[1]/variant/f's47_late_{arm}.json.gz')
            if expanded_joint:path=cw/'freeway_first/expanded_seed53/expanded'/f'{arm}_prediction.json.gz'
            pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:pred=json.load(f)
            pc={(round(z['time_s'],6),int(z['cell'])):z for z in pred['cells']}
            pf={(round(z['window_end_s'],6),int(z['cell'])):z for z in pred['flows']}
            for basis in bases:
                states=native[arm]['cells_30s.csv'] if basis=='native' else pc
                flows=native[arm]['flows_30s.csv'] if basis=='native' else pf
                if recovery_pair or expanded_joint:
                    observed_local,observed_up,observed_down=(int(x[-1]) for x in basis.split('_'))
                    states=native[arm]['cells_30s.csv'] if observed_local else pc
                    flows=native[arm]['flows_30s.csv'] if observed_local else pf
                for j in range(1,15):
                    stamp=round(2670.1+30*j,6)
                    for cell in (21,22,23,24,25):
                        local=states[stamp,cell];up=states[stamp,cell-1];down=states[stamp,cell+1]
                        if recovery_pair or expanded_joint:
                            up=(native[arm]['cells_30s.csv'] if observed_up else pc)[stamp,cell-1]
                            down=(native[arm]['cells_30s.csv'] if observed_down else pc)[stamp,cell+1]
                        v=float(local['v_kmh']);vu=float(up['v_kmh'])
                        rho=float(local['rho_veh_per_km_lane']);rd=float(down['rho_veh_per_km_lane'])
                        p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell]
                        length=p['segment_length_km'];lanes=net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][cell]
                        assert abs(lanes-3)<1e-10 and abs(net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][cell+1]-3)<1e-10
                        vsl=accounting._mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',cell,cfg)
                        desired=accounting._mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,vsl,
                            net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                        desired=literature_desired_speed((getattr(net,'freeway_vsl_fd_response',{}) or {}).get('FW_E'),
                            cfg,'FW_E',cell,rho,desired,vsl,False)
                        nu0=accounting._mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,vsl)
                        spec=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',cell)
                        tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,rd,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                        kappa=p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'];dt=1/3600
                        relaxation=dt/tau*(desired-v)
                        convection=dt/length*v*(vu-v)
                        anticipation=-nu*dt/(tau*length)*(rd-rho)/(rho+kappa)
                        canonical=accounting._mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(v,vu,rho,rd,desired,dt,length,
                            net.metanet_tau_h,nu0,net.metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane,net.v_min)
                        reconstructed=max(net.v_min,v+relaxation+convection+anticipation)
                        assert abs(canonical-reconstructed)<1e-10,(variant,arm,basis,stamp,cell,canonical,reconstructed)
                        rate=float(flows[stamp,cell]['ramp_merges'])*120
                        delta=spec.get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)
                        merge=delta*dt*rate*v/(length*lanes*(rho+kappa))
                        final=max(net.v_min,canonical-merge)
                        following=float(states[round(stamp+30,6),cell]['v_kmh'])
                        rows.append(dict(variant=variant,arm=arm,basis=basis,time_s=stamp,cell=cell,
                            speed_kmh=v,upstream_speed_kmh=vu,rho=rho,downstream_rho=rd,desired_speed_kmh=desired,
                            preceding30_merge_vph=rate,tau_sec=tau*3600,nu=nu,delta_merge=delta,
                            relaxation_kmh_per_s=relaxation,convection_kmh_per_s=convection,
                            anticipation_kmh_per_s=anticipation,merge_loss_kmh_per_s=merge,
                            total_kmh_per_s=final-v,next30_snapshot_trend_kmh_per_s=(following-v)/30,
                            canonical_reconstruction_error=abs(canonical-reconstructed)))
    groups=[]
    for variant in manifests:
        for arm in native:
            for basis in bases:
                for cell in (21,22,23,24,25):
                    selected=[r for r in rows if (r['variant'],r['arm'],r['basis'],r['cell'])==(variant,arm,basis,cell)]
                    metrics=('relaxation_kmh_per_s','convection_kmh_per_s','anticipation_kmh_per_s',
                        'merge_loss_kmh_per_s','total_kmh_per_s','next30_snapshot_trend_kmh_per_s')
                    groups.append(dict(variant=variant,arm=arm,basis=basis,cell=cell,samples=len(selected),
                        means={k:sum(r[k] for r in selected)/len(selected) for k in metrics},
                        positive_acceleration_samples=sum(r['total_kmh_per_s']>0 for r in selected),
                        recovery_active_samples=sum(r['tau_sec']<12 for r in selected)))
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=groups,rows=rows,pins=pins,source_sha256=sha(Path(__file__)),
        new_rollouts=0,new_native=0,new_coefficients=0,canonical_evaluations=len(rows),
        exact_reconstruction=True,scope='Conditional local derivative audit. Next30 snapshot changes include vehicle mixing and sampling; not observed individual acceleration.'))
    with (out/'terms.csv').open('w',encoding='utf-8',newline='') as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
    print(json.dumps(dict(evaluations=len(rows),cell23=[g for g in groups if g['cell']==23])),flush=True)


def cached_wave_audit():
    """Locate discharge-response errors in existing 30s caches; no forecasting."""
    manifest=load(OUT/'capture.json'); t0=manifest['cutoff']; end=t0+manifest['horizon']
    assert manifest['horizon']==450
    target=OUT/'wave_response_20260928.json'
    if target.exists():raise ValueError('Preserve the previous cached wave audit')
    pins={str(OUT/'capture.json'):sha(OUT/'capture.json')}; data={}
    for arm in ('hold','release'):
        record=next(r for r in manifest['records'] if r['arm']==arm)
        truth=Path(record['truth']); native_manifest=load(truth/'manifest.json')
        cells={}; flows={}
        for name,dest,time_key in (('cells_30s.csv',cells,'time_s'),
                                    ('flows_30s.csv',flows,'window_end_s')):
            path=truth/name; digest=sha(path); assert digest==native_manifest['files'][name]
            pins[str(path)]=digest
            with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                    time=round(float(row[time_key]),6)
                    if row['road']=='FW_E' and t0<time<=end:
                        dest[time,int(row['cell'])]=row
        path=OUT/(arm+'_prediction.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:pred=json.load(f)
        data['actual',arm]=(cells,flows)
        data['predicted',arm]=({(round(r['time_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in pred['cells']},
            {(round(r['window_end_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in pred['flows']})
    rows=[]
    for cell in range(31):
        for block in range(3):
            a=t0+150*block;b=a+150;times=[round(a+30*j,6) for j in range(1,6)]
            row=dict(cell=cell,start_sec=a,end_sec=b)
            for kind in ('actual','predicted'):
                arms={}
                for arm in ('hold','release'):
                    cells,flows=data[kind,arm]; c=[cells[t,cell] for t in times]; f=[flows[t,cell] for t in times]
                    eligible=[r for r in c if r['v_kmh'] not in ('',None) and float(r['n_veh'])>0]
                    weight=sum(float(r['n_veh']) for r in eligible)
                    arms[arm]=dict(downstream_veh=sum(float(r['downstream_crossings']) for r in f),
                        merge_veh=sum(float(r['ramp_merges']) for r in f),
                        off_veh=sum(float(r['off_departures']) for r in f),
                        end_stock_veh=float(c[-1]['n_veh']),
                        mean_density=sum(float(r['rho_veh_per_km_lane']) for r in c)/5,
                        vehicle_weighted_snapshot_speed_kmh=(sum(float(r['n_veh'])*float(r['v_kmh']) for r in eligible)/weight if weight else None))
                row[kind]=dict(arms=arms,delta={k:(arms['release'][k]-arms['hold'][k]
                    if arms['release'][k] is not None and arms['hold'][k] is not None else None) for k in arms['hold']})
            rows.append(row)
    with (OUT/'cell_response.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        reference={int(r['cell']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['arm']=='release'}
    for cell in range(31):
        for kind in ('actual','predicted'):
            value=sum(r[kind]['delta']['downstream_veh'] for r in rows if r['cell']==cell)
            assert abs(value-float(reference[cell][kind+'_downstream_crossings']))<1e-7
    save(target,dict(rows=rows,pins=pins,new_rollouts=0,new_native_runs=0,fzp_rescans=0,
        full_horizon_flow_parity=True,control='release minus hold at the same2670.1s microscopic state',
        speed_basis='Vehicle-weighted mean of five30s endpoint speed snapshots per150s; not a continuous trajectory mean.',
        limitations=['Retrospective localization only; not future prediction inputs or a capacity estimate.',
                     'Cell-specific speed, density and discharge differences do not alone identify their causal mechanism.']))
    print(json.dumps({'output':str(target),'cells22_23_24':[
        dict(cell=r['cell'],start_sec=r['start_sec'],actual=r['actual']['delta'],predicted=r['predicted']['delta'])
        for r in rows if r['cell'] in (22,23,24)]}),flush=True)


def local_lane_response():
    """One causal sparse-lane candidate and its constant-rate ablation, no grid."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'closedloop9000_d4e2_analysis';base=parent/'recovery_flow_audit'
    out=base/'local_lane_candidate';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    retry = (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    if retry:
        assert not (out/'summary.json').exists() and not (out/'attempt2_source_pins.json').exists()
        assert load(out/'failure.json')['error'] == "ValueError('Missing or extra component desired-speed observations')"
        (out/'failure_attempt1.json').write_bytes((out/'failure.json').read_bytes())
    local=base/'native10484/local_state';initial=load(local/'initial_state.json')
    with gzip.open(local/'past_frames.json.gz','rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:past=json.load(f)['frames']
    import bisect
    geometry_manifest=load(HERE/'selected/plant_n31_v2.json')
    geometry_path=ROOT/geometry_manifest['sources']['geometry']['path'];geometry=load(geometry_path)
    assert sha(geometry_path)==geometry_manifest['sources']['geometry']['sha256']
    offsets={z['link']:z['offset_m'] for z in geometry['chains']['FW_E']}
    with (local/'exchange_5s.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        observations={(float(z['start']),int(z['donor'])):int(z['inside_region_endpoint_changes'])
                      for z in csv.DictReader(f) if z['arm']=='past'}
    # Three shared exchange parameters: two baseline hazards and one speed-gap
    # sensitivity. Likelihood uses only past endpoint changes and donor stock.
    training=[];speed_scale=110.
    for t in sorted(map(float,past))[:-1]:
        grouped={(c,g):[] for c in (22,23) for g in (0,1)}
        for z in past[str(t)]:
            if z[1] not in offsets:continue
            c=bisect.bisect_right(geometry['bounds']['FW_E'],offsets[z[1]]+z[3])-1
            if c in (22,23):grouped[c,0 if z[2]==1 else 1].append(z)
        for g in (0,1):
            terms=[]
            for c in (22,23):
                donor,recipient=grouped[c,g],grouped[c,1-g]
                assert donor and recipient, 'Empty-group speed is not identified by this fit'
                dv=sum(z[4] for z in recipient)/len(recipient)-sum(z[4] for z in donor)/len(donor)
                terms.append((len(donor)*5,dv/speed_scale))
            training.append(dict(time=t,g=g,y=observations[t,g],terms=terms))
    def profile(beta):
        exposures=[sum(n*math.exp(beta*x) for n,x in z['terms']) for z in training]
        counts=[sum(z['y'] for z in training if z['g']==g) for g in (0,1)]
        totals=[sum(e for e,z in zip(exposures,training) if z['g']==g) for g in (0,1)]
        hazards=[c/e for c,e in zip(counts,totals)]
        loss=sum(e*hazards[z['g']]-z['y']*math.log(e*hazards[z['g']]) for e,z in zip(exposures,training))
        return loss,hazards
    # One scalar bounded likelihood solve; no physical-error/TTT objective.
    lo,hi=0.,20.;phi=(math.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c=hi-phi*(hi-lo);d=lo+phi*(hi-lo)
    for _ in range(60):
        if profile(c)[0]<profile(d)[0]:hi,d=d,c;c=hi-phi*(hi-lo)
        else:lo,c=c,d;d=lo+phi*(hi-lo)
    beta=min((0.,20.,(lo+hi)/2),key=lambda x:profile(x)[0])
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(parent/'local_merge_cell23/candidate_manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    spec=dict(schema='local-merge-lanes/v1',road='FW_E',cutoff=initial['cutoff'],
              cells=initial['cells'],ramps={'RM_C10484':0})
    variants={name:dict(spec,exchange=dict(rates_per_sec=profile(b)[1],speed_sensitivity=b,speed_scale_kmh=speed_scale))
              for name,b in [('constant',0.),('speed_response',beta)]}
    sources=[Path(__file__),local/'initial_state.json',local/'past_frames.json.gz',local/'exchange_5s.csv',
             ROOT/'evaluation/controllers/physical_lane_groups.py',ROOT/'evaluation/controllers/area_freeway_accounting.py',
             ROOT/'diagnostics/demand_sweep/user_native_20260914/metanet_calibration_v1/canonical_harness.py']
    protocol=dict(train_window=[2520.1,2670.1],calibration='Past endpoint-change Poisson likelihood; three shared exchange parameters only',
        beta_domain=[0.,20.],coefficient_grid=0,max_new_rollouts=10,max_wall_sec=180,
        active_cells=[22,23],unchanged='Canonical31 cells elsewhere, FD/merge/recovery coefficients, physical jam storage, off dynamics, commands, demand, objective; VSL cohort fractions preserved and shared by local groups.',
        assumptions='Uniform lane shares at aggregate neighbours; lane1/lanes2-3 share within-cell VSL command fractions. Same-cell and cross-cell endpoint changes pooled; unresolved5s paths are not exact hazards.',
        qualification='Candidate only. Full v2/SDMPC and independent seed not qualified. No new native or push.',
        physical_gate='All mass and source/receiving constraints; no speed above200km/h; no negative/projected inventory. No grid if it fails.',
        comparison='Existing cell23 model vs sparse constant/speed-responsive exchange, four executed-command arms.',pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources})
    frozen=dict(beta=beta,at_bound=beta in (0.,20.),loss0=profile(0.)[0],loss_selected=profile(beta)[0],variants=variants)
    if retry:
        assert load(out/'frozen_exchange.json')==frozen, 'Do not refit after validation'
        save(out/'attempt2_source_pins.json',dict(reason='Repair pure desired-speed audit for two actual local groups plus unused aggregate call; physical equations and fitted coefficients unchanged.',pins=protocol['pins']))
    else:
        save(out/'protocol.json',protocol)
        save(out/'frozen_exchange.json',frozen)
    begin=time.perf_counter();results=[];disabled={};new_count=0
    records=load(HERE/'heldout53_response_v2/capture.json')['records']
    for rec in records:
        arm=rec['arm'];args,kwargs=read_primitive_capture(rec['input'],rec['sha256'])
        truth=ObservationData(HERE/'heldout53_response_v2/observations'/arm)
        times=[round(2670.1+j*30,6) for j in range(16)]
        observed={(t,int(z['cell'])):z for t in times for z in truth.cells[t] if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        oldpath=parent/'local_merge_cell23/nu0.25_delta2'/('s53_late_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        with gzip.open(oldpath,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:old=json.load(f)
        if arm in ('hold','release'):
            current=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kwargs));new_count+=1
            normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(current,allow_nan=False))
            disabled[arm]={k:normalized[k]==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
            assert all(disabled[arm].values()), 'Disabled path changed recorded physical trajectory'
            save(out/'disabled_check_normalized.json',dict(exact=disabled,note='Serialize numeric map keys exactly as historical JSON before comparing. First raw in-memory equality failed only on key types.'))
        for name in ('existing','constant','speed_response'):
            if name=='existing':pred=old
            else:
                assert new_count<10 and time.perf_counter()-begin<180
                kw=copy.deepcopy(kwargs);kw['local_merge_group_dynamics']={'FW_E':variants[name]}
                try:pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**kw)
                except Exception as exc:
                    save(out/'failure.json',dict(variant=name,arm=arm,error=repr(exc),new_count=new_count,results=results))
                    raise
                new_count+=1
                with gzip.open(out/(name+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            tab={(round(z['time_s'],6),z['cell']):z for z in pred['cells']}
            pflows={(round(z['window_end_s'],6),z['cell']):z for z in pred['flows']}
            rmse=lambda pairs:math.sqrt(sum((x-y)**2 for x,y in pairs)/len(pairs))
            cells={}
            for c in range(21,26):
                cells[c]=dict(speed_rmse=rmse([(tab[t,c]['v_kmh'],float(observed[t,c]['v_kmh'])) for t in times[1:]]),
                    mean_speed=sum(tab[t,c]['v_kmh'] for t in times[1:])/15,
                    observed_mean_speed=sum(float(observed[t,c]['v_kmh']) for t in times[1:])/15,
                    end_n=tab[times[-1],c]['n_veh'],observed_end_n=float(observed[times[-1],c]['n_veh']),
                    discharge=sum(pflows[t,c]['downstream_crossings'] for t in times[1:]),
                    observed_discharge=sum(float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times[1:]))
            port_ids={str(v['connector']) for v in model.ramps.values() if v['road']=='FW_E'}|{str(o) for o,v in model.offramps.items() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
            costs={}
            for kind,table in [('actual',observed),('predicted',tab)]:
                ns={t:sum(float(table[t,c]['n_veh']) for c in range(31)) for t in times[1:]}
                ns[times[0]]=sum(float(observed[times[0],c]['n_veh']) for c in range(31))
                ports={t:(sum(z['n_veh'] for z in pred['ports'] if abs(z['time_s']-t)<1e-6)+sum(z['end']['connector_veh'] for z in pred['ramps'] if abs(z['end_sec']-t)<1e-6)
                    if kind=='predicted' else sum(float(truth.ports[t,o]['end_n_veh']) for o in port_ids)) for t in times[1:]}
                ports[times[0]]=sum(float(truth.ports[times[0],o]['end_n_veh']) for o in port_ids)
                integral=lambda n:sum((n[x]+n[y])*(y-x)/7200 for x,y in zip(times,times[1:]))
                costs[kind]=dict(main=integral(ns),ports=integral(ports),total=integral(ns)+integral(ports))
            local_detail=pred.get('local_merge_groups',{}).get('FW_E',{})
            ramp=[z for z in pred['ramps'] if z['ramp']=='RM_C10484']
            whole_n=rmse([(tab[t,c]['n_veh'],float(observed[t,c]['n_veh'])) for t in times[1:] for c in range(31)])
            whole_q=rmse([(pflows[t,c]['terminal_exits' if c==30 else 'downstream_crossings'],float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['terminal_exits_inferred' if c==30 else 'downstream_crossings'])) for t in times[1:] for c in range(31)])
            result=dict(variant=name,arm=arm,cells=cells,costs=costs,whole_stock_rmse=whole_n,whole_flow_rmse=whole_q,
                merge10484=sum(z['accepted_merge_veh'] for z in ramp),posthead_final=ramp[-1]['end']['downstream_travelling_veh']+ramp[-1]['end']['merge_ready_veh'],
                local_mass_residual=local_detail.get('maximum_mass_residual'),maximum_local_speed=local_detail.get('maximum_speed'),
                global_mass_residual=max(z['continuity_residual_max_veh'] for z in pred['diagnostics']['roads']),
                port_mass_residual=max(abs(z['conservation_residual_veh']) for z in pred['ports']+pred['ramps']))
            if name!='existing':assert result['local_mass_residual']<1e-7 and result['maximum_local_speed']<200
            assert result['global_mass_residual']<1e-7 and result['port_mass_residual']<1e-7
            results.append(result)
            save(out/'partial_results.json',results)
            print(json.dumps(dict(variant=name,arm=arm,stock_rmse=whole_n,flow_rmse=whole_q,merge10484=result['merge10484'])),flush=True)
    for z in results:
        held=next(r for r in results if r['variant']==z['variant'] and r['arm']=='hold')
        z['predicted_delta']=z['costs']['predicted']['total']-held['costs']['predicted']['total']
        z['actual_delta']=z['costs']['actual']['total']-held['costs']['actual']['total']
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=results,disabled=disabled,new_rollouts=new_count,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-begin,
        objective_scope='East31 plus8connectors using matching30s trapezoids; not full Omega or outside waits.',
        new_native=0,gain_qualified=False,production_adopted=False,future_observation_inputs=False))


def diagnose_branch_response():
    """Separate total discharge and realized exit mix; future mix is diagnostic only."""
    sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    cw=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    out=cw/'freeway_first/branch_response'
    assert (out/'helper_before.py.txt').is_file() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    source=cw/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(source);model=context['component']
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['role']=='train']
    assert len(records)==8 and {r['case'] for r in records}=={'s29_early','s29_late'}
    offcells={str(o):int(v['from_cell']) for o,v in model.offramps.items() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
    assert offcells=={'10643':9,'10682':11,'10481':18,'10483':20}
    oldproxy=cw/'freeway_first/off_entry_proxy'
    pins={str(source):sha(source),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for v in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    (out/'helper_executed.py.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def read_prediction(path):
        pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:return json.load(f)
    payloads={};truths={};native={};predictions={};observed_mix={}
    for r in records:
        key=r['case'],r['arm'];name='_'.join(key)
        payloads[key]=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);truths[key]=truth
        for filename in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv','geometry.json'):
            p=Path(r['truth'])/filename;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        t0=r['cutoff'];end=t0+450
        with (Path(r['truth'])/'flows_30s.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            native[key]=[x for x in csv.DictReader(f) if x['road']=='FW_E' and t0<float(x['window_end_s'])<=end]
        assert len(native[key])==31*15
        predictions['proxy',key]=read_prediction(cw/'expanded_joint/selected_predictions'/(name+'.json.gz'))
        sp=(oldproxy/('storage_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz') if r['case']=='s29_late' else
            oldproxy/'coverage/storage'/(name+'.json.gz'))
        predictions['storage',key]=read_prediction(sp)
        observed_mix[key]={}
        for x in native[key]:
            for off,cell in offcells.items():
                if int(x['cell'])!=cell:continue
                q=float(x['off_departures'])+float(x['downstream_crossings'])
                assert q>0, 'No realized split identified when no vehicles depart'
                observed_mix[key][round(float(x['window_end_s']),6),off]=float(x['off_departures'])/q
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: priority clarification only; this turn executes the next available diagnostic.',
        hypothesis='Fixed historical exit shares miss route-composition/differential-service changes; separate these from total discharge before modifying dynamics.',
        records=[(r['case'],r['arm']) for r in records],max_new_rollouts=10,parity=2,conditional_rollouts=8,
        cached_forecasts=16,refit=False,native=0,production_adopted=False,
        conditional='Future30s realized off/(off+through) substituted in boundary steps; not latent destination demand or autonomous forecast. Dynamic storage/drain/lane loss unchanged; storage-only mode matches cached diagnostic baseline.',
        limits='Realized share combines destinations, timing, lane access and receiving. Substitution is neither a route-stock model nor independent proof of causality; do not fit its result or use in control.',
        unchanged='All parameters, initial conditions, source/ramp arrival forecasts, commands, travel, storage/drain and costs.',pins=pins))
    parity={};rows=[];start=time.perf_counter()
    for r in records:
        key=r['case'],r['arm'];name='_'.join(key)
        if r['arm']==r['reference']:
            a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[key]);k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            p=json.loads(json.dumps(model.rollout(*a,**k),allow_nan=False))
            parity[name]={f:p[f]==predictions['storage',key][f] for f in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
            assert all(parity[name].values()),name
        a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[key]);k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        for step in a[1]:
            index=math.floor((step['window_start_s']-r['cutoff']+1e-7)/30)+1
            end=round(r['cutoff']+30*index,6)
            for off in offcells:step['off_split_ratio'][off]=observed_mix[key][end,off]
        pred=model.rollout(*a,**k);predictions['observed_mix',key]=pred
        with gzip.open(out/(name+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        residual=max(abs(x['conservation_residual_veh']) for x in pred['ports']+pred['ramps'])
        assert residual<1e-7
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truths[key]);row['conservation_max']=residual;rows.append(row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(case=key,main=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],actual=row['actual']['mainline_ttt'])),flush=True)
    save(out/'parity.json',parity)
    def totals(flows,cell):
        off=sum(float(x['off_departures']) for x in flows if int(x['cell'])==cell)
        through=sum(float(x['downstream_crossings']) for x in flows if int(x['cell'])==cell)
        return dict(off=off,through=through,q=off+through,beta=off/(off+through))
    decompositions=[];contrasts=[]
    for r in records:
        if r['arm']==r['reference']:continue
        key=r['case'],r['arm'];ref=r['case'],r['reference']
        for off,cell in offcells.items():
            for mode in ('actual','proxy','storage','observed_mix'):
                flow=lambda kk:native[kk] if mode=='actual' else predictions[mode,kk]['flows']
                b,z=totals(flow(ref),cell),totals(flow(key),cell)
                volume=.5*(b['beta']+z['beta'])*(z['q']-b['q'])
                mix=.5*(b['q']+z['q'])*(z['beta']-b['beta'])
                assert abs(volume+mix-(z['off']-b['off']))<1e-8
                decompositions.append(dict(case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],off=off,cell=cell,mode=mode,
                    reference=b,changed=z,delta_off=z['off']-b['off'],total_discharge_term=volume,realized_mix_term=mix))
        for mode in ('proxy','storage','observed_mix'):
            def measure(kk):
                rr=next(x for x in records if (x['case'],x['arm'])==kk)
                return _cellwise_measure(model,predictions[mode,kk],rr,truths[kk])
            b,z=measure(ref),measure(key)
            contrasts.append(dict(case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],mode=mode,
                actual_main_delta=z['actual']['mainline_ttt']-b['actual']['mainline_ttt'],
                predicted_main_delta=z['predicted']['mainline_ttt']-b['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],
                actual_component_delta=z['actual']['ttt']-b['actual']['ttt'],
                predicted_component_delta=z['predicted']['ttt']-b['predicted']['ttt']))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,contrasts=contrasts,decompositions=decompositions,
        pins_unchanged=True,new_rollouts=10,new_native=0,fit=0,parity_exact=True,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-start,
        production_adopted=False,gain_qualified=False,future_mix_diagnostic=True))
    print(json.dumps(contrasts),flush=True)


def run_route_inventory_response(*, case='s29_late', snapshot_only=False):
    """Use native current routes and compiled future probabilities, never future exits."""
    from collections import Counter
    sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, vehicle_routes, offramp_routing
    from evaluation.controllers.projection_support import complete_records
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    cw=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    parent=cw/'freeway_first/route_inventory'
    assert case in ('s29_late','s29_early','s43_early','s61_late','s67_late')
    assert not snapshot_only or case=='s67_late'
    assert case!='s67_late' or snapshot_only
    early=case.endswith('early');seed=int(case[1:3]);heldout=seed in (61,67)
    separate=early or heldout
    out=parent/case if separate else parent
    assert (parent/'geometry_preflight.json').is_file()
    if separate:out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    assert not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    source=cw/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(source);model=context['component']
    contract=load(parent/'contract.json');runtime=load(parent/'compiled.json')
    if heldout:
        heldout_dir=HERE/f'heldout{seed}_freeway_20260930'
        if snapshot_only:
            from diagnostics.sdmpc_n31_20260924.integration_20260926.heldout53_response_v2 import postprocess
            postprocess.configure_case(heldout_dir);postprocess.closed()
        captured=load(heldout_dir/'capture.json')
        assert captured['cutoff']==2670.1 and captured['horizon']==450 and not captured['future_truth_used_as_input']
        records=[dict(r,case=case,role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,
                      truth=str(heldout_dir/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in captured['records']]
    else:
        records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==case]
    assert len(records)==4
    assert all(r['role']==('heldout' if heldout else 'check' if seed==43 else 'train') for r in records)
    reference=records[0]['reference'];assert all(r['reference']==reference for r in records)
    ref=next(r for r in records if r['arm']==reference)
    payloads={r['arm']:read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']) for r in records}
    old=OLD/'diagnostics/metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924/legal_release2670'
    if separate:
        import re
        root=Path(f'D:/VISSIM_runs/20260930_release2670_s{seed}' if heldout else
                  f'D:/VISSIM_runs/20260924_bottleneck90_full_s{seed}')
        stem=f'release2670_s{seed}_hold' if heldout else f'mtfc_s{seed}_none'
        native=root/'source'/(stem+'.inpx');profile=load(root/(reference+'.json'))
        assert sha(native)==profile['network_sha256']
        raw_network=native.read_bytes()
        normalized=re.sub(rb'(<simulation\b[^>]*\brandSeed=")'+str(seed).encode()+rb'(")',rb'\g<1>29\2',raw_network)
        assert normalized==(ROOT/contract['network']['path']).read_bytes(), 'Network differs beyond traffic seed'
        observation_manifest=load(Path(ref['truth'])/'manifest.json')
        path=(root/'hold/run/vissim_eval'/(stem+'_001.fzp') if heldout else
              Path(observation_manifest.get('source',observation_manifest.get('source_fzp'))))
        stat=path.stat()
        meta=dict(source=str(path),size=stat.st_size,mtime_ns=stat.st_mtime_ns,cutoff=ref['cutoff'],
            network_sha256=sha(native),network=str(native),same_network_except_seed=True,
            original_observation_manifest=str(Path(ref['truth'])/'manifest.json'))
    else:meta=load(old/'current_route_snapshot/manifest.json')
    t0=meta['cutoff'];assert t0==(2220.1 if early else 2670.1)
    if not separate:assert contract['network']['sha256']==meta['network_sha256']
    path=Path(meta['source']);stat=path.stat()
    assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(meta['size'],meta['mtime_ns'])
    digest=hashlib.sha256();count=0;physical=[];routes=[];observed={}
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        header=None
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith(b'$VEHICLE:'):
                header=line.decode('ascii').strip().split(':',1)[1].split(';')
                fields={k:header.index(k) for k in ('NO','LANE\\LINK\\NO','LANE\\INDEX','POS','SPEED','ROUTDECNO','ROUTENO','ROUTDECTYPE')}
                digest.update(line);continue
            if header is None or line.startswith((b'*',b'$')) or not line.strip():
                digest.update(line);continue
            sec=float(line.split(b';',1)[0])
            if sec>t0+1e-7:break
            digest.update(line);count+=1
            if abs(sec-t0)>1e-7:continue
            values=line.decode('ascii').strip().split(';');r={k:values[i].strip() for k,i in fields.items()}
            no=int(r['NO']);assert no not in observed;observed[no]=r
            physical.append(dict(veh_no=no,link_no=int(r['LANE\\LINK\\NO']),lane_index=int(r['LANE\\INDEX']),
                position_m=float(r['POS']),speed_kph=float(r['SPEED'])))
            identity=[r[k] for k in ('ROUTDECNO','ROUTENO','ROUTDECTYPE')]
            assert all(identity) or not any(identity),'Partial native route identity'
            routes.append(dict(veh_no=no,route_decision_no=int(identity[0]) if identity[0] else None,
                route_no=int(identity[1]) if identity[1] else None,route_decision_type=identity[2] or None))
    if not separate:assert digest.hexdigest()==meta['prefix_sha256'] and count==meta['scanned_data_rows']
    assert (path.stat().st_size,path.stat().st_mtime_ns)==(stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)
    if not separate:
        initial=load(OUT/'initial_network_cohort.json')['vehicles']
        assert set(map(int,initial))==set(observed)
        for no,row in initial.items():assert all(observed[int(no)][k]==v for k,v in row.items())
    raw=dict(sim_sec=t0,vehicle_records=dict(complete=True,records=physical,record_count=len(physical),
        collection_count_before=len(physical),collection_count_after=len(physical),
        capture_sim_sec_before=t0,capture_sim_sec_after=t0,
        full_network_link_counts=dict(Counter(str(r['link_no']) for r in physical))),
        vehicle_routes=dict(schema_version='vissim-vehicle-routes-v1',complete=True,records=routes,
            source_attributes=vehicle_routes.ATTRIBUTES,record_count=len(routes),collection_count_before=len(routes),
            collection_count_after=len(routes),sim_sec_before=t0,sim_sec_after=t0))
    complete_records(raw);vehicle_routes.complete_vehicle_routes(raw,required=True)
    # Bind the newly recovered route snapshot to the existing initial physics,
    # not merely to matching file names or recycled observation metadata.
    by_cell={c:[] for c in range(31)}
    for vehicle in physical:
        address=runtime['physical'].get(str(vehicle['link_no']))
        if address is None or address[0]!='FW_E':continue
        fw,position,cell=offramp_routing._position(runtime,vehicle['link_no'],vehicle['position_m'])
        by_cell[cell].append(vehicle['speed_kph'])
    v_error=0.
    for args,kwargs in payloads.values():
        for row in args[0]:
            if row['road']!='FW_E':continue
            values=by_cell[row['cell']];assert len(values)==row['n_veh']
            if values:v_error=max(v_error,abs(sum(values)/len(values)-row['v_kmh']))
    assert v_error<1e-8
    save(out/'initial_raw.json',raw)
    save(out/'snapshot_provenance.json',dict(source_meta=meta,full_network_count=len(physical),
        existing_initial_ids_and_fields_exact=True if not separate else None,
        same_initial_N_all_arms=True,initial_v_error=v_error,prefix_data_rows=count,
        future_rows_retained=0,full_prefix_sha256=digest.hexdigest(),
        whole_network_snapshot=True,raw_sha256=sha(out/'initial_raw.json')))
    print(json.dumps(dict(snapshot_count=len(physical),cutoff=t0,future_inputs=False)),flush=True)
    if snapshot_only:
        save(out/'protocol.json',dict(case=case,snapshot_only=True,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,
            scope='Current whole-network route/position extraction through2670.1 only; all four initial N/v states matched.',
            capture_sha256=sha(heldout_dir/'capture.json'),helper_sha256=sha(Path(__file__)),
            contract_sha256=sha(parent/'contract.json'),raw_sha256=sha(out/'initial_raw.json')))
        return
    truths={r['arm']:ObservationData(r['truth']) for r in records}
    pins={str(source):sha(source),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(out/'initial_raw.json'):sha(out/'initial_raw.json')}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for v in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    for p in (parent/'contract.json',parent/'mapping31.json',parent/'compiled.json',
              Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP')):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    if separate:
        for p in (native,root/(reference+'.json'),Path(ref['truth'])/'manifest.json'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        if heldout:
            for p in (heldout_dir/'capture.json',heldout_dir/'protocol.json'):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    else:pins[str(old/'current_route_snapshot/manifest.json')]=sha(old/'current_route_snapshot/manifest.json')
    for r in records:
        pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):
            p=Path(r['truth'])/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    (out/'helper_executed.py.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: current-route late-state forecasts completed.' if early else 'PROGRESS:10 numerical branch-mixture diagnostics completed.',
        hypothesis='10639 merge vehicles retain routes bypassing10682; fixed local beta incorrectly makes this new stock exit there. Existing accepted-flow route advection can preserve source/destination composition.',
        model='Frozen expanded036; no coefficient fitting.',case=case,seed_role=ref['role'],
        max_rollouts=(9 if seed==43 else 5) if separate else 10,baseline_parity=1 if separate else 2,
        routed_forecasts=4 if separate else 8,new_storage_baselines=4 if seed==43 else 0,
        variants=['storage'] if separate else ['storage_and_proxy','storage'],scope='31east cells+4on/4off connectors; not full Omega.',
        source=f'Only current{t0} route/position records and constant native static probabilities; all arms share same initial snapshot.',
        future_observations=False,controls_demand_unchanged=True,new_native=0,production_adopted=False,
        acceptance='Check conservation, destination identity, branch/terminal response and large RM loss direction; no general qualification from one training state. Recheck early and other states before adoption.',pins=pins))
    rows=[];parity={};predictions={};baselines=[];begin=time.perf_counter()
    if heldout or (early and seed==43):
        a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[reference]);baseline=json.loads(json.dumps(model.rollout(*a,**k),allow_nan=False))
        expected_path=(heldout_dir/'candidate'/(reference+'_prediction.json.gz') if heldout else
                       cw/'expanded_joint/checks'/(case+'_'+reference+'.json.gz'))
        with gzip.open(expected_path,'rt') as f:expected=json.load(f)
        parity['proxy_check']={field:baseline[field]==expected[field] for field in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
        assert all(parity['proxy_check'].values())
        pins[str(expected_path)]=sha(expected_path)
    for mode in (('storage',) if separate else ('storage_and_proxy','storage')):
        if not heldout:
            a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[reference]);k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']=mode
            baseline=json.loads(json.dumps(model.rollout(*a,**k),allow_nan=False))
        if not separate or seed==29:
            saved=(cw/'freeway_first/off_entry_proxy/coverage/storage'/(case+'_'+reference+'.json.gz') if early else
                   cw/'freeway_first/off_entry_proxy'/(mode+'_hold.json.gz'))
            with gzip.open(saved,'rt') as f:expected=json.load(f)
            parity[mode]={field:baseline[field]==expected[field] for field in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
            assert all(parity[mode].values())
        for r in records:
            if heldout:
                saved=heldout_dir/'candidate'/(r['arm']+'_prediction.json.gz');pins[str(saved)]=sha(saved)
                with gzip.open(saved,'rt') as f:bp=json.load(f)
                br=_cellwise_measure(model,bp,r,truths[r['arm']]);br['mode']='proxy';baselines.append(br)
            if early:
                if seed==43:
                    if r['arm']==reference:bp=baseline
                    else:
                        a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[r['arm']]);k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
                        bp=model.rollout(*a,**k)
                    with gzip.open(out/('baseline_storage_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(bp,f,allow_nan=False)
                else:
                    with gzip.open(cw/'freeway_first/off_entry_proxy/coverage/storage'/(case+'_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz'),'rt') as f:bp=json.load(f)
                br=_cellwise_measure(model,bp,r,truths[r['arm']]);br['mode']='storage';baselines.append(br)
                with gzip.open(cw/'expanded_joint'/('checks' if seed==43 else 'selected_predictions')/(case+'_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz'),'rt') as f:bp=json.load(f)
                br=_cellwise_measure(model,bp,r,truths[r['arm']]);br['mode']='proxy';baselines.append(br)
            a,k=copy.deepcopy(payloads[r['arm']]);k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']=mode
            k['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=contract,raw=raw)
            pred=model.rollout(*a,**k);predictions[mode,r['arm']]=pred
            with gzip.open(out/(mode+'_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truths[r['arm']]);row['mode']=mode
            row['conservation_max']=max(abs(x['conservation_residual_veh']) for x in pred['ports']+pred['ramps'])
            assert not row['score']['invalid'] and row['conservation_max']<1e-7
            road=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]
            assert road['continuity_residual_max_veh']<1e-7
            row['route_initial']=road['route_inventory_initial'];row['route_final']=road['route_inventory_final']
            row['branch_flows']={off:sum(x['off_departures'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==cell['from_cell'])
                for off,cell in model.offramps.items() if cell['road']=='FW_E'}
            row['terminal']=sum(x['terminal_exits'] for x in pred['flows'])
            rows.append(row)
            save(out/(mode+'_'+r['arm']+'_result.json'),row)
            print(json.dumps(dict(mode=mode,arm=r['arm'],main=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],
                routes=row['route_initial'],off=row['branch_flows'])),flush=True)
    for row in rows:
        base=next(x for x in rows if x['mode']==row['mode'] and x['arm']==reference)
        row['delta']={label:{k:row[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')}
                      for label in ('actual','predicted')}
        row['branch_delta']={k:v-base['branch_flows'][k] for k,v in row['branch_flows'].items()}
        row['terminal_delta']=row['terminal']-base['terminal']
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for row in baselines:
        base=next(x for x in baselines if x['mode']==row['mode'] and x['arm']==reference)
        row['delta']={label:{key:row[label][key]-base[label][key] for key in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')}
                      for label in ('actual','predicted')}
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,baselines=baselines,parity=parity,new_rollouts=((9 if seed==43 else 5) if separate else 10),wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-begin,
        pins_unchanged=True,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False,new_native=0,fit=0,future_inputs=False))
    print(json.dumps([dict(mode=r['mode'],arm=r['arm'],delta=r['delta'],branch=r['branch_delta'],terminal=r['terminal_delta']) for r in rows]),flush=True)


def audit_vsl_native_exposure():
    """Native desired-speed/DSD timing diagnosis; no future model input or fit."""
    from bisect import bisect_right
    from collections import Counter
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    out=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first/vsl_native_exposure'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    ri=out.parent/'route_inventory';runtime=load(ri/'compiled.json');contract=load(ri/'contract.json')
    bounds=runtime['bounds']['FW_E']
    network=ROOT/contract['network']['path'];assert sha(network)==contract['network']['sha256']
    tree=ET.parse(network);signs=[]
    for item in tree.findall('./desSpeedDecisions/desSpeedDecision'):
        link,lane=map(int,item.get('lane').split());address=runtime['physical'].get(str(link))
        if address is None or address[0]!='FW_E':continue
        x=float(address[1])+float(item.get('pos'))
        signs.append(dict(no=int(item.get('no')),lane=lane,x_m=x,cell=bisect_right(bounds,x)-1))
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if
             (r['case'],r['arm']) in (('s29_early','none'),('s29_early','vsl'),('s29_late','hold'),('s29_late','hold_vsl90'))]
    assert len(records)==4
    moment_path=OLD/'diagnostics/metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924/mainline_moments/manifest.json'
    moments=load(moment_path)
    pins={str(network):sha(network),str(ri/'compiled.json'):sha(ri/'compiled.json'),
          str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(moment_path):sha(moment_path),
          'D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP':sha('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP')}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:5 frozen seed61 forecasts completed; big RM response improved, VSL flow direction remains wrong.',
        cases=[dict(case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],cutoff=r['cutoff']) for r in records],
        scope='Only existing seed29 training native trajectories; 450s,5s samples,31east cells; detailed vehicle rows only cells14..26.',
        question='Does the native VSL interaction/desired-speed change occur upstream of the modeled sign/cohort change, and when does the downstream110 reset occur?',
        paired_vehicles='Only IDs already present in the identical whole-network cutoff frame; new IDs after the split are not paired.',
        limits=['Native future samples are diagnosis and scoring only, never autonomous prediction inputs.',
                'Five-second change brackets are not exact event times; target IDs/types are native reported interaction fields, not assumed causes.',
                'Do not fit on seed61 or infer a capacity increase from mean speed alone.'],
        maximum_fzp_passes=4,new_rollouts=0,new_native=0,fit=0,signs=signs,pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.py.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    results=[]
    for record in records:
        case,arm=record['case'],record['arm'];t0=record['cutoff'];end=round(t0+450,6)
        expected=None
        if case=='s29_early':
            expected=next(r for r in moments['rows'] if r['seed']==29 and r['arm']==arm)
            source=Path(expected['source'])
        else:
            source=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260924_release2670_s29')/arm/'run/vissim_eval'/f'release2670_s29_{arm}_001.fzp'
        stat=source.stat()
        if expected:assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(expected['source_bytes'],expected['source_mtime_ns'])
        frames={};initial_ids=[];initial_digest=hashlib.sha256();digest=hashlib.sha256();columns=None;window_rows=0
        totals={};native_targets=Counter();changes=[];previous={};endpoint_overshoot_samples=0
        with source.open('rb') as f:
            for line in f:
                if columns is None:
                    if line.startswith(b'$VEHICLE:'):
                        columns=line.rstrip().split(b':',1)[1].split(b';');ci={x:i for i,x in enumerate(columns)}
                        assert set((b'SIMSEC',b'NO',b'LANE\\LINK\\NO',b'LANE\\INDEX',b'POS',b'SPEED',b'DESSPEED',b'ACCELERATION',b'LNCHG',b'INTERACTSTATE',b'INTERACTTARGTYPE',b'INTERACTTARGNO'))<=set(ci)
                    continue
                if not line.strip():continue
                t=round(float(line.split(b';',1)[0]),6)
                if t>end+1e-7:break
                digest.update(line)
                if t<t0-1e-7:continue
                window_rows+=1;fields=line.rstrip().split(b';');vehicle=int(fields[ci[b'NO']])
                if abs(t-t0)<1e-7:initial_ids.append(vehicle);initial_digest.update(line)
                link=int(fields[ci[b'LANE\\LINK\\NO']]);address=runtime['physical'].get(str(link))
                if address is None or address[0]!='FW_E':continue
                x=float(address[1])+float(fields[ci[b'POS']]);assert x>=0
                # Match the existing Observer's last-cell membership for the
                # N/v audit. Detailed DSD analysis excludes this terminal cell.
                endpoint_overshoot_samples+=int(x>bounds[-1])
                cell=min(len(bounds)-2,bisect_right(bounds,x)-1);speed=float(fields[ci[b'SPEED']]);desired=float(fields[ci[b'DESSPEED']])
                group=totals.setdefault((t,cell),[0,0.,0.]);group[0]+=1;group[1]+=speed;group[2]+=desired
                if not 14<=cell<=26:continue
                target_type=fields[ci[b'INTERACTTARGTYPE']].decode('ascii');target=fields[ci[b'INTERACTTARGNO']].decode('ascii')
                native_targets[target_type]+=1
                row=[vehicle,cell,x,int(fields[ci[b'LANE\\INDEX']]),speed,desired,float(fields[ci[b'ACCELERATION']]),
                     fields[ci[b'LNCHG']].decode('ascii'),fields[ci[b'INTERACTSTATE']].decode('ascii'),target_type,target]
                frames.setdefault(t,[]).append(row)
                before=previous.get(vehicle)
                if before and abs(t-before[0]-5)<1e-7 and x>=before[1][2] and abs(desired-before[1][5])>.02:
                    crossed=[s['no'] for s in signs if before[1][2]<s['x_m']<=x and s['lane']==row[3]]
                    changes.append(dict(vehicle=vehicle,time_s=t,x_before=before[1][2],x_after=x,
                        desired_before=before[1][5],desired_after=desired,crossed_signs_by_end_lane=crossed))
                previous[vehicle]=(t,row)
        assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(source.stat().st_size,source.stat().st_mtime_ns)
        if expected:
            assert digest.hexdigest()==expected['data_prefix_sha256'] and window_rows==expected['window_raw_rows']
        actual_cells=Path(record['truth'])/'cells_30s.csv';pins[str(actual_cells)]=sha(actual_cells);checks=0
        with actual_cells.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                t=round(float(row['time_s']),6);cell=int(row['cell'])
                if row['road']!='FW_E' or not t0<=t<=end:continue
                n,sv,sd=totals.get((t,cell),(0,0.,0.));assert n==float(row['n_veh'])
                if n:assert abs(sv/n-float(row['v_kmh']))<1e-7
                checks+=1
        assert checks==16*31 and len(frames)==91 and len(initial_ids)==len(set(initial_ids))
        frame_path=out/(case+'_'+arm+'_frames.json.gz')
        with gzip.open(frame_path,'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(dict(fields=['vehicle','cell','x_m','lane','speed_kmh','desired_kmh','acceleration_mps2','lane_change','interaction_state','target_type','target_no'],
            initial_whole_network_ids=initial_ids,frames=frames),f,allow_nan=False)
        result=dict(case=case,arm=arm,source=str(source),source_size=stat.st_size,source_mtime_ns=stat.st_mtime_ns,
            prefix_sha256=digest.hexdigest(),window_raw_rows=window_rows,initial_frame_sha256=initial_digest.hexdigest(),
            frames=91,matched_cell_n_v=checks,endpoint_overshoot_samples=endpoint_overshoot_samples,
            frame_path=str(frame_path),frame_sha256=sha(frame_path),target_types=dict(native_targets),
            desired_changes=changes,cell_samples=[dict(time_s=t,cell=c,n=n,speed_sum=sv,desired_sum=sd) for (t,c),(n,sv,sd) in totals.items()])
        save(out/(case+'_'+arm+'_source.json'),result);results.append(result)
        print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k in ('case','arm','window_raw_rows','matched_cell_n_v','target_types')}),flush=True)
    for case in ('s29_early','s29_late'):
        group=[r for r in results if r['case']==case]
        assert group[0]['initial_frame_sha256']==group[1]['initial_frame_sha256']
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'extraction.json',dict(results=[{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('desired_changes','cell_samples')} for r in results],
        common_cutoff_frames_exact=True,pins=pins,new_native=0,new_rollouts=0,fit=0))


def check_vsl_distribution_ratio():
    """One native-CDF mean-ratio alternative, with all fitted physics frozen."""
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, freeway_fd
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    ri=parent/'route_inventory';out=parent/'vsl_native_exposure/mean_ratio';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    source=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(source);model=context['component']
    network=ROOT/load(ri/'contract.json')['network']['path'];tree=ET.parse(network)
    distributions={}
    for node in tree.findall('./desSpeedDistributions/desSpeedDistribution'):
        no=int(node.get('no'))
        if no not in range(50,111,10):continue
        points=[(float(p.get('fx')),float(p.get('x'))) for p in node.findall('./speedDistrDatPts/speedDistributionDataPoint')]
        assert points[0][0]==0 and points[-1][0]==1
        mean=sum((q-p)*(a+b)/2 for (p,a),(q,b) in zip(points,points[1:]))
        second=sum((q-p)*(a*a+a*b+b*b)/3 for (p,a),(q,b) in zip(points,points[1:]))
        distributions[no]=dict(mean_kmh=mean,std_kmh=math.sqrt(second-mean*mean),points=points)
    assert 90 in distributions and 110 in distributions
    ratios={c:r['mean_kmh']/distributions[110]['mean_kmh'] for c,r in distributions.items()}
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case'] in ('s29_early','s29_late')]
    assert len(records)==8
    pins={str(source):sha(source),str(network):sha(network),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),
          str(Path(freeway_fd.__file__)):sha(freeway_fd.__file__)}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for case in ('s29_early','s29_late'):
        folder=ri/case if case=='s29_early' else ri
        for name in ('initial_raw.json','summary.json'):
            p=folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:5 frozen seed61 forecasts; this turn four native exposure extracts complete.',
        candidate='Only Carlson b=mean(DSD command)/mean(DSD110), derived from the pinned native CDF. Same A/E/FD/merge/recovery parameters.',
        nominal_command_unchanged=True,distribution_moments=distributions,ratios=ratios,
        max_rollouts=6,parity_rollouts=2,modified_rollouts=4,fit=0,new_native=0,
        scope='Training seed29 early and late, same route-storage candidate; no43/61 refit/check yet.',
        acceptance='Preserve no-VSL arrays exactly, mass, big RM signs and choices; report all signed cost/discharge effects. Reject as an explanation if it only weakens the penalty without restoring physical benefit. No parameter search.',
        limitations='Mean-ratio is a testable actuator approximation, not a claim that desired distribution means equal free traffic speeds. Native variance/composition remains unmodeled.',pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.py.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    original=freeway_fd.literature_vsl_parameters
    def mapped(spec,v_free,critical,shape,command,maximum):
        assert spec['law']=='carlson' and maximum==110 and command in ratios
        effective=command if command==maximum else maximum*ratios[command]
        return original(spec,v_free,critical,shape,effective,maximum)
    rows=[];parity=[];baselines=[]
    try:
        freeway_fd.literature_vsl_parameters=mapped
        for record in records:
            case,arm=record['case'],record['arm'];folder=ri/case if case=='s29_early' else ri
            cached_path=folder/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(cached_path)]=sha(cached_path)
            with gzip.open(cached_path,'rt') as f:cached=json.load(f)
            baseline=next(r for r in load(folder/'summary.json')['rows'] if r['arm']==arm and r['mode']=='storage')
            baselines.append(baseline)
            # Reuse the other two unchanged no-VSL forecasts; one exact parity
            # forecast per state demonstrates the maximum-command path.
            if arm in ('none','release'):
                rows.append(dict(baseline,reused_unchanged=True));continue
            args,kwargs=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
            kwargs['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            kwargs['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(folder/'initial_raw.json'))
            pred=model.rollout(*args,**kwargs)
            serialized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred,allow_nan=False))
            if arm in ('rm','hold'):
                check={k:serialized[k]==cached[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
                parity.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,checks=check));assert all(check.values())
            with gzip.open(out/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth)
            row['terminal']=sum(f['terminal_exits'] for f in pred['flows']);row['off']=sum(f['off_departures'] for f in pred['flows'])
            rows.append(row)
            print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,main=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],component=row['predicted']['ttt'])),flush=True)
    finally:freeway_fd.literature_vsl_parameters=original
    for row in rows:
        reference='none' if row['case']=='s29_early' else 'hold'
        base=next(r for r in rows if r['case']==row['case'] and r['arm']==reference)
        row['delta']={label:{k:row[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')} for label in ('actual','predicted')}
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,baselines=baselines,parity=parity,pins=pins,
        new_rollouts=6,reused_forecasts=2,fit=0,new_native=0,adopted=False))
    print(json.dumps([dict(case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],delta=r['delta']) for r in rows]),flush=True)


def check_vsl_long_response(case='s29_early', conditional_source=False, connected_fd=False):
    """Two frozen750s runs; future source forcing is explicitly diagnostic."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    assert case in ('s29_early','s43_early')
    assert not conditional_source or case=='s43_early'
    assert not connected_fd or (conditional_source and case=='s43_early')
    ri=parent/'route_inventory';input_folder=ri/case
    out=parent/'vsl_native_exposure'/('source43' if conditional_source else ('horizon750' if case=='s29_early' else 'h750_s43'))
    if connected_fd:out=parent/'connected_fd/source43'
    if case=='s43_early':out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    if connected_fd:manifest=parent/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==case and r['arm'] in ('none','vsl')]
    assert len(records)==2 and float(model.base.simulation.T_f_sec)==1
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),
        str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json'),str(input_folder/'initial_raw.json'):sha(input_folder/'initial_raw.json')}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    scorer=ROOT/'diagnostics/demand_sweep/user_native_20260914/metanet_calibration_v1/scoring.py';pins[str(scorer)]=sha(scorer)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:two native3000 mean/shape runs and450/750postprocessing completed.',
        connected_fd_frozen_diagnostic=connected_fd,model_manifest=str(manifest),
        max_new_rollouts=2,seed=int(case[1:3]),cutoff=2220.1,horizon=750,controller_horizon_unchanged=450,
        future_source_observations_as_diagnostic_only=conditional_source,
        validation_scope='Seed43 coefficients unchanged and previously inspected, not a pristine blind holdout.' if case=='s43_early' else 'Existing training seed29; no fitting in this test.',
        boundary=('DIAGNOSTIC ONLY: request the recorded future30s source admissions as a uniform1s source demand; receiving remains active. Other inputs persist from cutoff. NOT autonomous validation.' if conditional_source else 'Persist the original current-only constant rate forecast for300more seconds; hold each already planned command. No future observations or future initial resets.'),
        changes='Offline horizon validation/scoring accepts up to750s, default450 unchanged. Same physical equations and parameters.',
        acceptance=('Future-source diagnostic: first450 arrays are expected to change; preserve default-score parity on cached450 predictions, conservation, and report actual accepted source flows. Never count this as autonomous qualification.' if conditional_source else 'Exact first450 physical-array and default-score replay, conservative continuous tail, inspect both total response and flow timing. Diagnostic only, no fitting/adoption.'),pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[];parity=[]
    for record in records:
        arm=record['arm'];args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and kw['horizon_sec']==450 and kw['roads']==['FW_E']
        args=list(args);original=copy.deepcopy(args[1]);assert len(original)==450
        strip=lambda r:{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('window_start_s','window_end_s','vsl_commands')}
        assert all(strip(s)==strip(original[0]) for s in original)
        assert all(s['vsl_commands']==original[-1]['vsl_commands'] for s in original[30:])
        for offset in range(450,750):
            step=copy.deepcopy(original[-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(record['cutoff']+offset,6),window_end_s=round(record['cutoff']+offset+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        assert args[1][:450]==original and len(args[1])==750
        if conditional_source:
            # The explicit diagnostic answers whether boundary uncertainty can
            # explain the wrong sign; never promote it as autonomous accuracy.
            truth=ObservationData(record['truth'])
            source_path=Path(record['truth'])/'flows_30s.csv';pins[str(source_path)]=sha(source_path)
            for index,step in enumerate(args[1]):
                window_end=round(record['cutoff']+30*(index//30+1),6)
                step['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']=float(truth.flows[window_end,'FW_E',0]['source_admissions'])*120.
        kw['horizon_sec']=750;kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(input_folder/'initial_raw.json'))
        pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        with gzip.open(out/(arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        # Autonomous cases first open future truth after rollout. The explicitly
        # conditional case already read only its declared source forcing above.
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth,horizon_sec=750)
        path=input_folder/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:cached=json.load(f)
        fields={'cells':'time_s','flows':'window_end_s','ports':'time_s','ramps':'end_sec'}
        prefix={k:[r for r in pred[k] if r[t]<=2670.1+1e-7] for k,t in fields.items()}
        equality={k:json.loads(json.dumps(prefix[k]))==cached[k] for k in fields}
        if not conditional_source:assert all(equality.values()),equality
        # Default450 scoring still scores the identical saved physical arrays.
        from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.scoring import score_rollout
        score=score_rollout(truth,record['cutoff'],cached,'FW_E',include_source_boundary=True)
        oldrow=next(r for r in load(input_folder/'summary.json')['rows'] if r['arm']==arm and r['mode']=='storage')
        assert score==oldrow['score'], 'Default450 score changed'
        parity.append(dict(arm=arm,first450_arrays=equality,default450_score_exact=True,
            arrays_required_exact=not conditional_source))
        row['flows']={k:sum(float(r[k]) for r in pred['flows']) for k in ('source_admissions','ramp_merges','off_departures','terminal_exits')}
        row['cell_downstream']={str(c):sum(float(r['downstream_crossings']) for r in pred['flows'] if int(r['cell'])==c) for c in range(31)}
        row['source_wait_veh_h']=sum(float(r['origin_queue_veh_h']) for r in pred['diagnostics']['roads']) if all('origin_queue_veh_h' in r for r in pred['diagnostics']['roads']) else None
        rows.append(row);save(out/(arm+'_result.json'),row);print(json.dumps(dict(arm=arm,predicted=row['predicted'],actual=row['actual'])),flush=True)
    base=next(r for r in rows if r['arm']=='none')
    for row in rows:row['delta']={label:{k:row[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')} for label in ('actual','predicted')}
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,parity=parity,pins=pins,new_rollouts=2,fit=0,new_native=0,adopted=False,
        future_source_observations_as_diagnostic_only=conditional_source))
    print(json.dumps([dict(arm=r['arm'],delta=r['delta']) for r in rows]),flush=True)


def audit_source_discharge_response():
    """Four exact saved750 replays; observe terms without changing the physics."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, source_boundary
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from diagnostics.sdmpc_n31_20260924.integration_20260926.probe_selected_arrival_path import observe_first_interval_speed_terms
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'source_discharge';ri=parent/'route_inventory';ve=parent/'vsl_native_exposure'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
             if r['case'] in ('s29_early','s43_early') and r['arm'] in ('none','vsl')]
    assert len(records)==4 and model.base.simulation.T_f_sec==1
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),
          str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json')}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    probe=Path(sys.modules[observe_first_interval_speed_terms.__module__].__file__)
    pins[str(probe)]=sha(probe);pins[str(Path(source_boundary.__file__))]=sha(Path(source_boundary.__file__))
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(max_new_rollouts=4,physics_changes=0,coefficient_fits=0,new_native=0,
        cases=[(r['case'],r['arm']) for r in records],horizon=750,controller_horizon=450,
        purpose='Source-contract and local flux/time audit, not new model accuracy or causal attribution.',
        acceptance='All four output arrays must match saved autonomous forecasts exactly; future labels only after rollout.',
        limits=['Expected schedule minus cumulative admissions is not a measured stochastic input queue.',
                'Weighted flow decomposition is an identity, not a causal control benefit.',
                'Observed equation terms alone do not identify the cause of native response.'],pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (out/'probe_executed.txt').write_bytes(probe.read_bytes())
    checks=[];sources=[];blocks=[];term_groups=[];native={};forecasts={}
    for record in records:
        case,arm=record['case'],record['arm'];tag=case+'_'+arm;t0=record['cutoff']
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and kw['horizon_sec']==450
        args=list(args);original=copy.deepcopy(args[1])
        strip=lambda r:{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('window_start_s','window_end_s','vsl_commands')}
        assert len(original)==450 and all(strip(x)==strip(original[0]) for x in original)
        assert all(x['vsl_commands']==original[-1]['vsl_commands'] for x in original[30:])
        for offset in range(450,750):
            step=copy.deepcopy(original[-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(t0+offset,6),window_end_s=round(t0+offset+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=750,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        terms=[];started=time.perf_counter()
        with observe_first_interval_speed_terms(t0,terms,horizon_sec=750):
            prediction=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        path=ve/('horizon750' if case=='s29_early' else 'h750_s43')/(arm+'.json.gz')
        pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:cached=json.load(f)
        prediction=json.loads(json.dumps(prediction,allow_nan=False))
        equal={k:prediction[k]==cached[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
        assert all(equal.values()),equal
        with gzip.open(out/(tag+'_terms.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(terms,f,allow_nan=False)
        checks.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,arrays_exact=equal,term_count=len(terms),wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,
            numeric={k:v for k,v in prediction['diagnostics']['roads'][0].items() if isinstance(v,(int,float))}))
        # No observed future quantity reaches model.rollout above.
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);native[case,arm]=truth;forecasts[case,arm]=prediction
