        for name in ('geometry.json','cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','boundaries_30s.csv'):
            p=Path(record['truth'])/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        source=next(k for k,b in truth.definitions.items() if b['road']=='FW_E' and b['kind']=='source')
        recent={str(w):sum(float(truth.flows[round(t0-j,6),'FW_E',0]['source_admissions']) for j in range(0,w,30))*3600/w
                for w in (150,300,450,600)}
        admitted=float(truth.boundaries[t0,source]['cumulative_crossings'])
        gap=max(0.,truth.desired_before('FW_E',t0)-admitted)
        schedule=source_boundary.geometry_schedules(truth.geometry)['FW_E']
        same=source_boundary.forecast(recent['150'],gap,schedule,t0,150,5,step_s=1)
        request=[sum(float(x['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']) for x in args[1][j:j+150])/150 for j in range(0,750,150)]
        assert all(abs(a-b)<1e-8 for a,b in zip(same,request))
        cell0=[x for t,rs in truth.cells.items() if t0<=t<=t0+750 for x in rs if x['road']=='FW_E' and x['cell']==0]
        sources.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,desired_vph=truth.demand_vph('FW_E',t0),historical_rates=recent,
            expected_schedule_minus_admitted_at_cutoff=gap,initial_internal_origin_queue=args[3],
            forecast_blocks_vph=request,controller_source_forecast_exact=True,
            native_cell0_speed_range=[min(x['v_kmh'] for x in cell0),max(x['v_kmh'] for x in cell0)],
            actual_750_admissions=sum(float(truth.flows[round(t0+j,6),'FW_E',0]['source_admissions']) for j in range(30,751,30)),
            predicted_750_admissions=sum(x['source_admissions'] for x in prediction['flows'])))
        for start in range(0,750,150):
            a,b=round(t0+start,6),round(t0+start+150,6)
            for c in (0,*range(14,27)):
                actual_states=[x for t,rs in truth.cells.items() if a<=t<=b for x in rs if x['road']=='FW_E' and x['cell']==c]
                actual_flows=[truth.flows[round(a+j,6),'FW_E',c] for j in range(30,151,30)]
                ps=[x for x in prediction['cells'] if a<=x['time_s']<=b and x['cell']==c]
                pf=[x for x in prediction['flows'] if a<x['window_end_s']<=b and x['cell']==c]
                initial=next(x for x in args[0] if x['road']=='FW_E' and x['cell']==c)
                if start==0:ps=[initial,*ps]
                def measures(states,flows,is_native):
                    states=sorted(states,key=lambda x:x['time_s']);assert len(states)==6 and len(flows)==5
                    return dict(n_start=states[0]['n_veh'],n_end=states[-1]['n_veh'],
                        speed_start=states[0]['v_kmh'],speed_end=states[-1]['v_kmh'],
                        speed_mean=sum(x['v_kmh'] for x in states)/6,
                        ttt=sum((x['n_veh']+y['n_veh'])*30/7200 for x,y in zip(states,states[1:])),
                        downstream=sum(float(x['downstream_crossings']) for x in flows),
                        source=sum(float(x['source_admissions']) for x in flows),
                        merge=sum(float(x['ramp_merges']) for x in flows),
                        off=sum(float(x['off_departures']) for x in flows))
                blocks.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,cell=c,start_s=a,end_s=b,actual=measures(actual_states,actual_flows,True),
                    predicted=measures(ps,pf,False)))
                selected=[x for x in terms if x['cell']==c and a-1e-7<=x['time_sec']<b-1e-7]
                if selected:
                    keys=('speed_before','rho','downstream_rho','desired','relaxation','convection','anticipation',
                          'lane_drop_raw','after_speed_equation','post_equation_change','speed_final')
                    term_groups.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,cell=c,start_s=a,end_s=b,samples=len(selected),
                        means={k:sum(x[k] for x in selected)/len(selected) for k in keys}))
        save(out/'partial.json',dict(checks=checks,sources=sources))
        print(json.dumps(checks[-1]),flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for r in blocks:
        if r['arm']!='vsl':continue
        base=next(x for x in blocks if x['case']==r['case'] and x['arm']=='none' and x['cell']==r['cell'] and x['start_s']==r['start_s'])
        paired.append({k:r[k] for k in ('case','cell','start_s','end_s')}|
            {label:{k:r[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in r[label]} for label in ('actual','predicted')})
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(checks=checks,sources=sources,blocks=blocks,paired=paired,term_groups=term_groups,pins=pins,
        new_rollouts=4,physical_arrays_changed=False,parameters_changed=False,adopted=False,new_native=0))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,exact_replays=4,source_contract_matches=4)),flush=True)


def diagnose_vsl_source_cross():
    """Complete a frozen model's 2x2 source/control diagnostic, not validation."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'source_cross';cf=root/'connected_fd';ri=root/'route_inventory'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    manifest=cf/'review/candidate_manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records={r['arm']:r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']=='s43_early' and r['arm'] in ('none','vsl')}
    assert len(records)==2
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(DECISIONS/'capture.json'):sha(DECISIONS/'capture.json')}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('contract.json','s43_early/initial_raw.json'):pins[str(ri/name)]=sha(ri/name)
    truth={arm:ObservationData(r['truth']) for arm,r in records.items()}
    for data in truth.values():
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(data.folder/name)]=sha(data.folder/name)
    saved={};prepared={}
    for arm,r in records.items():
        a,k=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];a=list(a)
        assert a[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(a[1])==450 and k['roads']==['FW_E']
        for j in range(450,750):
            s=copy.deepcopy(a[1][-1]);s.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));a[1].append(s)
        k.update(horizon_sec=750,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(ri/'s43_early/initial_raw.json')))
        k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage';prepared[arm]=(a,k)
        path=cf/'source43'/(arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:saved[arm,arm]=json.load(f)
    # Same saved initial state and all non-command inputs. Only the requested
    # source profile below is crossed; model states are never reset afterward.
    a,k=prepared['none'];b,l=prepared['vsl'];assert a[0]==b[0] and a[2:]==b[2:] and k==l
    strip=lambda s:{key:value for key,value in s.items() if key!='vsl_commands'}
    assert [strip(s) for s in a[1]]==[strip(s) for s in b[1]]
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:completed failed connected-exit experiment report and freeway-first checkpoint.',
        max_new_forecasts=2,reused_forecasts=2,fit=0,new_native=0,future_source=True,
        purpose='Cross NC/VSL with each recorded source profile in the frozen connected-FD model; isolate model source sensitivity from control sensitivity.',
        limitations=['All four source-conditioned runs are DIAGNOSTIC ONLY; none qualifies autonomous prediction.',
            'Crossed source/control combinations have no matching native counterfactual, so effects are model sensitivities, not identified native causal effects.',
            'Connected-FD candidate still failed its previous response gate; this experiment cannot reverse that verdict.',
            'Native source differences are not assumed entirely stochastic or entirely caused by control.'],
        changes='Only source_demand_vph from recorded30s counts; physical equations, all coefficients, ramps, off storage, VSL histories, and other forecast boundaries frozen.',
        checks='Same initial states/non-command inputs, exact mass checks, source admissions match supplied profile, old files untouched.',pins=pins)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[]
    for arm,source in (('none','none'),('vsl','vsl'),('none','vsl'),('vsl','none')):
        r=records[arm];t0=r['cutoff'];tag=arm+'_source_'+source
        requested={round(t0+j,6):float(truth[source].flows[round(t0+j,6),'FW_E',0]['source_admissions']) for j in range(30,751,30)}
        if (arm,source) in saved:pred=saved[arm,source]
        else:
            a,k=copy.deepcopy(prepared[arm])
            for j,s in enumerate(a[1]):s['source_demand_vph']['FW_E']=requested[round(t0+30*(j//30+1),6)]*120
            pred=model.rollout(*a,**k)
            with gzip.open(out/(tag+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            saved[arm,source]=pred
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth[arm],horizon_sec=750);assert row['conservation_max']<1e-9
        admissions={x['window_end_s']:x['source_admissions'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==0}
        source_error=max(abs(admissions[t]-n) for t,n in requested.items());assert source_error<1e-8
        row.update(source_profile=source,native_comparison_is_matched=arm==source,source_max_error=source_error,
            cell19_downstream=sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==19))
        rows.append(row);save(out/(tag+'_result.json'),row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(arm=arm,source=source,mainline=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],cell19=row['cell19_downstream'])),flush=True)
    index={(r['arm'],r['source_profile']):r for r in rows};effects={}
    for key in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','exits','cell19_downstream'):
        value=lambda c,s:index[c,s][key] if key=='cell19_downstream' else index[c,s]['predicted'][key]
        control_nc=value('vsl','none')-value('none','none');control_vsl=value('vsl','vsl')-value('none','vsl')
        source_nc=value('none','vsl')-value('none','none');source_vsl=value('vsl','vsl')-value('vsl','none')
        total=value('vsl','vsl')-value('none','none')
        assert abs((control_nc+control_vsl+source_nc+source_vsl)/2-total)<1e-8
        effects[key]=dict(control_at_nc_source=control_nc,control_at_vsl_source=control_vsl,
            source_at_nc_control=source_nc,source_at_vsl_control=source_vsl,interaction=control_vsl-control_nc,
            symmetric_control=(control_nc+control_vsl)/2,symmetric_source=(source_nc+source_vsl)/2,diagonal_total=total)
    # Decompose response error into each arm's absolute error, using existing
    # autonomous forecasts only. Never interpret this identity as causation.
    blocks=[]
    all_records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    for seed in (29,43):
        case=f's{seed}_early';native={};predicted={}
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            rec=next(r for r in all_records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));native[arm]=ObservationData(rec['truth'])
            path=(cf/'eval_20' if seed==29 else cf/'review')/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:predicted[arm]=json.load(f)
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):pins[str(native[arm].folder/name)]=sha(native[arm].folder/name)
        for offset in range(0,750,150):
            times=[round(2220.1+offset+j,6) for j in range(30,151,30)]
            for cell in range(31):
                vals={}
                for arm in ('none','vsl'):
                    actual=sum(float(native[arm].flows[t,'FW_E',cell]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times)
                    forecast=sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in predicted[arm]['flows'] if x['cell']==cell and x['window_end_s'] in times)
                    vals[arm]=dict(actual=actual,predicted=forecast,error=forecast-actual)
                actual_delta=vals['vsl']['actual']-vals['none']['actual'];pred_delta=vals['vsl']['predicted']-vals['none']['predicted']
                error=pred_delta-actual_delta;assert abs(error-(vals['vsl']['error']-vals['none']['error']))<1e-8
                blocks.append(dict(case=case,cell=cell,start_s=times[0]-30,end_s=times[-1],arms=vals,
                    actual_delta=actual_delta,predicted_delta=pred_delta,response_error=error))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'flow_error_decomposition.json',blocks)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,effects=effects,flow_decompositions=len(blocks),new_rollouts=2,reused_rollouts=2,
        fit=0,new_native=0,future_source_diagnostic_only=True,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False,pins=pins))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,effects=effects)),flush=True)


def diagnose_recovery_interface(mode='density'):
    """Future density at the20->21 speed interface only; no state resets."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    assert mode in ('density','past_arrival')
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/('recovery_interface' if mode=='density' else 'recovery_arrival_history')
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);cf=root/'connected_fd';ri=root/'route_inventory'
    manifest=cf/'review/candidate_manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
    protocol=dict(max_new_forecasts=4,fit=0,new_native=0,cases=['s29_early','s43_early'],arms=['none','vsl'],horizon=750,
        hypothesis='Slow/high-density merge21 state may delay recovery upstream through cell20 anticipation. Test that one input path before changing VSL coefficients.',
        intervention='Use linearly interpolated native30s cell21 density ONLY as downstream_rho argument of the cell20 speed update; receiving, all stocks, all other speed arguments, sources and exchanges remain predicted.',
        interpretation='Future-conditioned structural sensitivity ONLY. Not autonomous accuracy or a native causal effect. Density used for speed pressure can differ from density used for receiving; deliberate path isolation, not a deployable model.',
        acceptance='No qualification or adoption from this test. Report absolute NC/VSL discharge errors, their difference, and conservation.',
        stop='Four finite diagnoses only; no coefficient search.',pins=pins)
    if mode=='past_arrival':
        protocol.update(hypothesis='Uniform10490 ramp arrivals may erase gaps that allow merge21 recovery; preserve the known150s mean but repeat the last150s observed5s arrival shape.',
            intervention='Only10490 ramp_arrival_vph temporal shape changes. Same150s total, initial state, head service, travel/receiving, sources and other boundaries.',
            interpretation='Autonomous fixed-history forecast candidate; no event after cutoff is used. Repeating150s history is a testable forecast assumption, not a claim that the actual process repeats.',
            acceptance='NC tail300 cell19 absolute discharge error reduces20percent in both seeds; first450 error grows no more than10vehicles. Also report both VSL contrasts and all conservation checks before any consideration of live integration.',
            future_inputs=False,production_adoption=False)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol)
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];samples=[]
    for case in ('s29_early','s43_early'):
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));a,k=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);a=list(a);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];t0=r['cutoff']
            assert a[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(a[1])==450
            for j in range(450,750):
                s=copy.deepcopy(a[1][-1]);s.update(window_start_s=round(t0+j,6),window_end_s=round(t0+j+1,6));a[1].append(s)
            raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
            k.update(horizon_sec=750,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
            density={t:next(x['rho_veh_per_km_lane'] for x in rs if x['road']=='FW_E' and x['cell']==21) for t,rs in truth.cells.items() if t0<=t<=t0+750.000001}
            assert len(density)==26 and all(math.isfinite(x) and x>=0 for x in density.values())
            arrival_profile=None
            if mode=='past_arrival':
                ep=truth.folder/'port_events.csv';pins[str(ep)]=sha(ep);counts=[0]*30;selected=[]
                with ep.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as stream:
                    for event in csv.DictReader(stream):
                        event_time=float(event['time_s'])
                        if event['connector']=='10490' and event['kind']=='arrival' and t0-150+1e-7<event_time<=t0+1e-7:
                            index=int(round((event_time-(t0-150))/5))-1
                            assert 0<=index<30 and abs(event_time-(t0-150+(index+1)*5))<1e-6
                            counts[index]+=1;selected.append(event_time)
                assert sum(counts)>0 and max(selected)<=t0+1e-7
                historical=sum(float(truth.ports[round(t0-j,6),'10490']['arrivals_veh']) for j in (0,30,60,90,120))
                assert historical==sum(counts)
                means=[s['ramp_arrival_vph']['RM_C10490'] for s in a[1]]
                for j,s in enumerate(a[1]):s['ramp_arrival_vph']['RM_C10490']=means[j]*30*counts[(j%150)//5]/sum(counts)
                residuals=[abs(sum(s['ramp_arrival_vph']['RM_C10490'] for s in a[1][j:j+150])-sum(means[j:j+150])) for j in range(0,750,150)]
                assert max(residuals)<1e-8
                arrival_profile=dict(case=case,arm=arm,historical_start=t0-150,historical_end=t0,counts_5s=counts,
                    observed_arrivals=sum(counts),max_event_time=max(selected),total_150s_residual_veh=max(residuals)/3600)
            mn=accounting._mn;step=accounting._freeway_substep_events;segment=mn.segment_vsl;update=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh;active={};seen=[]
            def locate(control,road,cell,cfg,**kwargs):
                active['address']=(road,cell);return segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kwargs)
            def timed(state,*args,**kwargs):
                active['time']=state.time_sec;return step(state,*args,**kwargs)
            def pressure(speed,upstream,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum):
                if mode=='density' and active.get('address')==('FW_E',20):
                    elapsed=active['time']-t0;index=int(round(elapsed))//30;left=round(t0+index*30,6);right=round(left+30,6);weight=(active['time']-left)/30
                    actual=(1-weight)*density[left]+weight*density[right]
                    seen.append(dict(time_s=active['time'],predicted_down_rho=down,conditioned_down_rho=actual));down=actual
                return update(speed,upstream,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum)
            if mode=='density':
                accounting._freeway_substep_events=timed;mn.segment_vsl=locate;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=pressure
            try:pred=model.rollout(*a,**k)
            finally:accounting._freeway_substep_events=step;mn.segment_vsl=segment;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=update
            assert mode!='density' or (len(seen)==750 and all(abs(z['time_s']-(t0+i))<1e-6 for i,z in enumerate(seen)))
            with gzip.open(out/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=750);assert row['conservation_max']<1e-9
            path=(cf/'eval_20' if case=='s29_early' else cf/'review')/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:baseline=json.load(f)
            local=[]
            for cell in (18,19,20,21,23,24):
                for start,end in ((t0,t0+450),(t0+450,t0+750)):
                    times=[round(start+j,6) for j in range(30,int(end-start)+1,30)]
                    aq=sum(float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',cell]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times)
                    bq=sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in baseline['flows'] if x['cell']==cell and x['window_end_s'] in times)
                    pq=sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==cell and x['window_end_s'] in times)
                    local.append(dict(cell=cell,start_s=start,end_s=end,actual_q=aq,baseline_q=bq,conditioned_q=pq))
            row['local_flow']=local;rows.append(row);samples.append(arrival_profile if mode=='past_arrival' else dict(case=case,arm=arm,samples=seen));save(out/(case+'_'+arm+'_result.json'),row)
            print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,mainline=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],cell19=[z for z in local if z['cell']==19])),flush=True)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    guards=[]
    if mode=='past_arrival':
        for row in rows:
            if row['arm']!='none':continue
            early,tail=[z for z in row['local_flow'] if z['cell']==19]
            error=lambda z,key:abs(z[key]-z['actual_q'])
            guards.append(dict(case=row['case'],early=error(early,'conditioned_q')<=error(early,'baseline_q')+10,
                tail=error(tail,'conditioned_q')<=.8*error(tail,'baseline_q')))
    save(out/'pressure_inputs.json' if mode=='density' else out/'arrival_profiles.json',samples)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,new_forecasts=4,fit=0,new_native=0,mode=mode,guards=guards,
        future_density_diagnostic_only=mode=='density',physical_state_resets=0,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False,pins=pins))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_forecasts=4)),flush=True)


def calibrate_vsl_density_response():
    """Two bounded Carlson A candidates, using three existing seed29 contrasts."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'vsl_a';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    ri=parent/'route_inventory';ve=parent/'vsl_native_exposure'
    original_manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';document=load(original_manifest)
    original_config=ROOT/document['sources']['reference_config']['path'];config=load(original_config)
    assert config['freeway']['vsl_fd_response']['FW_E']==dict(law='carlson',A=.5,E=2.,alpha=0.)
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    specs=[('s29_early','vsl','none',750),('s29_late','hold_vsl90','hold',450),
           ('s29_late','release_vsl90','release',450)]
    pins={str(original_manifest):sha(original_manifest),str(original_config):sha(original_config),
          str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json')}
    protocol=dict(candidates=[.25,0.],unchanged_E=2.,specs=specs,max_new_rollouts=7,
        changes='Only the existing Carlson critical-density response A, throughout the active VSL region; no extra physical term.',
        cause_hypothesis='Existing A=.5 may overstate persistent high-density VSL throughput; term audit is motivation, not proof.',
        train_seed=29,not_used_for_fitting=[43,61],future_inputs=False,new_native=0,
        acceptance={'flow_and_stock_response_loss_ratio_max':.9,'per_case_delta_cost_error_increase_max_veh_h':.5,
                    'per_case_speed_rmse_increase_max_kmh':2.,'conservation_and_inactive_exact':True},
        loss='Mean squared VSL-NC/hold/release150s flow response error/20veh and end-stock response error/5veh, cells15-20. No cost reward.',
        stop='No new coefficient search after these two. Freeze a qualifying candidate before any independent-state validation.',pins=pins)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def cached(case,arm,horizon):
        p=(ve/'horizon750'/(arm+'.json.gz') if horizon==750 else ri/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz'))
        pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:return json.load(f)
    def prepare(record,horizon):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        args=list(args);base=copy.deepcopy(args[1]);assert len(base)==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            s=copy.deepcopy(base[-1]);s.update(window_start_s=round(record['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(record['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(s)
        raw=ri/'s29_early'/'initial_raw.json' if record['case']=='s29_early' else ri/'initial_raw.json'
        pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);kw['horizon_sec']=horizon;kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw))
        return args,kw
    def response_metrics(pred,base,record,truth,base_truth,horizon):
        t0=record['cutoff'];pf={(x['window_end_s'],x['cell']):x for x in pred['flows']}
        bf={(x['window_end_s'],x['cell']):x for x in base['flows']}
        pc={(x['time_s'],x['cell']):x for x in pred['cells']};bc={(x['time_s'],x['cell']):x for x in base['cells']}
        ac={(t,x['cell']):x for t,rows in truth.cells.items() for x in rows if x['road']=='FW_E'}
        ab={(t,x['cell']):x for t,rows in base_truth.cells.items() for x in rows if x['road']=='FW_E'}
        terms=[];rows=[]
        for start in range(0,horizon,150):
            end=round(t0+start+150,6);times=[round(t0+start+j,6) for j in range(30,151,30)]
            for c in range(15,21):
                pq=sum(pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-bf[t,c]['downstream_crossings'] for t in times)
                aq=sum(float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings'])-float(base_truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times)
                pn=pc[end,c]['n_veh']-bc[end,c]['n_veh'];an=ac[end,c]['n_veh']-ab[end,c]['n_veh']
                terms.extend([((pq-aq)/20.)**2,((pn-an)/5.)**2])
                rows.append(dict(end_s=end,cell=c,actual_delta_q=aq,predicted_delta_q=pq,actual_delta_n=an,predicted_delta_n=pn))
        return dict(loss=sum(terms)/len(terms),blocks=rows)
    def evaluate(model,record,base_record,horizon,pred):
        base=cached(record['case'],base_record['arm'],horizon)
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);base_truth=ObservationData(base_record['truth'])
        for data in (truth,base_truth):
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):
                p=data.folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        measured=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        reference=_cellwise_measure(model,base,base_record,base_truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        measured['delta']={label:{k:measured[label][k]-reference[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','end_n','exits')}
                           for label in ('actual','predicted')}
        measured['response']=response_metrics(pred,base,record,truth,base_truth,horizon)
        return measured
    context=lpr.load_sources(original_manifest);baseline=[]
    for case,arm,ref,horizon in specs:
        record=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));br=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,ref))
        baseline.append(evaluate(context['component'],record,br,horizon,cached(case,arm,horizon)))
    save(out/'baseline.json',baseline);runs=[];parity=None
    for label,A in (('a25',.25),('a00',0.)):
        folder=out/label;folder.mkdir();candidate=copy.deepcopy(config);candidate['freeway']['vsl_fd_response']['FW_E']['A']=A
        cp=folder/'config.json';save(cp,candidate);manifest=copy.deepcopy(document)
        manifest['sources']['reference_config']=dict(path=cp.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(cp))
        manifest['qualification']='Unqualified two-candidate freeway response diagnostic; not SDMPC deployment.'
        mp=folder/'manifest.json';save(mp,manifest);model=lpr.load_sources(mp)['component'];rows=[]
        pins[str(cp)]=sha(cp);pins[str(mp)]=sha(mp)
        if label=='a25':
            record=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_early','none'))
            args,kw=prepare(record,750);pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw);old=cached('s29_early','none',750)
            parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(pred[k]))==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        for case,arm,ref,horizon in specs:
            record=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));br=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,ref))
            args,kw=prepare(record,horizon);started=time.perf_counter();pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
            with gzip.open(folder/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            row=evaluate(model,record,br,horizon,pred);row['wall_sec']=time.perf_counter()-started;rows.append(row)
            print(json.dumps(dict(candidate=label,case=case,arm=arm,loss=row['response']['loss'],delta=row['delta'])),flush=True)
        guards=[]
        for row,old in zip(rows,baseline):
            error=lambda r,k:abs(r['delta']['predicted'][k]-r['delta']['actual'][k])
            guards.append(dict(case=row['case'],arm=row['arm'],
                mainline_cost=error(row,'mainline_ttt')<=error(old,'mainline_ttt')+.5,
                component_cost=error(row,'ttt')<=error(old,'ttt')+.5,
                absolute_speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=old['score']['speed']['rmse']+2.))
        loss=sum(r['response']['loss'] for r in rows)/len(rows);base_loss=sum(r['response']['loss'] for r in baseline)/len(baseline)
        result=dict(candidate=label,A=A,loss=loss,baseline_loss=base_loss,loss_ratio=loss/base_loss,guards=guards,
                    train_pass=loss<=.9*base_loss and all(all(r[k] for k in ('mainline_cost','component_cost','absolute_speed')) for r in guards),rows=rows)
        save(folder/'summary.json',result);runs.append(result)
    qualified=[r for r in runs if r['train_pass']];selected=min(qualified,key=lambda x:x['loss'])['candidate'] if qualified else None
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'selection.json',dict(candidates=[{k:r[k] for k in ('candidate','A','loss','baseline_loss','loss_ratio','guards','train_pass')} for r in runs],
        selected=selected,parity=parity,new_rollouts=7,new_native=0,adopted=False,pins=pins,
        next='Frozen independent-state validation required before adoption.' if selected else 'Stop A search; two candidates did not pass. Do not launch another grid.'))
    print(json.dumps(dict(selected=selected,new_rollouts=7)),flush=True)


def calibrate_regional_vsl_shape():
    """Two local FD-response candidates; nominal physics stays byte-for-byte fixed."""
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, freeway_fd
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'regional_vsl_shape';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    ri=root/'route_inventory';ve=root/'vsl_native_exposure'
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    records += [dict(r,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/r['arm']))
                for r in load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records']]
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,ri/'contract.json',
        Path(freeway_fd.__file__),Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP'))}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for source in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/source['path'])]=sha(ROOT/source['path'])
    training=[('s29_early','vsl','none',750),('s29_late','hold_vsl90','hold',450),
              ('s29_late','release_vsl90','release',450)]
    validation=[('s43_early','vsl','none',450),('s43_early','both','rm',450),
                ('s67_late','hold_vsl90','hold',450),('s67_late','release_vsl90','release',450)]
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: reset-location sensitivity and FD-only feasibility audit completed.',
        hypothesis='One directional Carlson E imposes the same VSL shape response on the lane-drop region and recovery cells. Test a local response parameter without altering nominal recovery dynamics.',
        region=list(range(16,21)),geometry='Physical4-to3 lane drop18->19; include sign16 and first downstream19/20, stop before merge21.',
        candidates_E=[1.,4.],baseline_E=2.,A=.5,alpha=0.,training=training,validation=validation,
        max_new_forecasts=11,training_forecasts=7,validation_forecasts_if_train_pass=4,
        no_VSL_parity='One new750s nominal run must match saved cells/flows/ports/ramps exactly; other unchanged110 arms reused.',
        objective='Same prior response loss: mean squared150s VSL-minus-reference flow error/20veh and end-stock error/5veh over cells15..20.',
        gates=dict(train_response_ratio_max=.9,cost_error_increase_max_veh_h=.5,speed_rmse_increase_max_kmh=2.,
            validation_response_ratio_max=1.,new_wrong_cost_sign_threshold_veh_h=.5),
        scope='Autonomous31east cells+4on/4off connectors. Not fullOmega or SDMPC. Previously inspected seeds are confirmation, not blind holdout.',
        changes='Only Carlson E for currently exposed command cohorts in cells16..20. No capacity/reward or observed future inputs. All nominal coefficients, flow conservation, accepted merges/waiting, dynamic off storage and transport unchanged.',
        stop='Choose at most one candidate after training; freeze before confirmation. No interpolation/grid extension or automatic production adoption.',
        new_native=0,new_fzp=0,pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def record(case,arm):return next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
    def cached(case,arm,horizon):
        if case=='s67_late':p=root/'choice67'/('route036_s67_late_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        elif horizon==750:p=ve/'horizon750'/(arm+'.json.gz')
        else:p=(ri if case=='s29_late' else ri/case)/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:return json.load(f)
    def prepare(case,arm,horizon):
        r=record(case,arm);args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];args=list(args)
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=(ri if case=='s29_late' else ri/case)/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        return args,kw
    def measure(spec,pred):
        case,arm,ref,horizon=spec;r=record(case,arm);br=record(case,ref)
        base=cached(case,ref,horizon);truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);bt=ObservationData(br['truth'])
        for data in (truth,bt):
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(data.folder/name)]=sha(data.folder/name)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        reference=_cellwise_measure(model,base,br,bt,horizon_sec=horizon)
        row['delta']={label:{k:row[label][k]-reference[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','end_n','exits')}
                      for label in ('actual','predicted')}
        pf={(x['window_end_s'],x['cell']):x for x in pred['flows']};bf={(x['window_end_s'],x['cell']):x for x in base['flows']}
        pc={(x['time_s'],x['cell']):x for x in pred['cells']};bc={(x['time_s'],x['cell']):x for x in base['cells']}
        ac={(t,x['cell']):x for t,z in truth.cells.items() for x in z if x['road']=='FW_E'}
        ab={(t,x['cell']):x for t,z in bt.cells.items() for x in z if x['road']=='FW_E'}
        residuals=[];blocks=[]
        for start in range(0,horizon,150):
            end=round(r['cutoff']+start+150,6);times=[round(r['cutoff']+start+j,6) for j in range(30,151,30)]
            for cell in range(15,21):
                pq=sum(pf[t,cell]['downstream_crossings']-bf[t,cell]['downstream_crossings'] for t in times)
                aq=sum(float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',cell]['downstream_crossings'])-float(bt.flows[t,'FW_E',cell]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times)
                pn=pc[end,cell]['n_veh']-bc[end,cell]['n_veh'];an=ac[end,cell]['n_veh']-ab[end,cell]['n_veh']
                residuals.extend([((pq-aq)/20.)**2,((pn-an)/5.)**2])
                blocks.append(dict(end_s=end,cell=cell,actual_delta_q=aq,predicted_delta_q=pq,actual_delta_n=an,predicted_delta_n=pn))
        row['response']=dict(loss=sum(residuals)/len(residuals),blocks=blocks)
        return row
    baselines={tuple(s):measure(s,cached(s[0],s[1],s[3])) for s in training+validation}
    save(out/'baseline.json',list(baselines.values()))
    original=freeway_fd._literature_speed;counts=Counter();candidate_E=2.;completed=[]
    def local_shape(spec,cfg,road,cell,rho,command):
        if road=='FW_E' and 16<=cell<=20 and float(command)<110.:
            assert spec==dict(law='carlson',A=.5,E=2.,alpha=0.)
            spec=dict(spec,E=candidate_E);counts[cell,float(command)]+=1
        return original(spec,cfg,road,cell,rho,command)
    def execute(spec,label):
        assert len(completed)<11
        case,arm,ref,horizon=spec;args,kw=prepare(case,arm,horizon);counts.clear();started=time.perf_counter()
        pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw);elapsed=time.perf_counter()-started
        stem=label+'_'+case+'_'+arm
        with gzip.open(out/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        row=measure(spec,pred);row.update(E=candidate_E,label=label,wall_sec=elapsed,
            calls=[dict(cell=c,command=v,count=n) for (c,v),n in sorted(counts.items())])
        completed.append(row);save(out/(stem+'.json'),row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(label=label,case=case,arm=arm,wall_sec=elapsed,response_loss=row['response']['loss'],delta=row['delta'])),flush=True)
        return pred,row
    def judge(rows,specs,ratio):
        guards=[]
        for row,spec in zip(rows,specs):
            base=baselines[tuple(spec)];error=lambda x,k:abs(x['delta']['predicted'][k]-x['delta']['actual'][k])
            signs=all(not (abs(row['delta']['actual'][k])>=.5 and row['delta']['actual'][k]*row['delta']['predicted'][k]<=0
                          and base['delta']['actual'][k]*base['delta']['predicted'][k]>0) for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt'))
            guards.append(dict(case=row['case'],arm=row['arm'],main_cost=error(row,'mainline_ttt')<=error(base,'mainline_ttt')+.5,
                component_cost=error(row,'ttt')<=error(base,'ttt')+.5,speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=base['score']['speed']['rmse']+2.,no_new_wrong_large_sign=signs))
        loss=sum(r['response']['loss'] for r in rows)/len(rows);base_loss=sum(baselines[tuple(s)]['response']['loss'] for s in specs)/len(specs)
        return dict(loss=loss,baseline_loss=base_loss,ratio=loss/base_loss,guards=guards,
            passed=loss<=ratio*base_loss and all(all(r[k] for k in ('main_cost','component_cost','speed','no_new_wrong_large_sign')) for r in guards))
    try:
        freeway_fd._literature_speed=local_shape
        candidate_E=1.;pred,parity_row=execute(('s29_early','none','none',750),'parity')
        previous=cached('s29_early','none',750)
        parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(pred[k]))==previous[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
        assert all(parity.values()) and not parity_row['calls']
        candidates=[]
        for candidate_E in (1.,4.):
            label='E'+str(int(candidate_E));rows=[]
            for spec in training:
                _,row=execute(spec,label);assert row['calls'];rows.append(row)
            result=dict(E=candidate_E,**judge(rows,training,.9));candidates.append(result)
            save(out/(label+'_selection.json'),result)
        eligible=[r for r in candidates if r['passed']];selected=min(eligible,key=lambda r:r['loss']) if eligible else None
        save(out/'selection.json',dict(candidates=candidates,selected=selected,parity=parity,adopted=False))
        confirmation=None
        if selected:
            candidate_E=selected['E'];save(out/'frozen_candidate.json',dict(E=candidate_E,cells=list(range(16,21)),A=.5,other_E=2.,new_fitting_after_this=False))
            rows=[]
            for spec in validation:
                _,row=execute(spec,'confirmation');rows.append(row)
            confirmation=judge(rows,validation,1.)
            save(out/'confirmation.json',confirmation)
        for name,h in pins.items():assert sha(name)==h,name
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(candidates=candidates,selected=selected,confirmation=confirmation,rows=completed,
            new_forecasts=len(completed),nominal_parity=parity,pins=pins,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
        print(json.dumps(dict(done=True,new_forecasts=len(completed),selected=selected,confirmation=confirmation)),flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        save(out/'failure.json',dict(type=type(exc).__name__,message=str(exc),completed_forecasts=len(completed)));raise
    finally:freeway_fd._literature_speed=original


def diagnose_exit10481_drain():
    """Two explicitly future-conditioned diagnostics, never autonomous scores."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'source_discharge/drain29';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);ri=parent/'route_inventory'
    manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']=='s29_early' and r['arm'] in ('none','vsl')]
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json'),
          str(ri/'s29_early/initial_raw.json'):sha(ri/'s29_early/initial_raw.json')}
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for v in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(max_rollouts=2,horizon=750,coefficient_changes=0,new_native=0,
        future_observations=True,qualification=False,
        intervention='Only10481 off_drain_vph uses each arm future30s observed exits*120; states are not reset, current readiness and storage remain.',
        interpretation='Cause-isolation: recorded departures are not saturation capacity and not a usable future controller input.',pins=pins))
    (out/'helper.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[]
    for r in records:
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];t0=r['cutoff']
        original=copy.deepcopy(args[1]);assert len(original)==450
        for j in range(450,750):
            s=copy.deepcopy(original[-1]);s.update(window_start_s=round(t0+j,6),window_end_s=round(t0+j+1,6));args[1].append(s)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);p=truth.folder/'ports_30s.csv';pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        for j,s in enumerate(args[1]):s['off_drain_vph']['10481']=float(truth.ports[round(t0+30*(j//30+1),6),'10481']['departures_veh'])*120.
        kw.update(horizon_sec=750,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(ri/'s29_early/initial_raw.json')))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage';pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        with gzip.open(out/(r['arm']+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=750)
        row['end_exit10481']=next(x for x in pred['ports'] if x['connector']=='10481' and x['time_s']==2970.1)
        row['cell19_tail_flow']=sum(x['downstream_crossings'] for x in pred['flows'] if x['cell']==19 and x['window_end_s']>2670.1)
        row['cell19_final']=next(x for x in pred['cells'] if x['cell']==19 and x['time_s']==2970.1)
        rows.append(row);save(out/(r['arm']+'_result.json'),row);print(json.dumps({k:row[k] for k in ('arm','predicted','end_exit10481','cell19_tail_flow','cell19_final')}),flush=True)
    base=next(r for r in rows if r['arm']=='none')
    for r in rows:r['delta']={label:{k:r[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','exits','end_n')} for label in ('actual','predicted')}
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,pins=pins,new_rollouts=2,future_conditioned=True,adopted=False,qualification=False))


def diagnose_local1820_balance():
    """Current-state scalar factorial audit, not an autonomous forecast."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers import vissim_stackelberg_adapter as adapter
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'local1820_balance';assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    assert not set(range(15,21)) & set(net.ramp_merge_segment_index.values())
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(manifest):sha(manifest)}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:13 numerical checks, source contract exact, two A candidates rejected,10481drain insufficient.',
        seeds=[29,43],arms=['NC110'],cells=list(range(15,21)),timestamps='2670.1..2940.1 at30s',
        factors=['local N/v from model or current native','upstream v from model or current native','downstream rho from model or current native'],
        max_scalar_updates=960,full_forecasts=0,new_native=0,parameters_changed=False,
        limits=['Instantaneous hybrid states are equation diagnostics, not physically continuous trajectories or gain validation.',
                'Native next30s snapshot speed trend includes changing populations and is not instantaneous individual acceleration.',
                'Lane-drop geometry/current effective lane profile is held to the saved prediction in all eight factors; off inventory is not varied.',
                'NC110 avoids inventing observed VSL cohort tags. No new capacity or bonus.'],pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];parity=[]
    old_context=copy.deepcopy(adapter._FW_SEG_CTX);old_profile=copy.deepcopy(adapter._FW_SEG_CTX_STATE)
    try:
        for seed in (29,43):
            case=f's{seed}_early';r=next(r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==case and r['arm']=='none')
            truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);p=Path(r['truth'])/'cells_30s.csv';pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            native={(t,x['cell']):x for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for x in rs if x['road']=='FW_E'}
            path=parent/'source_discharge'/(case+'_none_terms.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:terms=json.load(f)
            terms={(round(x['time_sec'],6),x['cell']):x for x in terms}
            for offset in range(450,750,30):
                stamp=round(r['cutoff']+offset,6)
                for cell in range(15,21):
                    term=terms[stamp,cell];p=dict(net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell]);dt=1/3600.
                    length=p['segment_length_km'];critical=p['rho_crit'];kappa=p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']
                    upstream=term['speed_before']+term['convection']*length/(dt*term['speed_before'])
                    lanes=(term['phi']*dt*term['delta_lanes']*term['rho']*term['speed_before']**2/
                           (length*critical*term['lane_drop_raw']) if term['lane_drop_raw'] else net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][cell])
                    assert abs(term['post_equation_change'])<1e-10
                    for observed_local in (False,True):
                        for observed_up in (False,True):
                            for observed_down in (False,True):
                                local=native[stamp,cell]
                                speed=local['v_kmh'] if observed_local else term['speed_before']
                                rho=local['rho_veh_per_km_lane'] if observed_local else term['rho']
                                vu=native[stamp,cell-1]['v_kmh'] if observed_up else upstream
                                rd=native[stamp,cell+1]['rho_veh_per_km_lane'] if observed_down else term['downstream_rho']
                                spec=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',cell)
                                adapter._FW_SEG_CTX.clear();adapter._FW_SEG_CTX.update(dict(p=p,armed=True,state_response=spec,
                                    response_rho_crit=critical,phi=term['phi'],dlam=term['delta_lanes'],lanes=lanes,rho_crit_link=net.rho_crit))
                                desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,110,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                                nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,110)
                                tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(spec,speed,desired,rho,rd,critical,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                                relax=dt/tau*(desired-speed);conv=dt/length*speed*(vu-speed)
                                ant=-nu*dt/(tau*length)*(rd-rho)/(rho+kappa)
                                drop=term['phi']*dt*term['delta_lanes']*rho*speed**2/(length*lanes*critical)
                                calculated=max(net.v_min,max(net.v_min,speed+relax+conv+ant)-drop)
                                executed=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(speed,vu,rho,rd,desired,dt,length,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,kappa,net.v_min)
                                assert abs(executed-calculated)<1e-9
                                if not any((observed_local,observed_up,observed_down)):
                                    error=max(abs(desired-term['desired']),abs(executed-term['speed_final']))
                                    assert error<1e-8,(seed,cell,stamp,error,desired,term['desired'])
                                    parity.append(error)
                                rows.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=cell,time_s=stamp,local_observed=observed_local,
                                    up_observed=observed_up,down_observed=observed_down,speed=speed,rho=rho,upstream_speed=vu,downstream_rho=rd,
                                    desired=desired,relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,lane_drop=drop,
                                    predicted_one_second_change=executed-speed,
                                    native_next30_change=native[round(stamp+30,6),cell]['v_kmh']-local['v_kmh']))
    finally:
        adapter._FW_SEG_CTX.clear();adapter._FW_SEG_CTX.update(old_context)
        adapter._FW_SEG_CTX_STATE.clear();adapter._FW_SEG_CTX_STATE.update(old_profile)
    assert len(rows)==960 and len(parity)==120
    grouped=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        for cell in range(15,21):
            for bits in ((a,b,c) for a in (False,True) for b in (False,True) for c in (False,True)):
                selected=[r for r in rows if r['seed']==seed and r['cell']==cell and
                          (r['local_observed'],r['up_observed'],r['down_observed'])==bits]
                metrics=('predicted_one_second_change','native_next30_change','relaxation','convection','anticipation','lane_drop')
                grouped.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=cell,basis=''.join(str(int(b)) for b in bits),count=len(selected),
                    means={k:sum(r[k] for r in selected)/len(selected) for k in metrics},
                    native_recovery_count=sum(r['native_next30_change']>=3 for r in selected),
                    predicts_acceleration_during_native_recovery=sum(r['native_next30_change']>=3 and r['predicted_one_second_change']>0 for r in selected)))
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,groups=grouped,scalar_updates=len(rows),executed_saved_samples=len(parity),
        exact_reconstruction_error_max=max(parity),pins=pins,new_full_forecasts=0,new_native=0,coefficient_fits=0,
        role='Conditional scalar cause isolation only; not autonomous prediction or model adoption.'))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,scalar_updates=len(rows),saved_equations_exact=len(parity))),flush=True)


def calibrate_connected_recovery_fd(recovery23=False, regional_response=False):
    """Finite three-factor fit with the current conservative route contract."""
    import numpy as np
    if not regional_response:
        from scipy.optimize import least_squares, minimize_scalar
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    assert not (recovery23 and regional_response)
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/('regional_joint' if regional_response else 'recovery23_fit' if recovery23 else 'connected_fd')
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    manifest=(root/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json' if recovery23 else root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json')
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    ri=root/'route_inventory';records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    train=[('s29_early','none',750),('s29_early','vsl',750),('s29_early','rm',450),('s29_early','both',450),('s29_late','hold',450),('s29_late','release',450)]
    check=[('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750),('s43_early','rm',450),('s43_early','both',450),('s29_late','hold_vsl90',450),('s29_late','release_vsl90',450)]
    if regional_response:
        cap=HERE/'heldout61_freeway_20260930/capture.json'
        records += [dict(r,case='s61_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,
                         truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
        check += [('s61_late','hold',450),('s61_late','release',450)]
    pins=load(out/'before.json');pins[str(Path(__file__))]=sha(Path(__file__));pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
    pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    old_config=model._config;factors=np.ones(3);evaluations=[];prepared={};best=[float('inf'),None];new_forecasts=0
    if recovery23:
        current=old_config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']).network.freeway_state_response['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['23']['anticipation']
        nu_bounds=[current['downstream_lt_local'],current['downstream_ge_local']];assert 0<nu_bounds[0]<nu_bounds[1]
        factors=np.asarray([nu_bounds[0]])
    def config(road,parameters):
        cfg=old_config(road,parameters)
        if road=='FW_E' and regional_response:
            local=cfg.network.freeway_state_response[road]['cell_overrides']
            local['21']['delta_merge']*=float(factors[0])
            for cell in ('24','25'):
                for branch in ('acceleration_sec','deceleration_sec'):
                    local[cell]['relaxation'][branch]*=float(factors[1])
                local[cell]['anticipation']['downstream_lt_local']*=float(factors[2])
        elif road=='FW_E' and recovery23:
            cfg.network.freeway_state_response['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['23']['anticipation']['downstream_lt_local']=float(factors[0])
        elif road=='FW_E':
            for c in range(21,26):
                p=cfg.network.freeway_segment_params[road][c]
                p['rho_crit']*=float(factors[0 if c<=23 else 1]);p['metanet_a_m']*=float(factors[2])
                assert 0<p['rho_crit']<cfg.network.rho_max and .5<=p['metanet_a_m']<=8
        return cfg
    model._config=config
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:500scalar/200flow reconstructions and two rejected merge21 forecasts.',
        factors=['rho_crit21-23','rho_crit24-25','FDshape21-25'],bounds=[[.85,.85,.85],[1.3,1.3,1.4]],
        train=train,check=check,source='frozen expanded036 with current route inventory+storage entry; no urban changes',
        max_residual_evaluations=24,max_optimizer_nfev=6,finite_difference_relative_step=.02,max_new_train_forecasts=144,
        evaluation='Absolute N/v/flow plus paired N/cumulative flow/mainline TTT. Ramp/off/component TTT remain in every forecast and are reported separately before any adoption. These calibration residuals do not change the Omega controller objective.',
        acceptance='Training combined loss reduces>=5%; state loss<=110% baseline; response loss<=105% baseline; no meaningful >=2vehh mainline pair has wrong sign. Then inspect other states without refitting.',
        constraints='No vfree/merge/nu/tau/capacity/command/source/route changes; no future observations passed to rollout; initial1s step and450s MPC preserved.',
        limitations='Previously inspected seeds, not pristine blind validation. Passing this local fit is not whole-Omega/SDMPC qualification.',new_native=0)
    if recovery23:
        protocol.update(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:10 response-isolation forecasts; current fixednuGE candidate completed15 forecasts, improves NC but reverses29VSL benefit.',
            factors=['cell23 anticipation downstream_lt_local'],bounds=nu_bounds,
            source='Frozen connected-FD plus one recovery coefficient. Its endpoints are already measured, not production qualified.',
            max_residual_evaluations=8,max_optimizer_nfev=6,max_new_train_forecasts=36,
            endpoint_reuse='Twelve saved predictions: connected_fd/eval_20 and connected_recovery23, six training conditions each.',
            optimizer='Bounded scalar minimization, at most6 new interior values; xatol0.1. Budget exhaustion is not convergence.',
            constraints='Only existing cell23 recovery anticipation coefficient. No FD/merge/tau/capacity/source/command/route changes. Future measurements only in scoring.',
            acceptance=protocol['acceptance']+' Also require response loss<=105percent of pre-FD expanded036 anchor; do not waive the previously failed response gate.')
        protocol.pop('finite_difference_relative_step')
    if regional_response:
        protocol.update(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: frozen seed29 SDMPC9000 restarted; separate route bridge failed response checks. Runtime remains immutable.',
            factors=['delta_merge21','tau24-25','nu_downstream_lt24-25'],bounds=[[.75]*3,[1.25]*3],
            source='Frozen expanded036 plus existing route inventory/storage contract; diagnosed merge21 and recovery24/25 jointly, not rejected connected-FD coefficients.',
            optimizer='One forward-difference Jacobian at1, one ridge Gauss-Newton direction, infinity-norm step<=0.25 and half-step. No iterative grid or retry.',
            max_residual_evaluations=6,max_optimizer_nfev=6,max_new_train_forecasts=36,max_new_validation_forecasts=8,
            constraints='Only three regional multipliers, seven existing coefficient leaves; all FD/source/route/commands/other cells unchanged. Current-predicted density selects recovery branch. No future observations in rollout.',
            acceptance='Same six training cases: combined loss>=5% lower, state<=110%, response>=5% lower, meaningful mainline signs preserved, no >=2vehh component sign wrong. If failed stop; if passed inspect eight frozen check cases without refit.',
            limitations='Independent states/seeds already inspected; not pristine holdout. No live native model updates or production adoption. External/urban costs not in component score; Omega objective unchanged.')
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def prepare(key):
        if key in prepared:return prepared[key]
        case,arm,horizon=key;r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        prepared[key]=(r,args,kw,truth);return prepared[key]
    def arrays(pred,truth,r,horizon):
        times=[round(r['cutoff']+j,6) for j in range(30,horizon+1,30)]
        pc={(z['time_s'],z['cell']):z for z in pred['cells']};pf={(z['window_end_s'],z['cell']):z for z in pred['flows']}
        ac={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for z in rs if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        obs=[];forecast=[]
        for t in times:
            obs.append([[ac[t,c]['n_veh'],ac[t,c]['v_kmh'] or 0.,float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings'])] for c in range(31)])
            forecast.append([[pc[t,c]['n_veh'],pc[t,c]['v_kmh'],pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']] for c in range(31)])
        return np.array(obs),np.array(forecast)
    def run_set(keys,directory,reuse=None):
        nonlocal new_forecasts
        directory.mkdir(exist_ok=False);rows=[];data={}
        for key in keys:
            r,a,k,truth=prepare(key)
            if reuse is None:
                pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(a),**copy.deepcopy(k));new_forecasts+=1
            else:
                path=reuse/(key[0]+'_'+key[1]+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
                with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
            row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=key[2]);assert row['conservation_max']<1e-9
            rows.append(row);data[key[:2]]=(arrays(pred,truth,r,key[2]),row)
            if reuse is None:
                with gzip.open(directory/(key[0]+'_'+key[1]+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        return rows,data
    def residuals(data):
        state=[];response=[];pairs=[]
        for (case,arm),((obs,forecast),row) in data.items():
            error=(forecast-obs)/np.array([10.,20.,1000/120])
            error[:,:,1]=np.where(obs[:,:,0]>=5,error[:,:,1],0.)
            # Give the diagnosed connected region its own equal-sized block,
            # while retaining the other26 cells as an explicit guard.
            local=error[:,21:26,:].ravel();whole=error.ravel()
            state.extend((local/np.sqrt(len(local))).tolist());state.extend((whole/np.sqrt(len(whole))).tolist())
            if arm in ('none','hold'):continue
            base=data[case,'none' if case=='s29_early' else 'hold'];n=len(obs)
            delta_true=obs-base[0][0][:n];delta_pred=forecast-base[0][1][:n]
            errs=np.stack(((delta_pred[:,:,0]-delta_true[:,:,0])/5,
                           (np.cumsum(delta_pred[:,:,2],axis=0)-np.cumsum(delta_true[:,:,2],axis=0))/20),axis=-1).ravel()
            response.extend((errs/np.sqrt(len(errs))).tolist())
            # Use the baseline at exactly the paired horizon, not its750s total.
            r,a,k,truth=prepare(next(key for key in train if key[:2]==(case,arm)))
            t0=r['cutoff'];n0=sum(z['n_veh'] for z in a[0] if z['road']=='FW_E')
            integrate=lambda x:(n0+2*sum(np.sum(x[:-1,:,0],axis=1))+np.sum(x[-1,:,0]))*30/7200
            da=integrate(obs)-integrate(base[0][0][:n]);dp=integrate(forecast)-integrate(base[0][1][:n])
            response.append((dp-da)/2)
            pairs.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,actual_mainline_delta=da,predicted_mainline_delta=dp))
        state=np.asarray(state)/np.sqrt(len(data)*2);response=np.asarray(response)/np.sqrt(max(1,len(pairs)*2))
        return np.r_[state,response],dict(state=float(state@state),response=float(response@response),pairs=pairs)
    def evaluate(x,reuse=None):
        nonlocal factors
        assert len(evaluations)<(6 if regional_response else 8 if recovery23 else 24),'Fixed fitting budget exhausted'
        factors=np.asarray(x,dtype=float);idx=len(evaluations);directory=out/f'eval_{idx:02d}'
        rows,data=run_set(train,directory,reuse);res,loss=residuals(data);total=float(res@res)
        item=dict(index=idx,factors=factors.tolist(),total=total,loss=loss,rows=rows)
        if reuse is not None:item['predictions_reused_from']=str(reuse)
        save(directory/'result.json',item);evaluations.append(item)
        if total<best[0]:best[:]=[total,idx]
        print(json.dumps(dict(evaluation=idx,factors=item['factors'],total=total,state=loss['state'],response=loss['response'])),flush=True)
        return res
    try:
        if regional_response:
            from types import SimpleNamespace
            baseline=evaluate(np.ones(3));columns=[]
            for j in range(3):
                x=np.ones(3);x[j]+=.02
                columns.append((evaluate(x)-baseline)/.02)
            jacobian=np.column_stack(columns)
            ridge=.1
            direction=np.linalg.lstsq(np.vstack((jacobian,np.sqrt(ridge)*np.eye(3))),
                                     np.r_[-baseline,np.zeros(3)],rcond=None)[0]
            if np.max(np.abs(direction))>.25:direction*=.25/np.max(np.abs(direction))
            save(out/'local_step.json',dict(direction=direction.tolist(),ridge=ridge,
                singular_values=np.linalg.svd(jacobian,compute_uv=False).tolist(),
                derivative_step=.02,iteration_budget=1,converged=False))
            if np.max(np.abs(direction))>1e-8:
                evaluate(np.ones(3)+direction)
                evaluate(np.ones(3)+.5*direction)
            result=SimpleNamespace(nfev=len(evaluations),njev=1,status=0,
                message='Finite one-step calibration budget completed; convergence not claimed.',success=False)
        elif recovery23:
            evaluate([nu_bounds[0]],root/'connected_fd/eval_20')
            prior=load(root/'connected_fd/eval_20/result.json')
            assert abs(evaluations[0]['total']-prior['total'])<1e-10
            evaluate([nu_bounds[1]],root/'connected_recovery23')
            def objective(value):
                residual=evaluate([value]);return float(residual@residual)
            result=minimize_scalar(objective,bounds=nu_bounds,method='bounded',options=dict(maxiter=6,xatol=.1))
        else:result=least_squares(evaluate,np.ones(3),bounds=protocol['bounds'],diff_step=.02,max_nfev=6,ftol=1e-4,xtol=1e-4,gtol=1e-4)
        base=evaluations[0];candidate=evaluations[best[1]]
        gates=dict(total=candidate['total']<=.95*base['total'],state=candidate['loss']['state']<=1.1*base['loss']['state'],
            response=candidate['loss']['response']<=1.05*base['loss']['response'],
            significant_signs=all(p['actual_mainline_delta']*p['predicted_mainline_delta']>0 for p in candidate['loss']['pairs'] if abs(p['actual_mainline_delta'])>=2))
        if recovery23:
            original=load(root/'connected_fd/eval_00/result.json');pins[str(root/'connected_fd/eval_00/result.json')]=sha(root/'connected_fd/eval_00/result.json')
            gates['original_response_anchor']=candidate['loss']['response']<=1.05*original['loss']['response']
        if regional_response:
            gates['response']=candidate['loss']['response']<=.95*base['loss']['response']
            component_pairs=[]
            for row in candidate['rows']:
                if row['arm'] in ('none','hold'):continue
                base_row=next(z for z in candidate['rows'] if z['case']==row['case'] and z['arm'] in ('none','hold'))
                # Early RM/both use450s while NC is750s; their component prefix is
                # checked later from saved predictions, not mismatched totals.
                if row['case']=='s29_early' and row['arm'] in ('rm','both'):continue
                component_pairs.append(dict(case=row['case'],arm=row['arm'],
                    actual=row['actual']['ttt']-base_row['actual']['ttt'],
                    predicted=row['predicted']['ttt']-base_row['predicted']['ttt']))
            gates['meaningful_component_signs']=all(p['actual']*p['predicted']>0 for p in component_pairs if abs(p['actual'])>=2)
            save(out/'component_sign_guard.json',dict(pairs=component_pairs,scope='Equal-horizon750 early VSL and450 late RM release; early450 RM/both still retained in saved full predictions.'))
        selected=all(gates.values());save(out/'selection.json',dict(selected=selected,gates=gates,best=candidate,
            optimizer=dict(nfev=result.nfev,njev=getattr(result,'njev',None),status=result.status,message=result.message,success=result.success),production_adopted=False))
        validation=[]
        if selected:
            factors=np.asarray(candidate['factors']);validation,_=run_set(check,out/'validation')
        # Verify the diagnostic no-change configuration against the saved current750 result.
        if recovery23:
            oldpath=root/'connected_recovery23/selection.json';pins[str(oldpath)]=sha(oldpath)
            parity=load(oldpath)['parity'];assert all(parity.values())
            save(out/'parity_source.json',dict(reused_exact_nochange_check=str(oldpath),new_disabled_rollout=False,cached_objective_matches=True))
        else:
            path=out/'eval_00/s29_early_none.json.gz';oldpath=root/'vsl_native_exposure/horizon750/none.json.gz';pins[str(oldpath)]=sha(oldpath)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:new=json.load(f)
            with gzip.open(oldpath,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
            parity={k:new[k]==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
        save(out/'completion.json',dict(evaluations=len(evaluations),new_train_forecasts=new_forecasts-len(validation),new_validation_forecasts=len(validation),
            selection_pass=selected,validation=validation,parity=parity,pins=pins,goal_qualified=False,production_adopted=False,new_native=0))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,evaluations=len(evaluations),selected=selected,gates=gates,validation_cases=len(validation))),flush=True)
    finally:model._config=old_config


def review_connected_recovery_fd():
    """Seven frozen checks of a tradeoff candidate; do not revise its failed gate."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';fit=root/'connected_fd';out=fit/'review'
    resumed=out.is_dir()
    if resumed:assert (out/'protocol.json').is_file() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True);selection=load(fit/'selection.json');completion=load(fit/'completion.json')
    assert completion['evaluations']==24 and not selection['selected']
    factors=selection['best']['factors'];baseline_manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    doc=load(baseline_manifest);reference=ROOT/doc['sources']['reference_config']['path'];conf=load(reference)
    before=copy.deepcopy(conf);fd=conf['freeway']['physical_cell_fd']['FW_E']
    for c in range(21,26):
        fd[str(c)]['rho_crit']*=factors[0 if c<=23 else 1];fd[str(c)]['metanet_a_m']*=factors[2]
    # Exactly ten existing physical FD scalars; no new model, source, or cost.
    restored=copy.deepcopy(conf)
    for c in range(21,26):restored['freeway']['physical_cell_fd']['FW_E'][str(c)]=copy.deepcopy(before['freeway']['physical_cell_fd']['FW_E'][str(c)])
    assert restored==before
    if resumed:assert load(out/'reference_config.json')==conf
    save(out/'reference_config.json',conf)
    doc['sources']['reference_config']=dict(path=(out/'reference_config.json').relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(out/'reference_config.json'))
    save(out/'candidate_manifest.json',doc);ctx=lpr.load_sources(out/'candidate_manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    base_ctx=lpr.load_sources(baseline_manifest);base_net=base_ctx['component']._config('FW_E',base_ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']).network
    net=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']).network
    for c in range(31):
        bp=copy.deepcopy(base_net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c]);p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c]
        if c in range(21,26):bp['rho_crit']*=factors[0 if c<=23 else 1];bp['metanet_a_m']*=factors[2]
        assert bp.keys()==p.keys() and all(abs(bp[k]-p[k])<1e-9 if isinstance(bp[k],(int,float)) else bp[k]==p[k] for k in bp)
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(fit/'selection.json'):sha(fit/'selection.json'),str(reference):sha(reference),str(baseline_manifest):sha(baseline_manifest)}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];cap=HERE/'heldout61_freeway_20260930/capture.json';pins[str(cap)]=sha(cap)
    records += [dict(r,case='s61_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,
        truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
    keys=[('s29_early','none',750),('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750)]+[('s61_late',arm,450) for arm in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]
    ri=root/'route_inventory';pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(reason='Training overall/state loss improved and significant signs remained correct, but aggregate response gate failed. Inspect a fixed tradeoff candidate on unfit states, without changing that failure or refitting.',
        factors=factors,keys=keys,max_new_forecasts=7,coefficient_fits=0,new_native=0,old_training_gate_still_failed=True,
        serialized_changes='10existing FD scalars across cells21-25; all other settings identical',
        scope='Seed61 was previously inspected but never fitted; seed43 previously inspected. No blind qualification or generalization guarantee.',
        interpretation='Same current-only prediction inputs. Source realization differences remain diagnostic uncertainty, not fitted control bonuses.'))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];parity=None
    for case,arm,horizon in keys:
        r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));a,k=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);a=list(a);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert a[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(a[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(a[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));a[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        k.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        predpath=out/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        if resumed and predpath.exists():
            with gzip.open(predpath,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
        else:
            pred=model.rollout(*a,**k)
            with gzip.open(predpath,'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon);assert row['conservation_max']<1e-9
        if case=='s29_early':
            oldp=fit/('eval_'+str(selection['best']['index']).zfill(2))/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(oldp)]=sha(oldp)
            with gzip.open(oldp,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
            errors=[]
            def compare(a,b):
                if isinstance(a,dict):
                    assert a.keys()==b.keys()
                    for key in a:compare(a[key],b[key])
                elif isinstance(a,(list,tuple)):
                    assert len(a)==len(b)
                    for x,y in zip(a,b):compare(x,y)
