                elif isinstance(a,(int,float)) and not isinstance(a,bool):errors.append(abs(a-b))
                else:assert a==b
            for name in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps'):compare(pred[name],old[name])
            parity=max(errors);assert parity<1e-7,parity
        rows.append(row);save(out/(case+'_'+arm+'_result.json'),row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,predicted=row['predicted'],actual=row['actual'])),flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for row in rows:
        if row['arm'] in ('none','hold'):continue
        base=next(z for z in rows if z['case']==row['case'] and z['arm'] in ('none','hold'))
        paired.append(dict(case=row['case'],arm=row['arm'],**{which:{k:row[which][k]-base[which][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')} for which in ('actual','predicted')}))
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,pairs=paired,serialization_max_error=parity,new_forecasts=len(rows),fits=0,training_gate_failed=True,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False,pins=pins))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,pairs=paired,serialization_error=parity)),flush=True)


def audit_native_flux67(resume=False):
    """Two completed FZPs, one scan each: spatial flow vs actual boundaries."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    from bisect import bisect_right
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'flow67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume)
    if resume:
        assert load(out/'failure.json')['completed_sources']==0
        assert not list(out.glob('*_frames.json.gz')) and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    case=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930';runs=Path(load(case/'protocol.json')['results_root'])
    assert load(runs/'task_status.json')['phase']=='native_complete' and not (runs/'STOP').exists()
    runtime_path=root/'route_inventory/compiled.json';runtime=load(runtime_path)
    bounds=runtime['bounds']['FW_E'];lengths=[(b-a)/1000 for a,b in zip(bounds,bounds[1:])]
    assert len(lengths)==31
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP')
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),runtime_path,case/'protocol.json',stop)}
    start,end=2670.1,3120.1;times=[round(start+5*j,6) for j in range(91)]
    arms=('release','release_vsl90');targets=range(14,26);all_blocks=[];checks=[]
    save(out/('retry_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:two exact conditional replays ruled out active receiving caps at14-25.',
        arms=arms,window=[start,end],cells=list(targets),maximum_fzp_scans=2,
        new_native=0,new_forecasts=0,fit=False,pins=pins,
        hypothesis='Determine whether missing VSL discharge response is already missing from N*v/L or appears mainly when spatial averages approximate boundary flow.',
        scope='Retrospective5s snapshot quadrature only. No native future state is used in an autonomous prediction.',
        limitations=['Spatial mean flow need not equal downstream boundary flow during transient storage changes.',
                    'Five-second trapezoids approximate continuous travel distance; front-half values are diagnostic only.',
                    'This previously inspected seed is not a blind calibration holdout.']))
    (out/('helper_resumed.txt' if resume else 'helper_executed.txt')).write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    for arm in arms:
        run=runs/arm/'run';assert load(run/'run.json')['completed'] and not load(run/'run.json')['owned_native_alive']
        source,=(run/'vissim_eval').glob('*.fzp');stat=source.stat()
        truth=ObservationData(case/'observations'/arm);proof=load(truth.folder/'manifest.json')
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','geometry.json'):
            p=truth.folder/name;assert sha(p)==proof['files'][name];pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        geo=load(truth.folder/'geometry.json')
        for c in targets:
            g=next(r for r in geo['cells'] if r['road']=='FW_E' and r['cell']==c)
            assert abs(g['start_m']-bounds[c])<1e-7 and abs(g['length_km']-lengths[c])<1e-9
        receipt_path=case/(arm+'_native.json');receipt=load(receipt_path);pins[str(receipt_path)]=sha(receipt_path)
        frames={t:{} for t in times};header=None;prefix=hashlib.sha256();window=hashlib.sha256();prefix_rows=0;window_rows=0
        seen=set();previous=-1.;tail_rows=0;tail_max_m=0.
        with source.open('rb') as stream:
            for line in stream:
                if header is None:
                    if line.startswith(b'$VEHICLE:'):header={x:i for i,x in enumerate(line.rstrip().split(b':',1)[1].split(b';'))}
                    continue
                if not line.strip():continue
                t=round(float(line.split(b';',1)[0]),6)
                assert t>=previous;previous=t
                if t>end+1e-7:break
                if t<2700:
                    prefix.update(line.rstrip(b'\r\n')+b'\n');prefix_rows+=1
                if t<start-1e-7:continue
                assert t in frames
                seen.add(t);window.update(line.rstrip(b'\r\n')+b'\n');window_rows+=1
                fields=line.rstrip().split(b';');link=int(fields[header[b'LANE\\LINK\\NO']])
                address=runtime['physical'].get(str(link))
                if address is None or address[0]!='FW_E':continue
                x=address[1]+float(fields[header[b'POS']])
                # Match the frozen native observer's end-cell membership.
                # Expose out-of-chain rows; do not pass them to runtime inputs.
                if x>bounds[-1]:tail_rows+=1;tail_max_m=max(tail_max_m,x-bounds[-1])
                cell=min(30,max(0,bisect_right(bounds,x)-1))
                no=int(fields[header[b'NO']]);assert no not in frames[t]
                frames[t][no]=[cell,float(fields[header[b'SPEED']]),x,int(fields[header[b'LANE\\INDEX']]),
                    float(fields[header[b'ACCELERATION']]),float(fields[header[b'DESSPEED']])]
        assert seen==set(times) and prefix.hexdigest()==receipt['prefix']['sha256'] and prefix_rows==receipt['prefix']['rows']
        after=source.stat();assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns)
        moments={};verified=0
        native={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rows in truth.cells.items() for r in rows if r['road']=='FW_E'}
        for t,frame in frames.items():
            grouped=defaultdict(list)
            for r in frame.values():grouped[r[0]].append(r)
            for c in range(31):
                rr=grouped[c];n=len(rr);nv=sum(r[1] for r in rr)
                moments[t,c]=dict(n=n,nv=nv,front_nv=sum(r[1] for r in rr if r[2]>=(bounds[c]+bounds[c+1])/2))
                if (t,c) in native:
                    actual=native[t,c];assert n==actual['n_veh']
                    if n:assert abs(nv/n-actual['v_kmh'])<1e-7
                    verified+=1
        with gzip.open(out/(arm+'_frames.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(dict(fields=['cell','speed_kmh','x_m','lane','acceleration_mps2','desired_speed_kmh'],frames=frames),f,allow_nan=False)
        trace_path=root/'b67'/(arm+'_trace.json.gz');pins[str(trace_path)]=sha(trace_path)
        with gzip.open(trace_path,'rt',encoding='utf-8') as f:trace=json.load(f)['allocations']
        for offset in range(0,450,30):
            t0=round(start+offset,6);t1=round(t0+30,6);stamps=[round(t0+j*5,6) for j in range(7)]
            for c in targets:
                actual=truth.flows[t1,'FW_E',c]
                def integral(key,scale=1.):
                    return sum((moments[a,c][key]+moments[b,c][key])*5/7200/lengths[c]*scale for a,b in zip(stamps,stamps[1:]))
                rr=[r for r in trace if r['cell']==c and t0-1e-7<=r['time_s']<t1-1e-7];assert len(rr)==30
                actual_main=float(actual['downstream_crossings']);actual_off=float(actual['off_departures'])
                all_blocks.append(dict(arm=arm,cell=c,start=t0,end=t1,actual_main=actual_main,actual_off=actual_off,
                    actual_total=actual_main+actual_off,native_spatial=integral('nv'),native_front_half=integral('front_nv',2),
                    model_spatial=sum(r['kinematic_veh'] for r in rr),model_main=sum(r['accepted_veh'] for r in rr),
                    model_off=sum(r['off_accepted_veh'] for r in rr),
                    native_start_n=moments[t0,c]['n'],native_end_n=moments[t1,c]['n']))
        check=dict(arm=arm,source=str(source),bytes=stat.st_size,mtime_ns=stat.st_mtime_ns,
            prefix=receipt['prefix'],window_sha256=window.hexdigest(),window_rows=window_rows,frames=len(frames),native30s_checks=verified,
            tail_beyond_chain_rows=tail_rows,tail_maximum_beyond_m=tail_max_m,tail_policy='Frozen native observer clamps at cell30; target14-25 unaffected; no runtime state injection.')
        checks.append(check);save(out/(arm+'_source.json'),check);print(json.dumps(dict(arm=arm,frames=len(frames),checks=verified)),flush=True)
    groups=[];pairs=[]
    fields=('actual_main','actual_off','actual_total','native_spatial','native_front_half','model_spatial','model_main','model_off')
    for arm in arms:
        for c in targets:
            for block in (None,0,1,2):
                rr=[r for r in all_blocks if r['arm']==arm and r['cell']==c and (block is None or int(round((r['start']-start)/30))//5==block)]
                groups.append(dict(arm=arm,cell=c,block=block,totals={k:sum(r[k] for r in rr) for k in fields},
                    spatial_minus_boundary_rmse=math.sqrt(sum((r['native_spatial']-r['actual_total'])**2 for r in rr)/len(rr))))
    for c in targets:
        for block in (None,0,1,2):
            base=next(r for r in groups if (r['arm'],r['cell'],r['block'])==(arms[0],c,block))
            alt=next(r for r in groups if (r['arm'],r['cell'],r['block'])==(arms[1],c,block))
            pairs.append(dict(cell=c,block=block,delta={k:alt['totals'][k]-base['totals'][k] for k in fields}))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'blocks.json',all_blocks)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=groups,pairs=pairs,checks=checks,pins=pins,new_native=0,new_forecasts=0,new_fit=0,
        source_scans=2,production_adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,pairs=[r for r in pairs if r['block'] is None])),flush=True)


def audit_merge_recovery_balance():
    """Reuse late native frames and exact saved equations; no fit or rollout."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'merge_recovery_balance';assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    lanes=list(net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E']);assert all(abs(lanes[c]-3)<1e-9 for c in range(19,27))
    pins=load(out/'before.json');pins[str(Path(__file__))]=sha(Path(__file__))
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: explanatory answer only; resume with a new bounded cache audit.',
        target_cells=list(range(21,26)),window=[2670.1,2970.1],seeds=[29,43],arms=['none','vsl'],
        new_native=0,new_full_rollouts=0,new_fits=0,new_fzp_scans=0,
        checks=['Recover accepted merge from the actual post-speed term and reconcile30s totals.',
                'Reconstruct mainline sending vs receiving-after-merge and match saved30s crossings.',
                'Compare actual boundary counts with integral of contemporaneous spatial N*v/L.',
                'NC instantaneous current-state factorial: native local, upstream, downstream or all-model; common saved merge.'],
        limits=['Native time integral is a diagnostic using observed future frames, not a prediction input.',
                'Scalar hybrid states are not autonomous or mass-conserving trajectories; no gain qualification.',
                'Native next30s mean change is not an individual acceleration measurement.',
                'No calibration or model change follows automatically from a term magnitude.']))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];blocks=[];balances=[];checks=[];scalar=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        folder=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            record=next(r for r in records if r['case']==f's{seed}_early' and r['arm']==arm)
            truth=ObservationData(record['truth'])
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
            native={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for z in rs if z['road']=='FW_E'}
            tp=root/'source_discharge'/f's{seed}_early_{arm}_terms.json.gz'
            pp=folder/(arm+'.json.gz');fp=root/'recovery_transport'/f's{seed}_{arm}_frames.json.gz'
            for p in (tp,pp,fp):pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            with gzip.open(tp,'rt') as f:terms={(round(z['time_sec'],6),z['cell']):z for z in json.load(f)}
            with gzip.open(pp,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
            with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):z for t,z in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
            pf={(z['window_end_s'],z['cell']):z for z in pred['flows']}
            releases={};max_merge_error=0.;max_flow_error=0.;max_scalar_error=0.
            for j in range(450,750):
                t=round(record['cutoff']+j,6)
                for c in (21,23):
                    z=terms[t,c];p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c]
                    delta=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c)['delta_merge']
                    assert z['speed_final']>net.v_min+1e-8 and z['delta_lanes']==0
                    rate=-z['post_equation_change']*lengths[c]*lanes[c]*(z['rho']+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'])*3600/(delta*z['speed_before'])
                    assert rate>=-1e-7;releases[t,c]=max(0.,rate)
            for offset in range(450,750,30):
                start=round(record['cutoff']+offset,6);end=round(start+30,6)
                for c in range(21,26):
                    flow=pf[end,c];assert flow['off_departures']==0
                    selected=[terms[round(start+j,6),c] for j in range(30)]
                    q_kin=[];q_out=[];receiving=[]
                    for j,z in enumerate(selected):
                        stamp=round(start+j,6);sending=z['rho']*lanes[c]*z['speed_before']
                        room=max(0.,(net.rho_max-z['downstream_rho'])*lengths[c+1]*lanes[c+1]*3600-releases.get((stamp,c+1),0.))
                        q_kin.append(sending/3600);q_out.append(min(sending,room)/3600);receiving.append(room<sending-1e-7)
                    qerror=abs(sum(q_out)-flow['downstream_crossings']);assert qerror<1e-7
                    max_flow_error=max(max_flow_error,qerror)
                    rerror=abs(sum(releases.get((round(start+j,6),c),0.) for j in range(30))/3600-flow['ramp_merges'])
                    assert rerror<1e-7;max_merge_error=max(max_merge_error,rerror)
                    sample=[]
                    for j in range(7):
                        rows=[z for z in frames[round(start+5*j,6)].values() if z[0]==c]
                        sample.append(sum(z[1] for z in rows)/lengths[c])
                    native_q=sum((a+b)*.5*5/3600 for a,b in zip(sample,sample[1:]))
                    actual=truth.flows[end,'FW_E',c]
                    blocks.append(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,cell=c,start=start,end=end,
                        actual_crossings=float(actual['downstream_crossings']),native_integrated_n_v_over_length=native_q,
                        predicted_crossings=flow['downstream_crossings'],predicted_kinematic_crossings=sum(q_kin),
                        receiving_limited_seconds=sum(receiving),predicted_merge=flow['ramp_merges'],actual_merge=float(actual['ramp_merges']),
                        actual_n=native[end,c]['n_veh'],predicted_n=next(z['n_veh'] for z in pred['cells'] if z['cell']==c and z['time_s']==end),
                        actual_v=native[end,c]['v_kmh'],predicted_v=selected[-1]['speed_final']))
                    keys=('speed_before','rho','desired','relaxation','convection','anticipation','post_equation_change')
                    balances.append(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,cell=c,start=start,
                        means={k:sum(z[k] for z in selected)/30 for k in keys},
                        net_speed_change=selected[-1]['speed_final']-selected[0]['speed_before']))
                if arm!='none':continue
                control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110})
                for c in range(21,26):
                    z=terms[start,c];p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c];length=lengths[c];dt=1/3600
                    assert z['delta_lanes']==0
                    vu_model=z['speed_before']+z['convection']*length/(dt*z['speed_before'])
                    for basis in ('model','local','upstream','downstream','all_native'):
                        n=native[start,c];up=native[start,c-1];down=native[start,c+1]
                        local=basis in ('local','all_native')
                        v=n['v_kmh'] if local else z['speed_before'];rho=n['rho_veh_per_km_lane'] if local else z['rho']
                        vu=up['v_kmh'] if basis in ('upstream','all_native') else vu_model
                        rd=down['rho_veh_per_km_lane'] if basis in ('downstream','all_native') else z['downstream_rho']
                        vsl=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',c,cfg)
                        desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,vsl,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                        nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,vsl);spec=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c)
                        tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,rd,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                        relax=dt/tau*(desired-v);conv=dt/length*v*(vu-v)
                        ant=-nu*dt/(tau*length)*(rd-rho)/(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'])
                        merge=spec.get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)*dt*releases.get((start,c),0.)*v/(length*lanes[c]*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                        executed=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(v,vu,rho,rd,desired,dt,length,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'],net.v_min)
                        assert abs(executed-max(net.v_min,v+relax+conv+ant))<1e-8
                        final=max(net.v_min,executed-merge)
                        if basis=='model':
                            error=abs(final-z['speed_final']);assert error<1e-8;max_scalar_error=max(max_scalar_error,error)
                        scalar.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=c,start=start,basis=basis,rho=rho,v=v,desired=desired,downstream_rho=rd,
                            relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,merge_loss=merge,change=final-v,
                            native_next30_change=native[end,c]['v_kmh']-n['v_kmh'],merge_basis='same saved instantaneous accepted flow'))
            checks.append(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,merge30_error_max=max_merge_error,downstream30_error_max=max_flow_error,scalar_parity_error_max=max_scalar_error))
    grouped=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            for c in range(21,26):
                bs=[z for z in blocks if (z['seed'],z['arm'],z['cell'])==(seed,arm,c)]
                ts=[z for z in balances if (z['seed'],z['arm'],z['cell'])==(seed,arm,c)]
                sums={k:sum(z[k] for z in bs) for k in ('actual_crossings','native_integrated_n_v_over_length','predicted_crossings','predicted_kinematic_crossings','receiving_limited_seconds','predicted_merge','actual_merge')}
                grouped.append(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,cell=c,totals=sums,
                    mean_terms={k:sum(z['means'][k] for z in ts)/len(ts) for k in ts[0]['means']},
                    speed_means={k:sum(z[k] for z in bs)/len(bs) for k in ('actual_v','predicted_v')},
                    native_spatial_flow_rmse=math.sqrt(sum((z['native_integrated_n_v_over_length']-z['actual_crossings'])**2 for z in bs)/len(bs))))
    scalar_groups=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        for c in range(21,26):
            for basis in ('model','local','upstream','downstream','all_native'):
                rs=[z for z in scalar if (z['seed'],z['cell'],z['basis'])==(seed,c,basis)]
                scalar_groups.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=c,basis=basis,means={k:sum(z[k] for z in rs)/len(rs) for k in ('rho','v','desired','relaxation','convection','anticipation','merge_loss','change','native_next30_change')}))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',dict(blocks=blocks,balances=balances,scalar=scalar))
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=grouped,scalar_groups=scalar_groups,checks=checks,pins=pins,
        full_forecasts=0,native_runs=0,fit=0,adopted=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,groups=grouped,scalar_groups=[g for g in scalar_groups if g['cell'] in (21,23,24) and g['basis'] in ('model','all_native')]),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def check_nominal_exit_lane_drop(variant='nominal_exit'):
    """One geometry-supported local ablation; preserve dynamic excess lane loss."""
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import configure_state_response
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    assert variant in ('nominal_exit','merge21_half','connected_exit_drop','connected_recovery23','connected_recovery24_25')
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/('exit_lane_drop' if variant=='nominal_exit' else 'recovery_pair' if variant=='connected_recovery24_25' else variant)
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    manifest=(root/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json' if variant in ('connected_exit_drop','connected_recovery23','connected_recovery24_25')
              else root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json')
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    network=HERE/'selected/network/native_seed29.inpx';tree=ET.parse(network)
    links={int(x.get('no')):x for x in tree.findall('.//links/link')}
    def origin(connector):
        e=links[connector];link,lane=map(int,e.find('fromLinkEndPt').get('lane').split())
        return link,set(range(lane,lane+len(e.find('lanes'))))
    main,through=origin(10613);other,off=origin(10481);physical=set(range(1,len(links[main].find('lanes'))+1))
    exiting=physical-through;assert main==other==2 and exiting=={1} and exiting<=off
    nominal=float(len(exiting));old_config=model._config;enabled=False
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(network):sha(network),str(manifest):sha(manifest)}
    for v in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];ri=root/'route_inventory';pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
    if variant=='connected_recovery24_25':
        fit_path=root/'acceleration_balance/recovery_pair_fit.json';pins[str(fit_path)]=sha(fit_path)
        fit=load(fit_path);assert fit['train_seed']==29 and fit['conditional_gate_passed']
        recovery_factor=fit['multiplier'];assert 1<recovery_factor<4
    if variant in ('connected_exit_drop','connected_recovery23','connected_recovery24_25'):
        cap=HERE/'heldout61_freeway_20260930/capture.json';pins[str(cap)]=sha(cap)
        records += [dict(r,case='s61_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,
            truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
    def config(road,parameters):
        cfg=old_config(road,parameters)
        if enabled and road=='FW_E':
            spec=copy.deepcopy(cfg.network.freeway_state_response)
            if variant=='connected_recovery23':
                response=spec['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['23']['anticipation']
                response['downstream_lt_local']=response['downstream_ge_local']
            elif variant=='connected_recovery24_25':
                for cell in (24,25):
                    spec['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]['anticipation']['downstream_lt_local']*=recovery_factor
            elif variant!='merge21_half':
                spec['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['18']['nominal_exit_lanes']=nominal
            else:
                spec['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['21']['delta_merge']*=.5
            configure_state_response(cfg,dict(freeway=dict(state_response=spec)))
            if variant in ('nominal_exit','connected_exit_drop'):assert cfg.network.freeway_state_response['FW_E']['cell_overrides']['18']['nominal_exit_lanes']==1.
        return cfg
    model._config=config
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(hypothesis='The nominal lane ending at10481 is an exiting lane, not a forced merge into the three continuous mainline lanes. Test subtracting only its1lane from local additional speed-loss delta_lanes.',
        geometry=dict(link=main,physical=sorted(physical),continuing=sorted(through),off_connected=sorted(off),nominal_exiting=sorted(exiting)),
        evidence='Recovery cache2670..2970:2268 sampled cell18->downstream movements,1 was in lane1 at previous5s frame. Does not exclude earlier upstream lane changes.',
        candidate='cell18 only: speed-penalty lane reduction=max(0,current effective lane reduction-1). Physical lanes,FD,density gradient,off storage/drain/split and all other cells stay unchanged.',
        fit=False,max_new_forecasts=13,new_native=0,
        train_gate='NC750 first450 local15-20 loss<=110%,last300<=90%,whole speed RMSE<=baseline+2. If failed,stop after2rollouts.',
        validation='If NC passes:29early/43early four arms and29late four arms. EarlyNC/VSL750,other450. No refit on action results.',
        limitations='Structural ablation, not proof all geometric lane drops should be removed, not a capacity bonus, not production adoption.'))
    target_cells=range(15,21)
    if variant=='connected_exit_drop':
        target_cells=range(15,26)
        save(out/'protocol.json',dict(hypothesis='Prior nominal-exit ablation overloaded the biased downstream recovery region. With that region FD recalibrated, test the same geometry distinction jointly, without another coefficient grid.',
            baseline='connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json: frozen tradeoff candidate,not production qualified',
            candidate='Only cell18 additional speed-loss delta_lanes=max(0,effective loss-1physical exit lane). Physical density,receiving,routing,off storage and excess spillback unchanged.',
            geometry=dict(link=main,physical=sorted(physical),continuing=sorted(through),off_connected=sorted(off),nominal_exiting=sorted(exiting)),
            max_new_forecasts=7,fit=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,
            cases='disabled750 parity;29NC/VSL750,43NC/VSL750,61hold/release450',
            gate='NC local15-25 first450 loss<=110%,tail300<=90%,whole-speed RMSE<=baseline+2. All action pairs inspected even if this gate fails, to isolate mechanism; no automatic adoption.',
            acceptance='Conservation/exact disabled arrays; preserve significant RM direction, reduce VSL intermediate-flow overresponse and inspect paired costs,not speed alone.',
            limitations='Previously inspected states. Recalibrated FD itself failed aggregate training response gate; this compound ablation does not waive that failure. No future measurements in forecast.'))
    if variant=='merge21_half':
        target_cells=range(21,26)
        save(out/'protocol.json',dict(hypothesis='Isolate the cell21 merge-loss coefficient from other calibrated terms; receiving never binds in the saved tail, local observed states expose excessive negative drift.',
            evidence='merge_recovery_balance/summary.json',candidate='delta_merge21 multiplied by0.5; all other coefficients/physical flow/route/VSL/port/source settings unchanged.',
            factor=.5,coefficient_fit=False,scope='mechanism ablation, not an estimated optimum',max_new_forecasts=13,new_native=0,
            train_gate='NC750 cell21-25 first450 normalized N/v/q loss<=110%,last300<=90%; whole speed RMSE<=baseline+2.',
            validation='Only if training gate passes: seed29late4 arms,29earlyRM/both,43early4; significant response signs and masses; never adopt based on NC recovery alone.',
            limitations='Previously inspected seeds; no pristine blind holdout. Future measurements enter scoring only. Existing 450s MPC unchanged.'))
    if variant=='connected_recovery23':
        target_cells=range(21,26)
        save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:source crossed2x2, density interface path diagnosis and rejected history-arrival candidate;10new forecasts.',
            hypothesis='Persistently congested merge/recovery23 may transmit high density upstream because its recovery-branch anticipation coefficient is much lower than its own other branch and adjacent cells.',
            evidence='Saved750 NC tail has recovery branch active300/300 seconds at23, nuLT4.644838 versus nuGE18.375; conditional21 pressure in prior4 diagnoses greatly improved19 NC tail but not all onset.',
            baseline='Frozen connected-FD candidate, still not production-qualified; all FD/routing/storage parameters retained.',
            candidate='Only cell23 anticipation downstream_lt_local=its existing downstream_ge_local18.375; old value4.644838002682434. No state/control/time-specific reward and no capacity or merge-flow change.',
            coefficient_fit=False,max_new_forecasts=15,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,
            train_gate='Same NC750: cell21-25 first450 N/v/q loss<=110%,tail300<=90%; whole speed RMSE<=baseline+2. Failed gate stops after2forecasts.',
            validation='Only if gate passes:29/43early VSL750 and RM/both450,43NC750,29late4arms450,61latehold/release450. Report large action signs, discharge response, waits and stocks before any adoption.',
            limitations='This is a single mechanism candidate, not an optimum. Local recovery improvement cannot qualify full Omega or SDMPC; previous failed FD gate is not waived. No future observations in rollout.'))
    if variant=='connected_recovery24_25':
        target_cells=range(21,26)
        save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: sequencing explanation; resumed with600conditional scalar checks and an observed-state regional fit.',
            hypothesis='The24-25 local speed balance drifts downward even with native neighbors, so strengthen only its existing downstream-lower recovery branch.',
            fit=str(fit_path),multiplier=recovery_factor,train_seed=29,train_cells=[24,25],
            fit_target='Twenty30s-average native spatial derivative residuals; one least-squares common multiplier, not individual acceleration fitting.',
            baseline='Frozen connected-FD, unqualified; same FD/merge/flows/storage/sources and other cell coefficients.',
            candidate='Scale only24/25 downstream_lt_local nu; branch chosen from predicted states. No control/seed/time bonus.',
            max_new_forecasts=7,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,
            train_gate='NC750 cells21-25 first450 loss<=110%,tail300<=90%,whole speed RMSE<=baseline+2.',
            response_checks='If NC gate passes:29VSL750,43NC/VSL750,61hold/release450. Future native states are never rollout inputs.',
            limits='Previously inspected43 is diagnostic transfer, not pristine holdout. This stage cannot waive the existing response failure or qualify production.'))
    exe=out/'executed';exe.mkdir()
    for n in ('freeway_fd.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py'):(exe/(n+'.txt')).write_bytes((ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/n).read_bytes())
    (exe/'helper.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def record(case,arm):return next(r for r in records if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm)
    def run(r,horizon,name):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        last=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1])
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(last);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if r['case']=='s29_late' else ri/r['case']/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        if variant=='connected_recovery23' and name=='baseline':
            from diagnostics.sdmpc_n31_20260924.integration_20260926.probe_selected_arrival_path import observe_first_interval_speed_terms
            terms=[]
            with observe_first_interval_speed_terms(r['cutoff'],terms,horizon_sec=horizon):pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
            with gzip.open(out/'baseline_terms.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(terms,f,allow_nan=False)
        else:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        with gzip.open(out/(name+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
        for n in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/n)]=sha(truth.folder/n)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        assert not row['score']['invalid'] and row['conservation_max']<1e-9
        ac={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for z in rs if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        pf={(z['window_end_s'],z['cell']):z for z in pred['flows']};losses={'first450':[],'tail300':[]}
        for z in pred['cells']:
            if z['cell'] not in target_cells:continue
            t,c=z['time_s'],z['cell'];a=ac[t,c];q=truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]
            group=losses['first450' if t<=r['cutoff']+450+1e-7 else 'tail300']
            group.extend([((z['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])/10)**2,((z['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])/20)**2,((pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-float(q['downstream_crossings']))/(1000/120))**2])
        row['local_losses']={k:sum(v)/len(v) if v else None for k,v in losses.items()}
        save(out/(name+'.json'),row);print(json.dumps(dict(name=name,losses=row['local_losses'],predicted=row['predicted'])),flush=True)
        return pred,row
    count=0
    try:
        nc=record('s29_early','none');pred,base=run(nc,750,'baseline');count+=1
        p=(root/'connected_fd/review/s29_early_none.json.gz' if variant in ('connected_exit_drop','connected_recovery23','connected_recovery24_25')
           else root/'vsl_native_exposure/horizon750/none.json.gz');pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
        parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(pred[k]))==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        enabled=True;pred,candidate=run(nc,750,'s29_early_none');count+=1
        guards=dict(early=candidate['local_losses']['first450']<=1.1*base['local_losses']['first450'],
                    tail=candidate['local_losses']['tail300']<=.9*base['local_losses']['tail300'],
                    speed=candidate['score']['speed']['rmse']<=base['score']['speed']['rmse']+2)
        selected=all(guards.values());save(out/'selection.json',dict(selected=selected,guards=guards,parity=parity,production_adopted=False))
        rows=[candidate]
        cases=[('s29_early','vsl',750),('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750)]+[
                ('s29_late',a,450) for a in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]+[
                (c,a,450) for c in ('s29_early','s43_early') for a in ('rm','both')]
        if variant in ('connected_exit_drop','connected_recovery24_25'):cases=[('s29_early','vsl',750),('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750),('s61_late','hold',450),('s61_late','release',450)]
        if variant=='connected_recovery23':cases += [('s61_late','hold',450),('s61_late','release',450)]
        if selected or variant=='connected_exit_drop':
            for case,arm,horizon in cases:
                _,row=run(record(case,arm),horizon,case+'_'+arm);count+=1;rows.append(row)
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'completion.json',dict(new_rollouts=count,selected=selected,rows=rows,pins=pins,goal_qualified=False,production_adopted=False,new_native=0))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,rollouts=count,selected=selected)),flush=True)
    finally:model._config=old_config


def calibrate_bounded_recovery():
    """Finite NC-only fit of one local speed-law alternative, then frozen actions."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.optimize import least_squares
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import configure_state_response, bounded_virtual_density
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'bounded_recovery';assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    ri=parent/'route_inventory';ve=parent/'vsl_native_exposure'
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for v in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
    old_config=model._config;active=None;calls=[];saved={};native={};new_count=0
    def config(road,parameters):
        cfg=old_config(road,parameters)
        if active is not None and road=='FW_E':
            state=copy.deepcopy(cfg.network.freeway_state_response)
            assert not set(range(15,21)) & set(cfg.network.ramp_merge_segment_index.values())
            for cell in range(15,21):state['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]['bounded_density']=active
            configure_state_response(cfg,dict(freeway=dict(state_response=state)))
            for cell in range(15,21):assert cfg.network.freeway_state_response['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]['bounded_density']==active
        return cfg
    model._config=config
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: scalar equation cause isolation and two rejected phi candidates, report preserved.',
        source='https://arxiv.org/html/2503.17680v1',equations=[13,14,16],
        hypothesis='Large opposing velocity terms can lock cell15-20 into slow states; replace this local speed law by bounded virtual-density relaxation without adding capacity.',
        scope='FW_E cells15-20 only, no on-ramp cells. Other cells/actual merge/routing/storage/drainage/receiving/physical conservation and VSL exposure unchanged.',
        caveat='Local hybrid using existing cell FD and VSL exposure, not whole-road reproduction of the paper. Native v_min and outlet receiving-speed cap retained. No convection or original anticipation/lane-drop added in selected cells.',
        fit='seed29 NC2220.1..2970.1 only; N/10,v/20,downstream30s counts/(1000/120); four shared parameters, existing FD frozen.',
        initial=[12.,.5,150.,.5],bounds=[[5.,0.,1.,0.],[80.,1.,300.,1.]],parameter_names=['tau_sec','eta','kappa','phi'],
        max_optimizer_nfev=3,max_fit_rollouts=15,max_new_rollouts=27,
        train_gate='Tail300 local loss >=10% lower, first450 <=10% worse, whole-road speed RMSE <=2km/h worse.',
        validation='Only if train gate passes: frozen29early/43early four arms plus29late four arms; earlyNC/VSL750 and others450. These are previously inspected states, not pristine blind.',
        no_future_observations=True,production_adopted=False,new_native=0)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol)
    exe=out/'executed';exe.mkdir()
    for n in ('freeway_fd.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py'):(exe/(n+'.txt')).write_bytes((ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/n).read_bytes())
    (exe/'helper.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    # Physical-domain and formula examples before any autonomous forecast.
    spec=dict(tau_sec=12.,eta=.5,kappa=150.,phi=.5)
    h=40+.5*150/190*40;expected=h+.5*(1/4)*(40/180)*(80/120)*(180-h)
    assert abs(bounded_virtual_density(spec,40,80,80,120,180,4,3)-expected)<1e-12
    checks=1
    for rho in (0.,30.,180.):
        for down in (0.,90.,180.):
            for speed in (0.,60.,120.):
                v=bounded_virtual_density(spec,rho,down,speed,120,180,4,3)
                assert 0<=v<=180;checks+=1
    for bad in (-1.,181.):
        try:bounded_virtual_density(spec,bad,40,80,120,180,4,3)
        except ValueError:checks+=1
        else:raise AssertionError('Invalid density accepted')
    def record(case,arm):return next(r for r in records if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm)
    def prepare(r,horizon):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        last=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1])
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(last);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if r['case']=='s29_late' else ri/r['case']/'initial_raw.json'
        pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage';return args,kw
    def measurement(pred,r,horizon):
        if r['case']+'_'+r['arm'] not in native:
            truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);native[r['case']+'_'+r['arm']]=truth
            for n in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/n)]=sha(truth.folder/n)
        truth=native[r['case']+'_'+r['arm']]
        if horizon!=pred['diagnostics']['horizon_s']:
            end=r['cutoff']+horizon;fields={'cells':'time_s','flows':'window_end_s','ports':'time_s','ramps':'end_sec'}
            pred={**pred,**{k:[x for x in pred[k] if x[s]<=end+1e-7] for k,s in fields.items()}}
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        ac={(t,x['cell']):x for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for x in rs if x['road']=='FW_E'}
        pf={(x['window_end_s'],x['cell']):x for x in pred['flows']};res=[];early=[];tail=[]
        for x in pred['cells']:
            if x['cell'] not in range(15,21):continue
            t,c=x['time_s'],x['cell'];a=ac[t,c];q=truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]
            z=[(x['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])/10.,(x['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])/20.,
               (pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-float(q['downstream_crossings']))/(1000/120.)]
            res.extend(z);(early if t<=r['cutoff']+450+1e-7 else tail).extend(z)
        row['local_losses']={k:float(np.mean(np.square(v))) if v else None for k,v in [('first450',early),('tail300',tail),('all',res)]}
        return row,np.array(res)
    def run(r,horizon,folder):
        nonlocal new_count
        args,kw=prepare(r,horizon);pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw);new_count+=1
        row,res=measurement(pred,r,horizon);assert not row['score']['invalid'] and row['conservation_max']<1e-9
        folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        stem=r['case']+'_'+r['arm']
        with gzip.open(folder/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        save(folder/(stem+'.json'),row);return pred,row,res
    try:
        nc=record('s29_early','none');pred,baseline,_=run(nc,750,out/'baseline')
        with gzip.open(ve/'horizon750/none.json.gz','rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
        parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(pred[k]))==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        def evaluate(x):
            nonlocal active
            key=tuple(float(a) for a in x)
            if key in saved:return saved[key][3]
            assert len(calls)<15,'Fit forecast budget exhausted'
            active=dict(zip(protocol['parameter_names'],key));folder=out/f'e{len(calls):02}'
            pred,row,res=run(nc,750,folder);save(folder/'parameters.json',active)
            entry=dict(parameters=active,folder=folder.name,local_losses=row['local_losses'],speed_rmse=row['score']['speed']['rmse'])
            calls.append(entry);saved[key]=(pred,row,folder,res)
            print(json.dumps(dict(fit=len(calls),**entry)),flush=True);return res
        result=least_squares(evaluate,protocol['initial'],bounds=protocol['bounds'],max_nfev=3,x_scale=[12.,.5,150.,.5],diff_step=.03)
        best=min(calls,key=lambda r:r['local_losses']['all']);key=tuple(best['parameters'][n] for n in protocol['parameter_names'])
        pred,row,folder,_=saved[key]
        guards=dict(tail=row['local_losses']['tail300']<=.9*baseline['local_losses']['tail300'],
                    early=row['local_losses']['first450']<=1.1*baseline['local_losses']['first450'],
                    whole_speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=baseline['score']['speed']['rmse']+2.)
        selected=best if all(guards.values()) else None
        save(out/'selection.json',dict(baseline=baseline,fit=calls,selected=selected,guards=guards,
             optimizer=dict(nfev=result.nfev,njev=result.njev,status=result.status,message=result.message),
             fit_converged=result.status>0,selected_before_action_validation=True,adopted=False))
        validation=[]
        if selected:
            active=selected['parameters'];save(out/'candidate.json',active)
            validation.append(measurement(pred,nc,450)[0]);dest=out/'validation';dest.mkdir()
            todo=[('s29_early','vsl',750),('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750)]+[
                ('s29_late',a,450) for a in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]+[
                (c,a,450) for c in ('s29_early','s43_early') for a in ('rm','both')]
            for case,arm,horizon in todo:
                r=record(case,arm);p,_,_=run(r,horizon,dest);v=measurement(p,r,450)[0];validation.append(v)
                print(json.dumps(dict(validation=case,arm=arm,actual=v['actual'],predicted=v['predicted'])),flush=True)
            for v in validation:
                base=next(r for r in validation if r['case']==v['case'] and r['arm'] in ('none','hold'))
                v['delta']={s:{k:v[s][k]-base[s][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','exits')} for s in ('actual','predicted')}
            save(out/'validation.json',dict(rows=validation,fit_seeds=[29],adopted=False))
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'completion.json',dict(new_rollouts=new_count,fit_rollouts=len(calls),max_rollouts=27,parity=parity,
             formula_checks=checks,selected=selected,validation_rows=len(validation),pins=pins,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False,new_native=0))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,rollouts=new_count,selected=selected)),flush=True)
    finally:model._config=old_config


def calibrate_freeway_drop_recovery():
    """Bounded existing-phi fit to NC recovery; then frozen action checks."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=parent/'drop_recovery';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);ri=parent/'route_inventory';ve=parent/'vsl_native_exposure'
    manifest=parent.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';document=load(manifest);ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json')}
    for v in document['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/v['path'])]=sha(ROOT/v['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    protocol=dict(previous_phi_zero_ablation=str(HERE/'lane_drop_response/summary.json'),
        justification='Current expanded036/current-route model and newly observed NC750 recovery; previous phi0 experiment used older delta1/E4 and failed RM cost. Not repeating phi0.',
        candidates=[1.5,1.],baseline_phi=3.,train='seed29 NC only,2220.1..2970.1; no control-effect objective in fitting',
        changes='Existing directional lane_drop_phi parameter only in FW_E; all physical lane counts/off storage/routing/merge coefficients unchanged. This also affects dynamic lane-loss deceleration in FW_E, not cell18 only.',
        train_loss='Mean squared cell15-20 errors in N/10veh, v/20kmh,30s downstream count/(1000/120)veh; before450 and last300 separately.',
        train_gate='Tail loss at least10% lower; first450 local loss no more than10% higher; whole-road speed RMSE no more than2km/h worse.',
        validation='If train passes, freeze lower-tail-loss phi and check29 early four arms,29late four arms,43early four arms. Existing inspected seed43 not a blind holdout.',
        max_new_forecasts=14,fit_seed=29,excluded_fit_seeds=[43,61],future_inputs=False,new_native=0,
        stop='Only two phi candidates. No refinement grid. No adoption from NC-only fit; preserve meaningful RM/VSL response directions and assess flow/queue costs.')
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def get_record(case,arm):return next(r for r in records if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm)
    def prepare(record,phi,horizon):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        args[2]['by_direction']['FW_E']['lane_drop_phi']=phi
        original=copy.deepcopy(args[1])
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(original[-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(record['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(record['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if record['case']=='s29_late' else ri/record['case']/'initial_raw.json'
        pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        cfg=model._config('FW_E',args[2]['by_direction']['FW_E'])
        assert cfg.network.freeway_lane_drop_phi==phi
        assert not (getattr(cfg.network,'metanet_parameters_by_direction',{}) or {}).get('FW_E',{}).get('freeway_lane_drop_phi'), 'Direction override would mask this test'
        return args,kw
    def measure(pred,record,horizon):
        if pred['diagnostics']['horizon_s'] != horizon:
            end=record['cutoff']+horizon
            fields={'cells':'time_s','flows':'window_end_s','ports':'time_s','ramps':'end_sec'}
            pred={**pred,**{key:[r for r in pred[key] if r[stamp]<=end+1e-7] for key,stamp in fields.items()}}
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth'])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):
            p=truth.folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        ac={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for r in rs if r['road']=='FW_E'}
        losses={'first450':[],'tail300':[]};t0=record['cutoff']
        pf={(r['window_end_s'],r['cell']):r for r in pred['flows']}
        for p in pred['cells']:
            if not 15<=p['cell']<=20 or p['time_s']>t0+horizon+1e-7:continue
            t,c=p['time_s'],p['cell'];a=ac[t,c];af=truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]
            target=losses['first450' if t<=t0+450+1e-7 else 'tail300']
            target.extend([((p['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])/10.)**2,((p['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])/20.)**2,
                           ((pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-float(af['downstream_crossings']))/(1000/120.))**2])
        row['local_losses']={k:sum(v)/len(v) if v else None for k,v in losses.items()};return row
    def run(record,phi,horizon,folder):
        args,kw=prepare(record,phi,horizon);pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        with gzip.open(folder/(record['case']+'_'+record['arm']+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        row=measure(pred,record,horizon);save(folder/(record['case']+'_'+record['arm']+'.json'),row)
        return pred,row
    nc=get_record('s29_early','none');path=ve/'horizon750/none.json.gz';pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
    with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:baseline_pred=json.load(f)
    args,kw=prepare(nc,3.,750);parity_pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
    parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(parity_pred[k]))==baseline_pred[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
    baseline=measure(baseline_pred,nc,750);save(out/'baseline.json',baseline);training=[];saved={}
    for label,phi in (('phi15',1.5),('phi10',1.)):
        folder=out/label;folder.mkdir();candidate_params=copy.deepcopy(ctx['parameters']);candidate_params['by_direction']['FW_E']['lane_drop_phi']=phi
        pp=folder/'parameters.json';save(pp,candidate_params);candidate_manifest=copy.deepcopy(document)
        candidate_manifest['sources']['parameters']=dict(path=pp.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(pp))
        candidate_manifest['qualification']='Unqualified NC-recovery candidate; action response validation required.'
        save(folder/'manifest.json',candidate_manifest)
        pred,row=run(nc,phi,750,folder);saved[label]=(pred,row)
        guards=dict(tail=row['local_losses']['tail300']<=.9*baseline['local_losses']['tail300'],
                    early=row['local_losses']['first450']<=1.1*baseline['local_losses']['first450'],
                    whole_speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=baseline['score']['speed']['rmse']+2.)
        training.append(dict(candidate=label,phi=phi,local_losses=row['local_losses'],guards=guards,train_pass=all(guards.values())))
        print(json.dumps(training[-1]),flush=True)
    passed=[r for r in training if r['train_pass']];selected=min(passed,key=lambda r:r['local_losses']['tail300']) if passed else None
    save(out/'selection.json',dict(training=training,selected=selected,baseline=baseline['local_losses'],parity=parity,
                                 selection_before_action_validation=True,adopted=False))
    new_count=3;validation=[]
    if selected:
        label,phi=selected['candidate'],selected['phi'];folder=out/'validation';folder.mkdir()
        # A450 prefix of the selected750 NC is reused, not forecast again.
        validation.append(measure(saved[label][0],nc,450))
        for case,arm,horizon in [('s29_early',a,450) for a in ('rm','vsl','both')]+[
            ('s29_late',a,450) for a in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]+[
            ('s43_early',a,450) for a in ('none','rm','vsl','both')]:
            record=get_record(case,arm);pred,row=run(record,phi,horizon,folder);new_count+=1;validation.append(row)
            print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,predicted=row['predicted'],actual=row['actual'])),flush=True)
        for row in validation:
            base=next(r for r in validation if r['case']==row['case'] and r['arm'] in ('none','hold'))
            row['delta']={label:{k:row[label][k]-base[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','exits')} for label in ('actual','predicted')}
        save(out/'validation.json',dict(rows=validation,selected_before_validation=selected,seed43_fitted=False,adopted=False))
    for p,digest in pins.items():assert sha(p)==digest,p
    save(out/'completion.json',dict(new_rollouts=new_count,max_budget=14,selected=selected,parity=parity,pins=pins,
        native_runs=0,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False,validation_rows=len(validation)))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_rollouts=new_count,selected=selected)),flush=True)


def audit_freeway_choice_ranking(fresh_seed=None):
    """Decision regret at a common horizon; do not turn it into an Omega gate."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    assert fresh_seed in (None,67)
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/('choice450' if fresh_seed is None else 'choice67');out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    ri=root/'route_inventory';records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];cap=HERE/'heldout61_freeway_20260930/capture.json'
    records += [dict(r,case='s61_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
    cases={case:(('none','rm','vsl','both') if case.endswith('early') else ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')) for case in ('s29_early','s43_early','s29_late','s61_late')}
    manifests={'route036':root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json',
        'connected_fd':root/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json','recovery_pair':root/'recovery_pair/review/candidate_manifest.json'}
    if fresh_seed is not None:
        from diagnostics.sdmpc_n31_20260924.integration_20260926.heldout53_response_v2 import postprocess
        case_dir=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930';postprocess.configure_case(case_dir);native_protocol=postprocess.closed()
        for path,digest in native_protocol['comparison_pins'].items():assert sha(path)==digest,path
        cap=case_dir/'capture.json'
        records=[dict(r,case='s67_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(case_dir/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
        cases={'s67_late':('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')}
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json')}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:600conditional checks and18forecasts; recovery candidate improves state but worsens calibration response loss.',
        rationale='User prioritizes large physical directions and decisions over perfect trajectories. Quantify finite-menu choice regret separately, without waiving failed calibration or declaring full qualification.',
        cases=cases,horizon_sec=450,models={k:str(v) for k,v in manifests.items()},new_forecast_budget=4 if fresh_seed is None else 12,new_fits=0,new_native=0,
        reuse='44existing predictions; fill only4missing connected-FD arms. Truncate750s records at450s before all stocks/flows/costs.' if fresh_seed is None else 'New seed67; all three models were pinned before native execution.12predictions,no refit.',
        areas=['FW_E31mainline','FW_E31mainline+4on+4offconnector'],
        metrics=['Chosen action actual cost minus actual finite-menu minimum','Regret relative to actual baseline TTT','Chosen actual gain versus baseline','Predicted versus actual command order'],
        no_acceptance_threshold=fresh_seed is None,
        fresh_seed_thresholds=dict(mainline_delta_veh_h=.5,choice_regret_veh_h=.5,flow_delta_veh=10) if fresh_seed is not None else None,
        limitations=['Finite historical menu, not an optimizer, feasible-set certificate, or global optimum.',
                    'No full Omega/urban costs, SDMPC prices/caps/proximal costs or actuator trust-region qualification.',
                    'Observed seeds already inspected; no claim of a pristine holdout.' if fresh_seed is None else 'Seed67 coefficients frozen before native traffic; no fitting or candidate revision from its results.',
                    'Failed state/response/calibration criteria remain reported separately.']))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];forecasts=0;cached=0;initial={}
    for version,manifest in manifests.items():
        ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component'];pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
        for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
        for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
        for case,arms in cases.items():
            for arm in arms:
                r=next(r for r in records if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm);a,k=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);a=list(a);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
                if case in initial:assert a[0]==initial[case]
                else:initial[case]=copy.deepcopy(a[0])
                assert a[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(a[1])==450
                if version=='route036':
                    path=(ri if case=='s29_late' else ri/case)/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz')
                elif version=='connected_fd':
                    path=(root/'connected_fd/eval_20' if case.startswith('s29') and arm in ('none','vsl','rm','both','hold','release') else root/'connected_fd/review')/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
                else:
                    path=root/'recovery_pair/review'/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
                    if not path.exists():path=root/'recovery_pair'/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
                if path.exists() and fresh_seed is None:
                    pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
                    with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
                    cached+=1
                else:
                    assert fresh_seed is not None or (version=='connected_fd' and (case,arm) in (('s43_early','rm'),('s43_early','both'),('s29_late','hold_vsl90'),('s29_late','release_vsl90')))
                    raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
                    k.update(horizon_sec=450,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
                    pred=model.rollout(*a,**k);forecasts+=1
                    path=out/((version+'_' if fresh_seed is not None else '')+case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
                    with gzip.open(path,'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
                end=r['cutoff']+450
                for name,key in (('cells','time_s'),('flows','window_end_s'),('ports','time_s'),('ramps','end_sec')):
                    pred[name]=[z for z in pred[name] if z[key]<=end+1e-7]
                truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
                for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
                row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=450);row.update(model=version,horizon_sec=450,source=str(path))
                if fresh_seed is not None:
                    actual_flows=[v for (t,road,c),v in truth.flows.items() if road=='FW_E' and r['cutoff']<t<=end]
                    quantities={which:{'merge_'+rid:value for rid,value in row[which]['merges'].items()} for which in ('actual','predicted')}
                    for c in range(31):
                        quantities['actual']['cell_'+str(c)+'_down']=sum(float(v['downstream_crossings']) for v in actual_flows if int(v['cell'])==c)
                        quantities['predicted']['cell_'+str(c)+'_down']=sum(v['downstream_crossings'] for v in pred['flows'] if v['cell']==c)
                    for name,actual_name in (('source_admissions','source_admissions'),('off_departures','off_departures'),('terminal_exits','terminal_exits_inferred')):
                        quantities['actual'][name]=sum(float(v[actual_name]) for v in actual_flows)
                        quantities['predicted'][name]=sum(v[name] for v in pred['flows'])
                    row['flow_quantities']=quantities
                    row['native_accounting']={name:sum(float(v[name]) for v in actual_flows) for name in ('unexplained_entries','unexplained_losses','native_removals')}
                assert not row['score']['invalid'] and row['conservation_max']<1e-9
                rows.append(row)
    assert (forecasts,cached)==((4,44) if fresh_seed is None else (12,0))
    choices=[]
    for case,arms in cases.items():
        for metric in ('mainline_ttt','ttt'):
            actual={r['arm']:r['actual'][metric] for r in rows if r['model']=='route036' and r['case']==case}
            best=min(arms,key=actual.get);baseline=arms[0]
            for version in manifests:
                rr=[r for r in rows if r['model']==version and r['case']==case]
                assert {r['arm']:r['actual'][metric] for r in rr}==actual
                predicted={r['arm']:r['predicted'][metric] for r in rr};selected=min(arms,key=predicted.get)
                regret=actual[selected]-actual[best];gain=actual[baseline]-actual[selected];opportunity=actual[baseline]-actual[best]
                choices.append(dict(case=case,area=metric,model=version,baseline=baseline,actual_best=best,selected=selected,
                    predicted_order=sorted(arms,key=predicted.get),actual_order=sorted(arms,key=actual.get),
                    predicted_costs=predicted,actual_costs=actual,actual_regret_veh_h=regret,
                    actual_regret_pct_baseline=100*regret/actual[baseline],actual_selected_gain_veh_h=gain,
                    actual_available_gain_veh_h=opportunity,captured_gain_fraction=gain/opportunity if opportunity>1e-9 else None,
                    predicted_selected_gain_veh_h=predicted[baseline]-predicted[selected]))
    pair_checks=[]
    if fresh_seed is not None:
        for version in manifests:
            group=[r for r in rows if r['model']==version]
            for index,a in enumerate(group):
                for b in group[index+1:]:
                    actual=b['actual']['mainline_ttt']-a['actual']['mainline_ttt'];predicted=b['predicted']['mainline_ttt']-a['predicted']['mainline_ttt']
                    flows=[]
                    for name in a['flow_quantities']['actual']:
                        da=b['flow_quantities']['actual'][name]-a['flow_quantities']['actual'][name]
                        dp=b['flow_quantities']['predicted'][name]-a['flow_quantities']['predicted'][name]
                        flows.append(dict(quantity=name,actual_delta=da,predicted_delta=dp,meaningful=abs(da)>=10,sign_matches=da*dp>0 if abs(da)>=10 else None))
                    pair_checks.append(dict(model=version,reference=a['arm'],candidate=b['arm'],actual_main_delta=actual,predicted_main_delta=predicted,
                        meaningful=abs(actual)>=.5,sign_matches=actual*predicted>0 if abs(actual)>=.5 else None,flows=flows))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,choices=choices,pair_checks=pair_checks,new_forecasts=forecasts,reused_forecasts=cached,pins=pins,
        failed_calibration_gates_unchanged=True,production_adopted=False,full_omega_validated=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_forecasts=forecasts,choices=choices),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def review_recovery_pair():
    """Serialize the frozen regional coefficient and check the remaining actions."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';prior=root/'recovery_pair';out=prior/'review'
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);ri=root/'route_inventory';factor=load(root/'acceleration_balance/recovery_pair_fit.json')['multiplier']
    assert load(prior/'completion.json')['selected']
    source=root/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json';doc=load(source);reference=ROOT/doc['sources']['reference_config']['path']
    conf=load(reference);old=copy.deepcopy(conf)
    for cell in (24,25):conf['freeway']['state_response']['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]['anticipation']['downstream_lt_local']*=factor
    restored=copy.deepcopy(conf)
    for cell in (24,25):restored['freeway']['state_response']['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]=old['freeway']['state_response']['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(cell)]
    assert restored==old
    save(out/'reference_config.json',conf)
    doc['sources']['reference_config']=dict(path=(out/'reference_config.json').relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(out/'reference_config.json'))
    doc['qualification']='Frozen recovery24-25 regional candidate; not production or full Omega qualified.'
    save(out/'candidate_manifest.json',doc);ctx=lpr.load_sources(out/'candidate_manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];cap=HERE/'heldout61_freeway_20260930/capture.json'
    records += [dict(r,case='s61_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(cap)['records']]
    existing=[('s29_early','none',750),('s29_early','vsl',750),('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750),('s61_late','hold',450),('s61_late','release',450)]
    new=[(c,a,450) for c in ('s29_early','s43_early') for a in ('rm','both')]+[('s29_late',a,450) for a in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]+[('s61_late',a,450) for a in ('hold_vsl90','release_vsl90')]
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(source):sha(source),str(reference):sha(reference),str(ri/'contract.json'):sha(ri/'contract.json')}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(frozen_multiplier=factor,serialized_changes='Only two downstream-lower anticipation coefficients at24/25.',
        new_cases=new,existing_cases=existing,new_forecast_budget=11,coefficient_searches=0,new_native=0,
        checks=['One exact serialization parity forecast.','Same-horizon NC/RM/VSL/both, late four-command rankings, and independent-seed response.',
                'Conservation, absolute N/v/q and actual accepted merges; ramp/off wait costs retained.'],
        qualification='Freeway/component response only; not full Omega or SDMPC acceptance. Existing states previously inspected; no pristine holdout.'))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];cached={};recorded={};initial={};count=0
    for case,arm,horizon in existing+new:
        r=next(r for r in records if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm);a,k=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);a=list(a);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert a[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(a[1])==450
        if case in initial:assert a[0]==initial[case],f'Initial freeway state differs in {case}'
        else:initial[case]=copy.deepcopy(a[0])
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(a[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));a[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        k.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));k['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        is_existing=(case,arm,horizon) in existing
        if is_existing:
            path=prior/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
        if not is_existing or (case,arm)==('s29_early','none'):
            computed=model.rollout(*a,**k);count+=1
            if is_existing:
                parity={key:json.loads(json.dumps(computed[key]))==pred[key] for key in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
                save(out/'serialization_parity.json',parity)
            pred=computed;path=out/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
            with gzip.open(path,'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth'])
        for n in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/n)]=sha(truth.folder/n)
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon);row['horizon_sec']=horizon
        assert not row['score']['invalid'] and row['conservation_max']<1e-9
        rows.append(row);cached[case,arm]=pred;recorded[case,arm]=(r,truth)
        save(out/(case+'_'+arm+'_result.json'),row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,reused=is_existing,predicted=row['predicted'],actual=row['actual'])),flush=True)
    pairs=[];prefix_rows=[]
    for row in rows:
        if row['arm'] in ('none','hold'):continue
        case=row['case'];base_arm='none' if case.endswith('early') else 'hold'
        base=next(z for z in rows if z['case']==case and z['arm']==base_arm)
        if base['horizon_sec']!=row['horizon_sec']:
            r,truth=recorded[case,base_arm];end=r['cutoff']+row['horizon_sec'];prefix=copy.deepcopy(cached[case,base_arm])
            for name,key in (('cells','time_s'),('flows','window_end_s'),('ports','time_s'),('ramps','end_sec')):
                prefix[name]=[z for z in prefix[name] if z[key]<=end+1e-7]
            base=_cellwise_measure(model,prefix,r,truth,horizon_sec=row['horizon_sec']);base['horizon_sec']=row['horizon_sec'];prefix_rows.append(base)
        pairs.append(dict(case=case,arm=row['arm'],horizon_sec=row['horizon_sec'],
            **{which:{k:row[which][k]-base[which][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','ttt','end_n','exits')} for which in ('actual','predicted')}))
    assert count==11
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,prefix_rows=prefix_rows,pairs=pairs,new_forecasts=count,reused_forecasts=6,
        serialization_parity=parity,pins=pins,new_native=0,coefficient_searches=0,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_forecasts=count,pairs=pairs)),flush=True)


def audit_local_state67():
    """Saved/current-native local-neighbor factorial, no new trajectories."""
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers import vissim_stackelberg_adapter as adapter
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'local67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    frame_path=root/'flow67/release_frames.json.gz';trace_path=root/'b67/release_trace.json.gz'
    case=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930';events_path=case/'observations/release/port_events.csv'
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,frame_path,trace_path,events_path)}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('freeway_fd.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','lane_plant_runtime.py'):
        p=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    cap=load(case/'capture.json');record=next(r for r in cap['records'] if r['arm']=='release')
    args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
    assert all(all(v==110 for v in step['vsl_commands'].values()) for step in args[1])
    assert args[2]==ctx['parameters']
    with gzip.open(frame_path,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):z for t,z in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
    with gzip.open(trace_path,'rt') as f:data=json.load(f)
    terms={(round(z['time_sec'],6),z['cell']):z for z in data['speed_terms']}
    allocations={(round(z['time_s'],6),z['cell']):z for z in data['allocations']}
    control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110});targets=range(15,23);t0=2670.1
    counts=Counter()
    with events_path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            if r['kind']=='departure':counts[round(float(r['time_s']),6),r['connector']]+=1
    connectors={spec['to_cell']:str(spec['connector']) for spec in model.ramps.values() if spec['road']=='FW_E'}
    groups={(t,c):{v:z for v,z in frame.items() if z[0]==c} for t,frame in frames.items() for c in range(14,24)}
    mean=lambda group:sum(z[1] for z in group.values())/len(group)
    bases=('model','native_local','native_neighbors','all_native')
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:actual N*v/L shows VSL gain absent from model states; new5s caches verified.',
        arm='release',cells=list(targets),window=[2670.1,3120.1],samples_per_cell=90,bases=bases,
        max_scalar_evaluations=2880,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,pins=pins,
        substitution='Replace local N/v and/or upstream v/downstream N. FD target and anticipation branch are recalculated. Same current predicted effective lanes/drop profile and observed5s merges in every basis.',
        parity='All-model scalar speed and FD target must equal recorded canonical1s equations before interpreting native substitutions.',
        limits=['Only110 arm: no guessed VSL90 cohort labels.',
