                'Instantaneous equation is compared with following5s spatial-mean change, not an exact derivative or individual acceleration.',
                'Local dynamic lane state remains the saved prediction; off storage and queue propagation are not replayed in these scalar probes.',
                'Hybrid states and future-native labels are conditional diagnostics only, never feasible mass-conserving autonomous predictions.']))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[];parity=[]
    for t in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
        end=round(t+5,6)
        for c in targets:
            z=terms[t,c];p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c];spec=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c)
            lane=allocations[t,c]['effective_lanes'];down_lane=allocations[t,c+1]['effective_lanes']
            before,after=groups[t,c],groups[end,c];assert before and after and groups[t,c-1] and groups[t,c+1]
            v0=mean(before);v1=mean(after);actual_rate=(v1-v0)/5
            staying=before.keys()&after.keys();enter=after.keys()-before.keys();leave=before.keys()-after.keys()
            stayer=sum(after[v][1]-before[v][1] for v in staying)/len(after)/5
            replacement=(sum(after[v][1]-v0 for v in enter)-sum(before[v][1]-v0 for v in leave))/len(after)/5
            assert abs(stayer+replacement-actual_rate)<1e-8
            assert z['speed_before']>0
            saved_up=z['speed_before']+z['convection']*lengths[c]*3600/z['speed_before']
            merge=counts[end,connectors[c]]*720 if c in connectors else 0.
            for basis in bases:
                local=basis in ('native_local','all_native');neighbor=basis in ('native_neighbors','all_native')
                v=v0 if local else z['speed_before'];rho=len(before)/(lengths[c]*lane) if local else z['rho']
                up=mean(groups[t,c-1]) if neighbor else saved_up
                down=len(groups[t,c+1])/(lengths[c+1]*down_lane) if neighbor else z['downstream_rho']
                command=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',c,cfg)
                adapter._FW_SEG_CTX.update(phi=z['phi'],dlam=z['delta_lanes'],lanes=lane)
                desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,command,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,command)
                tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,down,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                relax=(desired-v)/(tau*3600);conv=v*(up-v)/(lengths[c]*3600)
                ant=-nu*(down-rho)/(tau*lengths[c]*3600*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                drop=z['phi']*z['delta_lanes']*rho*v*v/(3600*lengths[c]*lane*p['rho_crit'])
                merge_loss=spec.get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)*merge*v/(3600*lengths[c]*lane*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                equation=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(v,up,rho,down,desired,1/3600,lengths[c],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'],net.v_min)
                assert abs(equation-max(net.v_min,max(net.v_min,v+relax+conv+ant)-drop))<1e-8
                final=max(net.v_min,equation-merge_loss)
                if basis=='model':
                    error=max(abs(final-z['speed_final']),abs(desired-z['desired']))
                    assert error<1e-8,(t,c,error);parity.append(error)
                rows.append(dict(time_s=t,cell=c,basis=basis,rho=rho,speed=v,upstream_speed=up,downstream_rho=down,
                    relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,lane_drop=drop,merge_loss=merge_loss,
                    predicted_change=final-v,desired=desired,actual_spatial_rate=actual_rate,
                    native_stayer=stayer,native_replacement=replacement,
                    native_acceleration=sum(x[4]*3.6 for x in before.values())/len(before),
                    clipped=abs(final-(v+relax+conv+ant-drop-merge_loss))>1e-8))
    summary=[]
    for c in targets:
        for basis in bases:
            for block in (None,0,1,2):
                rr=[r for r in rows if r['cell']==c and r['basis']==basis and (block is None or int(round((r['time_s']-t0)/5))//30==block)]
                keys=('rho','speed','desired','relaxation','convection','anticipation','lane_drop','merge_loss','predicted_change','actual_spatial_rate','native_stayer','native_replacement','native_acceleration')
                summary.append(dict(cell=c,basis=basis,block=block,samples=len(rr),means={k:sum(r[k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in keys},
                    rmse=math.sqrt(sum((r['predicted_change']-r['actual_spatial_rate'])**2 for r in rr)/len(rr)),
                    clipped=sum(r['clipped'] for r in rr)))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',rows);save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=summary,pins=pins,scalar_evaluations=len(rows),
        parity_checks=len(parity),maximum_parity_error=max(parity),new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,parity=max(parity),groups=[r for r in summary if r['basis']=='all_native' and r['block'] is None])),flush=True)


def check_route_pressure_recovery(resume=False):
    """Two bounded causal hypotheses; conditional fit, autonomous acceptance."""
    from collections import Counter
    from contextlib import contextmanager
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers import vissim_stackelberg_adapter as adapter
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'through_pressure';out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume)
    if resume:
        assert load(out/'failure.json')['completed_forecasts']==1
        assert not (out/'selection.json').exists()
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',context['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,ri/'contract.json')}
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for path in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/path)]=sha(ROOT/path)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    network=HERE/'selected/network/native_seed29.inpx';pins[str(network)]=sha(network)
    links={int(e.get('no')):e for e in ET.parse(network).findall('./links/link')}
    def origin(connector):
        e=links[connector];link,lane=map(int,e.find('fromLinkEndPt').get('lane').split())
        return link,set(range(lane,lane+len(e.find('lanes'))))
    link,through=origin(10613);offlink,exit_lanes=origin(10481)
    assert link==offlink==2 and through=={2,3,4} and exit_lanes=={1,2}
    save(out/('resume_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:current-state local67 balance and lane populations identified possible pressure mismatch; no coefficient changed.',
        candidates=['pressure18','pressure18_merge21_23'],new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,maximum_full_forecasts=14,
        pressure='Only cell18: route inventory removes10481-target stock; remaining stock divided by current usable continuing lanes=min(3,effective lanes18). This is a homogeneous through-route pressure hypothesis, not an observed lane partition.',
        equation='rho_through=N_continuing/(L*lambda_continuing); virtual_down=max(0,rho+(rho_down-rho_through)*(rho+kappa)/(rho_through+kappa)). Existing speed update receives virtual_down only at18; physical density,FD,q,storage,lane-drop andmerge flow unchanged.',
        calibration='One common delta_merge multiplier at21/23 from20seed29 NC30s mean equation balances, with actual5s merges and current native states. Bound[0.25,1],10%quadratic prior toward1; no control-effect fit.',
        train_gate='Seed29 NC750: cell15-25 tail300 normalized N/v/q loss<=90% baseline; first450<=110%; whole speed RMSE<=baseline+2km/h.',
        validation='If either train gate passes, freeze lower-tail-loss candidate before seed29VSL750/RM/both450,seed43all4(NC/VSL750),seed67lateall4(450). Seeds43/67 previously inspected, not blind.',
        limits=['The equivalent density is a proposed local closure, not a published formula or capacity bonus.',
                'Routes do not uniquely determine lanes; lane2 is shared. All same-step route masses are predicted causally.',
                'Speed-gradient branch and existing recovery predicates see the equivalent pressure; no new cost/reward/control labels.',
                'Conditional derivative fit is approximate; acceptance requires autonomous states,physical flows,queues and paired costs.',
                'No controller integration or production adoption from training success alone.']))
    (out/('helper_resumed.txt' if resume else 'helper_executed.txt')).write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    # Fit one coefficient from native NC training frames, not predicted merge rates.
    train=next(r for r in records if r['case']=='s29_early' and r['arm']=='none')
    truth=ObservationData(train['truth']);frames_path=root/'recovery_transport/s29_none_frames.json.gz';pins[str(frames_path)]=sha(frames_path)
    with gzip.open(frames_path,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
    events_path=truth.folder/'port_events.csv';pins[str(events_path)]=sha(events_path)
    counts=Counter()
    with events_path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            if r['kind']=='departure':counts[round(float(r['time_s']),6),r['connector']]+=1
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']];lanes=net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E']
    connector={v['to_cell']:str(v['connector']) for v in model.ramps.values() if v['road']=='FW_E'}
    control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110});groups={(t,c):[r for r in v.values() if r[0]==c] for t,v in frames.items() for c in range(20,25)}
    mean=lambda rr:sum(r[1] for r in rr)/len(rr);fit_rows=[]
    for t in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
        for cell in (21,23):
            local=groups[t,cell];assert local and groups[t,cell-1] and groups[t,cell+1]
            p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell];response=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',cell)
            speed=mean(local);rho=len(local)/(lengths[cell]*lanes[cell]);down=len(groups[t,cell+1])/(lengths[cell+1]*lanes[cell+1])
            command=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',cell,cfg)
            desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,command,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
            tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(response,speed,desired,rho,down,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,command))
            relax=(desired-speed)/(tau*3600);conv=speed*(mean(groups[t,cell-1])-speed)/(lengths[cell]*3600)
            ant=-nu*(down-rho)/(tau*lengths[cell]*3600*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
            merge=counts[round(t+5,6),connector[cell]]*720
            loss=response['delta_merge']*merge*speed/(3600*lengths[cell]*lanes[cell]*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
            fit_rows.append(dict(time=t,cell=cell,base=relax+conv+ant,merge_loss=loss))
    blocks=[]
    for t in [round(2670.1+30*j,6) for j in range(10)]:
        for c in (21,23):
            rr=[r for r in fit_rows if r['cell']==c and t-1e-7<=r['time']<t+30-1e-7];assert len(rr)==6
            blocks.append(dict(time=t,cell=c,base=sum(r['base'] for r in rr)/6,merge_loss=sum(r['merge_loss'] for r in rr)/6,
                actual=(mean(groups[round(t+30,6),c])-mean(groups[t,c]))/30))
    energy=sum(r['merge_loss']**2 for r in blocks);assert energy>0
    raw=(sum(r['merge_loss']*(r['base']-r['actual']) for r in blocks)+.1*energy)/(1.1*energy)
    factor=max(.25,min(1.,raw))
    fit=dict(seed=29,arms=['none'],cells=[21,23],rows=blocks,raw_multiplier=raw,multiplier=factor,bounds=[.25,1.],prior_strength=.1,
             conditional_rmse_before=math.sqrt(sum((r['base']-r['merge_loss']-r['actual'])**2 for r in blocks)/20),
             conditional_rmse_after=math.sqrt(sum((r['base']-factor*r['merge_loss']-r['actual'])**2 for r in blocks)/20))
    if resume:assert fit==load(out/'fit.json')
    else:save(out/'fit.json',fit)
    @contextmanager
    def candidate(enabled,merge_factor):
        old_step=accounting._freeway_substep_events;old_segment=mn.segment_vsl;old_update=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh;old_config=model._config
        active={};diagnostics=dict(calls=0,virtual_density_clipped=0,minimum_virtual_density=None,maximum_pressure_density=0.)
        def config(road,params):
            result=old_config(road,params)
            if road=='FW_E' and merge_factor!=1:
                result.network.freeway_state_response=copy.deepcopy(result.network.freeway_state_response)
                for c in (21,23):result.network.freeway_state_response['FW_E']['cell_overrides'][str(c)]['delta_merge']*=merge_factor
            return result
        def step(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw):
            runtime=cfg.network.offramp_route_inventory
            assert {k for k,b in runtime['branches'].items() if b['freeway']=='FW_E' and b['source_cell']==18}=={'10481'}
            stock=state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E'][18]
            active['continuing_n']=sum(n for key,n in stock.items() if key.rsplit('|',1)[-1]!='10481')
            return old_step(state,control,demand,cfg,*a,**kw)
        def segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw):
            active['address']=(road,cell)
            return old_segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw)
        def update(speed,up,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum):
            if enabled and active.get('address')==('FW_E',18) and adapter._FW_SEG_CTX['armed']:
                p=adapter._FW_SEG_CTX['p'];length=p['segment_length_km'];kappa=p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']
                profile=adapter._FW_SEG_CTX_STATE['profile']['FW_E'];continuing_lanes=min(float(len(through)),profile[18])
                pressure=active['continuing_n']/(length*continuing_lanes)
                virtual=rho+(down-pressure)*(rho+kappa)/(pressure+kappa)
                diagnostics['calls']+=1;diagnostics['virtual_density_clipped']+=int(virtual<0)
                diagnostics['minimum_virtual_density']=virtual if diagnostics['minimum_virtual_density'] is None else min(diagnostics['minimum_virtual_density'],virtual)
                diagnostics['maximum_pressure_density']=max(diagnostics['maximum_pressure_density'],pressure)
                down=max(0.,virtual)
            return old_update(speed,up,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum)
        model._config=config;accounting._freeway_substep_events=step;mn.segment_vsl=segment;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=update
        try:yield diagnostics
        finally:model._config=old_config;accounting._freeway_substep_events=old_step;mn.segment_vsl=old_segment;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=old_update
    def prepare(r,horizon):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            z=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);z.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(z)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if r['case']=='s29_late' else ri/r['case']/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        return args,kw
    def measure(pred,r,horizon):
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);pf={(v['window_end_s'],v['cell']):v for v in pred['flows']}
        row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon);loss={'first450':[],'tail300':[]}
        ac={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rr in truth.cells.items() for z in rr if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        for z in pred['cells']:
            if not 15<=z['cell']<=25:continue
            t,c=z['time_s'],z['cell'];a=ac[t,c];af=truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]
            loss['first450' if t<=r['cutoff']+450+1e-7 else 'tail300'].extend([((z['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])/10)**2,((z['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])/20)**2,((pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-float(af['downstream_crossings']))/(1000/120))**2])
        row['local_losses']={k:sum(v)/len(v) if v else None for k,v in loss.items()};return row
    results=[]
    def run(r,label,enabled,multiplier,horizon):
        a,k=prepare(r,horizon)
        with candidate(enabled,multiplier) as diagnostics:pred=model.rollout(*a,**k)
        assert diagnostics['calls']==(horizon if enabled else 0), 'Pressure hook did not cover the actual canonical steps'
        row=measure(pred,r,horizon);row.update(candidate=label,pressure_diagnostics=diagnostics,horizon=horizon)
        stem=label+'_'+r['case']+'_'+r['arm']
        with gzip.open(out/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        save(out/(stem+'.json'),row);results.append(row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(candidate=label,case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],loss=row['local_losses'],predicted=row['predicted'])),flush=True)
        return pred,row
    if resume:
        baseline_path=out/'baseline_s29_early_none.json.gz';pins[str(baseline_path)]=sha(baseline_path)
        with gzip.open(baseline_path,'rt') as f:baseline=json.load(f)
        br=load(out/'baseline_s29_early_none.json');results.append(dict(br,reused=True))
    else:baseline,br=run(train,'baseline',False,1.,750)
    oldpath=root/'vsl_native_exposure/horizon750/none.json.gz';pins[str(oldpath)]=sha(oldpath)
    with gzip.open(oldpath,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
    serialized=json.loads(json.dumps(baseline,allow_nan=False))
    parity={k:serialized[k]==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
    training=[];saved={}
    for label,multiplier in (('pressure18',1.),('pressure18_merge21_23',factor)):
        pred,row=run(train,label,True,multiplier,750);saved[label]=(pred,row,multiplier)
        gates=dict(tail=row['local_losses']['tail300']<=.9*br['local_losses']['tail300'],early=row['local_losses']['first450']<=1.1*br['local_losses']['first450'],whole_speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=br['score']['speed']['rmse']+2)
        training.append(dict(candidate=label,multiplier=multiplier,gates=gates,passed=all(gates.values()),loss=row['local_losses']))
    passed=[r for r in training if r['passed']];selected=min(passed,key=lambda r:r['loss']['tail300']) if passed else None
    save(out/'selection.json',dict(training=training,selected=selected,baseline=br['local_losses'],parity=parity,adopted=False))
    validation=[]
    if selected:
        label=selected['candidate'];multiplier=selected['multiplier']
        for case,arm,horizon in [('s29_early',a,750 if a=='vsl' else 450) for a in ('vsl','rm','both')]+[('s43_early',a,750 if a in ('none','vsl') else 450) for a in ('none','vsl','rm','both')]:
            r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));pred,row=run(r,label,True,multiplier,horizon);validation.append(row)
        cap=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json';pins[str(cap)]=sha(cap)
        for r in load(cap)['records']:
            r=dict(r,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm']))
            pred,row=run(r,label,True,multiplier,450);validation.append(row)
    assert len(results)<=14
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=results,fit=fit,training=training,selected=selected,parity=parity,pins=pins,
        new_forecasts=sum(not r.get('reused',False) for r in results),reused_forecasts=int(resume),total_forecasts=len(results),new_native=0,new_fzp=0,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_forecasts=len(results),selected=selected)),flush=True)


def check_center_gradient_distance():
    """One geometry-only candidate; keep storage and source terms at physical Li."""
    from contextlib import contextmanager
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers import vissim_stackelberg_adapter as adapter
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'center_distance';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component'];mn=accounting._mn
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',context['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    assert len(lengths)==31 and all(x>0 for x in lengths)
    assert all('recovery_relaxation' not in cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c) for c in range(31))
    ri=root/'route_inventory';records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,ri/'contract.json',DECISIONS/'capture.json')}
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for path in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/path)]=sha(ROOT/path)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    geometry=[]
    for c in range(1,30):
        li=lengths[c];du=(li+lengths[c-1])/2;dd=(li+lengths[c+1])/2
        # Linear fields at cell centers must recover their slope on unequal cells.
        v,rho=60.,30.;sv,sr=7.,11.
        up=v-sv*du;down=rho+sr*dd
        u=v+li/du*(up-v);d=rho+li/dd*(down-rho)
        error=max(abs((v-u)/li-sv),abs((d-rho)/li-sr));assert error<1e-10
        geometry.append(dict(cell=c,length=li,up_distance=du,down_distance=dd,
                             convection_multiplier=li/du,anticipation_multiplier=li/dd,linear_field_error=error))
    # Equal-length cells and uniform fields retain the old gradients exactly.
    assert 60+.2/.2*(45-60)==45 and 30+.2/.2*(50-30)==50
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: freeway-first sequencing explanation; now execute one frozen-coefficient geometry candidate.',
        hypothesis='Unequal physical cell lengths may distort speed/density gradients when both use the local cell length.',
        candidate='FW_E interior cells1..29 only: convection distance=(Lprev+Li)/2; anticipation=(Li+Lnext)/2. Source/terminal virtual-boundary conventions unchanged.',
        invariants=['Original Li in vehicle conservation, FD, merge, lane-drop, capacity and storage.',
                    'Current physical density and upstream/downstream states remain stored unchanged; only equivalent gradient arguments enter speed update.',
                    'Positive distance ratios preserve anticipation sign branches; no recovery_relaxation predicate in this frozen model.',
                    'No clipping of equivalent arguments: negative values are algebraic gradients, never physical densities/speeds.',
                    'No coefficient fitting, no future native input, no native runs or FZP reads.'],
        training='Same2220.1 seed29NC750. Cell15-25 first450 loss<=110% baseline; tail300<=90%; global speedRMSE<=baseline+2km/h.',
        validation='Only after training gate:29VSL750/RM,both450;43NC,VSL750/RM,both450;67four450. No acceptance from training alone.',
        caveat='Prior calibrated coefficients may have absorbed local-L gradients. Failure of this frozen candidate does not establish that a center-distance discretization is invalid.',
        geometry=geometry,maximum_new_forecasts=13,production_adopted=False,pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    @contextmanager
    def gradient(enabled):
        old_segment=mn.segment_vsl;old_update=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh
        active={};counts=Counter();groups={};diag=dict(maximum_equation_error=0.,negative_equivalent_up=0,negative_equivalent_down=0,coefficient_branch_checks=0)
        def segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw):
            active['address']=(road,cell)
            return old_segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw)
        def update(v,up,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum):
            address=active.get('address');armed=adapter._FW_SEG_CTX['armed']
            if not enabled or not armed or address[0]!='FW_E' or not 1<=address[1]<=29:
                return old_update(v,up,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum)
            c=address[1];ctx=adapter._FW_SEG_CTX;p=ctx['p'];li=p['segment_length_km']
            assert abs(li-lengths[c])<1e-12
            du=(li+lengths[c-1])/2;dd=(li+lengths[c+1])/2
            equiv_up=v+li/du*(up-v);equiv_down=rho+li/dd*(down-rho)
            spec=ctx['state_response'];critical=ctx['response_rho_crit']
            t,n=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,down,critical,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu)
            te,ne=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,equiv_down,critical,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu)
            assert (t,n)==(te,ne), 'Gradient transform changed a physical regime predicate'
            diag['coefficient_branch_checks']+=1;diag['negative_equivalent_up']+=int(equiv_up<0);diag['negative_equivalent_down']+=int(equiv_down<0)
            k=p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']
            relax=dt/t*(desired-v);conv=dt/du*v*(up-v);ant=-n*dt/(t*dd)*(down-rho)/(rho+k)
            drop=0.
            if ctx['phi']>0 and ctx['dlam']>0:drop=ctx['phi']*dt*ctx['dlam']*max(rho,0.)*v*v/(li*ctx['lanes']*p['rho_crit'])
            expected=max(minimum,max(minimum,v+relax+conv+ant)-drop)
            result=old_update(v,equiv_up,rho,equiv_down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum)
            error=abs(result-expected);diag['maximum_equation_error']=max(diag['maximum_equation_error'],error)
            assert error<1e-8,(c,result,expected)
            key=(counts[c]//150,c);counts[c]+=1
            group=groups.setdefault(key,dict(samples=0,relaxation=0.,convection=0.,anticipation=0.,lane_drop=0.,convection_delta=0.,anticipation_delta=0.))
            group['samples']+=1
            for name,value in dict(relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,lane_drop=-drop,
                    convection_delta=conv-dt/li*v*(up-v),anticipation_delta=ant+n*dt/(t*li)*(down-rho)/(rho+k)).items():group[name]+=value
            return result
        mn.segment_vsl=segment;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=update
        try:yield counts,groups,diag
        finally:mn.segment_vsl=old_segment;mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=old_update
    def prepare(r,horizon):
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            z=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);z.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(z)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if r['case']=='s29_late' else ri/r['case']/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)))
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        return args,kw
    def measure(pred,r,horizon):
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        pf={(v['window_end_s'],v['cell']):v for v in pred['flows']};ac={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rr in truth.cells.items() for z in rr if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        loss=dict(first450=[],tail300=[])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        for z in pred['cells']:
            if not 15<=z['cell']<=25:continue
            t,c=z['time_s'],z['cell'];a=ac[t,c];af=truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]
            loss['first450' if t<=r['cutoff']+450+1e-7 else 'tail300'].extend([((z['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])/10)**2,((z['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])/20)**2,
                ((pf[t,c]['downstream_crossings']-float(af['downstream_crossings']))/(1000/120))**2])
        row['local_losses']={k:sum(v)/len(v) if v else None for k,v in loss.items()};return row
    rows=[]
    def run(r,enabled,horizon):
        args,kw=prepare(r,horizon);started=time.perf_counter()
        with gradient(enabled) as (counts,groups,diag):pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        assert dict(counts)==({c:horizon for c in range(1,30)} if enabled else {})
        row=measure(pred,r,horizon);row.update(candidate='center' if enabled else 'baseline',horizon=horizon,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,
            gradient_checks=diag,changed_calls_by_cell=dict(counts),term_means=[dict(block=block,cell=c,**{k:v/g['samples'] for k,v in g.items() if k!='samples'},samples=g['samples']) for (block,c),g in sorted(groups.items())])
        stem=row['candidate']+'_'+r['case']+'_'+r['arm']
        with gzip.open(out/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        save(out/(stem+'.json'),row);rows.append(row)
        print(json.dumps(dict(case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],candidate=row['candidate'],loss=row['local_losses'],wall=row['wall_sec'])),flush=True)
        return pred,row
    train=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_early','none'))
    try:
        baseline,br=run(train,False,750)
        oldpath=root/'through_pressure/baseline_s29_early_none.json.gz';pins[str(oldpath)]=sha(oldpath)
        with gzip.open(oldpath,'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
        serial=json.loads(json.dumps(baseline,allow_nan=False))
        parity={k:serial[k]==prior[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        pred,row=run(train,True,750)
        gates=dict(early=row['local_losses']['first450']<=1.1*br['local_losses']['first450'],
            tail=row['local_losses']['tail300']<=.9*br['local_losses']['tail300'],whole_speed=row['score']['speed']['rmse']<=br['score']['speed']['rmse']+2)
        save(out/'selection.json',dict(gates=gates,passed=all(gates.values()),baseline=br['local_losses'],candidate=row['local_losses'],parity=parity,adopted=False))
        if all(gates.values()):
            for case,arm,horizon in [('s29_early',a,750 if a=='vsl' else 450) for a in ('vsl','rm','both')]+[('s43_early',a,750 if a in ('none','vsl') else 450) for a in ('none','vsl','rm','both')]:
                run(next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm)),True,horizon)
            cap=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json';pins[str(cap)]=sha(cap)
            for r in load(cap)['records']:
                run(dict(r,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm'])),True,450)
        assert len(rows)<=13
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,parity=parity,gates=gates,pins=pins,new_forecasts=len(rows),new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,
            coefficient_fit=False,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,forecasts=len(rows),training_gates=gates)),flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        save(out/'failure.json',dict(type=type(exc).__name__,message=str(exc),completed_forecasts=len(rows)));raise


def audit_merge_inlet_speeds():
    """Use already extracted events/frames; distinguish bracketing from crossing speeds."""
    from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    import numpy as np
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'merge_speed_audit';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    source=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930';t0,t1=2670.1,3120.1
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    rec=next(r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_early','none'))
    _,kw=read_primitive_capture(rec['input'],rec['sha256']);specs=kw['ramp_dynamics']['ramps']
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,Path(rec['input']),source/'capture.json',source/'protocol.json')}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('area_freeway_accounting.py','lane_plant_runtime.py','freeway_fd.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py'):
        p=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:center-distance candidate rejected and existing merge-mixing OFF/earlier rejected test confirmed.',
        window=[t0,t1],seed=67,arms=['hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90'],connectors=8,
        sources='Existing port_events/ports/cells CSV and two previously verified complete freeway frame caches. No FZP read.',
        question='Does whole-connector/posthead travel-speed proxy represent speed of merging vehicles, and does the sample support simply enabling the existing flow-weighted virtual inlet?',
        definitions=['departure.speed_kmh is the last ramp sample at the lower time, NOT crossing speed.',
                     'The cache match is first freeway sample at upper time, up to5s after crossing; post-minus-pre includes acceleration and merge interaction.',
                     'No rigorous speed bound is inferred from the two endpoint speeds, and missing/skipped records are not assigned synthetic speeds.',
                     'Mainline means are current lower-time spatial averages, not boundary passage velocities.',
                     'All future actual observations are diagnostic labels only; no calibration/rollout or controller input from this audit.'],
        maximum_new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,coefficient_fit=False,adopted=False,pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    verify=load(root/'flow67/verification.json');pins[str(root/'flow67/verification.json')]=sha(root/'flow67/verification.json')
    frames={}
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        p=root/'flow67'/f'{arm}_frames.json.gz';assert sha(p)==verify['artifacts'][p.name];pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:d=json.load(f)
        assert d['fields']==['cell','speed_kmh','x_m','lane','acceleration_mps2','desired_speed_kmh']
        frames[arm]={round(float(t),6):x for t,x in d['frames'].items()};assert len(frames[arm])==91
    def stats(values):
        a=np.array(values,dtype=float)
        return dict(n=len(a),mean=float(a.mean()),median=float(np.median(a)),p10=float(np.percentile(a,10)),p90=float(np.percentile(a,90))) if len(a) else dict(n=0)
    all_events=[];groups=[];count_checks=0;count_missing_post=Counter()
    for arm in ('hold','release','hold_vsl90','release_vsl90'):
        folder=source/'observations'/arm;mf=load(folder/'manifest.json');truth=ObservationData(folder)
        for name in ('manifest.json','port_events.csv','ports_30s.csv','cells_30s.csv','geometry.json'):
            p=folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            if name!='manifest.json':assert pins[str(p)]==mf['files'][name]
        geometry={str(b['connector']):b for b in load(folder/'geometry.json')['boundaries'] if b['kind']=='ramp'}
        ramp_by_connector={str(v['connector']):r for r,v in model.ramps.items()};assert len(ramp_by_connector)==8
        with (folder/'port_events.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            selected=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['kind']=='departure' and r['connector'] in ramp_by_connector and t0+1e-7<float(r['time_s'])<=t1+1e-7]
        for con,ramp in ramp_by_connector.items():
            event=[r for r in selected if r['connector']==con]
            for j in range(1,16):
                end=round(t0+30*j,6);n=sum(end-30+1e-7<float(e['time_s'])<=end+1e-7 for e in event)
                assert n==float(truth.ports[end,con]['departures_veh']),(arm,con,end,n)
                count_checks+=1
            s=specs[ramp];proxy=s.get('posthead_travel_speed_kmh',s['travel_speed_kmh']);geo=geometry[con]
            for e in event:
                hi=round(float(e['upper_time_s']),6);lo=round(float(e['lower_time_s']),6);assert abs(hi-lo-5)<1e-8
                vpre=float(e['speed_kmh']) if e['speed_kmh'] else None
                pos=float(e['position_m']) if e['position_m'] else None
                row=dict(arm=arm,ramp=ramp,connector=con,road=geo['road'],cell=geo['to_cell'],vehicle=int(e['vehicle']),lo=lo,hi=hi,
                    proxy_speed_kmh=proxy,last_ramp_speed_kmh=vpre,last_ramp_remaining_m=geo['length_m']-pos if pos is not None else None,
                    first_mainline_speed_kmh=None,post_distance_m=None,current_cell_mean=None,current_upstream_mean=None,current_target_lane_mean=None)
                if arm in frames and geo['road']=='FW_E':
                    f0,f1=frames[arm][lo],frames[arm][hi];z=f1.get(e['vehicle'])
                    if z is not None:
                        assert z[2]>=geo['chain_pos_m']-.05 and z[0]>=geo['to_cell'],(arm,con,z,geo)
                        row['first_mainline_speed_kmh']=z[1];row['post_distance_m']=z[2]-geo['chain_pos_m']
                        current=[x[1] for x in f0.values() if x[0]==geo['to_cell']]
                        up=[x[1] for x in f0.values() if x[0]==geo['to_cell']-1]
                        lane=[x[1] for x in f0.values() if x[0]==geo['to_cell'] and x[3]==z[3]]
                        row['current_cell_mean']=sum(current)/len(current) if current else None
                        row['current_upstream_mean']=sum(up)/len(up) if up else None
                        # Endpoint lane is a sample label, not a claim of unchanged merge lane.
                        row['current_target_lane_mean']=sum(lane)/len(lane) if lane else None
                    else:count_missing_post[(arm,con)]+=1
                all_events.append(row)
            rr=[r for r in all_events if r['arm']==arm and r['connector']==con]
            for block in (None,0,1,2):
                subset=[r for r in rr if block is None or int(round((r['hi']-t0)/5)-1)//30==block]
                pre=[r['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] for r in subset if r['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] is not None]
                paired=[r for r in subset if r['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] is not None and r['first_mainline_speed_kmh'] is not None]
                g=dict(arm=arm,ramp=ramp,connector=con,road=geo['road'],cell=geo['to_cell'],block=block,proxy=proxy,departures=len(subset),
                    missing_pre=len(subset)-len(pre),pre=stats(pre),post=stats([r['first_mainline_speed_kmh'] for r in paired]),
                    paired_end_speed_change=stats([r['first_mainline_speed_kmh']-r['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] for r in paired]),
                    pre_remaining_m=stats([r['last_ramp_remaining_m'] for r in subset if r['last_ramp_remaining_m'] is not None]),
                    post_distance_m=stats([r['post_distance_m'] for r in paired]),
                    proxy_pre_mae=sum(abs(v-proxy) for v in pre)/len(pre) if pre else None,
                    proxy_post_mae=sum(abs(r['first_mainline_speed_kmh']-proxy) for r in paired)/len(paired) if paired else None)
                for label,key in (('cell','current_cell_mean'),('upstream','current_upstream_mean')):
                    vals=[r for r in paired if r[key] is not None]
                    g[label+'_pre_mae']=sum(abs(r['last_ramp_speed_kmh']-r[key]) for r in vals)/len(vals) if vals else None
                    g[label+'_post_mae']=sum(abs(r['first_mainline_speed_kmh']-r[key]) for r in vals)/len(vals) if vals else None
                groups.append(g)
    assert count_checks==480
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'events.json',all_events)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=groups,event_count=len(all_events),port_count_checks=count_checks,
        missing_post=[dict(arm=a,connector=c,count=n) for (a,c),n in count_missing_post.items()],pins=pins,
        current_aggregate_merge_speed=model.aggregate_merge_speed,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,adopted=False,qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,events=len(all_events),count_checks=count_checks,groups=[g for g in groups if g['block'] is None and g['road']=='FW_E']),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def audit_merge_inlet_transport():
    """Conditional term scale only; no boundary speeds or model state are fitted."""
    from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'merge_speed_audit'
    assert (out/'summary.json').exists() and not (out/'transport.json').exists()
    previous=load(out/'summary.json');events=load(out/'events.json');runtime=load(root/'route_inventory/compiled.json')
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    bounds=runtime['bounds']['FW_E'];assert all(abs(lengths[c]-(bounds[c+1]-bounds[c])/1000)<1e-10 for c in range(31))
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),out/'summary.json',out/'events.json',manifest,root/'route_inventory/compiled.json')}
    for p,h in previous['pins'].items():
        # Historical entrypoint changed only by adding this audit; verify its archived source.
        assert sha(out/'helper_executed.txt' if p==str(Path(__file__)) else p)==h,p
    save(out/'transport_protocol.json',dict(conditional_only=True,fit=False,autonomous=False,new_fzp_reads=0,
        flux='Actual5s mainline crossings reconstructed from paired freeway positions plus already recorded upstream-ramp entry skips, crosschecked to native30s.',
        velocity='Pre-ramp and post-mainline5s endpoints; they do not bound true crossing speed. Native current-cell/upstream spatial means.',
        terms='Additional standard-convection change from virtual inlet; existing explicit merge loss using physical nominal lanes. Diagnostic one-second term equivalents from5s flux, not observed individual acceleration.',
        pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'transport_helper.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[];flux_checks=0
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        fp=root/'flow67'/f'{arm}_frames.json.gz';assert sha(fp)==previous['pins'][str(fp)];pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):x for t,x in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        folder=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm;truth=ObservationData(folder)
        p=folder/'flows_30s.csv';assert sha(p)==load(folder/'manifest.json')['files'][p.name];pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        geo={str(z['connector']):z for z in load(folder/'geometry.json')['boundaries'] if z['kind']=='ramp' and z['road']=='FW_E'}
        by_time=defaultdict(list)
        for e in events:
            if e['arm']==arm and e['road']=='FW_E':by_time[e['hi']].append(e)
        reconstructed=Counter()
        for lo in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
            hi=round(lo+5,6);f0,f1=frames[lo],frames[hi];common=f0.keys()&f1.keys()
            for con,g in geo.items():
                c=g['to_cell'];boundary=bounds[c]
                crossed={vid for vid in common if f0[vid][2]<boundary<=f1[vid][2]}
                skip={str(e['vehicle']) for e in by_time[hi] if geo[e['connector']]['chain_pos_m']<boundary
                      and str(e['vehicle']) not in f0 and str(e['vehicle']) in f1 and f1[str(e['vehicle'])][2]>=boundary}
                assert not crossed&skip
                qmain=(len(crossed)+len(skip))*720.
                end=round(2670.1+30*(int(round((hi-2670.1)/5)-1)//6+1),6);reconstructed[end,c]+=len(crossed)+len(skip)
                merge=[e for e in by_time[hi] if e['connector']==con];r=len(merge)*720.
                local=[z[1] for z in f0.values() if z[0]==c];up=[z[1] for z in f0.values() if z[0]==c-1]
                assert local and up,(arm,lo,c)
                v=sum(local)/len(local);vu=sum(up)/len(up);n=len(local);p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c]
                delta=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c).get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)
                loss=delta*r*v/(3600*(n+lengths[c]*net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][c]*p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                corrections={}
                for name,key in [('proxy','proxy_speed_kmh'),('pre','last_ramp_speed_kmh'),('post','first_mainline_speed_kmh')]:
                    assert all(e[key] is not None for e in merge)
                    vr=sum(e[key] for e in merge)/len(merge) if merge else None
                    corrections[name]=v/(3600*lengths[c])*(r/(qmain+r))*(vr-vu) if merge else 0.
                rows.append(dict(arm=arm,time_s=lo,cell=c,connector=con,mainline_5s_veh=qmain/720,merge_5s_veh=len(merge),
                    native_n=n,native_v=v,native_upstream_v=vu,merge_loss=loss,additional_convection=corrections,
                    upstream_entries_skipped=len(skip),nominal_lanes=net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][c]))
        for (end,c),n in reconstructed.items():
            assert n==float(truth.flows[end,'FW_E',c]['upstream_crossings']),(arm,end,c,n,truth.flows[end,'FW_E',c])
            flux_checks+=1
    groups=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        for con in sorted({r['connector'] for r in rows}):
            rr=[r for r in rows if r['arm']==arm and r['connector']==con]
            assert len(rr)==90
            groups.append(dict(arm=arm,connector=con,cell=rr[0]['cell'],samples=len(rr),
                mainline=sum(r['mainline_5s_veh'] for r in rr),merges=sum(r['merge_5s_veh'] for r in rr),merge_loss_mean=sum(r['merge_loss'] for r in rr)/len(rr),
                correction_mean={key:sum(r['additional_convection'][key] for r in rr)/len(rr) for key in ('proxy','pre','post')}))
    assert flux_checks==120
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'transport.json',dict(rows=rows,groups=groups,pins=pins,flux_checks=flux_checks,fit=False,adopted=False,
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,flux_checks=flux_checks,groups=groups)),flush=True)


def audit_merge_population_balance(resume=False):
    """Partition sampled mean-speed change by ramp entry vs existing freeway populations."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';source=root/'merge_speed_audit'
    out=root/'merge_population';out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume)
    if resume:
        assert (out/'failure.json').exists() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    audit=load(source/'summary.json');transport=load(source/'transport.json');events=load(source/'events.json')
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json');model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110});targets=(10,12,21,22,23,24,25)
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),source/'summary.json',source/'transport.json',source/'events.json',root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json')}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('area_freeway_accounting.py','lane_plant_runtime.py','freeway_fd.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py'):
        p=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/('retry_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:measured merge-speed proxy mismatch replicated in53; virtual-inlet term sign clarified without a new forecast.',
        seeds=[67],arms=['release','release_vsl90'],cells=list(targets),window=[2670.1,3120.1],new_forecasts=0,new_fzp_reads=0,new_native=0,fit=False,
        identity='Delta mean = stayers speed change + entering own-ramp pre-speed composition + own-ramp endpoint acceleration + entering other pre-speed composition + other endpoint acceleration - exiting old-speed composition; denominator=N_after, divide by5s.',
        own_ramp='Only a ramp that actually joins this cell; upstream-ramp vehicles traversing into this cell are other inflow.',
        limitations=['Five-second endpoints do not identify where acceleration/lane change occurs or the instantaneous boundary speed.',
                    'A merge may bypass its source cell before the first freeway sample; count and report rather than fabricate endpoint stock.',
                    'Stayer acceleration includes relaxation, interactions and population selection, not causally identified merge friction.',
                    'Native-state one-second METANET balance is conditional only and differs in temporal operator from5s mean derivative.',
                    'Only all110 release uses direct scalar equation comparison. VSL exposure distributions are not replaced by nominal90 for this audit.'],
        pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/('helper_resumed.txt' if resume else 'helper_executed.txt')).write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    eventmap={(e['arm'],e['hi'],str(e['vehicle'])):e for e in events if e['road']=='FW_E'}
    terms={(r['arm'],round(r['time_s'],6),r['cell']):r for r in transport['rows']}
    legacy_path=root/'local67/rows.json';pins[str(legacy_path)]=sha(legacy_path)
    legacy={(round(r['time_s'],6),r['cell']):r for r in load(legacy_path) if r['basis']=='all_native'}
    rows=[];error_max=0.;scalar_parity=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        fp=root/'flow67'/f'{arm}_frames.json.gz';assert sha(fp)==audit['pins'][str(fp)];pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):x for t,x in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        for lo in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
            hi=round(lo+5,6);f0,f1=frames[lo],frames[hi]
            for c in targets:
                before={v:z for v,z in f0.items() if z[0]==c};after={v:z for v,z in f1.items() if z[0]==c}
                assert before and after
                v0=sum(z[1] for z in before.values())/len(before);v1=sum(z[1] for z in after.values())/len(after);den=5*len(after)
                stay=before.keys()&after.keys();enter=after.keys()-before.keys();leave=before.keys()-after.keys()
                parts=dict(stayer_acceleration=sum(after[v][1]-before[v][1] for v in stay)/den,
                    own_ramp_pre_composition=0.,own_ramp_endpoint_acceleration=0.,
                    other_inflow_pre_composition=0.,other_inflow_endpoint_acceleration=0.,
                    exiting_composition=-sum(before[v][1]-v0 for v in leave)/den)
                categories=defaultdict(int)
                for vid in enter:
                    ramp=eventmap.get((arm,hi,vid));own=ramp is not None and ramp['cell']==c
                    if vid in f0:pre=f0[vid][1];origin='mainline'
                    elif ramp is not None and ramp['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] is not None:pre=ramp['last_ramp_speed_kmh'];origin='own_ramp' if own else 'upstream_ramp'
                    else:raise AssertionError(('Missing inflow provenance',arm,lo,c,vid))
                    key='own_ramp' if own else 'other_inflow'
                    parts[key+'_pre_composition']+=(pre-v0)/den
                    parts[key+'_endpoint_acceleration']+=(after[vid][1]-pre)/den;categories[origin]+=1
                own_events=[e for e in events if e['arm']==arm and e['road']=='FW_E' and e['cell']==c and e['hi']==hi]
                bypass=sum(str(e['vehicle']) not in after for e in own_events)
                assert categories['own_ramp']+bypass==len(own_events)
                error=abs(sum(parts.values())-(v1-v0)/5);error_max=max(error_max,error);assert error<1e-10
                row=dict(arm=arm,time_s=lo,cell=c,n_before=len(before),n_after=len(after),speed_before=v0,speed_after=v1,
                    observed_mean_rate=(v1-v0)/5,parts=parts,categories=dict(categories),own_merges=len(own_events),own_merge_bypass=bypass,
                    leave_count=len(leave),leave_unobserved_next=sum(v not in f1 for v in leave))
                if arm=='release' and c in (21,22,23,24,25):
                    p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c];length=p['segment_length_km'];lanes=net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][c]
                    up=[z[1] for z in f0.values() if z[0]==c-1];down=[z for z in f0.values() if z[0]==c+1]
                    assert up and down and abs(lanes-3)<1e-9,(lo,c,lanes)
                    vu=sum(up)/len(up);rho=len(before)/(length*lanes)
                    pd=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c+1];rd=len(down)/(pd['segment_length_km']*net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'][c+1])
                    vsl=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',c,cfg)
                    desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,vsl,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                    nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,vsl)
                    tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c),v0,desired,rho,rd,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                    relax=(desired-v0)/(tau*3600);conv=v0*(vu-v0)/(length*3600)
                    ant=-nu*(rd-rho)/(tau*length*3600*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                    eq=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(v0,vu,rho,rd,desired,1/3600,length,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'],net.v_min)
                    assert abs(eq-max(net.v_min,v0+relax+conv+ant))<1e-8,(c,eq,relax,conv,ant)
                    tr=terms.get((arm,lo,c));loss=tr['merge_loss'] if tr else 0.
                    total=max(net.v_min,eq-loss)-v0
                    row['model']=dict(relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,merge_loss=loss,total_change=total,
                        premerge_balance=eq-v0,desired_speed=desired,rho=rho,rho_crit=p['rho_crit'])
                    if (lo,c) in legacy:
                        z=legacy[lo,c];err=max(abs(total-z['predicted_change']),abs(rho-z['rho']),abs(desired-z['desired']))
                        assert err<1e-8,(lo,c,err);scalar_parity.append(err)
                rows.append(row)
    groups=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        for c in targets:
            for block in (None,0,1,2):
                rr=[r for r in rows if r['arm']==arm and r['cell']==c and (block is None or int(round((r['time_s']-2670.1)/5))//30==block)]
                group=dict(arm=arm,cell=c,block=block,samples=len(rr),observed_mean_rate=sum(r['observed_mean_rate'] for r in rr)/len(rr),
                    parts={k:sum(r['parts'][k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in rr[0]['parts']},
                    own_merges=sum(r['own_merges'] for r in rr),own_merge_bypass=sum(r['own_merge_bypass'] for r in rr),
                    unobserved_leavers=sum(r['leave_unobserved_next'] for r in rr))
                if 'model' in rr[0]:group['model']={k:sum(r['model'][k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in rr[0]['model']}
                groups.append(group)
    assert len(rows)==1260 and len(scalar_parity)==180
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',rows);save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=groups,pins=pins,identities=len(rows),identity_error_max=error_max,
        scalar_parity_checks=len(scalar_parity),scalar_parity_error_max=max(scalar_parity),new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,adopted=False,qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,identities=len(rows),maximum_error=error_max,groups=[r for r in groups if r['arm']=='release' and r['block'] is None and r['cell']>=21])),flush=True)


def audit_recovery_acceleration_balance():
    """Observed-state NC consistency check, never an autonomous gain test."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response, state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'acceleration_balance';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root/'connected_fd/review/candidate_manifest.json'
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    lanes=list(net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E']);control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110})
    assert all(abs(lanes[c]-3)<1e-9 for c in range(20,27))
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('freeway_fd.py','vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','lane_plant_runtime.py'):
        p=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: sequencing explanation only; now execute a cache-only freeway diagnosis.',
        seeds=[29,43],arms=['none'],cells=list(range(21,26)),window=[2670.1,2970.1],
        source='Previously verified 5s native frames including vehicle acceleration; frozen connected-FD equations.',
        hypothesis='Observed local states may still produce a biased spatial mean-speed balance; distinguish native material acceleration from population replacement before any recovery fit.',
        diagnostic_only=True,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,
        limits=['Native future frames and observed30s accepted merges are conditional labels, never controller inputs.',
                'Instantaneous mean acceleration, five-second material speed differences, and spatial-mean derivatives are different quantities.',
                'METANET non-convection terms are not identified individual accelerations; heterogeneity and transport closure remain coupled.',
                'Use all-start-vehicle forward decomposition only when every start vehicle remains observed anywhere on the freeway.',
                'No parameter acceptance or full-network qualification from this audit.'],pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    tp=root/'connected_recovery23/baseline_terms.json.gz';pins[str(tp)]=sha(tp)
    with gzip.open(tp,'rt') as f:terms={(round(z['time_sec'],6),z['cell']):z for z in json.load(f)}
    def equation(c,v,rho,up,down,merge):
        p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c];spec=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c);length=lengths[c];dt=1/3600
        vsl=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',c,cfg)
        desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,vsl,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
        nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,vsl)
        tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(spec,v,desired,rho,down,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
        relax=dt/tau*(desired-v);conv=dt/length*v*(up-v)
        ant=-nu*dt/(tau*length)*(down-rho)/(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'])
        loss=spec.get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)*dt*merge*v/(length*lanes[c]*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
        result=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(v,up,rho,down,desired,dt,length,p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'],net.v_min)
        assert abs(result-max(net.v_min,v+relax+conv+ant))<1e-8
        final=max(net.v_min,result-loss)
        return dict(relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,merge_loss=loss,
                    nonconvection=relax+ant-loss,total=final-v,desired=desired,tau_sec=tau*3600,nu=nu,
                    unclipped=abs(final-(v+relax+conv+ant-loss))<1e-8)
    parity=[];rows=[];records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];native_checks=0
    for seed in (29,43):
        fp=root/'recovery_transport'/f's{seed}_none_frames.json.gz';pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):z for t,z in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        record=next(r for r in records if r['case']==f's{seed}_early' and r['arm']=='none');truth=ObservationData(record['truth'])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        native={(t,z['cell']):z for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for z in rs if z['road']=='FW_E'}
        groups={(t,c):{v:z for v,z in frame.items() if z[0]==c} for t,frame in frames.items() for c in range(20,27)}
        mean=lambda group:sum(z[1] for z in group.values())/len(group)
        for t in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
            end=round(t+5,6);window_end=round(2670.1+30*(int(round((t-2670.1)/5))//6+1),6)
            for c in range(21,26):
                before,after=groups[t,c],groups[end,c];assert before and after
                v0,v1=mean(before),mean(after);rho=len(before)/(lengths[c]*lanes[c])
                if (t,c) in native:
                    assert len(before)==native[t,c]['n_veh'] and abs(v0-native[t,c]['v_kmh'])<1e-7;native_checks+=1
                accepted=float(truth.flows[window_end,'FW_E',c]['ramp_merges'])*120
                calc=equation(c,v0,rho,mean(groups[t,c-1]),len(groups[t,c+1])/(lengths[c+1]*lanes[c+1]),accepted)
                staying=before.keys()&after.keys();enter=after.keys()-before.keys();leave=before.keys()-after.keys()
                stayer=sum(after[v][1]-before[v][1] for v in staying)/len(after)/5
                composition=(sum(after[v][1]-v0 for v in enter)-sum(before[v][1]-v0 for v in leave))/len(after)/5
                assert abs(stayer+composition-(v1-v0)/5)<1e-8
                matched=before.keys()&frames[end].keys();complete=len(matched)==len(before)
                cohort=sum(frames[end][v][1]-before[v][1] for v in matched)/len(matched)/5 if matched else None
                replacement=(v1-v0)/5-cohort if complete else None
                a0=sum(z[4]*3.6 for z in before.values())/len(before)
                a1=sum(z[4]*3.6 for z in after.values())/len(after)
                rows.append(dict(seed=seed,time_s=t,cell=c,n_veh=len(before),rho=rho,v_kmh=v0,actual_spatial_rate=(v1-v0)/5,
                    instantaneous_acceleration=a0,endpoint_average_acceleration=(a0+a1)/2,
                    stayers_contribution=stayer,composition_contribution=composition,
                    all_start_cohort_complete=complete,matched_start_vehicles=len(matched),cohort_material_rate=cohort,
                    forward_replacement_rate=replacement,accepted_merge_vph=accepted,model=calc))
                if seed==29:
                    z=terms[t,c];p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][c];delta=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',c).get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)
                    assert z['delta_lanes']==0 and z['speed_final']>net.v_min+1e-8
                    merge=-z['post_equation_change']*lengths[c]*lanes[c]*(z['rho']+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'])*3600/(delta*z['speed_before']) if c in (21,23) else 0.
                    up=z['speed_before']+z['convection']*lengths[c]*3600/z['speed_before']
                    back=equation(c,z['speed_before'],z['rho'],up,z['downstream_rho'],merge)
                    err=abs(back['total']-(z['speed_final']-z['speed_before']));assert err<1e-8;parity.append(err)
    summary=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        for c in range(21,26):
            rr=[r for r in rows if r['seed']==seed and r['cell']==c]
            keys=('actual_spatial_rate','instantaneous_acceleration','endpoint_average_acceleration','stayers_contribution','composition_contribution','cohort_material_rate','forward_replacement_rate')
            means={k:sum(r[k] for r in rr if r[k] is not None)/sum(r[k] is not None for r in rr) for k in keys}
            modelmeans={k:sum(r['model'][k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in ('relaxation','convection','anticipation','merge_loss','nonconvection','total')}
            summary.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=c,samples=len(rr),complete_start_cohorts=sum(r['all_start_cohort_complete'] for r in rr),
                native_means_kmh_per_sec=means,model_means_kmh_per_sec=modelmeans,
                spatial_derivative_rmse=math.sqrt(sum((r['model']['total']-r['actual_spatial_rate'])**2 for r in rr)/len(rr)),
                negative_model_drift_samples=sum(r['model']['total']<0 for r in rr),clipped_samples=sum(not r['model']['unclipped'] for r in rr)))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',rows)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=summary,conditional_scalar_updates=len(rows),saved_equation_parity_checks=len(parity),
        maximum_parity_error=max(parity),native30s_state_checks=native_checks,pins=pins,
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,coefficient_fit=False,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,groups=summary,parity=max(parity)),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def audit_vsl_response_cohorts67():
    """Attribute observed/predicted mainline residence without changing flows."""
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,offramp_routing as routing
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'cohort67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
    records=load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records'];pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(manifest):sha(manifest)}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    for name in ('offramp_routing.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','canonical_harness.py'):
        path=ROOT/('evaluation/controllers/'+name if name!='canonical_harness.py' else 'diagnostics/demand_sweep/user_native_20260914/metanet_calibration_v1/'+name);pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:connected lane model and recovery fit still missVSL response; audit benchmark cohort contributions before more calibration.',
        cases=['release','release_vsl90'],cutoff=2670.1,end=3120.1,scope='FW_E31 mainline only; not Omega or overall waiting cost.',
        cohorts=['vehicles already on mainline at cutoff','subsequent source admissions','subsequent ramp merges'],
        native='Existing5s frames; disjoint identities, ramp merge events and source counts checked against native30s flows.',
        forecast='Exact route036 replays with read-only route class observer; no physical change/future input. Native/predicted same5s and30s quadrature.',
        caveat='Cohort accounting is not a causal separation of control from stochastic demand or backward spillback; new-source membership/arrival times differ between arms.',
        max_forecasts=2,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,fit=False,production_adopted=False))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());results=[];first_native=None
    def residence(series,step):
        selected=[r for r in series if abs((r['time_s']-2670.1)/step-round((r['time_s']-2670.1)/step))<1e-6]
        return {k:sum((a[k]+b[k])*(b['time_s']-a['time_s'])/7200 for a,b in zip(selected,selected[1:])) for k in ('initial','source','ramp')}
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        fp=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz');pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):z for t,z in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        times=sorted(frames);initial=frames[times[0]];seen=set(initial);label={v:'initial' for v in initial};counts=[];admissions=[]
        if first_native is None:first_native=initial
        else:assert initial==first_native
        for t in times:
            for vehicle,z in frames[t].items():
                if vehicle not in seen:
                    kind='source' if z[0]==0 else 'ramp';label[vehicle]=kind;seen.add(vehicle)
                    admissions.append(dict(vehicle=vehicle,cohort=kind,first_time_s=t,first_cell=z[0]))
            row=Counter(label[v] for v in frames[t]);assert sum(row.values())==len(frames[t])
            counts.append(dict(time_s=t,**{k:row[k] for k in ('initial','source','ramp')}))
        obs=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm
        fp=obs/'port_events.csv';pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with fp.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:events=list(csv.DictReader(f))
        ramp_ids={r['vehicle'] for r in events if r['kind']=='departure' and r['connector'] in ('10484','10490','10639','10681') and 2670.1<float(r['time_s'])<=3120.1}
        assert {r['vehicle'] for r in admissions if r['cohort']=='ramp'}==ramp_ids
        fp=obs/'flows_30s.csv';pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with fp.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:flows=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['road']=='FW_E' and 2670.1<float(r['window_end_s'])<=3120.1]
        for key,flow_key in [('source','source_admissions'),('ramp','ramp_merges')]:
            assert sum(r['cohort']==key for r in admissions)==sum(float(r[flow_key]) for r in flows)
        save(out/(arm+'_native.json'),dict(counts=counts,admissions=admissions,initial_vehicles=sorted(initial)))
        capture=next(r for r in records if r['arm']==arm);args,kw=read_primitive_capture(capture['input'],capture['sha256']);pins[capture['input']]=capture['sha256']
        kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json'));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        trace=[];original=routing.advance_inventory
        def inventory_counts(state):
            result=dict(initial=0.,source=0.,ramp=0.)
            for cell in state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E']:
                for key,n in cell.items():
                    group='initial' if key.startswith(('observed_route:','observed_null|')) else 'source' if key.startswith('expected_input:') else 'ramp' if key.startswith('expected_merge:') else None
                    assert group is not None,key;result[group]+=n
            return result
        def observe(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs):
            if fw=='FW_E' and not trace:trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec,**inventory_counts(state)))
            result=original(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs)
            if fw=='FW_E':trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec+kwargs['duration_h']*3600,**inventory_counts(state)))
            return result
        routing.advance_inventory=observe
        try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        finally:routing.advance_inventory=original
        assert len(trace)==451
        reference=root/'choice67'/('route036_s67_late_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(reference)]=sha(reference)
        with gzip.open(reference,'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
        normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred,allow_nan=False));parity={k:normalized[k]==prior[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        for t in sorted({r['time_s'] for r in pred['cells']}):
            total=sum(r['n_veh'] for r in pred['cells'] if r['time_s']==t);z=next(z for z in trace if abs(z['time_s']-t)<1e-7)
            assert abs(total-sum(z[k] for k in ('initial','source','ramp')))<1e-7
        save(out/(arm+'_model.json'),trace)
        results.append(dict(arm=arm,native_admissions=Counter(r['cohort'] for r in admissions),native={str(dt):residence(counts,dt) for dt in (5,30)},
            predicted={str(dt):residence(trace,dt) for dt in (5,30)},parity=parity))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    differences={origin:{str(dt):{k:results[1][origin][str(dt)][k]-results[0][origin][str(dt)][k] for k in ('initial','source','ramp')} for dt in (5,30)} for origin in ('native','predicted')}
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=results,differences=differences,pins=pins,new_forecasts=2,fit=False,causal_source_attribution=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,differences=differences)),flush=True)


def audit_early_vsl_cohorts(*, resume=False):
    """Four frozen750s replays and native cohort accounting, no calibration."""
    from bisect import bisect_right
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,offramp_routing as routing
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'cohort_early';out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume);ri=root/'route_inventory'
    if resume:
        assert not (out/'summary.json').exists()
        attempt=1+len(list(out.glob('resume*_protocol.json')));assert attempt<=2
        prior_protocol=load(out/'protocol.json')
        for p,h in prior_protocol['pins'].items():
            if p!=str(Path(__file__)):assert sha(p)==h,p
        (out/f'failed_helper{attempt}.txt').write_bytes((out/'helper_executed.txt').read_bytes())
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    provenance_path=OLD/'diagnostics/metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924/mainline_moments/manifest.json'
    provenance=load(provenance_path);geometry=load(ri/'compiled.json');bounds=geometry['bounds']['FW_E']
    addresses={int(k):float(v[1]) for k,v in geometry['physical'].items() if v[0]=='FW_E'}
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];start,end=2220.1,2970.1;groups=('initial','source','ramp')
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,provenance_path,ri/'compiled.json',ri/'contract.json',
        Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP'))}
    for rel in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/rel)]=sha(ROOT/rel)
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    save(out/(f'resume{attempt}_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(resuming_verified_first_trace_and_frame_cache=resume,previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS:priority agreement only; resume with bounded existing-benchmark evidence.',
        seeds=[29,43],arms=['none','vsl'],cutoff=start,end=end,scope='FW_E31 mainline only; not Omega or external waits.',
        max_replays=4,max_completed_fzp_passes=4,fit=False,new_native=0,
        forecast='Frozen route036, original past-only boundary held through750s; same saved physical arrays required.',
        native='Initial mainline residence until first exit, then each subsequent source/merge entry episode (including reentries); exact sampled accounting and30s flow agreement.',
        caveat='Accounting attribution is not causal identification of source demand or spillback. Same seed alone does not equal identical later vehicles.',
        production_adopted=False,pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[];initial_by_seed={}
    def residence(series,dt,horizon):
        z=[r for r in series if r['time_s']<=start+horizon+1e-7 and abs((r['time_s']-start)/dt-round((r['time_s']-start)/dt))<1e-6]
        assert len(z)==horizon//dt+1
        return {k:sum((a[k]+b[k])*(b['time_s']-a['time_s'])/7200 for a,b in zip(z,z[1:])) for k in groups}
    for seed in (29,43):
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            name=f's{seed}_{arm}';case=f's{seed}_early';record=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
            args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
            args=list(args);assert len(args[1])==450 and args[2]==ctx['parameters']
            for offset in range(450,750):
                step=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(start+offset,6),window_end_s=round(start+offset+1,6));args[1].append(step)
            raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
            kw['horizon_sec']=750;kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw))
            trace=[];original=routing.advance_inventory
            def inventory_counts(state):
                result=dict.fromkeys(groups,0.)
                for cell in state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E']:
                    for key,n in cell.items():
                        group='initial' if key.startswith(('observed_route:','observed_null|')) else 'source' if key.startswith('expected_input:') else 'ramp' if key.startswith('expected_merge:') else None
                        assert group is not None,key;result[group]+=n
                return result
            def observe(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs):
                if fw=='FW_E' and not trace:trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec,**inventory_counts(state)))
                result=original(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs)
