                if fw=='FW_E':trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec+kwargs['duration_h']*3600,**inventory_counts(state)))
                return result
            cached_trace=out/(name+'_model.json')
            if resume and cached_trace.exists():
                assert name=='s29_none'
                trace=load(cached_trace)
                with gzip.open(root/'vsl_native_exposure/horizon750/none.json.gz','rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
            else:
                routing.advance_inventory=observe
                try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
                finally:routing.advance_inventory=original
            assert len(trace)==751
            reference=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')/(arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(reference)]=sha(reference)
            with gzip.open(reference,'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
            normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred,allow_nan=False));parity={k:normalized[k]==prior[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values()),parity
            save(out/(name+'_model.json'),trace)
            # No native future data have entered the forecast above.
            meta=next(r for r in provenance['rows'] if (r['seed'],r['arm'])==(seed,arm));source=Path(meta['source']);stat=source.stat()
            assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(meta['source_bytes'],meta['source_mtime_ns'])
            check_path=root/'recovery_transport'/(name+'_source.json');pins[str(check_path)]=sha(check_path)
            expected=load(check_path)['extended_prefix_sha256'];digest=hashlib.sha256();ci=None;frames={}
            frame_cache=out/(name+'_frames.json.gz')
            if resume and frame_cache.exists():
                assert name=='s29_none'
                with gzip.open(frame_cache,'rt') as f:frames={float(t):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
            else:
                with source.open('rb') as f:
                    for line in f:
                        if ci is None:
                            if line.startswith(b'$VEHICLE:'):ci={x:i for i,x in enumerate(line.rstrip().split(b':',1)[1].split(b';'))}
                            continue
                        if not line.strip():continue
                        t=float(line.split(b';',1)[0])
                        if t>end+1e-7:break
                        digest.update(line)
                        if t<start-1e-7:continue
                        z=line.rstrip().split(b';');link=int(z[ci[b'LANE\\LINK\\NO']])
                        if link not in addresses:continue
                        x=addresses[link]+float(z[ci[b'POS']]);cell=max(0,min(30,bisect_right(bounds,x)-1));v=z[ci[b'NO']].decode()
                        frame=frames.setdefault(round(t,6),{});assert v not in frame
                        frame[v]=[cell,float(z[ci[b'SPEED']]),x,int(z[ci[b'LANE\\INDEX']])]
                assert digest.hexdigest()==expected
            assert sorted(frames)==[round(start+5*j,6) for j in range(151)]
            with gzip.open(out/(name+'_frames.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(dict(fields=['cell','speed_kmh','x_m','lane'],frames=frames),f)
            initial=frames[start]
            if seed not in initial_by_seed:initial_by_seed[seed]=initial
            else:assert initial==initial_by_seed[seed]
            labels={v:'initial' for v in initial};counts=[];admissions=[];previous=set(initial);seen=set(initial)
            for t,frame in sorted(frames.items()):
                for v,z in frame.items():
                    if v not in previous:
                        reentry=v in seen;seen.add(v)
                        kind='source' if z[0]==0 else 'ramp';labels[v]=kind
                        admissions.append(dict(vehicle=v,cohort=kind,first_time_s=t,first_cell=z[0],reentry=reentry))
                c=Counter(labels[v] for v in frame);assert sum(c.values())==len(frame)
                counts.append(dict(time_s=t,**{k:c[k] for k in groups}));previous=set(frame)
            truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);flow_path=Path(record['truth'])/'flows_30s.csv';pins[str(flow_path)]=sha(flow_path)
            for cohort,flowkey in [('source','source_admissions'),('ramp','ramp_merges')]:
                total=sum(float(r[flowkey]) for (t,fw,c),r in truth.flows.items() if fw=='FW_E' and start<t<=end)
                assert sum(r['cohort']==cohort for r in admissions)==total,(name,cohort,total)
            ep=Path(record['truth'])/'port_events.csv';pins[str(ep)]=sha(ep)
            with ep.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:events=list(csv.DictReader(f))
            ramp_events=[r for r in events if start<float(r['time_s'])<=end and r['kind']=='departure' and r['connector'] in ('10484','10490','10639','10681')]
            assert Counter(r['vehicle'] for r in admissions if r['cohort']=='ramp')==Counter(r['vehicle'] for r in ramp_events)
            for t,actual in truth.cells.items():
                if start-1e-7<=t<=end+1e-7:
                    actual=[r for r in actual if r['road']=='FW_E'];assert len(actual)==31
                    for r in actual:assert sum(z[0]==r['cell'] for z in frames[t].values())==r['n_veh']
            for t in sorted({r['time_s'] for r in pred['cells']}):
                z=next(z for z in trace if abs(z['time_s']-t)<1e-7)
                assert abs(sum(z[k] for k in groups)-sum(r['n_veh'] for r in pred['cells'] if r['time_s']==t))<1e-7
            save(out/(name+'_native.json'),dict(counts=counts,admissions=admissions,initial_vehicles=sorted(initial)))
            row=dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,parity=parity,native_admissions=Counter(r['cohort'] for r in admissions),source_prefix=expected,
                native={str(h):{str(dt):residence(counts,dt,h) for dt in (5,30)} for h in (450,750)},
                predicted={str(h):{str(dt):residence(trace,dt,h) for dt in (5,30)} for h in (450,750)})
            rows.append(row);save(out/(name+'_result.json'),row);print(json.dumps(dict(complete=name,admissions=row['native_admissions'])),flush=True)
    differences=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        a,b=[next(r for r in rows if (r['seed'],r['arm'])==(seed,arm)) for arm in ('none','vsl')]
        differences.append(dict(seed=seed,**{origin:{str(h):{str(dt):{k:b[origin][str(h)][str(dt)][k]-a[origin][str(h)][str(dt)][k] for k in groups} for dt in (5,30)} for h in (450,750)} for origin in ('native','predicted')}))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,differences=differences,pins=pins,new_forecasts=4,fit=False,new_native=0,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,differences=differences)),flush=True)


def audit_early_cohort_cell_response():
    """Locate response errors without fitting different coefficients to each seed."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,offramp_routing as routing
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';prior=root/'cohort_early'
    out=prior/'cell_response';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);ri=root/'route_inventory'
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];old=load(prior/'summary.json');groups=('initial','source','ramp');rows=[]
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (manifest,prior/'summary.json',Path(__file__))}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(max_replays=4,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,fit=False,
        purpose='Common-initial and later-entry cost by physical cell, compare29gain and43loss; no change to frozen SDMPC9000 runtime.',
        interval=[2220.1,2970.1],model='route036 component, distinct from yesterday coupled9000 model'))
    def integrate(series):
        return {g:[sum((a['stock'][g][c]+b['stock'][g][c])*(b['time_s']-a['time_s'])/7200 for a,b in zip(series,series[1:])) for c in range(31)] for g in groups}
    for seed in (29,43):
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            name=f's{seed}_{arm}';case=f's{seed}_early';r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
            args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];args=list(args)
            for offset in range(450,750):
                z=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);z.update(window_start_s=round(2220.1+offset,6),window_end_s=round(2221.1+offset,6));args[1].append(z)
            kw['horizon_sec']=750;kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
            kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw));trace=[];original=routing.advance_inventory
            def record(state,t):
                if abs((t-2220.1)/5-round((t-2220.1)/5))>1e-7:return
                stock={g:[0.]*31 for g in groups}
                for c,cell in enumerate(state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E']):
                    for k,n in cell.items():
                        g='initial' if k.startswith(('observed_route:','observed_null|')) else 'source' if k.startswith('expected_input:') else 'ramp' if k.startswith('expected_merge:') else None
                        assert g is not None,k;stock[g][c]+=n
                trace.append(dict(time_s=round(t,6),stock=stock))
            def observe(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs):
                if fw=='FW_E' and not trace:record(state,state.time_sec)
                result=original(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs)
                if fw=='FW_E':record(state,state.time_sec+kwargs['duration_h']*3600)
                return result
            routing.advance_inventory=observe
            try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
            finally:routing.advance_inventory=original
            pp=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')/(arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(pp)]=sha(pp)
            with gzip.open(pp,'rt') as f:reference=json.load(f)
            normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred));assert all(normalized[k]==reference[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps'))
            assert len(trace)==151;modeled=integrate(trace)
            fp=prior/(name+'_frames.json.gz');pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
            with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={float(t):z for t,z in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
            labels={v:'initial' for v in frames[2220.1]};previous=set(labels);native=[]
            for t,fr in sorted(frames.items()):
                stock={g:[0.]*31 for g in groups}
                for v,z in fr.items():
                    if v not in previous:labels[v]='source' if z[0]==0 else 'ramp'
                    stock[labels[v]][z[0]]+=1
                native.append(dict(time_s=t,stock=stock));previous=set(fr)
            actual=integrate(native);oldrow=next(x for x in old['rows'] if (x['seed'],x['arm'])==(seed,arm))
            for origin,values in [('native',actual),('predicted',modeled)]:
                for g in groups:assert abs(sum(values[g])-oldrow[origin]['750']['5'][g])<1e-8
            with gzip.open(out/(name+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(dict(native=native,predicted=trace),f)
            rows.append(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,native=actual,predicted=modeled));print(json.dumps(dict(cell_response_complete=name)),flush=True)
    differences=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        a,b=[next(r for r in rows if (r['seed'],r['arm'])==(seed,arm)) for arm in ('none','vsl')]
        differences.append(dict(seed=seed,**{origin:{g:[b[origin][g][c]-a[origin][g][c] for c in range(31)] for g in groups} for origin in ('native','predicted')}))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,differences=differences,pins=pins,new_replays=4,fit=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,initial_differences=[dict(seed=r['seed'],native=r['native']['initial'],predicted=r['predicted']['initial']) for r in differences])),flush=True)


def check_early_connected_lane_response():
    """Check two already frozen local hypotheses on larger early responses."""
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'lane_early';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);archive=root/'joint_lane_fifo'
    table=load(archive/'restoration.json')['files'];originals={}
    for rel,z in table.items():
        assert sha(ROOT/rel)==z['before'] and sha(archive/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name)==z['candidate']
        originals[rel]=(ROOT/rel).read_bytes()
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),root/'joint_lane_rec/manifest.json',root/'joint_lane_rec/reference_config.json',root/'joint_lane_25/protocol.json')}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:requested expanded036 SDMPC9000 launched from immutable copy; initial-cohort response isolated in8observer replays.',
        max_forecasts=9,variants=['connected19_25','connected_recovery24_25'],seeds=[29,43],arms=['none','vsl'],horizon=750,
        hypothesis='Current lane/route distribution may distinguish early29 benefit from early43 weak initial-cohort response better than segment means.',
        frozen_parameters='Use previous candidate coefficients and lane-exchange hazards unchanged. No fresh fit or grid.',
        evaluation='Same750 physical inputs and past-only boundaries as saved route036. Initial cohort cost, mainline/cost/flows, and NC state errors all reported. Seed43 previously inspected, not blind.',
        screening='Check initial-cohort response error reduction without losing29 improvement or worsening NC state by>10%; this diagnostic screen alone is never overall gain qualification.',
        deployment='Native9000 reads separate verified frozen tree; current checkout cores temporarily replaced then byte-restored. No frozen runtime writes, new native, or FZP scans.',pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    try:
        for rel in originals:(ROOT/rel).write_bytes((archive/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name).read_bytes())
        from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,offramp_routing as routing
        from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
        baseline_manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
        contexts={'connected19_25':lpr.load_sources(baseline_manifest),'connected_recovery24_25':lpr.load_sources(root/'joint_lane_rec/manifest.json')}
        baseline=lpr.load_sources(baseline_manifest);spec=load(root/'joint_lane_25/protocol.json')['candidate'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
        records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];cohorts=load(root/'cohort_early/summary.json');rows=[];forecasts=0;parity=None
        for seed in (29,43):
            for arm in ('none','vsl'):
                case=f's{seed}_early';r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
                args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256'];args=list(args)
                for offset in range(450,750):
                    z=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);z.update(window_start_s=round(2220.1+offset,6),window_end_s=round(2221.1+offset,6));args[1].append(z)
                raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);kw['horizon_sec']=750;kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
                kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw))
                pp=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')/(arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(pp)]=sha(pp)
                with gzip.open(pp,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
                if parity is None:
                    pred=baseline['component'].rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kw));forecasts+=1
                    norm=json.loads(json.dumps(pred));parity={k:norm[k]==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
                    save(out/'disabled_parity.json',parity)
                truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);oldrow=next(x for x in cohorts['rows'] if (x['seed'],x['arm'])==(seed,arm))
                base=_cellwise_measure(baseline['component'],old,r,truth,horizon_sec=750)
                base.update(version='route036',initial_ttt=oldrow['predicted']['750']['5']['initial'],actual_initial_ttt=oldrow['native']['750']['5']['initial']);rows.append(base)
                for version,ctx in contexts.items():
                    model=ctx['component'];run_kw=copy.deepcopy(kw);run_kw['route_lane_regions']={'FW_E':copy.deepcopy(spec)}
                    trace=[];advance=routing.advance_inventory
                    def record(state,t):
                        if abs((t-2220.1)/5-round((t-2220.1)/5))>1e-7:return
                        n=sum(n for c in state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E'] for k,n in c.items() if k.startswith(('observed_route:','observed_null|')))
                        trace.append((round(t,6),n))
                    def observe(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs):
                        if fw=='FW_E' and not trace:record(state,state.time_sec)
                        result=advance(state,cfg,fw,**kwargs)
                        if fw=='FW_E':record(state,state.time_sec+kwargs['duration_h']*3600)
                        return result
                    routing.advance_inventory=observe
                    try:pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**run_kw);forecasts+=1
                    finally:routing.advance_inventory=advance
                    assert len(trace)==151
                    with gzip.open(out/(version+'_'+case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
                    row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=750)
                    row.update(version=version,initial_ttt=sum((a[1]+b[1])*(b[0]-a[0])/7200 for a,b in zip(trace,trace[1:])),actual_initial_ttt=base['actual_initial_ttt'])
                    assert row['conservation_max']<1e-7;rows.append(row)
                    save(out/'partial.json',dict(rows=rows,forecasts=forecasts));print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,version=version,main_ttt=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],initial_ttt=row['initial_ttt'])),flush=True)
        comparisons=[]
        for seed in (29,43):
            for version in ('route036',*contexts):
                a,b=[next(x for x in rows if (x['case'],x['arm'],x['version'])==(f's{seed}_early',arm,version)) for arm in ('none','vsl')]
                comparisons.append(dict(seed=seed,version=version,initial_delta=b['initial_ttt']-a['initial_ttt'],actual_initial_delta=b['actual_initial_ttt']-a['actual_initial_ttt'],
                    delta={label:{k:b[label][k]-a[label][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','off_ttt','exits','end_n')} for label in ('actual','predicted')}))
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=rows,comparisons=comparisons,pins=pins,forecasts=forecasts,new_fit=False,new_native=0,adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,comparisons=comparisons)),flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        save(out/'failure.json',dict(error=repr(exc)));raise
    finally:
        restored={}
        for rel,content in originals.items():
            assert sha(ROOT/rel)==table[rel]['candidate'],'Concurrent source change; do not overwrite'
            (ROOT/rel).write_bytes(content);assert sha(ROOT/rel)==table[rel]['before'];restored[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel)
        save(out/'restoration.json',dict(files=restored,adopted=False))


def audit_connected_lane_speed_balance():
    """Observed-state lane equations and one bounded, shared recovery fit."""
    import numpy as np
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response,state_response_coefficients
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    from src.models.state import ControlAction
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'lane_terms25';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    control=ControlAction(vsl={'FW_E':110});lengths=[p['segment_length_km'] for p in net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E']]
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];cases=[]
    for case,arm in [('s29_early','none'),('s29_late','hold')]:
        r=next(z for z in records if (z['case'],z['arm'])==(case,arm));cases.append((r,root/'vsl_native_exposure'/(case+'_'+arm+'_frames.json.gz')))
    rec=next(z for z in load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records'] if z['arm']=='release')
    cases.append((dict(rec,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations/release')),root/'flow67/release_frames.json.gz'))
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__)),str(manifest):sha(manifest)}
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:30forecasts; downstream lane state matters, but connected19-25 autonomous prediction overcongests and recovers slowly.',
        scope='Frozen route036 equations evaluated at native lane states21-25; cell25 downstream is actual aggregate26, matching regional boundary. Only nominal110 arms.',
        fit='One bounded linear fit using seed29 early NC only; two COMMON multipliers in24/25: relaxation and anticipation, serialized as tau/base and nu ratios. Each30s lane/cell block equally weighted.',
        bounds=[1/3,3.],prior_weight=.1,max_fits=1,no_future_autonomous_inputs=True,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,
        caveat='Native5s spatial mean changes include entering/leaving vehicles. They are not individual accelerations. Observed30s ramp merges used only for diagnostic21/23 loss. No control/seed/time bonus.')
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes());rows=[]
    for record,fp in cases:
        pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:raw=json.load(f)['frames']
        frames={round(float(t),6):(z if isinstance(z,dict) else {str(a[0]):[a[1],a[4],a[2],a[3],a[6]] for a in z}) for t,z in raw.items()}
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);pins[str(truth.folder/'flows_30s.csv')]=sha(truth.folder/'flows_30s.csv')
        for t in sorted(frames)[:-1]:
            t1=round(t+5,6);before,after=frames[t],frames[t1];window=round(record['cutoff']+30*(int(round((t-record['cutoff'])/5))//6+1),6)
            for cell in range(21,26):
                for lane in (1,2,3):
                    a={v:z for v,z in before.items() if z[0]==cell and z[3]==lane};b={v:z for v,z in after.items() if z[0]==cell and z[3]==lane}
                    up=[z for z in before.values() if z[0]==cell-1 and z[3]==lane]
                    if not a or not b or not up:continue
                    speed=sum(z[1] for z in a.values())/len(a);v1=sum(z[1] for z in b.values())/len(b)
                    upstream=sum(z[1] for z in up)/len(up);rho=len(a)/lengths[cell]
                    down=sum(z[0]==cell+1 and (cell==25 or z[3]==lane) for z in before.values())/lengths[cell+1]/(3 if cell==25 else 1)
                    p=net.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell];response=cell_state_response(net,'FW_E',cell)
                    vsl=mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',cell,cfg)
                    desired=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,vsl,net.alpha_vsl,False,net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
                    nu0=mn.select_anticipation_nu(rho,net,vsl);tau,nu=state_response_coefficients(response,speed,desired,rho,down,p['rho_crit'],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0)
                    dt=1/3600;relax=dt/tau*(desired-speed);conv=dt/lengths[cell]*speed*(upstream-speed)
                    ant=-nu*dt/(tau*lengths[cell])*(down-rho)/(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'])
                    result=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh(speed,upstream,rho,down,desired,dt,lengths[cell],p['metanet_tau_h'],nu0,p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane'],net.v_min)
                    assert abs(result-max(net.v_min,speed+relax+conv+ant))<1e-8
                    merges=float(truth.flows[window,'FW_E',cell]['ramp_merges'])*120. if lane==1 else 0.
                    loss=response.get('delta_merge',net.metanet_delta_merge)*dt*merges*speed/(lengths[cell]*(rho+p['metanet_kappa_veh_km_lane']))
                    final=max(net.v_min,result-loss);stay=a.keys()&b.keys();enter=b.keys()-a.keys();leave=a.keys()-b.keys()
                    stayer=sum(b[v][1]-a[v][1] for v in stay)/len(b)/5
                    replacement=(sum(b[v][1]-speed for v in enter)-sum(a[v][1]-speed for v in leave))/len(b)/5
                    actual=(v1-speed)/5;assert abs(stayer+replacement-actual)<1e-8
                    rows.append(dict(case=record['case'],time_s=t,window_end=window,cell=cell,lane=lane,n=len(a),v=speed,rho=rho,downstream_rho=down,
                        desired=desired,relaxation=relax,convection=conv,anticipation=ant,merge_loss=loss,predicted_rate=final-speed,
                        actual_rate=actual,stayer_rate=stayer,replacement_rate=replacement,acceleration=sum(z[4]*3.6 for z in a.values())/len(a),
                        clipped=abs(final-(speed+relax+conv+ant-loss))>1e-8))
    blocks=[]
    for key in sorted({(r['case'],r['window_end'],r['cell'],r['lane']) for r in rows}):
        rr=[r for r in rows if (r['case'],r['window_end'],r['cell'],r['lane'])==key]
        blocks.append(dict(case=key[0],window_end=key[1],cell=key[2],lane=key[3],samples=len(rr),
            **{k:sum(r[k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in ('relaxation','convection','anticipation','merge_loss','predicted_rate','actual_rate','acceleration')},
            clipped=sum(r['clipped'] for r in rr)))
    train=[r for r in blocks if r['case']=='s29_early' and r['cell'] in (24,25) and r['samples']==6 and r['clipped']==0]
    x=np.array([[r['relaxation'],r['anticipation']] for r in train]);y=np.array([r['actual_rate']-r['convection'] for r in train])
    assert len(train)>=30 and np.linalg.matrix_rank(x)==2
    weight=math.sqrt(protocol['prior_weight']*len(train));xx=np.vstack([x,weight*np.eye(2)]);yy=np.r_[y,weight*np.ones(2)]
    # Exact two-dimensional box-constrained quadratic: interior optimum and
    # each of four edges (including corners). No optimizer dependency/grid.
    candidates=[];unconstrained=np.linalg.lstsq(xx,yy,rcond=None)[0]
    if np.all(unconstrained>=1/3) and np.all(unconstrained<=3.):candidates.append(unconstrained)
    for j in (0,1):
        other=1-j
        for bound in (1/3,3.):
            point=np.zeros(2);point[j]=bound
            point[other]=np.clip(np.dot(xx[:,other],yy-xx[:,j]*bound)/np.dot(xx[:,other],xx[:,other]),1/3,3.)
            candidates.append(point)
    best=min(candidates,key=lambda q:float(np.sum((xx@q-yy)**2)));aa,bb=map(float,best)
    active_bounds=[-1 if abs(v-1/3)<1e-8 else 1 if abs(v-3.)<1e-8 else 0 for v in best]
    comparisons=[]
    for case in sorted({r['case'] for r in rows}):
        for cell in range(21,26):
            rr=[r for r in blocks if r['case']==case and r['cell']==cell and r['samples']==6 and not r['clipped']]
            if not rr:continue
            before=[r['predicted_rate']-r['actual_rate'] for r in rr]
            after=[aa*r['relaxation']+r['convection']+bb*r['anticipation']-r['merge_loss']-r['actual_rate'] for r in rr] if cell in (24,25) else before
            comparisons.append(dict(case=case,cell=cell,blocks=len(rr),means={k:sum(r[k] for r in rr)/len(rr) for k in ('relaxation','convection','anticipation','merge_loss','predicted_rate','actual_rate','acceleration')},
                baseline_rmse=math.sqrt(sum(e*e for e in before)/len(before)),candidate_rmse=math.sqrt(sum(e*e for e in after)/len(after))))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    with gzip.open(out/'rows.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(rows,f,allow_nan=False)
    save(out/'blocks.json',blocks);save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=len(rows),comparisons=comparisons,fit=dict(relaxation_multiplier=aa,anticipation_multiplier=bb,tau_multiplier=1/aa,nu_multiplier=bb/aa,
        active_bounds=active_bounds,training_blocks=len(train),condition_number=float(np.linalg.cond(x)),solver='exact 2D box quadratic'),pins=pins,
        new_forecasts=0,autonomous_qualified=False,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,fit=[aa,bb],comparisons=comparisons)),flush=True)


def calibrate_joint_lane_local():
    """Three predeclared local coefficient candidates; no held-out refitting."""
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'lane_fit29';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False);archived=root/'joint_lane_fifo';ri=root/'route_inventory'
    restoration=load(archived/'restoration.json')['files'];originals={};pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for rel,row in restoration.items():
        target=ROOT/rel;candidate=archived/'executed_sources'/target.name
        assert sha(target)==row['before'] and sha(candidate)==row['candidate']
        originals[rel]=target.read_bytes();pins[str(candidate)]=sha(candidate)
    source=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';document=load(source)
    original_path=ROOT/document['sources']['reference_config']['path'];original=load(original_path)
    assert sha(original_path)==document['sources']['reference_config']['sha256']
    variants={'shape':(1.5,1.),'merge':(1.,.5),'both':(1.5,.5)}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS:priority clarification; resumed bounded local calibration.',
        hypothesis='Aggregate-calibrated FD shape and merge coefficient may not transfer to the new per-lane representation: recovery is too slow but later lane accumulation is too small even with joint boundary matching.',
        candidates=variants,scope='Common FD-shape multiplier in19-21 and merge multiplier only at21. Critical densities, all other coefficients and lateral hazards frozen.',
        train='Four autonomous450s seed29 late arms only. Select once using local19-21 N/v/q errors plus physically scaled response errors.',
        validation='Selected candidate only: four seed67 late arms and seed29 early NC; no validation-driven retuning. All datasets previously inspected; not pristine holdout.',
        loss='Local mean squared errors: N/5veh, v/15kmh, flow/800vph. Plus mean squared mainline/component TTT response error with max(1vehh,abs(observed delta)) scale.',
        gate='No capacity/reward bonus; matched conservation and explicit coefficient binding. Local training error must improve5%, response not worsen; heldout major gain/loss signs and best choice must hold, state degradation recorded. Passing only a best-choice test is insufficient.',
        maximum_new_forecasts=19,future_inputs=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,production_adopted=False))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[];forecasts=0;status='failed';error=None;selection=None;begin=time.perf_counter()
    try:
        for rel in originals:(ROOT/rel).write_bytes((archived/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name).read_bytes())
        from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
        from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import cell_state_response
        from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
        spec=load(archived/'protocol.json')['candidate'];base=lpr.load_sources(source)
        records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];train=[r for r in records if r['case']=='s29_late'];assert len(train)==4
        folder=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930'
        validation=[dict(r,case='s67_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(folder/'observations'/r['arm'])) for r in load(folder/'capture.json')['records']]
        validation += [next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_early','none'))]
        contexts={'baseline':base};bindings=[]
        for name,(shape,merge) in variants.items():
            config=copy.deepcopy(original);fd=config['freeway']['physical_cell_fd']['FW_E']
            local=config['freeway']['state_response']['FW_E']['cell_overrides']
            for cell in ('19','20','21'):fd[cell]['metanet_a_m']*=shape
            local['21']['delta_merge']*=merge
            path=out/(name+'_config.json');save(path,config);manifest=copy.deepcopy(document)
            manifest['sources']['reference_config']=dict(path=path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(path))
            manifest['qualification']='Unqualified regional lane calibration candidate; archived source required. No production/native deployment.'
            path=out/(name+'_manifest.json');save(path,manifest);contexts[name]=lpr.load_sources(path)
            cfg=contexts[name]['component']._config('FW_E',contexts[name]['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E'])
            initial=base['component']._config('FW_E',base['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E'])
            for cell in (19,20,21):
                actual=cfg.network.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell]['metanet_a_m'];expected=initial.network.freeway_segment_params['FW_E'][cell]['metanet_a_m']*shape
                assert abs(actual-expected)<1e-10
                bindings.append(dict(variant=name,cell=cell,shape=actual,delta=cell_state_response(cfg.network,'FW_E',cell).get('delta_merge')))
            assert abs(cell_state_response(cfg.network,'FW_E',21)['delta_merge']-cell_state_response(initial.network,'FW_E',21)['delta_merge']*merge)<1e-10
        save(out/'bindings.json',bindings)
        def assess(pred,record,name,model):
            truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth);row['variant']=name
            errors={'n':[],'v':[],'q':[]}
            for z in pred['cells']:
                if z['cell'] not in (19,20,21):continue
                t=round(z['time_s'],6);a=next(v for v in truth.cells[t] if v['road']=='FW_E' and v['cell']==z['cell'])
                errors['n'].append(z['n_veh']-a['n_veh'])
                if a['v_kmh'] is not None:errors['v'].append(z['v_kmh']-a['v_kmh'])
            for z in pred['flows']:
                if z['cell'] not in (19,20,21):continue
                a=truth.flows[round(z['window_end_s'],6),'FW_E',z['cell']]
                errors['q'].append((z['downstream_crossings']-float(a['downstream_crossings']))*120.)
            row['local_rmse']={k:math.sqrt(sum(v*v for v in vals)/len(vals)) for k,vals in errors.items()}
            row['local_loss']=sum((row['local_rmse'][k]/scale)**2 for k,scale in [('n',5.),('v',15.),('q',800.)])/3
            detail=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]['joint_lane_region'];assert detail['route_marginal_max_error']<1e-7
            row['route_error']=detail['route_marginal_max_error'];rows.append(row)
            save(out/'partial_results.json',dict(rows=rows,forecasts=forecasts))
            return row
        def execute(record,name):
            nonlocal forecasts
            case,arm=record['case'],record['arm'];context=contexts[name];model=context['component']
            args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256']);pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
            rawpath=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(rawpath)]=sha(rawpath)
            kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(rawpath));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage';kw['route_lane_regions']={'FW_E':spec}
            pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kw));forecasts+=1
            with gzip.open(out/(name+'_'+case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            return pred
        def summary(name,case):
            group=[z for z in rows if z['variant']==name and z['case']==case];pairs=[]
            for a,b in [('hold','release'),('hold','hold_vsl90'),('release','release_vsl90')]:
                left=next(z for z in group if z['arm']==a);right=next(z for z in group if z['arm']==b)
                pairs.append(dict(reference=a,candidate=b,**{key:{k:right[key][k]-left[key][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','exits')} for key in ('actual','predicted')}))
            response=sum(((p['predicted'][k]-p['actual'][k])/max(1.,abs(p['actual'][k])))**2 for p in pairs for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt'))/6
            local=sum(z['local_loss'] for z in group)/len(group)
            return dict(variant=name,case=case,local_loss=local,response_loss=response,total_loss=local+response,pairs=pairs,
                actual_best=min(group,key=lambda z:z['actual']['ttt'])['arm'],predicted_best=min(group,key=lambda z:z['predicted']['ttt'])['arm'])
        for record in train:
            path=archived/(record['case']+'_'+record['arm']+'.json.gz');pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
            if forecasts==0:
                parity=json.loads(json.dumps(execute(record,'baseline'),allow_nan=False))
                assert all(parity[k]==prior[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps'))
                save(out/'parity.json',dict(exact=True,tables=['cells','flows','ports','ramps']))
            assess(prior,record,'baseline',base['component'])
            for name in variants:
                row=assess(execute(record,name),record,name,contexts[name]['component'])
                print(json.dumps(dict(stage='train',arm=record['arm'],variant=name,local_rmse=row['local_rmse'],main_ttt=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],forecasts=forecasts)),flush=True)
        train_scores=[summary(name,'s29_late') for name in contexts];ranked=sorted(train_scores[1:],key=lambda z:z['total_loss']);selected=ranked[0]['variant']
        selection=dict(selected=selected,train_scores=train_scores,scope='Selected before evaluating67/early29; do not select again after validation.')
        save(out/'selection.json',selection)
        for record in validation:
            path=archived/(record['case']+'_'+record['arm']+'.json.gz')
            if path.exists():
                pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
                with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
            else:prior=execute(record,'baseline')
            assess(prior,record,'baseline',base['component'])
            row=assess(execute(record,selected),record,selected,contexts[selected]['component'])
            print(json.dumps(dict(stage='validation',case=record['case'],arm=record['arm'],variant=selected,local_rmse=row['local_rmse'],main_ttt=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],forecasts=forecasts)),flush=True)
        selection['validation_scores']=[summary(name,'s67_late') for name in ('baseline',selected)]
        save(out/'selection.json',selection);assert forecasts<=19;status='complete_not_adopted'
    except BaseException as exc:
        error=repr(exc);raise
    finally:
        restored={}
        for rel,content in originals.items():
            assert sha(ROOT/rel)==restoration[rel]['candidate'],'Concurrent core change: do not overwrite'
            (ROOT/rel).write_bytes(content);restored[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel);assert restored[rel]==restoration[rel]['before']
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(status=status,error=error,rows=rows,selection=selection,new_forecasts=forecasts,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-begin,
            restored=restored,pins=pins,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))


def audit_joint_inlet_destinations():
    """Native posthoc fate at lane entry versus two exact saved-model replays."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'lane_dest67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    prior=root/'joint_lane_fifo';pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))};native=[];crossings=[];coverage=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        source=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz');events=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm/'port_events.csv'
        pins[str(source)]=sha(source);pins[str(events)]=sha(events)
        with gzip.open(source,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),5):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        exits=defaultdict(list)
        with events.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for z in csv.DictReader(f):
                if z['connector']=='10483' and z['kind']=='arrival':exits[z['vehicle']].append(float(z['time_s']))
        past=defaultdict(list)
        for t,frame in frames.items():
            for vehicle,z in frame.items():
                if z[0]>=21:past[vehicle].append(t)
        times=sorted(frames);counts=defaultdict(float)
        for t,t1 in zip(times,times[1:]):
            before,after=frames[t],frames[t1];block=int(round(t-2670.1)//150)
            for vehicle,z in after.items():
                if not (z[0]==19 and vehicle in before and before[vehicle][0]<=18):continue
                off=min((v for v in exits[vehicle] if v>=t1),default=math.inf)
                through=min((v for v in past[vehicle] if v>=t1),default=math.inf)
                if off==through==math.inf:target='unresolved'
                elif off<through:target='observed_exit10483'
                else:target='observed_past10483'
                counts[block,z[3],target]+=1
                crossings.append(dict(arm=arm,time=t1,vehicle=vehicle,lane=z[3],fate=target,
                    confirmation_time=min(off,through) if target!='unresolved' else None,
                    previous_cell=before[vehicle][0],previous_lane=before[vehicle][3]))
        for block in range(3):
            for lane in (1,2,3):
                row=dict(arm=arm,block=block,lane=lane,**{k:counts[block,lane,k] for k in ('observed_exit10483','observed_past10483','unresolved')})
                row['total']=sum(row[k] for k in ('observed_exit10483','observed_past10483','unresolved'));native.append(row)
        coverage.append(dict(arm=arm,total=sum(v for v in counts.values()),unresolved=sum(v for (b,g,k),v in counts.items() if k=='unresolved')))
    save(out/'native.json',dict(rows=native,crossings=crossings,coverage=coverage,pins=pins,
        interpretation='Later realized exit10483 or passage beyond its source, not desired-demand labels. Unresolved retained. Future labels are analysis only, never prediction inputs. Later port events may confirm exits after3120.1; pass observations stop at3120.1, giving unequal censoring.'))
    restore=load(prior/'restoration.json')['files'];originals={};traces={};parities={};status='failed';error=None
    for rel,row in restore.items():
        target=ROOT/rel;candidate=prior/'executed_sources'/target.name
        assert sha(target)==row['before'] and sha(candidate)==row['candidate']
        originals[rel]=target.read_bytes();pins[str(candidate)]=sha(candidate)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:flow-only boundary matching insufficient; marginal lane injection incomplete.',
        hypothesis='The fixed target-conditioned inlet distribution may differ from observed joint lane/fate arrivals. Observe accepted class flux before changing coefficients.',
        new_rollout_budget=2,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,new_fits=0,pins=pins,
        future_inputs=False,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    try:
        for rel in originals:(ROOT/rel).write_bytes((prior/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name).read_bytes())
        from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
        from evaluation.controllers.physical_lane_groups import RouteLaneRegion
        spec=load(prior/'protocol.json')['candidate'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
        context=lpr.load_sources(root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json');model=context['component']
        plan=RouteLaneRegion.plan
        records=load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records']
        for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
            trace=[]
            def observe(self,state,cfg,mainline,requests,inter,receiving,ramps,capacity):
                route=cfg.network.offramp_route_inventory;row=state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E'][18]
                eligible={k:n for k,n in row.items() if not(k.rsplit('|',1)[-1] in route['branches'] and route['branches'][k.rsplit('|',1)[-1]]['source_cell']==18)}
                total=sum(eligible.values());rate=mainline[18]*cfg.simulation.T_f_h
                requested=[{k:n*rate/total*self.inlet.get(k.rsplit('|',1)[-1],[1/3]*3)[g] for k,n in eligible.items()} if total else {} for g in range(3)]
                plan(self,state,cfg,mainline,requests,inter,receiving,ramps,capacity)
                accepted=[];combined=defaultdict(float)
                for g,row in enumerate(requested):
                    scale=self.incoming[19][g]/sum(row.values()) if sum(row.values()) else 0.
                    assert -.0000001<=scale<=1.0000001
                    by_target=defaultdict(float)
                    for k,n in row.items():combined[k]+=n*scale;by_target[k.rsplit('|',1)[-1]]+=n*scale
                    assert abs(sum(by_target.values())-self.incoming[19][g])<1e-9
                    accepted.append(dict(by_target))
                actual=self.face_classes[18]
                assert max([abs(combined.get(k,0.)-actual.get(k,0.)) for k in combined.keys()|actual.keys()]+[0.])<1e-9
                trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec,accepted_lane_targets=accepted,inlet=copy.deepcopy(self.inlet)))
            record=next(z for z in records if z['arm']==arm);args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256'])
            pins[record['input']]=record['sha256'];raw=ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
            kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            kw['route_lane_regions']={'FW_E':spec}
            RouteLaneRegion.plan=observe
            try:pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**kw)
            finally:RouteLaneRegion.plan=plan
            source=prior/('s67_late_'+arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(source)]=sha(source)
            with gzip.open(source,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
            normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred,allow_nan=False));check={k:old[k]==normalized[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
            assert all(check.values());parities[arm]=check;assert len(trace)==450
            save(out/(arm+'_trace.json'),trace);traces[arm]=trace
        status='complete'
    except BaseException as exc:error=repr(exc);raise
    finally:
        restored={}
        for rel,content in originals.items():
            assert sha(ROOT/rel)==restore[rel]['candidate']
            (ROOT/rel).write_bytes(content);restored[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel);assert restored[rel]==restore[rel]['before']
        rows=[]
        for arm,trace in traces.items():
            for block in range(3):
                for g in range(3):
                    targets=defaultdict(float)
                    for z in trace[block*150:(block+1)*150]:
                        for k,n in z['accepted_lane_targets'][g].items():targets[k]+=n
                    rows.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,lane=g+1,targets=dict(targets),total=sum(targets.values())))
        for path,h in pins.items():assert sha(path)==h,path
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(status=status,error=error,rows=rows,native=native,coverage=coverage,parity=parities,
            restored=restored,pins=pins,new_forecasts=len(parities),goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,coverage=coverage,native_lane1=[r for r in native if r['lane']==1],model_lane1=[r for r in rows if r['lane']==1])),flush=True)


def diagnose_joint_lane_inlet(*, joint_targets=False, downstream_lanes=False):
    """Finite future-boundary diagnostic; restore rejected candidate on exit."""
    assert not downstream_lanes or joint_targets
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/('lane_dn67' if downstream_lanes else 'lane_joint67' if joint_targets else 'lane_inlet67');out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    archived=root/'joint_lane_fifo';restoration=load(archived/'restoration.json')['files']
    originals={};pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))}
    for rel,row in restoration.items():
        target=ROOT/rel;candidate=archived/'executed_sources'/target.name
        assert sha(target)==row['before'] and sha(candidate)==row['candidate']
        originals[rel]=target.read_bytes();pins[str(candidate)]=sha(candidate)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:rejected regional joint lane model and isolated incomplete marginal boundary injection.',
        evidence='Middle150s native lane19.1 n6->31, incoming104/out78; model n8.26->9.43,in83.15/out81.60. Main contribution is incoming pulse/partition, not within-cell net lateral transfer.',
        candidate='Archived rejected joint_lane_fifo, frozen coefficients and exchange hazards.',
        variants=['observed_joint_target_lane_first300'] if joint_targets else ['exact_saved_candidate_parity','observed_total_face18_flow','observed_total_and_endpoint_lane_share'],
        max_forecasts=2 if joint_targets else 5,arms=['release','release_vsl90'],cutoff=2670.1,horizon_sec=300 if joint_targets else 450,
        future_inputs='First300s actual entering targetXlane matrix from posthoc observed10483 exit/passage, all entering vehicles resolved; source class inventory and lane receiving still constrain acceptance. No future speed/state resets.' if joint_targets else 'Only actual face18 flow in5s windows, spread uniformly over five1s steps; optional receiving-side lane proportions from same endpoints. No future speed/state resets.',
        interpretation='Cause separation only, not autonomous validation or global causal gain. Accepted route conservation and lane receiving budgets retained; requested-minus-accepted logged. Cross-boundary lane changes make endpoint shares approximate crossing shares.',
        downstream_density=('Actual5s lane22 densities replace only downstream anticipation input at21; no future speed or stock reset, no change to22 physical receiving.' if downstream_lanes else None),
        new_native=0,new_fzp=0,refit=False,production_adopted=False,pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    results=[];new_forecasts=0;final_status='failed';error=None
    executed_hashes={}
    try:
        for rel in originals:(ROOT/rel).write_bytes((archived/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name).read_bytes())
        if joint_targets:
            target=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers/physical_lane_groups.py';code=target.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
            old="""                total = sum(eligible.values());requests = []
                for g in range(self.groups):
                    row = {k:n*mainline[i]*dt/total*self.inlet.get(k.rsplit('|',1)[-1],[1./self.groups]*self.groups)[g]
                           for k,n in eligible.items()} if total else {}
                    requests.append((None,g,row))"""
            new="""                total = sum(eligible.values());requests = []
                diagnostic = getattr(self, '_diagnostic_joint_inlet', None)
                if diagnostic is not None:
                    totals = {}
                    for key,n in eligible.items():
                        label = key.rsplit('|',1)[-1]
                        totals[label] = totals.get(label,0.)+n
                    # Future observed target/lane requests are conditional only.
                    # Preserve every source class; no redraw or negative stock.
                    for g in range(self.groups):
                        row = {}
                        for key,n in eligible.items():
                            label = key.rsplit('|',1)[-1];available = totals[label]
                            requested = diagnostic.get(label,[0.]*self.groups)
                            stock_factor = min(1.,available/sum(requested)) if sum(requested) else 0.
                            row[key] = n/available*requested[g]*stock_factor if available else 0.
                        requests.append((None,g,row))
                else:
                    for g in range(self.groups):
                        row = {k:n*mainline[i]*dt/total*self.inlet.get(k.rsplit('|',1)[-1],[1./self.groups]*self.groups)[g]
                               for k,n in eligible.items()} if total else {}
                        requests.append((None,g,row))"""
            assert code.count(old)==1;target.write_text(code.replace(old,new),encoding='utf-8')
            (out/'conditional_source.txt').write_bytes(target.read_bytes())
        for rel in originals:executed_hashes[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel)
        from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
        from evaluation.controllers.physical_lane_groups import RouteLaneRegion
        from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
        spec=load(archived/'protocol.json')['candidate'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
        manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
        originals_plan=RouteLaneRegion.plan;original_speed=RouteLaneRegion.speed
        captures=load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records']
        if joint_targets:
            labels_path=root/'lane_dest67/native.json';labels=load(labels_path)['crossings'];pins[str(labels_path)]=sha(labels_path)
        for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
            capture=next(z for z in captures if z['arm']==arm)
            record=dict(capture,case='s67_late',role='conditional_diagnostic',reference='release',cutoff=2670.1,
                truth=str(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm))
            source=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz');pins[str(source)]=sha(source)
            with gzip.open(source,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),5):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
            times=sorted(frames);incoming=[]
            for t,t1 in zip(times,times[1:]):
                before,after=frames[t],frames[t1];counts=[0]*3
                for vehicle,z in after.items():
                    if z[0]==19 and vehicle in before and before[vehicle][0]<=18:counts[z[3]-1]+=1
                assert sum(counts)==sum(a[0]<=18 and after[v][0]>=19 for v,a in before.items() if v in after)
                incoming.append(counts)
            native_blocks=load(root/'bottleneck_lane67/summary.json')['boundaries']
            for block in range(3):
                native=next(z['downstream'] for z in native_blocks if (z['arm'],z['block'],z['cell'])==(arm,block,18))
                assert sum(sum(row) for row in incoming[block*30:(block+1)*30])==native
            args,kw=read_primitive_capture(capture['input'],capture['sha256']);pins[capture['input']]=capture['sha256']
            if joint_targets:
                args=list(args);args[1]=args[1][:300];kw['horizon_sec']=300
            rawpath=ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json';kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(rawpath))
            kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage';kw['route_lane_regions']={'FW_E':spec}
            pins[str(rawpath)]=sha(rawpath);pins[str(ri/'contract.json')]=sha(ri/'contract.json')
            if arm=='release' and not joint_targets:
                parity=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kw));new_forecasts+=1
                with gzip.open(archived/('s67_late_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'rt') as f:prior=json.load(f)
                normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(parity,allow_nan=False))
                check={k:normalized[k]==prior[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
                save(out/'parity.json',check);assert all(check.values())
            for mode in (('joint',) if joint_targets else ('total','total_and_lanes')):
                trace=[]
                def conditional_plan(self,state,cfg,mainline,requests,inter,receiving,ramps,capacity):
                    step=round(state.time_sec-2670.1);assert abs(state.time_sec-2670.1-step)<1e-6 and 0<=step<(300 if joint_targets else 450)
                    counts=incoming[step//5];rate=sum(counts)*720.;mainline[18]=rate
                    old_inlet=self.inlet
                    requested_joint=None
                    if joint_targets:
                        stamp=round(2670.1+5*(step//5+1),5)
                        observed=[z for z in labels if z['arm']==arm and abs(z['time']-stamp)<1e-6]
                        assert len(observed)==sum(counts) and all(z['fate']!='unresolved' for z in observed)
                        requested_joint={'10483':[0.]*3,'terminal':[0.]*3}
                        for z in observed:
                            target='10483' if z['fate']=='observed_exit10483' else 'terminal'
                            requested_joint[target][z['lane']-1]+=.2
                        self._diagnostic_joint_inlet=requested_joint
                    if mode=='total_and_lanes':
                        weights=[n/sum(counts) for n in counts] if sum(counts) else [1/3]*3
                        self.inlet={k.rsplit('|',1)[-1]:list(weights) for k in state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells']['FW_E'][18]}
                    try:originals_plan(self,state,cfg,mainline,requests,inter,receiving,ramps,capacity)
                    finally:self.inlet=old_inlet
                    trace.append(dict(time_s=state.time_sec,requested_vph=rate,accepted_vph=inter[18],
                        requested_endpoint_lanes=counts if mode=='total_and_lanes' else None,
                        requested_joint_veh=requested_joint,
                        accepted_joint_classes=dict(self.face_classes[18]) if joint_targets else None,
                        accepted_lane_veh=list(self.incoming[19])))
                def conditional_speed(self,i,state,cfg,control,mn,exposure,delta,kappa):
                    if i!=21:return original_speed(self,i,state,cfg,control,mn,exposure,delta,kappa)
                    step=round(state.time_sec-2670.1);stamp=round(2670.1+5*(step//5),5)
                    assert 22 not in self.n0
                    # Cause separation only. All actual lane22 state remains external;
                    # do not reset model stock/speed or alter accepted lane transfers.
                    self.n0[22]=[sum(z[0]==22 and z[3]==g for z in frames[stamp].values()) for g in (1,2,3)]
                    try:return original_speed(self,i,state,cfg,control,mn,exposure,delta,kappa)
                    finally:del self.n0[22]
                RouteLaneRegion.plan=conditional_plan
                if downstream_lanes:RouteLaneRegion.speed=conditional_speed
                try:pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kw))
                finally:RouteLaneRegion.plan=originals_plan;RouteLaneRegion.speed=original_speed
                new_forecasts+=1
                with gzip.open(out/(mode+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
                save(out/(mode+'_'+arm+'_input.json'),trace)
                truth=ObservationData(record['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,record,truth,horizon_sec=300 if joint_targets else 450)
                row.update(mode=mode,requested=sum(z['requested_vph']/3600 for z in trace),accepted=sum(z['accepted_vph']/3600 for z in trace),
                    flow_boundary_max_error=max(abs(z['requested_vph']-z['accepted_vph']) for z in trace))
                detail=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]['joint_lane_region'];rr=detail['rows']
                row['lane19']={str(block):dict(end_n=next(z['n_veh'] for z in rr if z['cell']==19 and z['lane']==1 and abs(z['time_s']-(2820.1+150*block))<1e-6),
                    weighted_v=sum(z['n_veh']*z['v_kmh'] for z in rr if z['cell']==19 and z['lane']==1 and 2670.1+150*block<z['time_s']<=2820.1+150*block+1e-6)/sum(z['n_veh'] for z in rr if z['cell']==19 and z['lane']==1 and 2670.1+150*block<z['time_s']<=2820.1+150*block+1e-6)) for block in range(2 if joint_targets else 3)}
                row['cell19_through']=sum(z['downstream_crossings'] for z in pred['flows'] if z['cell']==19)
                assert row['conservation_max']<1e-7 and detail['route_marginal_max_error']<1e-7
                results.append(row);save(out/'partial_results.json',dict(rows=results,forecasts=new_forecasts))
                print(json.dumps(dict(mode=mode,arm=arm,accepted=row['accepted'],requested=row['requested'],lane19=row['lane19'],through=row['cell19_through'])),flush=True)
        final_status='complete_conditional_only'
    except BaseException as exc:
        error=repr(exc);raise
    finally:
        restored={}
        for rel,content in originals.items():
            assert sha(ROOT/rel)==executed_hashes.get(rel,restoration[rel]['candidate']), 'Do not overwrite an unrelated concurrent source change'
            (ROOT/rel).write_bytes(content);restored[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel)
            assert restored[rel]==restoration[rel]['before']
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(status=final_status,error=error,rows=results,new_forecasts=new_forecasts,
            restored=restored,executed_hashes=executed_hashes,pins=pins,autonomous_qualified=False,production_adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))


def audit_joint_lane_accumulation():
    """Endpoint balances versus saved rejected-candidate accepted lane fluxes."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'lane_accumulation67'
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(Path(__file__))};all_rows=[];block_rows=[];errors=[]
    initial_path=root/'bottleneck_lane67/current_joint_inventory.json';pins[str(initial_path)]=sha(initial_path)
    initial=next(z for z in load(initial_path)['cases'] if z['case']=='s67_late')
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        source=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz');prediction=root/'joint_lane_fifo'/('s67_late_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        pins[str(source)]=sha(source);pins[str(prediction)]=sha(prediction)
        with gzip.open(source,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),5):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        with gzip.open(prediction,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
        lane_rows=pred['diagnostics']['roads'][0]['joint_lane_region']['rows']
        history={g:next(z['n'] for z in initial['cells'] if (z['road'],z['cell'],z['lane'])==('FW_E',19,g)) for g in (1,2,3)}
        model=[]
        for z in lane_rows:
            if z['cell']!=19:continue
            g=z['lane'];n0=history[g];n1=z['n_veh'];long_in=z['mainline_in_veh'];long_out=z['mainline_out_veh']
            assert z['merge_veh']==0
            lateral=n1-n0-long_in+long_out
            model.append(dict(kind='model',arm=arm,start=round(z['time_s']-1,5),end=round(z['time_s'],5),lane=g,
                n0=n0,n1=n1,long_in=long_in,long_out=long_out,lateral_net=lateral,
                unresolved_net=0.,crossing_change_in=0.,crossing_change_out=0.,v1=z['v_kmh']))
            history[g]=n1
        actual=[];times=sorted(frames)
        for t,t1 in zip(times,times[1:]):
            before,after=frames[t],frames[t1]
            for g in (1,2,3):
                old={k:v for k,v in before.items() if v[0]==19 and v[3]==g}
                new={k:v for k,v in after.items() if v[0]==19 and v[3]==g}
                long_in=long_out=lat_in=lat_out=unresolved_in=unresolved_out=cin=cout=0
                for vehicle,b in new.items():
                    a=before.get(vehicle)
                    if a is None:unresolved_in+=1
                    elif a[0]<19:
                        long_in+=1
                        # Physical lanes2/3/4 of link2 map to lanes1/2/3 after10613.
                        prior_lane=a[3]-1 if a[0]<=18 else a[3]
                        cin+=prior_lane!=g
                    elif a[0]==19:
                        lat_in+=a[3]!=g
                    else:unresolved_in+=1
                for vehicle,a in old.items():
                    b=after.get(vehicle)
                    if b is None:unresolved_out+=1
                    elif b[0]>19:
                        long_out+=1;cout+=b[3]!=g
                    elif b[0]==19:lat_out+=b[3]!=g
                    else:unresolved_out+=1
                row=dict(kind='actual',arm=arm,start=t,end=t1,lane=g,n0=len(old),n1=len(new),
                    long_in=long_in,long_out=long_out,lateral_net=lat_in-lat_out,
                    lateral_in=lat_in,lateral_out=lat_out,unresolved_net=unresolved_in-unresolved_out,
                    unresolved_events=unresolved_in+unresolved_out,crossing_change_in=cin,crossing_change_out=cout,
                    v1=sum(z[1] for z in new.values())/len(new) if new else None)
                residual=row['n1']-row['n0']-long_in+long_out-row['lateral_net']-row['unresolved_net']
                assert residual==0;actual.append(row)
        for kind,rows in [('actual',actual),('model',model)]:
            for block in range(3):
                lo=round(2670.1+150*block,5);hi=round(lo+150,5)
                for g in (1,2,3):
                    rr=[z for z in rows if z['lane']==g and lo<=z['start']<hi]
                    assert len(rr)==(30 if kind=='actual' else 150)
                    data=dict(kind=kind,arm=arm,block=block,lane=g,n0=rr[0]['n0'],n1=rr[-1]['n1'])
                    for key in ('long_in','long_out','lateral_net','unresolved_net','crossing_change_in','crossing_change_out'):
                        data[key]=sum(z[key] for z in rr)
                    residual=data['n1']-data['n0']-data['long_in']+data['long_out']-data['lateral_net']-data['unresolved_net']
                    assert abs(residual)<1e-8;data['residual']=residual
                    data['weighted_end_speed']=sum(z['v1']*z['n1'] for z in rr if z['n1'])/sum(z['n1'] for z in rr)
                    block_rows.append(data)
            all_rows.extend(rows)
        for block in range(3):
            for g in (1,2,3):
                a=next(z for z in block_rows if (z['arm'],z['kind'],z['block'],z['lane'])==(arm,'actual',block,g))
                m=next(z for z in block_rows if (z['arm'],z['kind'],z['block'],z['lane'])==(arm,'model',block,g))
                data={k:m[k]-a[k] for k in ('n0','n1','long_in','long_out','lateral_net','unresolved_net')}
                assert abs(data['n1']-data['n0']-data['long_in']+data['long_out']-data['lateral_net']-data['unresolved_net'])<1e-8
                errors.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,lane=g,model_minus_actual=data,
                    crossing_lane_uncertain=dict(in_count=a['crossing_change_in'],out_count=a['crossing_change_out'])))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=all_rows,blocks=block_rows,errors=errors,pins=pins,
        interpretation='Exact membership balances between5s endpoints. Cross-boundary lane changes may occur before/after the crossing: reported separately, not silently counted as within-cell lateral flux. Model uses exact1s accepted flux and lateral net inferred from its conserved group stock.',
        fit=False,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,rows=len(all_rows),blocks=len(block_rows),lane1=[r for r in block_rows if r['lane']==1]),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def check_joint_lane_downstream_extension(*, recovery_fit=False):
    """Test one connected local region without coefficient retuning."""
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    archived=root/'joint_lane_fifo';table=load(archived/'restoration.json')['files'];originals={}
    for rel,row in table.items():
        assert sha(ROOT/rel)==row['before'] and sha(archived/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name)==row['candidate']
        originals[rel]=(ROOT/rel).read_bytes()
    try:
        for rel in originals:(ROOT/rel).write_bytes((archived/'executed_sources'/Path(rel).name).read_bytes())
        check_joint_route_lane_region(extended=True,recovery_fit=recovery_fit)
    finally:
        restored={}
        for rel,content in originals.items():
            assert sha(ROOT/rel)==table[rel]['candidate'],'Concurrent core change: do not overwrite'
            (ROOT/rel).write_bytes(content);assert sha(ROOT/rel)==table[rel]['before'];restored[rel]=sha(ROOT/rel)
        folder=root/('joint_lane_rec' if recovery_fit else 'joint_lane_25')
        if folder.exists():save(folder/'restoration.json',dict(files=restored,adopted=False))


def check_joint_route_lane_region(*, accessible_fifo=False, extended=False, recovery_fit=False):
    """One bounded regional candidate; transport is shared with route036."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    assert not recovery_fit or extended
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/('joint_lane_rec' if recovery_fit else 'joint_lane_25' if extended else 'joint_lane_region');ri=root/'route_inventory'
    if extended:out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    elif accessible_fifo:
        assert (out/'summary.json').exists()
        out=root/'joint_lane_fifo';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    retry=(out/'protocol.json').exists()
    if retry:
        assert not (out/'summary.json').exists() and not (out/'attempt2_protocol.json').exists()
        assert load(out/'failure.json')['error']=="ValueError('Joint lane region requires contiguous interior cells of equal physical width')"
        (out/'failure_attempt1.json').write_bytes((out/'failure.json').read_bytes())
        (out/'helper_attempt1.txt').write_bytes((out/'helper_executed.txt').read_bytes())
        for p in (out/'executed_sources').iterdir():
            target=out/'attempt1_sources'/p.name;target.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True);target.write_bytes(p.read_bytes())
    cache=root/'vsl_native_exposure/s29_early_none_frames.json.gz'
    with gzip.open(cache,'rt') as f:frames=json.load(f)['frames']
    # Only seed29 early NC informs the six shared lane-pair hazards. No TTT,
    # late response, command label or seed67 state enters this fit.
    counts=[[0]*3 for _ in range(3)];exposures=[0]*3;unmatched=0;intervals=0
    times=sorted(map(float,frames))
    for t,t1 in zip(times,times[1:]):
        assert abs(t1-t-5.)<1e-7
        left={z[0]:z for z in frames[str(t)] if z[1] in (19,20,21)}
        right={z[0]:z for z in frames[str(t1)] if z[1] in (19,20,21)}
        for vehicle,z in left.items():
            if vehicle not in right:unmatched+=1;continue
            g=z[3]-1;k=right[vehicle][3]-1;assert 0<=g<3 and 0<=k<3
            exposures[g]+=1;intervals+=1
            if g!=k:counts[g][k]+=1
    rates=[]
    for g,row in enumerate(counts):
        changed=sum(row);assert 0<=changed<exposures[g]
        hazard=-math.log1p(-changed/exposures[g])/5.
        rates.append([hazard*n/changed if changed else 0. for n in row])
    spec=dict(cells=[19,20,21],lanes=3,exchange_rates_per_sec=rates,
        inlet_lane_to_group={'2':0,'3':1,'4':2},off_access={'10483':0},ramp_access={'RM_C10490':0})
    if extended:
        assert load(root/'joint_lane_fifo/protocol.json')['candidate']==spec
        spec['cells']=list(range(19,26));spec['ramp_access']['RM_C10484']=0
    sources=[ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name for name in ('physical_lane_groups.py','offramp_routing.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','freeway_fd.py')]
    sources += [ROOT/'diagnostics/demand_sweep/user_native_20260914/metanet_calibration_v1/canonical_harness.py',Path(__file__),cache,ri/'contract.json']
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS:priority clarification only; resumed implementation of required conserved local lane-route dynamics.',
        hypothesis='Averaging the shared exit/merge lane before its nonlinear dynamics suppresses the local recongestion and VSL response.',
        scope='Existing31cell METANET outside19-21; exact current lane-route partition and accepted class transfers inside; no extra vehicles or capacity bonus.',
        changed='Lane-resolved sending, accessible branch release, local receiving, route transport and per-lane existing METANET; fixed empirical lateral exchange.',
        unchanged='FD/relaxation/anticipation/merge coefficients, native network, commands, demand, dynamic off storage/drain, VSL cohorts, ramp buffers/costs, objective.',
        approximation='Inlet target-specific lane shares frozen from the current upstream cell; uniform only for unobserved targets. Cell VSL-cohort fractions shared by its lanes. Lane hazards from matched5s endpoints undercount unresolved paths and are not exact individual lane-change rates.',
        train=dict(seed=29,arm='early_none',window=[times[0],times[-1]],counts=counts,at_risk_intervals=exposures,unmatched_intervals=unmatched),
        candidate=spec,max_new_forecasts=9,fit_grid=0,new_native=0,
        evaluation='Four late29 and four late67 executed-command450s autonomous predictions. Both seeds already inspected; no pristine holdout claim.',
        gates='Mass and class conservation, no physical storage breach, no negative stock, large mainline/component cost direction and ordering; flow and state deterioration retained even if choice matches.',
        pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources})
    if extended:
        protocol.update(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS:bounded three-candidate local calibration failed the VSL response; future lane22 density diagnostic restored upstream accumulation/flow direction.',
            hypothesis='Cutting the resolved lanes at21 averages away downstream merge23 lane congestion and its upstream pressure. Preserve a connected lane region through recovery25.',
            scope='Extend same conserved route/lane dynamics19-21 to19-25 including10484 merge23; exact current initialization, same frozen parameters/rates, no future inputs.',
            changed='Only connected regional extent and physical10484 access lane. No FD/merge recalibration or additional fit.',
            limitation='Frozen exchange hazards from seed29 early19-21 are also used in22-25; empirical state-dependent lane changes remain approximate. This test is not production adoption.')
    if recovery_fit:
        protocol.update(hypothesis='Observed-state lane24-25 equations overbrake; test one frozen shared relaxation/anticipation fit within connected19-25 model.',
            changed='Only cell24-25 common tau/nu multipliers from lane_terms25 seed29 earlyNC fit. No new parameter search; every other coefficient and regional structure identical to joint_lane_25.',
            unchanged='Native network, actions, demand, routes, ramp flow/cost, dynamic off storage/drain, FD, merge and lateral coefficients, VSL cohorts, objective.',
            calibration='Observed-state fit hit a bound; this is a hypothesis to test, not an identified or qualified coefficient. Late29/67 data do not refit it.',
            max_new_forecasts=9,fit_grid=0)
    if accessible_fifo:
        assert load(root/'joint_lane_region/protocol.json')['candidate']==spec
        protocol['implementation_repair']='Regression test exposed off-exit speed cap applied to inaccessible lanes despite accessible-lane FIFO specification; cap now uses only the declared access lane. Same six frozen rates and all physical coefficients. One additional9forecast verification budget; first failed model results preserved.'
    if retry:
        prior=load(out/'protocol.json')
        assert prior['candidate']==spec and prior['train']==protocol['train']
        protocol['repair']='Physical lane width3 is represented as3.0000000000000004; use1e-9 equality tolerance only. No geometry, coefficient or control changes.'
    save(out/('attempt2_protocol.json' if retry else 'protocol.json'),protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    for p in sources:
        if p.name in ('physical_lane_groups.py','offramp_routing.py','area_freeway_accounting.py','canonical_harness.py'):
            target=out/'executed_sources'/p.name;target.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True);target.write_bytes(p.read_bytes())
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';baseline_context=lpr.load_sources(manifest)
    if recovery_fit:
        fp=root/'lane_terms25/summary.json';fitted=load(fp)['fit'];doc=load(manifest)
        original_path=ROOT/doc['sources']['reference_config']['path'];config=load(original_path)
        assert sha(original_path)==doc['sources']['reference_config']['sha256']
        cells=config['freeway']['state_response']['FW_E']['cell_overrides']
        for c in ('24','25'):
            cells[c]['relaxation']={k:v*fitted['tau_multiplier'] for k,v in cells[c]['relaxation'].items()}
            cells[c]['anticipation']={k:v*fitted['nu_multiplier'] for k,v in cells[c]['anticipation'].items()}
        cp=out/'reference_config.json';save(cp,config);doc['sources']['reference_config']=dict(path=cp.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),sha256=sha(cp))
        doc['qualification']='Unqualified connected-lane recovery fit; archived candidate sources required; not a production manifest.'
        manifest=out/'manifest.json';save(manifest,doc)
        save(out/'calibration.json',dict(fit=fitted,source=str(fp),source_sha256=sha(fp),new_fits=0,source_config=str(original_path),source_config_sha256=sha(original_path)))
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    pins=dict(protocol['pins']);pins[str(manifest)]=sha(manifest)
    records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']=='s29_late']
    folder=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930'
    records += [dict(r,case='s67_late',role='heldout',reference='hold',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(folder/'observations'/r['arm']))
                for r in load(folder/'capture.json')['records']]
    assert len(records)==8
    results=[];forecasts=0;parity=None
    begin=time.perf_counter()
    for record in records:
        case,arm=record['case'],record['arm'];args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256'])
        pins[record['input']]=record['sha256']
