        rawpath=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(rawpath)]=sha(rawpath)
        kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(rawpath));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        baseline=(ri/('storage_'+arm+'.json.gz') if case=='s29_late' else root/'choice67'/('route036_'+case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz'))
        pins[str(baseline)]=sha(baseline)
        with gzip.open(baseline,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
        if parity is None:
            current=baseline_context['component'].rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**copy.deepcopy(kw));forecasts+=1
            normalized=json.loads(json.dumps(current,allow_nan=False))
            parity={key:normalized[key]==old[key] for key in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')}
            save(out/'disabled_parity.json',parity);assert all(parity.values())
        kw['route_lane_regions']={'FW_E':copy.deepcopy(spec)}
        try:
            pred=model.rollout(*copy.deepcopy(args),**kw);forecasts+=1
        except Exception as exc:
            save(out/'failure.json',dict(case=case,arm=arm,error=repr(exc),new_forecasts=forecasts,rows=results))
            raise
        path=out/(case+'_'+arm+'.json.gz')
        with gzip.open(path,'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        truth=ObservationData(record['truth'])
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        for version,value in [('existing',old),('joint_lanes',pred)]:
            row=_cellwise_measure(model,value,record,truth,horizon_sec=450);row['version']=version
            row['cell_mean_speed']={c:sum(z['v_kmh'] for z in value['cells'] if z['cell']==c and z['time_s']>record['cutoff'])/15 for c in range(18,26)}
            row['cell_through']={c:sum(z['downstream_crossings'] for z in value['flows'] if z['cell']==c) for c in range(18,26)}
            row['maximum_speed']=max(z['v_kmh'] for z in value['cells'])
            if version=='joint_lanes':
                detail=value['diagnostics']['roads'][0]['joint_lane_region']
                row['joint_route_marginal_max_error']=detail['route_marginal_max_error']
                row['maximum_lane_speed']=max(z['v_kmh'] for z in detail['rows'])
                assert row['joint_route_marginal_max_error']<1e-7
            assert row['conservation_max']<1e-7
            results.append(row)
        save(out/'partial_results.json',dict(rows=results,new_forecasts=forecasts))
        print(json.dumps(dict(case=case,arm=arm,predicted_main_ttt=results[-1]['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],old_main_ttt=results[-2]['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],actual_main_ttt=results[-1]['actual']['mainline_ttt'],mass_error=results[-1]['conservation_max'])),flush=True)
    comparisons=[]
    for case in ('s29_late','s67_late'):
        for reference,candidate in [('hold','release'),('hold','hold_vsl90'),('release','release_vsl90')]:
            row=dict(case=case,reference=reference,candidate=candidate)
            for version in ('existing','joint_lanes'):
                a=next(z for z in results if (z['case'],z['arm'],z['version'])==(case,reference,version))
                b=next(z for z in results if (z['case'],z['arm'],z['version'])==(case,candidate,version))
                row[version]={k:b['predicted'][k]-a['predicted'][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','exits')}
                row['actual']={k:b['actual'][k]-a['actual'][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt','ramp_ttt','exits')}
            comparisons.append(row)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=results,comparisons=comparisons,new_forecasts=forecasts,parity=parity,
        wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-begin,pins=pins,production_adopted=False,full_omega_qualified=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,forecasts=forecasts,comparisons=comparisons)),flush=True)


def prepare_current_lane_route_inventory():
    """Observe existing initializer's route classes paired with current lanes; no rollout."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr,offramp_routing as routing,projection_support
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'bottleneck_lane67'
    assert (out/'destination_summary.json').exists() and not (out/'current_joint_inventory.json').exists()
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ri=root/'route_inventory'
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];late=load(HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json')['records']
    cases=[('s29_early',next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_early','none')),ri/'s29_early/initial_raw.json'),
        ('s29_late',next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s29_late','hold')),ri/'initial_raw.json'),
        ('s67_late',next(r for r in late if r['arm']=='release'),ri/'s67_late/initial_raw.json')]
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,ri/'contract.json',Path(routing.__file__),Path(projection_support.__file__))};results=[]
    class InitialCaptured(BaseException):pass
    original_initialize=routing.initialize_inventory
    for case_index,(name,record,path) in enumerate(cases,1):
        context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component'];args,kw=read_primitive_capture(record['input'],record['sha256'])
        pins[record['input']]=record['sha256'];pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
        raw=load(path);kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=raw);kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        def observe(state,cfg,raw_argument):
            original_records=projection_support.complete_records;original_add=routing._add;current={};rows=[]
            class ObservedRecords(list):
                def __iter__(self):
                    for physical in super().__iter__():
                        current['physical']=physical;yield physical
            def records_with_identity(source):
                return ObservedRecords(original_records(source))
            def add_with_identity(target,source,amount=1.):
                original_add(target,source,amount)
                physical=current['physical'];fw,x,c=routing._position(cfg.network.offramp_route_inventory,physical['link_no'],physical['position_m'])
                assert amount==1. and all(k.startswith(('observed_route:','observed_null|')) for k in source)
                rows.append(dict(vehicle=physical['veh_no'],road=fw,cell=c,lane=physical['lane_index'],speed_kmh=physical['speed_kph'],classes=dict(source)))
            projection_support.complete_records=records_with_identity;routing._add=add_with_identity
            try:metadata=original_initialize(state,cfg,raw_argument)
            finally:projection_support.complete_records=original_records;routing._add=original_add
            assert len({z['vehicle'] for z in rows})==len(rows)
            joint=defaultdict(lambda:dict(n=0.,speed_sum=0.,classes=defaultdict(float)))
            for z in rows:
                assert abs(sum(z['classes'].values())-1)<1e-9
                q=joint[z['road'],z['cell'],z['lane']];q['n']+=1.;q['speed_sum']+=z['speed_kmh']
                for k,v in z['classes'].items():q['classes'][k]+=v
            checks=[]
            for fw,cell_rows in state.offramp_route_inventory_state['cells'].items():
                for c,expected in enumerate(cell_rows):
                    actual=defaultdict(float)
                    for (road,cell,lane),q in joint.items():
                        if (road,cell)==(fw,c):
                            for k,v in q['classes'].items():actual[k]+=v
                    assert set(actual)==set(expected)
                    error=max([abs(actual[k]-expected[k]) for k in expected]+[0.]);assert error<1e-8
                    observed=next(z for z in args[0] if z['road']==fw and z['cell']==c)
                    grouped=[q for (road,cell,lane),q in joint.items() if (road,cell)==(fw,c)]
                    n=sum(q['n'] for q in grouped);nv=sum(q['speed_sum'] for q in grouped)
                    assert n==observed['n_veh']
                    if n:assert abs(nv/n-observed['v_kmh'])<1e-7
                    checks.append(dict(road=fw,cell=c,mass=n,route_marginal_max_error=error))
            results.append(dict(case=name,cutoff=raw['sim_sec'],metadata=metadata,checks=checks,
                cells=[dict(road=key[0],cell=key[1],lane=key[2],n=q['n'],speed_sum=q['speed_sum'],classes=dict(q['classes'])) for key,q in sorted(joint.items())],
                interpretation='Exact current observed lane paired with the canonical initializer route class; NULL future choices retain existing expected weights. No later observed exit labels used.'))
            raise InitialCaptured()
        routing.initialize_inventory=observe
        try:
            try:model.rollout(*args,**kw)
            except InitialCaptured:pass
            else:raise AssertionError('Initializer was not observed before rollout')
        finally:routing.initialize_inventory=original_initialize
        assert len(results)==case_index
    assert len(results)==3
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'current_joint_inventory.json',dict(cases=results,pins=pins,initializations=3,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,
        fit=False,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False,limitation='Initialization feasibility only; dynamic route/lane transport and actuator response remain unimplemented and unqualified. Do not remove the existing PhysicalLaneGroups/route-inventory compatibility guard.'))
    (out/'helper_joint_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,initializations=3,cases=[dict(case=r['case'],cells=len(r['checks']),lane_groups=len(r['cells']),vehicles=sum(z['n'] for z in r['cells'])) for r in results]),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def audit_bottleneck_destinations67():
    """Label already observed slow lane traffic by later observed exit/pass events."""
    from collections import defaultdict,Counter
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'bottleneck_lane67'
    assert (out/'summary.json').exists() and not (out/'destination_summary.json').exists()
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),out/'summary.json')};rows=[];checks=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        p=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz');assert sha(p)==load(out/'summary.json')['pins'][str(p)];pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        exits=defaultdict(list);passes=defaultdict(list)
        p=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm/'port_events.csv';pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for r in csv.DictReader(f):
                if r['connector']=='10483' and r['kind']=='arrival' and 2670.1<float(r['time_s'])<=3120.1:
                    exits[r['vehicle']].append(float(r['time_s']))
        for t,frame in frames.items():
            for vehicle,z in frame.items():
                if z[0]>=21:passes[vehicle].append(t)
        for block in range(3):
            lo=round(2670.1+150*block,6);hi=round(lo+150,6)
            for cell in (19,20):
                for lane in (1,2,3):
                    labels=Counter();unique=defaultdict(set);total=0;speed_sum=0.
                    for t,frame in frames.items():
                        if not lo<=t<hi:continue
                        for vehicle,z in frame.items():
                            if z[0]!=cell or z[3]!=lane or z[1]>=20:continue
                            total+=1;speed_sum+=z[1]
                            exit_t=min((x for x in exits[vehicle] if x>t),default=math.inf)
                            pass_t=min((x for x in passes[vehicle] if x>t),default=math.inf)
                            if exit_t==pass_t==math.inf:label='not_resolved_before3120'
                            elif exit_t<=pass_t:label='observed_exit10483'
                            else:label='observed_continue_past10483'
                            labels[label]+=1;unique[label].add(vehicle)
                    assert sum(labels.values())==total
                    rows.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,cell=cell,lane=lane,slow_vehicle_samples=total,mean_slow_speed=speed_sum/total if total else None,
                        sample_labels=dict(labels),unique_vehicle_labels={k:len(v) for k,v in unique.items()}))
        native=load(out/'summary.json')['boundaries']
        assert sum(len(v) for v in exits.values())==sum(r['off'] for r in native if r['arm']==arm and r['cell']==20)
        checks.append(dict(arm=arm,exit_events=sum(len(v) for v in exits.values())))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'destination_summary.json',dict(rows=rows,checks=checks,pins=pins,
        interpretation='Posthoc descriptive labels only: future native exit or continuation identifies destination. Not available MPC inputs. Unresolved vehicles remain explicit and cannot be treated as through demand. Counts are repeated vehicle-samples; unique counts are per block/category, not additive corridor flows.',
        fit=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,new_forecasts=0,adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    (out/'helper_destination_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,checks=checks,late_lane1=[r for r in rows if r['block']==2 and r['lane']==1]),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def audit_bottleneck_lane_response67():
    """Native lane response and merge/diverge geometry; descriptive, not a capacity fit."""
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'bottleneck_lane67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    geometry_path=HERE/'selected/port_gain/geometry.json';geometry=load(geometry_path)
    cells={r['cell']:r for r in geometry['cells'] if r['road']=='FW_E'}
    network=HERE/'selected/network/native_seed29.inpx';assert sha(network)=='64cf5f55fe9990f3e25bc4fedebf4ab1e4634c8138dbcf5697a136c48cb559dc'
    nodes={int(n.get('no')):n for n in ET.parse(network).findall('./links/link')}
    off=nodes[10483].find('fromLinkEndPt');merge=nodes[10490].find('toLinkEndPt')
    assert off.get('lane')==merge.get('lane')=='119 1'
    spacing=float(merge.get('pos'))-float(off.get('pos'));assert spacing>0
    compiled=root/'route_inventory/compiled.json';runtime=load(compiled)
    assert runtime['branches']['10483']['source_cell']==20
    assert all([p['link'] for p in cells[c]['physical_pieces']]==[2] for c in range(15,19))
    main_lo=runtime['physical']['2'][1];main_hi=runtime['physical']['10613'][1]
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP')
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),geometry_path,network,compiled,root/'flow67/verification.json',stop)}
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: recovery24/25 speed injection did not restore upstream VSL discharge response.',
        arms=['release','release_vsl90'],window=[2670.1,3120.1],blocks_sec=150,
        observations='Verified5s native frame cache only. Mean speeds are vehicle-sample weighted. Lane changes count net endpoint lane differences on the same physical link2; multi-change/return events within5s are unresolved.',
        comparisons='Late-policy trajectories after common2700 prefix, not matched local causal states. Counts and lane asymmetry are descriptive; no capacity or friction coefficient inferred.',
        geometry='10483 diverge and10490 merge both touch119 lane1; along-link spacing measured from saved XML. Geometric proximity alone does not prove collision, faulty geometry, or weaving loss.',
        fit=False,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    groups=[];changes=[];boundaries=[];port_rows=[];native_checks=0;frames_by_arm={}
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        path=root/'flow67'/(arm+'_frames.json.gz')
        assert sha(path)==load(root/'flow67/verification.json')['artifacts'][path.name];pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:frames={round(float(t),6):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
        frames_by_arm[arm]=frames;times=sorted(frames);assert len(times)==91
        truthdir=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm;tables={}
        for name in ('cells_30s','flows_30s','ports_30s'):
            p=truthdir/(name+'.csv');pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:tables[name]=list(csv.DictReader(f))
        for z in tables['cells_30s']:
            t=round(float(z['time_s']),6);c=int(z['cell'])
            if z['road']=='FW_E' and t in frames and c in range(15,26):
                rr=[r for r in frames[t].values() if r[0]==c];assert len(rr)==int(z['n_veh'])
                if rr:assert abs(sum(r[1] for r in rr)/len(rr)-float(z['v_kmh']))<1e-7
                native_checks+=1
        for block in range(3):
            lo=round(2670.1+150*block,6);hi=round(lo+150,6);stamps=[t for t in times if lo<=t<hi];assert len(stamps)==30
            for c in range(15,26):
                count=round(cells[c]['effective_lanes'])
                for lane in range(1,count+1):
                    rr=[r for t in stamps for r in frames[t].values() if r[0]==c and r[3]==lane]
                    groups.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,start=lo,end=hi,cell=c,lane=lane,samples=len(rr),mean_n=len(rr)/30,
                        mean_speed=sum(r[1] for r in rr)/len(rr) if rr else None,
                        mean_desired_speed=sum(r[5] for r in rr)/len(rr) if rr else None,
                        slow_fraction=sum(r[1]<20 for r in rr)/len(rr) if rr else None,
                        braking_fraction=sum(r[4]<-1 for r in rr)/len(rr) if rr else None))
                fr=[z for z in tables['flows_30s'] if z['road']=='FW_E' and int(z['cell'])==c and lo<float(z['window_end_s'])<=hi]
                assert len(fr)==5
                boundaries.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,cell=c,downstream=sum(float(z['downstream_crossings']) for z in fr),
                    off=sum(float(z['off_departures']) for z in fr),merge=sum(float(z['ramp_merges']) for z in fr)))
            for target in ((15,16,17,18),(17,18)):
                pairs=[]
                for t in stamps:
                    before,after=frames[t],frames[round(t+5,6)]
                    for veh in before.keys()&after.keys():
                        a,b=before[veh],after[veh]
                        if a[0] in target and main_lo<=b[2]<main_hi:pairs.append((a,b))
                changes.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,cells=list(target),matched_intervals=len(pairs),
                    changed_lane=sum(a[3]!=b[3] for a,b in pairs),leave_lane1=sum(a[3]==1 and b[3]!=1 for a,b in pairs),
                    enter_lane1=sum(a[3]!=1 and b[3]==1 for a,b in pairs)))
            for connector in ('10481','10483','10490','10484'):
                rr=[z for z in tables['ports_30s'] if z['connector']==connector and lo<float(z['window_end_s'])<=hi];assert len(rr)==5
                port_rows.append(dict(arm=arm,block=block,connector=connector,arrivals=sum(float(z['arrivals_veh']) for z in rr),
                    departures=sum(float(z['departures_veh']) for z in rr),mean_end_n=sum(float(z['end_n_veh']) for z in rr)/5,
                    stopped_snapshot_total=sum(float(z['stopped_veh']) for z in rr),unresolved_absences=sum(float(z['unresolved_absences_veh']) for z in rr)))
    common=[t for t in frames_by_arm['release'] if t<2700];assert all(frames_by_arm['release'][t]==frames_by_arm['release_vsl90'][t] for t in common)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    result=dict(groups=groups,changes=changes,boundaries=boundaries,ports=port_rows,pins=pins,
        geometry=dict(off_connector=10483,merge_connector=10490,mainline_link=119,lane=1,off_position_m=float(off.get('pos')),merge_position_m=float(merge.get('pos')),separation_m=spacing),
        native_cell_checks=native_checks,common_prefix_snapshots=len(common),fit=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,new_forecasts=0,adopted=False,goal_qualified=False)
    save(out/'summary.json',result)
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,native_cell_checks=native_checks,spacing_m=spacing,
        cell19_lane1=[r for r in groups if r['cell']==19 and r['lane']==1],changes=changes),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def audit_first_recovery_divergence():
    """Attribute saved inventory errors to accepted boundary fluxes, without rollout."""
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'first_recovery_divergence';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    dynamic=root/'face_recovery/dynamic';records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),dynamic/'summary.json',root/'face_recovery/verification.json')}
    cases=[]
    for row in load(dynamic/'summary.json')['rows']:
        r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(row['case'],row['arm']))
        cases.append((row['candidate'],r['case'],r['arm'],dynamic/(row['candidate']+'_'+r['case']+'_'+r['arm']+'.json.gz'),Path(r['truth'])))
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        cases.append(('conditional_observed_inflows','s67_late',arm,root/'b67'/('obs_'+arm+'.json.gz'),HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm))
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: rejected candidate verified and restored; freeway-first priority retained.',
        hypothesis='Find whether recovery-cell inventory error comes from excessive accepted inflow or deficient discharge before widening calibration.',
        scope='Saved full predictions only; cells15..26 for detailed comparison,21..25 for recovery. First30/60/150/450s and sustained speed bias>=10km/h for two30s samples; diagnostic thresholds, not new calibration gates.',
        method='Infer model initial inventory from first end state minus accepted net flux; compare with native initial inventory. Attribute later inventory error exactly to cumulative inflow minus outflow errors.',
        limitations=['Temporal resolution30s cannot establish sub30s causal ordering.','Native mean speed is a snapshot, flows are interval counts.','Seed67 inputs use future observed source/merge counts: conditional diagnosis only.','Candidate shared moment sum is not a proof of the full velocity discretization.'],
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,fit=False,pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    rows=[];groups=[];parity=[];balances=[]
    for candidate,case,arm,path,truthdir in cases:
        pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:prediction=json.load(f)
        tables={}
        for name,timekey in (('cells','time_s'),('flows','window_end_s')):
            p=truthdir/(name+'_30s.csv');pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
            with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                rr=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['road']=='FW_E']
            tables[name]={(round(float(r[timekey]),6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in rr}
        pc={(round(r['time_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in prediction['cells']}
        pf={(round(r['window_end_s'],6),int(r['cell'])):r for r in prediction['flows']}
        times=sorted(set(t for t,c in pc));start=round(min(r['window_start_s'] for r in prediction['flows']),6)
        cumulative={c:dict(inflow=0.,outflow=0.,merge=0.,off=0.) for c in range(15,27)}
        initial={};local=[]
        for ti,t in enumerate(times):
            for c in range(15,27):
                a=tables['cells'][t,c];b=pc[t,c];af=tables['flows'][t,c];bf=pf[t,c]
                av={k:float(af[k]) for k in ('upstream_crossings','source_admissions','ramp_merges','downstream_crossings','off_departures','terminal_exits_inferred','start_n_veh','end_n_veh','unexplained_entries','unexplained_losses','native_removals')}
                assert av['unexplained_entries']==av['unexplained_losses']==av['native_removals']==0,(case,arm,t,c)
                ai=av['upstream_crossings']+av['source_admissions']+av['ramp_merges'];ao=av['downstream_crossings']+av['off_departures']+av['terminal_exits_inferred']
                bi=pf[t,c-1]['downstream_crossings']+bf['source_admissions']+bf['ramp_merges'];bo=bf['downstream_crossings']+bf['off_departures']+bf['terminal_exits']
                native_error=av['end_n_veh']-av['start_n_veh']-ai+ao;assert abs(native_error)<1e-8
                if ti==0:
                    initial[c]=b['n_veh']-bi+bo-av['start_n_veh'];assert abs(initial[c])<1e-7
                    parity.append(abs(initial[c]))
                cum=cumulative[c];cum['inflow']+=bi-ai;cum['outflow']+=bo-ao
                cum['merge']+=bf['ramp_merges']-av['ramp_merges'];cum['off']+=bf['off_departures']-av['off_departures']
                en=b['n_veh']-float(a['n_veh']);ev=b['v_kmh']-float(a['v_kmh'])
                residual=en-initial[c]-cum['inflow']+cum['outflow'];assert abs(residual)<1e-7
                balances.append(abs(residual))
                r=dict(candidate=candidate,case=case,arm=arm,time_s=t,horizon_s=round(t-start,6),cell=c,
                    native_n=float(a['n_veh']),predicted_n=b['n_veh'],native_v=float(a['v_kmh']),predicted_v=b['v_kmh'],n_error=en,v_error=ev,
                    native_inflow=ai,predicted_inflow=bi,native_outflow=ao,predicted_outflow=bo,
                    cumulative_inflow_error=cum['inflow'],cumulative_outflow_error=cum['outflow'],cumulative_merge_error=cum['merge'],cumulative_off_error=cum['off'],inventory_error_identity_residual=residual)
                rows.append(r);local.append(r)
        for c in range(15,27):
            rr=[r for r in local if r['cell']==c]
            onset=next((r['horizon_s'] for r,nxt in zip(rr,rr[1:]) if r['v_error']<=-10 and nxt['v_error']<=-10 and min(r['native_n'],nxt['native_n'])>=5),None)
            groups.append(dict(candidate=candidate,case=case,arm=arm,cell=c,first_sustained_low_speed_horizon_s=onset,
                mean_speed_error=sum(r['v_error'] for r in rr)/len(rr),snapshots=[r for r in rr if r['horizon_s'] in (30.,60.,150.,450.)]))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',rows)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=groups,pins=pins,saved_predictions=len(cases),initial_inventory_checks=len(parity),
        maximum_initial_error=max(parity),inventory_error_identity_checks=len(balances),maximum_identity_error=max(balances),
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,fit=False,adopted=False,goal_qualified=False))
    first=[g for g in groups if g['candidate']!='face' and g['cell'] in (23,24,25)]
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,saved_predictions=len(cases),initial_checks=len(parity),balance_checks=len(balances),
        first=[{k:g[k] for k in ('candidate','case','arm','cell','first_sustained_low_speed_horizon_s','mean_speed_error')} for g in first]),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


def test_recovery_face_moment():
    """One frozen shared-interface correction; future data only in scoring."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';out=root/'face_recovery/dynamic'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    assert all(g['eligible'] for g in load(root/'face_recovery/summary.json')['gates'])
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json';ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];ri=root/'route_inventory'
    old_config=model._config;old_transition=accounting._freeway_substep_events;enabled=False;checks=[]
    def configure(road,params):
        cfg=old_config(road,params)
        if enabled:cfg.network._component_recovery_faces=(23,24,25)
        return cfg
    def transition(*a,**k):
        result=old_transition(*a,**k)
        if enabled:
            d=result[1];error=d['recovery_face_shared_moment_residual'];assert error<1e-9
            checks.append(error)
        return result
    model._config=configure;accounting._freeway_substep_events=transition
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,ri/'contract.json',root/'face_recovery/summary.json',out/'core_before.txt')}
    for p,h in model.provenance['model_files'].items():assert sha(ROOT/p)==h;pins[str(ROOT/p)]=h
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    train=[('s29_early','none',750),('s29_early','vsl',750),('s29_early','rm',450),('s29_early','both',450),('s29_late','hold',450),('s29_late','release',450)]
    scale=load(root.parent/'protocol.json')
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(faces=[23,24,25],fit=False,train=train,
        formula='For each shared face, dM=accepted_flow*dt*(interpolated_face_speed-donor_mean). Donor subtracts dM, receiver adds dM; each speed correction is dM/N_after. Equivalent convection argument; existing FD/merge/drop and physical states unchanged.',
        controls='Same current route inventory, dynamic off-ramp storage/drain, ramp queues, VSL cohorts, command histories,1s step; no future observations in rollout.',
        acceptance='Same legacy response metric evaluated at matched horizons: response loss at least5% lower; state loss at most10% higher; no newly wrong >=0.5vehh component-cost pair. Then examine43 and67 without refitting. No adoption from training alone.',
        metrics='Early750 NC/VSL and early450 four arms (750 data cut to450),late450 hold/release. Component scope=FW_E31cells+8connectors,not fullOmega.',
        maximum_forecasts=28,maximum_candidates=1,holdout='Previously inspected43 and67, not pristine blind data.',pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (out/'core_executed.txt').write_bytes((ROOT/'evaluation/controllers/area_freeway_accounting.py').read_bytes())
    summaries=[];predictions={};parity=None
    def execute(r,horizon,on):
        nonlocal enabled,checks
        enabled=on;checks=[]
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        raw=ri/'initial_raw.json' if r['case']=='s29_late' else ri/r['case']/'initial_raw.json';pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw)
        kw.update(horizon_sec=horizon,offramp_inventory=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(raw)));kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        start=time.perf_counter();pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        assert len(checks)==(horizon if on else 0)
        row.update(candidate='face' if on else 'base',horizon=horizon,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-start,
            shared_moment_checks=len(checks),max_shared_moment_error=max(checks,default=0.))
        stem=row['candidate']+'_'+r['case']+'_'+r['arm']
        with gzip.open(out/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        save(out/(stem+'.json'),row);summaries.append(row);predictions[on,r['case'],r['arm']]=pred
        print(json.dumps(dict(candidate=row['candidate'],case=r['case'],arm=r['arm'],mainline=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'],exits=row['predicted']['exits'],speed_rmse=row['score']['speed']['rmse'])),flush=True)
        return pred,row
    def matched_rows(on):
        result=[]
        for case,arm,horizon in train:
            row=next(r for r in summaries if r['case']==case and r['arm']==arm and (r['candidate']=='face')==on)
            if case=='s29_early' and horizon==750:
                r=copy.deepcopy(row);r['case']='s29_early750';result.append(r)
                pred=copy.deepcopy(predictions[on,case,arm]);stop_time=2220.1+450
                pred['cells']=[z for z in pred['cells'] if z['time_s']<=stop_time+1e-7]
                pred['flows']=[z for z in pred['flows'] if z['window_end_s']<=stop_time+1e-7]
                pred['ports']=[z for z in pred['ports'] if z['time_s']<=stop_time+1e-7]
                pred['ramps']=[z for z in pred['ramps'] if z['end_sec']<=stop_time+1e-7]
                rec=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm))
                cut=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,rec,ObservationData(rec['truth']),horizon_sec=450);result.append(cut)
            else:result.append(copy.deepcopy(row))
        return result
    try:
        for on in (False,True):
            for case,arm,horizon in train:
                r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(case,arm));pred,row=execute(r,horizon,on)
                if not on and case=='s29_early' and arm=='none':
                    source=root/'center_distance/baseline_s29_early_none.json.gz';pins[str(source)]=sha(source)
                    with gzip.open(source,'rt') as f:old=json.load(f)
                    serialized=json.loads(json.dumps(pred))
                    parity={k:serialized[k]==old[k] for k in ('cells','flows','ports','ramps')};assert all(parity.values())
        losses={name:_cellwise_losses(matched_rows(on),[0.],scale) for name,on in (('base',False),('face',True))}
        pairs={(p['case'],p['arm']):p for p in losses['base']['pairs']}
        regressions=[p for p in losses['face']['pairs'] if abs(p['actual']['ttt'])>=.5 and p['actual']['ttt']*p['predicted']['ttt']<=0 and pairs[p['case'],p['arm']]['actual']['ttt']*pairs[p['case'],p['arm']]['predicted']['ttt']>0]
        gates=dict(response=losses['face']['response']<=.95*losses['base']['response'],state=losses['face']['state']<=1.1*losses['base']['state'],no_new_wrong_meaningful_sign=not regressions)
        save(out/'selection.json',dict(losses=losses,gates=gates,regressions=regressions,selected=all(gates.values()),parity=parity,adopted=False))
        if all(gates.values()):
            for arm in ('none','vsl','rm','both'):
                r=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==('s43_early',arm))
                for on in (False,True):execute(r,750 if arm in ('none','vsl') else 450,on)
            cap=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/capture.json';pins[str(cap)]=sha(cap)
            for r in load(cap)['records']:
                r=dict(r,case='s67_late',cutoff=2670.1,truth=str(cap.parent/'observations'/r['arm']))
                for on in (False,True):execute(r,450,on)
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(rows=summaries,losses=losses,gates=gates,pins=pins,parity=parity,new_forecasts=len(summaries),fit=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
        print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,new_forecasts=len(summaries),gates=gates,losses={k:{m:v[m] for m in ('state','response')} for k,v in losses.items()})),flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        save(out/'failure.json',dict(type=type(exc).__name__,message=str(exc),completed_forecasts=len(summaries)));raise
    finally:accounting._freeway_substep_events=old_transition;model._config=old_config


def screen_recovery_face_velocity():
    """No-fit face reconstruction screen against sampled passing populations."""
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'face_recovery';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    compiled=load(root/'route_inventory/compiled.json');bounds=compiled['bounds']['FW_E']
    lengths=[(b-a)/1000 for a,b in zip(bounds,bounds[1:])]
    sources=[(f's{seed}_{arm}',root/'recovery_transport'/f's{seed}_{arm}_frames.json.gz') for seed in (29,43) for arm in ('none','vsl')]
    sources += [(f's67_{arm}',root/'flow67'/f'{arm}_frames.json.gz') for arm in ('release','release_vsl90')]
    oldpins=load(root/'recovery_transport/closure_summary.json')['pins']
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),root/'route_inventory/compiled.json',root/'recovery_transport/closure_summary.json',root/'flow67/verification.json')}
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    for name,p in sources:
        expected=(load(root/'flow67/verification.json')['artifacts'][p.name] if name.startswith('s67') else oldpins[str(p)])
        assert sha(p)==expected;pins[str(p)]=expected
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: observed passing speed improved conditional local recovery, but is not an autonomous input.',
        faces=[23,24,25],cases=[s for s,p in sources],fit=False,
        candidate='Face between centers: v_face=(L_next*v_cell + L_cell*v_next)/(L_cell+L_next), convex in two CURRENT spatial mean speeds.',
        hypothesis='Recovery gradient can produce faster boundary traffic than the donor spatial average without a new coefficient or lever bonus.',
        labels='5s matched passing vehicles: before.cell<=face<after.cell, previous/next sample speeds. Previous-sample mean is primary; neither endpoint is exact crossing speed.',
        limitations=['Matched populations exclude same5s new ramp entrants lacking a previous mainline record; counts/coverage are reported.',
                    'Observed current states are used to assess a closure, not an autonomous forecast.',
                    'Do not treat simultaneous native current-state results as final gain validation.',
                    'If used in dynamics, the same interface moment correction must leave the donor and enter the receiver; no recipient-only acceleration.'],
        gate='Per face, pooled passing-count-weighted mean-speed RMSE improves at least5%; no case worsens >10%. No fitting or selecting a different coefficient after results.',
        further_budget='Only if a face passes, one frozen shared-interface moment candidate may be evaluated; no full lane-group expansion.',pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];rows=[];summaries=[]
    for name,p in sources:
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:data=json.load(f)
        frames={round(float(t),6):v for t,v in data['frames'].items()};times=sorted(frames)
        if name.startswith('s67'):
            truthdir=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/name[4:]
        else:
            seed,arm=name.split('_');record=next(r for r in records if r['case']==seed+'_early' and r['arm']==arm);truthdir=Path(record['truth'])
        truth=ObservationData(truthdir);pins[str(truthdir/'flows_30s.csv')]=sha(truthdir/'flows_30s.csv')
        for t,end in zip(times,times[1:]):
            before,after=frames[t],frames[end]
            for c in (23,24,25):
                here=[v[1] for v in before.values() if v[0]==c];down=[v[1] for v in before.values() if v[0]==c+1]
                assert here and down,(name,t,c)
                current=sum(here)/len(here);downstream=sum(down)/len(down)
                face=(lengths[c+1]*current+lengths[c]*downstream)/(lengths[c]+lengths[c+1])
                assert min(current,downstream)-1e-9<=face<=max(current,downstream)+1e-9
                passing=[v for v in before.keys()&after.keys() if before[v][0]<=c<after[v][0]]
                if not passing:continue
                rows.append(dict(case=name,time_s=t,cell=c,count=len(passing),prior_same_cell=sum(before[v][0]==c for v in passing),
                    current_mean=current,forward_face=face,pre_mean=sum(before[v][1] for v in passing)/len(passing),
                    post_mean=sum(after[v][1] for v in passing)/len(passing)))
        for c in (23,24,25):
            rr=[r for r in rows if r['case']==name and r['cell']==c];n=sum(r['count'] for r in rr)
            total=sum(float(truth.flows[t,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings']) for t in times if t>times[0] and (t,'FW_E',c) in truth.flows)
            assert 0<n<=total,(name,c,n,total)
            metrics={}
            for method in ('current_mean','forward_face'):
                metrics[method]={label:dict(rmse=math.sqrt(sum(r['count']*(r[method]-r[label])**2 for r in rr)/n),
                    bias=sum(r['count']*(r[method]-r[label]) for r in rr)/n) for label in ('pre_mean','post_mean')}
            summaries.append(dict(case=name,cell=c,matched_passing_vehicles=n,native_crossings=total,coverage=n/total,
                pre_sample_mean=sum(r['count']*r['pre_mean'] for r in rr)/n,post_sample_mean=sum(r['count']*r['post_mean'] for r in rr)/n,metrics=metrics))
    gates=[]
    for c in (23,24,25):
        ss=[s for s in summaries if s['cell']==c];n=sum(s['matched_passing_vehicles'] for s in ss)
        pooled={m:math.sqrt(sum(s['matched_passing_vehicles']*s['metrics'][m]['pre_mean']['rmse']**2 for s in ss)/n) for m in ('current_mean','forward_face')}
        not_worse=all(s['metrics']['forward_face']['pre_mean']['rmse']<=1.1*s['metrics']['current_mean']['pre_mean']['rmse'] for s in ss)
        gates.append(dict(cell=c,pooled_rmse=pooled,no_case_over10percent_worse=not_worse,
            pooled_improvement=1-pooled['forward_face']/pooled['current_mean'],eligible=not_worse and pooled['forward_face']<=.95*pooled['current_mean']))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'rows.json',rows);save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=summaries,gates=gates,pins=pins,fit=False,new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,gain_qualified=False,adopted=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(groups=summaries,gates=gates)),flush=True)


def diagnose_local_recovery_boundary(*, resume=False, crossing_speeds=False):
    """Three open-domain diagnostics through the existing freeway transition."""
    from collections import Counter
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.control_area_objective import ModelAreaLedger
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'local_boundary67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=resume or crossing_speeds)
    if resume:assert load(out/'failure.json')['completed_domains']==0 and not (out/'domain24.json').exists()
    if crossing_speeds:
        assert not resume and len(load(out/'summary.json')['domains'])==3 and not (out/'domain24_pre.json').exists()
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component'];mn=accounting._mn
    base=model._config('FW_E',ctx['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);base.network.freeway_segments_per_link=31
    lengths=accounting.cell_lengths_km(base,'FW_E',31);widths=base.network.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E']
    assert not model.aggregate_merge_speed and not model.lane_groups_enabled
    assert all(s['from_cell']<21 for s in model.offramps.values() if s['road']=='FW_E')
    assert all(abs(widths[c]-3)<1e-9 for c in range(21,27))
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,root/'flow67/verification.json',root/'merge_population/rows.json',root/'merge_speed_audit/events.json')}
    for p,h in model.provenance['model_files'].items():assert sha(ROOT/p)==h;pins[str(ROOT/p)]=h
    for value in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/('crossing_protocol.json' if crossing_speeds else 'retry_protocol.json' if resume else 'protocol.json'),dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: lane-FD screen completed; no justification for global lane-model expansion.',
        seed=67,arm='release',cutoff=2670.1,horizon=450,domains=[[24,25],[24,25]] if crossing_speeds else [[24,25],[23,25],[21,25]],
        crossing_sensitivity=('Two additional diagnostics: mean speed of passing vehicles at their previous/next5s samples. Neither is an exact crossing speed or rigorous bound. No new fit.' if crossing_speeds else None),
        hypothesis='Separate inherited merge-region state error from intrinsic recovery error by moving an observed open boundary upstream.',
        boundary='Observed5s mainline crossings uniformly spread over5s; observed N/Nv linearly interpolated for exterior reservoirs; actual merges inside the domain.',
        state='Only domain N/v evolve autonomously after the common observed initial state. Exterior states are reset observations, never scored as a conserved full network.',
        implementation='Existing31cell allocator/speed equation, original cell addresses/coefficients. Replace one entering sending request; no copied transition.',
        exclusions=['Domain21..25 has no off-ramp branch or physical lane reduction; upstream off-ramp effects are in observed boundary flux/state.',
                    'All vehicles/commands110 in this release arm: no VSL cohort state needed. This is not a VSL response forecast.',
                    'No route redraw within a branchless domain. No ramp queue/cost forecast; merges are observed boundary inputs.',
                    'Cannot be used as autonomous gain/TTT qualification or MPC future input.'],
        checks=['5s crossing counts reproduce native30s boundaries20/22/23/25.',
                'Source/merge admission must be feasible and local conservation close each1s.',
                'First speed update matches previous observed-state scalar diagnostic.',
                'Core/STOP hashes unchanged; maximum3 conditional local trajectories, no fit/native/FZP.'],pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/('helper_crossing.txt' if crossing_speeds else 'helper_resumed.txt' if resume else 'helper_executed.txt')).write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    path=root/'flow67/release_frames.json.gz'
    assert sha(path)==load(root/'flow67/verification.json')['artifacts'][path.name];pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
    with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:data=json.load(f)
    frames={round(float(t),6):r for t,r in data['frames'].items()};times=sorted(frames);assert len(times)==91
    folder=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations/release';truth=ObservationData(folder)
    for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(folder/name)]=sha(folder/name)
    moments={}
    for t,fr in frames.items():
        for c in range(31):
            rr=[v for v in fr.values() if v[0]==c];moments[t,c]=(len(rr),sum(v[1] for v in rr))
    events=[e for e in load(root/'merge_speed_audit/events.json') if e['arm']=='release' and e['road']=='FW_E']
    event_by={(e['hi'],str(e['vehicle'])):e for e in events};assert len(event_by)==len(events)
    crossings={};velocities={};merges=Counter();bypasses=Counter()
    for e in events:merges[e['lo'],e['ramp']]+=1
    for t,end in zip(times,times[1:]):
        before,after=frames[t],frames[end]
        for c in (20,22,23,25):
            ids={v for v in before.keys()&after.keys() if before[v][0]<=c<after[v][0]}
            for v in after.keys()-before.keys():
                e=event_by.get((end,v))
                if e and e['cell']<=c<after[v][0]:ids.add(v);bypasses[c]+=1
            crossings[t,c]=len(ids)
            if ids:
                pre=[before[v][1] if v in before else event_by[end,v]['last_ramp_speed_kmh'] for v in ids]
                post=[after[v][1] for v in ids]
                assert all(v is not None and math.isfinite(v) and v>=0 for v in pre+post)
                velocities[t,c]=dict(pre=sum(pre)/len(pre),post=sum(post)/len(post))
    flow_checks=0
    for j in range(15):
        ts=times[j*6:(j+1)*6];end=times[(j+1)*6]
        for c in (20,22,23,25):
            total=sum(crossings[t,c] for t in ts)
            assert total==float(truth.flows[end,'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings']),(end,c,total,truth.flows[end,'FW_E',c])
            flow_checks+=1
    saved={r['cell']:r['model'] for r in load(root/'merge_population/rows.json') if r['arm']=='release' and r['time_s']==times[0] and 'model' in r}
    with gzip.open(root/'b67/obs_release.json.gz','rt') as f:baseline=json.load(f)
    pins[str(root/'b67/obs_release.json.gz')]=sha(root/'b67/obs_release.json.gz')
    native={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for r in rs if r['road']=='FW_E'}
    summaries=[]
    try:
        for first,inlet_mode in ([(24,'pre'),(24,'post')] if crossing_speeds else [(24,'space'),(23,'space'),(21,'space')]):
            cfg=copy.deepcopy(base);net=cfg.network;active=set(range(first,26))
            net.off_ramps=[];net.offramp_route_inventory=None;net.component_vsl_transport={}
            net.ramps=[r for r in net.ramps if net.ramp_merge_segment_index[r] in active]
            state=mn.TrafficState.initial(cfg);state.freeway_effective_lanes['FW_E']=list(widths)
            control=mn.ControlAction.uncontrolled(cfg)
            demand=mn.DemandStep(freeway_mainline={'FW_E':0.},urban_boundary={},ramp_arrival={},freeway_lane_loss={})
            output=[];audit=[];window=Counter();mass_error=0.;parity=[];rejected=0.;projection=0
            flow_function=mn.segment_flow_veh_h
            for step in range(450):
                stamp=round(times[0]+step,6);k=step//5;fraction=(step%5)/5;t,end=times[k:k+2]
                for c in range(31):
                    if step and c in active:continue
                    n0,nv0=moments[t,c];n1,nv1=moments[end,c]
                    n=n0+(n1-n0)*fraction;nv=nv0+(nv1-nv0)*fraction
                    state.freeway_density['FW_E'][c]=n/(lengths[c]*widths[c])
                    if n:state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][c]=nv/n
                upspace=state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][first-1]
                if inlet_mode!='space':
                    assert (t,first-1) in velocities,'No crossing speed; do not invent an inlet velocity'
                    state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][first-1]=velocities[t,first-1][inlet_mode]
                state.time_sec=stamp;state.mainline_origin_queue['FW_E']=0.
                before=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(state,cfg)['FW_E'];v_before=list(state.freeway_speed['FW_E'])
                rates={r:merges[t,r]*720. for r in net.ramps};requested=crossings[t,first-1]/5
                trace=ModelAreaLedger({'freeway:FW_E':{'inside':sum(before)},'origin:FW_E':{'outside':0.},
                    **{'merge_pending:'+r:{'outside':q/3600} for r,q in rates.items()}},capture_response=True)
                trace.begin_response_step('freeway',stamp,stamp+1);state._control_area_ledger=trace
                call=[0]
                def boundary_flow(rho,speed,lane):
                    index=call[0];call[0]+=1
                    value=flow_function(rho,speed,lane)
                    return requested*3600 if index==first-1 else value
                mn.segment_flow_veh_h=boundary_flow
                try:
                    _,diag=accounting._freeway_substep_events(state,control,demand,cfg,offramp_capacity_veh_h={},
                        ramp_release_veh_h=rates,ramp_release_diagnostics={'total_no_meter_flow':sum(rates.values()),'mean_ramp_receiving_factor':1.},
                        update_ramp_queues=False,include_ramp_queue_ttt=False,complete_allocator_scope=False)
                finally:mn.segment_flow_veh_h=flow_function
                assert call[0]==62,call
                after=accounting.continuity_vehicle_counts(state,cfg)['FW_E']
                flows={int(r['resource'].rsplit(':',1)[-1]):r['accepted_total_veh'] for r in trace._response['resource_allocations'] if r['kind']=='freeway_mainline_sending'}
                rejected+=max(0.,requested-flows[first-1]);assert abs(requested-flows[first-1])<1e-8,'Incompatible observed inlet'
                for c in active:
                    merge=sum(q/3600 for r,q in rates.items() if net.ramp_merge_segment_index[r]==c)
                    err=abs(after[c]-before[c]-flows[c-1]-merge+flows[c]);mass_error=max(mass_error,err)
                    assert err<1e-8 and after[c]>=0 and state.freeway_density['FW_E'][c]<=net.rho_max+1e-8
                    if step==0 and c in saved:
                        expected=saved[c]['total_change']
                        if c==first and inlet_mode!='space':expected+=v_before[c]*(v_before[c-1]-upspace)/(3600*lengths[c])
                        difference=abs(state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][c]-v_before[c]-expected);assert difference<1e-8,(first,c,difference)
                        parity.append(difference)
                    projection+=int(state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][c]<=net.v_min+1e-9)
                    window[c]+=flows[c]
                    audit.append(dict(time_s=stamp,cell=c,before_n=before[c],after_n=after[c],in_veh=flows[c-1],merge_veh=merge,out_veh=flows[c],speed=state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][c],mass_error=err))
                if (step+1)%30==0:
                    for c in active:output.append(dict(time_s=round(stamp+1,6),cell=c,n_veh=after[c],v_kmh=state.freeway_speed['FW_E'][c],downstream_crossings=window[c]));window[c]=0.
            bycell=[]
            for c in sorted(active):
                rr=[r for r in output if r['cell']==c];actual=[native[r['time_s'],c] for r in rr]
                br=[r for r in baseline['cells'] if r['cell']==c]
                assert len(rr)==len(br)==15
                def rmse(key,pred):return math.sqrt(sum((r[key]-a[key])**2 for r,a in zip(pred,actual))/15)
                bycell.append(dict(cell=c,actual_speed_mean=sum(a['v_kmh'] for a in actual)/15,
                    predicted_speed_mean=sum(r['v_kmh'] for r in rr)/15,baseline_speed_mean=sum(r['v_kmh'] for r in br)/15,
                    speed_rmse=rmse('v_kmh',rr),baseline_speed_rmse=rmse('v_kmh',br),n_rmse=rmse('n_veh',rr),baseline_n_rmse=rmse('n_veh',br),
                    exits=sum(r['downstream_crossings'] for r in rr),actual_exits=sum(float(truth.flows[r['time_s'],'FW_E',c]['downstream_crossings']) for r in rr),
                    final_n=rr[-1]['n_veh'],actual_final_n=actual[-1]['n_veh']))
            summary=dict(first=first,last=25,inlet_mode=inlet_mode,bycell=bycell,mass_error=mass_error,first_speed_parity_max=max(parity),
                local_minimum_speed_steps=projection,rejected_inlet_veh=rejected,steps=450)
            suffix='' if inlet_mode=='space' else '_'+inlet_mode
            save(out/f'domain{first}{suffix}.json',dict(summary=summary,frames=output))
            with gzip.open(out/f'audit{first}{suffix}.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(audit,f,allow_nan=False)
            summaries.append(summary);print(json.dumps(summary),flush=True)
        for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
        save(out/('crossing_summary.json' if crossing_speeds else 'summary.json'),dict(domains=summaries,boundary_count_checks=flow_checks,merge_bypasses=dict(bypasses),pins=pins,
            boundary_velocity_samples=[dict(time_s=t,cell=c,n_veh=crossings[t,c],**v) for (t,c),v in sorted(velocities.items())],
            conditional_local_trajectories=len(summaries),autonomous_full_forecasts=0,fit=False,new_native=0,new_fzp=0,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
    except Exception as exc:
        save(out/('crossing_failure.json' if crossing_speeds else 'retry_failure.json' if resume else 'failure.json'),dict(type=type(exc).__name__,message=str(exc),completed_domains=len(summaries)));raise


def audit_lane_fd_sensitivity():
    """Cache-only Jensen/sensitivity screen, not a lane-model forecast."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from evaluation.controllers.freeway_fd import literature_desired_speed
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'lane_fd67';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    cfg=model._config('FW_E',context['parameters']['by_direction']['FW_E']);net=cfg.network;mn=accounting._mn
    control=mn.ControlAction.uncontrolled(cfg)
    lengths=accounting.cell_lengths_km(cfg,'FW_E',31)
    widths=net.freeway_segment_lanes['FW_E'];cells=list(range(14,27))
    assert all(abs(w-round(w))<1e-9 for w in widths)
    law=net.freeway_vsl_fd_response['FW_E'];assert law['law']=='carlson'
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,root/'flow67/verification.json')}
    for p,h in model.provenance['model_files'].items():assert sha(ROOT/p)==h;pins[str(ROOT/p)]=h
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    stop=Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP');pins[str(stop)]=sha(stop)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='NO_PROGRESS: freeway-first sequencing only; this turn revalidated sources and executes a bounded mechanism screen.',
        hypothesis='Spatial averaging may hide VSL target sensitivity when faster and slower physical lanes coexist.',
        source='Verified seed67 release/release_vsl90 5s cache,2670.1..3120.1,cells14..26.',
        partitions=['aggregate','lane1_vs_rest','individual_lanes'],commands=[110,90],law=law,
        calculation='Same frozen cell FD evaluated at N/(L*width), then vehicle-weighted targets; occupied groups only.',
        controls=['Equal lane densities reproduce aggregate target for both commands.',
                  'All partitions preserve observed N and speed moment; native30s N/v parity.',
                  'Record nominal and active Carlson targets separately, including sign reversals.'],
        limits=['Hypothetical full exposure of the SAME current state, not the actual command-cohort mixture.',
                'Targets are desired equilibrium speeds, not current speeds, accelerations, capacity or TTT gains.',
                'Aggregate-calibrated coefficients are not yet calibrated per lane; sparse5s counts are noisy.',
                'No accepted-flow, route-stock or lane-change dynamics are altered; no autonomous qualification.',
                'Previously failed local22/23 lane and full31 flux-convection candidates are not rerun.'],
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,adopted=False,pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    def target(c,rho,command):
        mn.segment_vsl(control,'FW_E',c,cfg)
        active=command<max(cfg.freeway_follower.vsl_set)-.5
        nominal=mn.effective_desired_speed_kmh(rho,net.v_free,net.rho_crit,command,net.alpha_vsl,active,
            net.metanet_a_m,False,net.rho_max,0.)
        value=literature_desired_speed(law,cfg,'FW_E',c,rho,nominal,command,active)
        assert math.isfinite(value) and value>=0
        return value
    homogeneous=[]
    for c in cells:
        for rho in (5.,20.,40.,70.,100.):
            for command in (110,90):
                values=[target(c,rho,command) for _ in range(round(widths[c]))]
                error=abs(sum(values)/len(values)-target(c,rho,command));assert error<1e-10
                homogeneous.append(error)
    rows=[];checks=0;max_moment_error=0.
    verification=load(root/'flow67/verification.json')
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        p=root/'flow67'/f'{arm}_frames.json.gz';assert sha(p)==verification['artifacts'][p.name];pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:data=json.load(f)
        assert data['fields']==['cell','speed_kmh','x_m','lane','acceleration_mps2','desired_speed_kmh']
        folder=HERE/'heldout67_freeway_20260930/observations'/arm;truth=ObservationData(folder)
        cp=folder/'cells_30s.csv';pins[str(cp)]=sha(cp)
        native={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rs in truth.cells.items() for r in rs if r['road']=='FW_E'}
        for stamp,frame in sorted(data['frames'].items(),key=lambda x:float(x[0])):
            t=float(stamp)
            for c in cells:
                vehicles=[z for z in frame.values() if z[0]==c];lanes=round(widths[c]);n=len(vehicles)
                assert all(1<=int(z[3])<=lanes and z[3]==int(z[3]) for z in vehicles)
                if not n:continue
                moment=sum(z[1] for z in vehicles)
                if (t,c) in native:
                    actual=native[t,c];assert n==actual['n_veh'] and abs(moment/n-actual['v_kmh'])<1e-7;checks+=1
                partitions={'aggregate':[(lanes,vehicles)],
                    'lane1_vs_rest':[(1,[z for z in vehicles if z[3]==1]),(lanes-1,[z for z in vehicles if z[3]!=1])],
                    'individual_lanes':[(1,[z for z in vehicles if z[3]==lane]) for lane in range(1,lanes+1)]}
                record=dict(arm=arm,time_s=t,cell=c,n_veh=n,v_kmh=moment/n,
                    native_desired_speed=sum(z[5] for z in vehicles)/n,partitions={})
                for name,groups in partitions.items():
                    assert sum(len(v) for w,v in groups)==n
                    error=abs(sum(sum(z[1] for z in v) for w,v in groups)-moment)
                    max_moment_error=max(max_moment_error,error);assert error<1e-8
                    values={u:sum(len(v)*target(c,len(v)/(lengths[c]*w),u) for w,v in groups if v)/n for u in (110,90)}
                    record['partitions'][name]=dict(target110=values[110],target90=values[90],delta=values[90]-values[110],
                        groups=[dict(width=w,n=len(v),rho=len(v)/(lengths[c]*w),speed=sum(z[1] for z in v)/len(v) if v else None) for w,v in groups])
                rows.append(record)
    summary=[]
    for arm in ('release','release_vsl90'):
        for c in cells:
            rr=[r for r in rows if r['arm']==arm and r['cell']==c];total=sum(r['n_veh'] for r in rr)
            item=dict(arm=arm,cell=c,samples=len(rr),vehicle_samples=total,partitions={})
            for name in ('aggregate','lane1_vs_rest','individual_lanes'):
                mean={k:sum(r['n_veh']*r['partitions'][name][k] for r in rr)/total for k in ('target110','target90','delta')}
                mean.update(material_negative_samples=sum(r['partitions'][name]['delta']<=-1 for r in rr),
                    material_positive_samples=sum(r['partitions'][name]['delta']>=1 for r in rr),
                    sign_disagreement_vs_aggregate=sum(r['partitions'][name]['delta']*r['partitions']['aggregate']['delta']<0 and min(abs(r['partitions'][name]['delta']),abs(r['partitions']['aggregate']['delta']))>=1 for r in rr))
                item['partitions'][name]=mean
            summary.append(item)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    with gzip.open(out/'rows.json.gz','wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(rows,f,allow_nan=False)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(groups=summary,rows=len(rows),native_checks=checks,homogeneous_checks=len(homogeneous),
        max_homogeneous_error=max(homogeneous),max_partition_moment_error=max_moment_error,pins=pins,
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,fit=False,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,rows=len(rows),native_checks=checks,groups=summary)),flush=True)


def check_inlet_speed_boundary():
    """One current-observation inlet closure; no coefficient fit or live edits."""
    import statistics
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, area_freeway_accounting as accounting
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration'
    out=root/'freeway_first/inlet_speed_boundary';ri=root/'freeway_first/route_inventory'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    manifest=root/'coupled_expanded_joint/candidate_manifest.json'
    ctx=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=ctx['component']
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records']
    train=[('s29_early','none',750),('s29_early','vsl',750),('s29_late','hold',450),('s29_late','release',450)]
    check=[('s43_early','none',750),('s43_early','vsl',750)]
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),manifest,Path('D:/VISSIM_runs/20260928_sd31_d4e2_9000/STOP'))}
    for pin in ctx['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/pin['path'])]=sha(ROOT/pin['path'])
    for rel in ('evaluation/controllers/area_freeway_accounting.py','evaluation/controllers/lane_plant_runtime.py',
                'evaluation/controllers/vissim_stackelberg_adapter.py','evaluation/controllers/freeway_fd.py'):
        pins[str(ROOT/rel)]=sha(ROOT/rel)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(train=train,check=check,maximum_forecasts=12,maximum_candidates=1,fit=False,
        model='Live expanded036 component; experimental off-ramp route inventory bridge OFF as in live run.',
        hypothesis='First-cell upstream velocity held at current observed0..40m spatial mean, replacing only convection inlet argument. All later cells, FD, demand, VSL exposure, merge, dynamic off storage/drain and queue cost unchanged.',
        observation='Initial snapshot only; same initial speed in each paired arm.40m spatial mean is a proxy, not insertion velocity.',
        acceptance='First-cell mean N MAE decreases>=10%; whole31cell speed and N RMSE do not rise>10%; no newly wrong >=2vehh mainline or component delta. If passed check previously inspected seed43 once, no refit.',
        limitations='Component FW_E31cells+8connectors,not fullOmega. No independent pristine holdout or live deployment. No future boundary truth input.',pins=dict(pins)))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    mn=accounting._mn;segment=mn.segment_vsl;update=mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh
    active={};rows=[];predictions={};calls=[]
    def marked_segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw):
        result=segment(control,road,cell,cfg,**kw);active['address']=(road,cell);return result
    def inlet_update(speed,upstream,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum):
        if active.get('enabled') and active.get('address')==('FW_E',0):
            calls.append(dict(upstream_original=upstream,upstream_candidate=active['source_speed']))
            upstream=active['source_speed']
        return update(speed,upstream,rho,down,desired,dt,length,tau,nu,kappa,minimum)
    def execute(key,on):
        case,arm,horizon=key;r=next(z for z in records if (z['case'],z['arm'])==(case,arm))
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(r['input'],r['sha256']);args=list(args);pins[r['input']]=r['sha256']
        assert args[2]==ctx['parameters'] and len(args[1])==450 and not kw.get('offramp_inventory')
        for j in range(450,horizon):
            step=copy.deepcopy(args[1][-1]);step.update(window_start_s=round(r['cutoff']+j,6),window_end_s=round(r['cutoff']+j+1,6));args[1].append(step)
        kw['horizon_sec']=horizon
        rp=ri/'initial_raw.json' if case=='s29_late' else ri/case/'initial_raw.json';raw=load(rp);pins[str(rp)]=sha(rp)
        assert raw['sim_sec']==r['cutoff'] and raw['vehicle_records']['complete']
        current=[v['speed_kph'] for v in raw['vehicle_records']['records'] if v['link_no']==74 and 0<=v['position_m']<40]
        assert len(current)>=3 and all(math.isfinite(v) and v>=0 for v in current)
        active.update(enabled=on,source_speed=statistics.mean(current));calls.clear()
        started=time.perf_counter();pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        assert len(calls)==(horizon if on else 0)
        truth=ObservationData(r['truth']);row=_cellwise_measure(model,pred,r,truth,horizon_sec=horizon)
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv'):pins[str(truth.folder/name)]=sha(truth.folder/name)
        error=[z['n_veh']-next(v['n_veh'] for v in truth.cells[z['time_s']] if v['road']=='FW_E' and v['cell']==0) for z in pred['cells'] if z['cell']==0]
        row.update(candidate='observed_inlet' if on else 'base',horizon=horizon,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,
            source_speed=active['source_speed'],source_sample_n=len(current),cell0_n_mae=statistics.mean(map(abs,error)),
            changed_speed_equations=len(calls),original_source_speed_kmh=sorted(set(c['upstream_original'] for c in calls)))
        stem=row['candidate']+'_'+case+'_'+arm
        with gzip.open(out/(stem+'.json.gz'),'wt',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
        save(out/(stem+'.json'),row);rows.append(row);predictions[on,case,arm]=pred
        save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='running',forecasts=len(rows),last=stem))
        print(json.dumps(dict(name=stem,wall_sec=row['wall_sec'],cell0_n_mae=row['cell0_n_mae'],main_ttt=row['predicted']['mainline_ttt'])),flush=True)
    def assess(keys):
        means={on:{key:statistics.mean((r['cell0_n_mae'] if key=='cell0_n_mae' else r['score'][key]['rmse']) for r in rows if (r['candidate']=='observed_inlet')==on and (r['case'],r['arm'],r['horizon']) in keys)
                   for key in ('cell0_n_mae','speed','cell_n')} for on in (False,True)}
        pairs=[]
        for case,arm,horizon in keys:
            if arm in ('none','hold'):continue
            ref='none' if case.endswith('early') else 'hold'
            group={on:{a:next(r for r in rows if r['case']==case and r['arm']==a and (r['candidate']=='observed_inlet')==on) for a in (arm,ref)} for on in (False,True)}
            pairs.append(dict(case=case,arm=arm,actual={k:group[False][arm]['actual'][k]-group[False][ref]['actual'][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt')},
                **{label:{k:group[on][arm]['predicted'][k]-group[on][ref]['predicted'][k] for k in ('mainline_ttt','ttt')} for on,label in ((False,'base'),(True,'candidate'))}))
        passed=(means[True]['cell0_n_mae']<=.9*means[False]['cell0_n_mae'] and all(means[True][k]<=1.1*means[False][k] for k in ('speed','cell_n')) and
                all(p['candidate'][k]*p['actual'][k]>0 for p in pairs for k in p['actual'] if abs(p['actual'][k])>=2))
        return dict(passed=passed,means=means,pairs=pairs)
    mn.segment_vsl,mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=marked_segment,inlet_update
    try:
        for key in train:
            for on in (False,True):execute(key,on)
        training=assess(train);save(out/'training.json',training)
        validation=None
        if training['passed']:
            for key in check:
                for on in (False,True):execute(key,on)
            validation=assess(check)
        assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in pins.items())
        save(out/'summary.json',dict(training=training,validation=validation,rows=rows,pins=pins,
            forecasts=len(rows),new_native=0,fzp_reads=0,coefficient_changes=0,live_modified=False,adopted=False,gain_qualified=False))
        save(out/'status.json',dict(stage='complete',forecasts=len(rows),training_passed=training['passed'],validation_passed=None if validation is None else validation['passed'],adopted=False))
    finally:
        mn.segment_vsl,mn.metanet_speed_update_kmh=segment,update


def audit_past_speed_residual():
    """Screen a past-only bias estimate; conditional local data, not a rollout."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=root/'past_residual'
    assert (out/'helper_before.txt').is_file() and not (out/'protocol.json').exists()
    inputs=[root/name/'rows.json' for name in ('local67','merge_population','acceleration_balance')]
    pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in inputs+[Path(__file__)]}
    for name in ('area_freeway_accounting.py','freeway_fd.py','lane_plant_runtime.py'):
        p=ROOT/'evaluation/controllers'/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: actual SDMPC selection after AD correction; same physical commands.',
        hypothesis='A recent local speed-equation residual may persist enough to support a small causal disturbance estimate.',
        method='At t, add the mean actual-minus-model rate of six completed5s intervals [t-30,t) to the current conditional model rate.',
        residual_window_sec=30,score_horizon_sec=5,fit=False,new_rollouts=0,new_native=0,
        candidate_count=1,comparators=['unchanged_model','zero_speed_change','previous_completed5s_rate'],
        screen='At least10% lower rate RMSE than both unchanged model and zero-change in each separate case; at least75% of cells not worse than zero-change.',
        no_adoption_on_screen_alone=True,autonomous_prediction=False,
        limitations=['Current model terms use observed current neighbors and some actual5s fluxes/effective lanes; this remains a conditional diagnostic.',
            'The one-second equation increment and five-second spatial-mean rate are different operators.',
            'Seed29/43 use the archived connected-FD diagnostic; seed67 uses expanded036. Never pool them as one parameter model.',
            'Only past completed intervals enter the correction; current/future target is scoring only. No TTT/control gain claim.'],source_pins=pins))
    groups=defaultdict(list);overlap=[]
    first=load(inputs[0]);second=load(inputs[1]);third=load(inputs[2])
    for row in first:
        if row['basis']=='all_native':
            groups[('s67_release_expanded036',row['cell'])].append(dict(time=row['time_s'],actual=row['actual_spatial_rate'],model=row['predicted_change']))
    lookup={(round(r['time_s'],6),r['cell']):r for r in first if r['basis']=='all_native'}
    for row in second:
        if row['arm']!='release' or 'model' not in row:continue
        key=round(row['time_s'],6),row['cell']
        if key in lookup:
            err=max(abs(row['model']['total_change']-lookup[key]['predicted_change']),abs(row['observed_mean_rate']-lookup[key]['actual_spatial_rate']))
            assert err<1e-8;overlap.append(err)
        else:
            groups[('s67_release_expanded036',row['cell'])].append(dict(time=row['time_s'],actual=row['observed_mean_rate'],model=row['model']['total_change']))
    assert len(overlap)==180
    for row in third:
        groups[(f"s{row['seed']}_nc_connectedFD",row['cell'])].append(dict(time=row['time_s'],actual=row['actual_spatial_rate'],model=row['model']['total']))
    samples=[]
    for (case,cell),seq in sorted(groups.items()):
        seq.sort(key=lambda r:r['time'])
        assert len({r['time'] for r in seq})==len(seq)
        assert all(abs(b['time']-a['time']-5)<1e-8 for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))
        for j in range(6,len(seq)):
            current=seq[j];past=seq[j-6:j]
            assert max(r['time']+5 for r in past)<=current['time']+1e-8
            correction=sum(r['actual']-r['model'] for r in past)/6
            samples.append(dict(case=case,cell=cell,time=current['time'],history_start=past[0]['time'],history_end=past[-1]['time']+5,
                actual=current['actual'],model=current['model'],bias=correction,corrected=current['model']+correction,
                zero=0.,previous=past[-1]['actual']))
    def score(rr):
        result=dict(samples=len(rr))
        for method in ('model','corrected','zero','previous'):
            error=[r[method]-r['actual'] for r in rr]
            result[method]=dict(rate_rmse=math.sqrt(sum(x*x for x in error)/len(error)),rate_mae=sum(abs(x) for x in error)/len(error),rate_bias=sum(error)/len(error))
        return result
    cells=[];cases=[]
    for (case,cell) in sorted(groups):
        rr=[r for r in samples if r['case']==case and r['cell']==cell]
        cells.append(dict(case=case,cell=cell,**score(rr)))
    for case in sorted({r['case'] for r in samples}):
        rr=[r for r in samples if r['case']==case];s=score(rr)
        cc=[r for r in cells if r['case']==case]
        s['cells_not_worse_than_zero']=sum(r['corrected']['rate_rmse']<=r['zero']['rate_rmse'] for r in cc)
        s['cell_count']=len(cc)
        s['screen_pass']=s['corrected']['rate_rmse']<=.9*min(s['model']['rate_rmse'],s['zero']['rate_rmse']) and s['cells_not_worse_than_zero']>=.75*len(cc)
        cases.append(dict(case=case,**s))
    assert all(sha(Path(p))==h for p,h in pins.items())
    save(out/'samples.json',samples);save(out/'cells.json',cells)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(status='complete',cases=cases,screen_pass=all(r['screen_pass'] for r in cases),
        samples=len(samples),overlap_checks=len(overlap),maximum_overlap_error=max(overlap),causal_history_checks=len(samples),
        new_native=0,new_fzp_reads=0,new_rollouts=0,fit=False,adopted=False,autonomous_prediction=False,source_pins=pins))
    print(json.dumps(dict(cases=cases,samples=len(samples),new_rollouts=0,adopted=False)),flush=True)


def audit_source_realization_onset():
    """Read-only source identity/desired-speed diagnostic; never a forecast."""
    from collections import Counter
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=HERE/'source_realization_onset_20261001'
    protocol=load(out/'protocol.json')
    assert not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    assert protocol['max_full_fzp_prefix_passes']==4 and protocol['forecasts']==0
    provenance=OLD/'diagnostics/metanet_net_gain_goal_20260924/mainline_moments/manifest.json'
    geometry=root/'route_inventory/compiled.json'
    offsets={int(k):float(v[1]) for k,v in load(geometry)['physical'].items() if v[0]=='FW_E'}
    records=load(provenance)['rows'];pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),provenance,geometry,out/'protocol.json')}
    result=[];passes=0;started=time.perf_counter()
    for seed in (29,43):
        frames=[];admissions=[]
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            fp=root/'cohort_early'/f's{seed}_{arm}_frames.json.gz'
            ap=root/'cohort_early'/f's{seed}_{arm}_native.json'
            pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp);pins[str(ap)]=sha(ap)
            with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames.append({float(t):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()})
            admissions.append([r for r in load(ap)['admissions'] if r['cohort']=='source'])
        assert frames[0][2220.1]==frames[1][2220.1]
