        times=sorted(frames[0]);assert times==sorted(frames[1])
        prefix=set();first_identity=None
        for a,b in zip(*admissions):
            if a!=b:first_identity=[a,b];break
            prefix.add(a['vehicle'])
        assert first_identity is not None
        paired=set(frames[0][2220.1])|prefix
        counts=[Counter(r['first_time_s'] for r in rows) for rows in admissions]
        first_count=next(dict(time_s=t,counts=[c[t] for c in counts]) for t in times if counts[0][t]!=counts[1][t])
        onsets={}
        for t in times:
            for vehicle in sorted(paired & frames[0][t].keys() & frames[1][t].keys(),key=int):
                a,b=[frame[t][vehicle] for frame in frames]
                if abs(a[1]-b[1])>.1 or abs(a[2]-b[2])>.5 or a[3]!=b[3]:
                    region=str(int(max(a[2],b[2])//1000))
                    onsets.setdefault(region,dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vehicle,none=a,vsl=b))
        retained=[];source_proofs=[]
        for arm,frame in zip(('none','vsl'),frames):
            meta=next(r for r in records if (r['seed'],r['arm'])==(seed,arm))
            source=Path(meta['source']);stat=source.stat()
            assert (stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)==(meta['source_bytes'],meta['source_mtime_ns'])
            check=root/'recovery_transport'/f's{seed}_{arm}_source.json';pins[str(check)]=sha(check)
            expected=load(check)['extended_prefix_sha256']
            lo,hi=protocol['retained_entry_windows'][str(seed)]
            destination=out/f's{seed}_{arm}_entry.json'
            assert not destination.exists()
            rows={};digest=hashlib.sha256();ci=None;nrows=0
            with source.open('rb') as f:
                for line in f:
                    if ci is None:
                        if line.startswith(b'$VEHICLE:'):ci={k:i for i,k in enumerate(line.rstrip().split(b':',1)[1].split(b';'))}
                        continue
                    if not line.strip():continue
                    t=float(line.split(b';',1)[0])
                    if t>protocol['scan_end_sec']+1e-7:break
                    digest.update(line);nrows+=1
                    if not lo-1e-7<=t<=hi+1e-7:continue
                    z=line.rstrip().split(b';');link=int(z[ci[b'LANE\\LINK\\NO']])
                    if link not in offsets:continue
                    x=offsets[link]+float(z[ci[b'POS']])
                    if not 0<=x<1000:continue
                    vehicle=z[ci[b'NO']].decode();t=round(t,6)
                    original=frame[t][vehicle]
                    assert abs(original[2]-x)<1e-8 and original[1]==float(z[ci[b'SPEED']])
                    row={k:z[ci[k.encode()]].decode() for k in ('DESSPEED','ACCELERATION','TMINNETTOT','LENGTH','INTERACTSTATE','INTERACTTARGTYPE','INTERACTTARGNO')}
                    row.update(x_m=x,speed_kmh=original[1],lane=original[3],link=link)
                    rows.setdefault(t,{})[vehicle]=row
            assert ci is not None and digest.hexdigest()==expected
            after=source.stat();assert (after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns)==(stat.st_size,stat.st_mtime_ns)
            passes+=1;retained.append(rows)
            proof=dict(path=str(source),bytes=stat.st_size,mtime_ns=stat.st_mtime_ns,prefix_end_sec=protocol['scan_end_sec'],prefix_sha256=expected,rows_hashed=nrows)
            source_proofs.append(proof)
            save(destination,dict(fields='FZP native text fields; x/speed/lane checked against existing cache',source=proof,frames=rows))
            print(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,arm=arm,prefix_pass=True,retained_rows=sum(map(len,rows.values())))),flush=True)
        desired=[];motion=[];source_bins=[]
        for t in sorted(retained[0]):
            a,b=retained[0][t],retained[1][t]
            for vehicle in sorted(paired&a.keys()&b.keys(),key=int):
                da,db=float(a[vehicle]['DESSPEED']),float(b[vehicle]['DESSPEED'])
                if abs(da-db)>.01:desired.append(dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vehicle,none=a[vehicle],vsl=b[vehicle],in_source_prefix=vehicle in prefix))
                if (abs(a[vehicle]['speed_kmh']-b[vehicle]['speed_kmh'])>.1
                        or abs(a[vehicle]['x_m']-b[vehicle]['x_m'])>.5 or a[vehicle]['lane']!=b[vehicle]['lane']):
                    motion.append(dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vehicle,none=a[vehicle],vsl=b[vehicle]))
            source_bins.append(dict(time_s=t,admissions=[c[t] for c in counts],entry_counts=[len(a),len(b)],
                entry_speed=[sum(v['speed_kmh'] for v in f.values())/len(f) if f else None for f in (a,b)],
                entry_vehicle_ids_identical=set(a)==set(b)))
        row=dict(seed=seed,matching_source_prefix_count=len(prefix),first_identity_difference=first_identity,
            first_admission_count_difference=first_count,first_regional_motion_difference=onsets,
            first_entry_desired_speed_difference=desired[0] if desired else None,
            first_entry_motion_difference=motion[0] if motion else None,
            matched_desired_different_samples=len(desired),source_bins=source_bins,sources=source_proofs,
            caveat='Five-second samples and matched admission prefix do not prove complete latent vehicle identity or random-generator causality. Do not match source IDs beyond first identity divergence.')
        save(out/f's{seed}_desired_differences.json',desired);result.append(row)
    assert passes==4
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(status='complete',rows=result,source_pins=pins,prefix_passes=passes,
        wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,new_native=0,forecasts=0,fit=0,
        autonomous_gain_validation=False,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps(dict(complete=True,prefix_passes=passes,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started)),flush=True)


def score_source_matched_prefix():
    """Reuse frozen forecasts before observed source-motion divergence; no fit."""
    from collections import Counter
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    out=HERE/'source_realization_onset_20261001';onset=load(out/'summary.json')
    assert not (out/'prefix_response.json').exists()
    protocol=dict(start=2220.1,cutoff='Last30s grid endpoint strictly before first source-prefix entry motion discrepancy (>0.1km/h,>0.5m,or lane). Determined without selecting on gain.',
        model='Frozen route036 component; not full coupled SDMPC. Same existing750s forecast truncated, never reinitialized.',
        scope='FW_E31 cells only; not Omega. No source-difference adjustment, no future data in forecast. Other physical arrivals may differ.',
        new_forecasts=0,new_native=0,new_fzp_passes=0,fit=0)
    save(out/'prefix_protocol.json',protocol)
    records=load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'];rows=[];pins={str(p):sha(p) for p in (Path(__file__),out/'summary.json',out/'prefix_protocol.json',DECISIONS/'capture.json')}
    for prior in onset['rows']:
        seed=prior['seed'];start=2220.1;motion=prior['first_entry_motion_difference']['time_s']
        end=round(start+30*math.floor((motion-start-1e-6)/30),6);arms={}
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            rec=next(r for r in records if (r['case'],r['arm'])==(f's{seed}_early',arm))
            fp=root/'cohort_early'/f's{seed}_{arm}_frames.json.gz';pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
            with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:frames={float(t):v for t,v in json.load(f)['frames'].items()}
            pp=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')/(arm+'.json.gz');pins[str(pp)]=sha(pp)
            with gzip.open(pp,'rt') as f:pred=json.load(f)
            path=Path(rec['truth'])/'flows_30s.csv';pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
            with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:native=list(csv.DictReader(f))
            times=[round(start+30*k,6) for k in range(round((end-start)/30)+1)]
            actual={t:Counter(v[0] for v in frames[t].values()) for t in times}
            predicted={t:{r['cell']:r['n_veh'] for r in pred['cells'] if abs(r['time_s']-t)<1e-7 and r['road']=='FW_E'} for t in times[1:]}
            predicted[start]=dict(actual[start]);assert all(len(v)==31 for v in predicted.values())
            def totals(series,flows,terminal_key):
                selected=[r for r in flows if r['road']=='FW_E' and start+1e-7<float(r['window_end_s'])<=end+1e-7]
                assert len(selected)==31*(len(times)-1)
                return dict(ttt=sum(sum(series[t].values())*15/3600 for a,b in zip(times,times[1:]) for t in (a,b)),
                    source=sum(float(r['source_admissions']) for r in selected),merge=sum(float(r['ramp_merges']) for r in selected),
                    off=sum(float(r['off_departures']) for r in selected),terminal=sum(float(r[terminal_key]) for r in selected),
                    end_n=sum(series[end].values()),through={str(c):sum(float(r['downstream_crossings']) for r in selected if int(r['cell'])==c) for c in (16,18,19,20,23,24,25)})
            arms[arm]=dict(actual=totals(actual,native,'terminal_exits_inferred'),predicted=totals(predicted,pred['flows'],'terminal_exits'))
            # Exact admission-bin equality is checked below, not inferred from totals.
            arms[arm]['native_source_bins']=[sum(float(r['source_admissions']) for r in native if r['road']=='FW_E' and abs(float(r['window_end_s'])-t)<1e-7) for t in times[1:]]
        assert arms['none']['native_source_bins']==arms['vsl']['native_source_bins']
        diffs={}
        for origin in ('actual','predicted'):
            a,b=[arms[arm][origin] for arm in ('none','vsl')]
            diffs[origin]={k:b[k]-a[k] for k in a if k!='through'}
            diffs[origin]['through']={k:b['through'][k]-a['through'][k] for k in a['through']}
        rows.append(dict(seed=seed,start=start,end=end,source_bins_identical=True,arms=arms,deltas=diffs))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'prefix_response.json',dict(status='complete',protocol=protocol,rows=rows,pins=pins,goal_qualified=False))
    print(json.dumps([dict(seed=r['seed'],end=r['end'],deltas=r['deltas']) for r in rows]),flush=True)


def audit_vsl_exposure_timing():
    """Observe unchanged command cohorts; infer native tags from cached crossings."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, freeway_fd
    out=HERE/'vsl_exposure_timing_20261001'
    assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first'
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    ri=root/'route_inventory';pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__)}
    pre=load(out/'preflight.json')
    for p,h in pre['unchanged_pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for p in model.provenance['model_files']:pins[str(ROOT/p)]=sha(ROOT/p)
    for value in context['document']['sources'].values():pins[str(ROOT/value['path'])]=sha(ROOT/value['path'])
    signs=load(root/'vsl_native_exposure/protocol.json')['signs']
    active_signs={round(s['x_m'],6) for s in signs if s['cell']==16}
    reset_signs={round(s['x_m'],6) for s in signs if s['cell']==25}
    assert len(active_signs)==len(reset_signs)==1
    active=active_signs.pop();reset=reset_signs.pop()
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(max_rollouts=2,new_native=0,fzp_reads=0,fit=0,
        model_manifest=str(manifest),horizon_sec=450,cutoff=2220.1,activation=2250.,
        native_tagging='All initial/source/ramp vehicles110; observed active-sign crossing retags90; physical downstream sign restores110. Crossing bracket containing2250 is uncertain90/110. Five-second sampled positions, not exact events.',
        active_x_m=active,reset_x_m=reset,source_matched_ends={'29':2640.1,'43':2490.1},
        inference_limits=['Seed43 tags inferred from trajectories; no native DESSPEED reread.',
            'Seed29 cached native desired-speed changes validate crossing classification, not numerical speed-ID equality.',
            'Different exposure can reflect predicted flows as well as well-mixed cohort transport. No causal parameter fit from this comparison.'],pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    checks=[];comparisons=[];groups=[];native_checks=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        case=f's{seed}_early';rec=next(r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==case and r['arm']=='vsl')
        args,kw=read_primitive_capture(rec['input'],rec['sha256']);pins[rec['input']]=rec['sha256']
        assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and kw['horizon_sec']==450 and kw['roads']==['FW_E']
        kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
        raw=ri/case/'initial_raw.json';contract=ri/'contract.json'
        pins[str(raw)]=sha(raw);pins[str(contract)]=sha(contract)
        kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(contract),raw=load(raw))
        trace=[];count=[-1];original=freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target
        def observe(self,cfg,road,cell,rho,legacy,displayed):
            value=original(self,cfg,road,cell,rho,legacy,displayed)
            assert road=='FW_E'
            if cell==0:count[0]+=1
            if 14<=cell<=26 and count[0]%5==0:
                stock=sum(self.cohorts[cell].values())
                restricted=sum(n for command,n in self.cohorts[cell].items() if command<109.5)
                trace.append(dict(time_s=round(2220.1+count[0],6),cell=cell,stock=stock,
                    restricted=restricted,fraction=restricted/stock if stock else 0.,
                    rho=rho,legacy_target=legacy,mixed_target=value,displayed=displayed))
            return value
        started=time.perf_counter();freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target=observe
        try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
        finally:freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target=original
        assert count[0]==449 and len(trace)==90*13,(count,len(trace))
        reference=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')/'vsl.json.gz'
        pins[str(reference)]=sha(reference)
        with gzip.open(reference,'rt') as f:cached=json.load(f)
        parity={k:json.loads(json.dumps(pred[k]))==[x for x in cached[k] if x[t]<=2670.1+1e-7]
                for k,t in [('cells','time_s'),('flows','window_end_s'),('ports','time_s'),('ramps','end_sec')]}
        save(out/f's{seed}_parity.json',parity);assert all(parity.values()),parity
        with gzip.open(out/f's{seed}_model.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(trace,f,allow_nan=False)
        checks.append(dict(seed=seed,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-started,parity=parity,target_calls=450*31))
        path=root/f'cohort_early/s{seed}_vsl_frames.json.gz';pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:native=json.load(f)
        assert native['fields']==['cell','speed_kmh','x_m','lane']
        frames={float(t):row for t,row in native['frames'].items() if float(t)<=2670.1+1e-7}
        assert len(frames)==91
        labels={};previous={};previous_time=None;native_rows=[];crossings=[];gaps=[]
        for t,frame in sorted(frames.items()):
            for vid,row in frame.items():
                prior=previous.get(vid);before=labels.get(vid,{110})
                if prior is None:
                    labels[vid]={110}
                    if previous_time is not None and 16<=row[0]<=20:gaps.append(dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vid,cell=row[0]))
                else:
                    assert row[2]>=prior[2]-.02,(seed,t,vid,'backward mainline movement')
                    labels[vid]=before
                    if prior[2]<active<=row[2] and t>=2250.:
                        labels[vid]={90} if previous_time>=2250. else {90,110}
                        crossings.append(dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vid,event='activate',uncertain=len(labels[vid])==2))
                    if prior[2]<reset<=row[2]:
                        labels[vid]={110};crossings.append(dict(time_s=t,vehicle=vid,event='reset',uncertain=False))
            for cell in range(14,27):
                ids=[v for v,row in frame.items() if row[0]==cell];n=len(ids)
                lo=sum(labels[v]=={90} for v in ids);hi=sum(90 in labels[v] for v in ids)
                native_rows.append(dict(time_s=t,cell=cell,n=n,restricted_lower=lo,restricted_upper=hi,
                    fraction_lower=lo/n if n else 0.,fraction_upper=hi/n if n else 0.))
            previous=frame;previous_time=t
        # Retagging can be checked against independently cached desired-speed
        # changes for29; distributions overlap so desired speed is not a tag.
        if seed==29:
            dp=root/'vsl_native_exposure/s29_early_vsl_frames.json.gz';pins[str(dp)]=sha(dp)
            with gzip.open(dp,'rt') as f:desired=json.load(f)
            desired_frames={float(t):{str(v[0]):v for v in rows} for t,rows in desired['frames'].items()}
            validation=[]
            for event in crossings:
                t=event['time_s'];v=event['vehicle'];a=desired_frames.get(round(t-5,6),{}).get(v);b=desired_frames.get(t,{}).get(v)
                if a is not None and b is not None and not event['uncertain'] and event['event']=='activate':
                    validation.append(dict(**event,desired_before=a[5],desired_after=b[5],changed=abs(a[5]-b[5])>.02))
            native_checks=validation
        with gzip.open(out/f's{seed}_native.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(dict(rows=native_rows,crossings=crossings,gaps=gaps),f,allow_nan=False)
        lookup={(r['time_s'],r['cell']):r for r in native_rows}
        stop=2640.1 if seed==29 else 2490.1
        for r in trace:
            if not 2250.1<=r['time_s']<=stop:continue
            n=lookup[r['time_s'],r['cell']]
            comparisons.append(dict(seed=seed,**r,**{k:v for k,v in n.items() if k not in ('cell','time_s')},
                fraction_error_lower=r['fraction']-n['fraction_upper'],fraction_error_upper=r['fraction']-n['fraction_lower']))
        for cell in range(14,27):
            rows=[r for r in comparisons if r['seed']==seed and r['cell']==cell]
            def first(key):return next((r['time_s'] for r in rows if r[key]>=.5),None)
            groups.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=cell,first_half_model=first('fraction'),first_half_native_lower=first('fraction_lower'),
                first_half_native_upper=first('fraction_upper'),mean_fraction_error_bounds=[sum(r[k] for r in rows)/len(rows) for k in ('fraction_error_lower','fraction_error_upper')],
                mean_target_mixing_delta=sum(r['mixed_target']-r['legacy_target'] for r in rows)/len(rows),
                mean_stock_error=sum(r['stock']-r['n'] for r in rows)/len(rows)))
        print(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,parity=parity,gaps=len(gaps),seconds=checks[-1]['wall_sec'])),flush=True)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for p,h in pre['unchanged_pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(checks=checks,groups=groups,native29_activation_checks=native_checks,pins=pins,
        new_rollouts=2,new_native=0,fzp_reads=0,fit=0,adopted=False,autonomous_forecasts_unchanged=True))
    with gzip.open(out/'comparison.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(comparisons,f,allow_nan=False)


def diagnose_vsl_exposure_brackets():
    """Future-exposure ablation only: four forecasts, never an adopted model."""
    from evaluation.controllers import lane_plant_runtime as lpr, freeway_fd
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    parent=HERE/'vsl_exposure_timing_20261001';out=parent/'conditional'
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    audit=load(parent/'summary.json');assert audit['new_rollouts']==2 and audit['autonomous_forecasts_unchanged']
    root=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first';ri=root/'route_inventory'
    manifest=root.parent/'expanded_joint/eval_036/manifest.json'
    context=lpr.load_sources(manifest);model=context['component']
    protocol=load(parent/'protocol.json');active=protocol['active_x_m'];reset=protocol['reset_x_m']
    pins={str(manifest):sha(manifest),str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__),str(parent/'summary.json'):sha(parent/'summary.json')}
    pre=load(parent/'preflight.json')
    for p,h in pre['unchanged_pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'protocol.json',dict(max_new_rollouts=4,fit=0,new_native=0,fzp_reads=0,
        autonomous_prediction=False,future_observation_inputs=True,
        hypothesis='Replacing only desired-speed mixture with observed sign-exposure fraction bounds tests whether command transport explains local flow error. No future N/v/flows used.',
        bracket='At each sampled activation/reset crossing, allow either previous or new command until next5s frame. A crossing spanning initial2250 activation stays uncertain until the reset.',
        cases=['29_lower','29_upper','43_lower','43_upper'],horizon_sec=450,
        boundary='Original autonomous source/ramp forecasts and current-state dynamic receiving/storage/drain retained.',
        target_cells=list(range(14,27)),adoption_forbidden=True,pins=pins))
    (out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    results=[];paired=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        case=f's{seed}_early';fp=root/f'cohort_early/s{seed}_vsl_frames.json.gz';pins[str(fp)]=sha(fp)
        with gzip.open(fp,'rt') as f:d=json.load(f)
        frames={float(t):row for t,row in d['frames'].items() if float(t)<=2670.1+1e-7}
        labels={};pending={};previous={};previous_time=None;exposure={};rows=[]
        for t,frame in sorted(frames.items()):
            for vid,row in frame.items():
                before=pending.pop(vid,labels.get(vid,{110}));prior=previous.get(vid)
                labels[vid]=before if prior is not None else {110}
                if prior is not None:
                    if prior[2]<active<=row[2] and t>=2250.:
                        labels[vid]=before|{90}
                        pending[vid]={90} if previous_time>=2250. else labels[vid]
                    if prior[2]<reset<=row[2]:
                        labels[vid]=labels[vid]|{110};pending[vid]={110}
            for cell in range(14,27):
                ids=[v for v,r in frame.items() if r[0]==cell];n=len(ids)
                lo=sum(labels[v]=={90} for v in ids)/n if n else 0.
                hi=sum(90 in labels[v] for v in ids)/n if n else 0.
                assert 0<=lo<=hi<=1
                exposure[t,cell]=(lo,hi);rows.append(dict(time_s=t,cell=cell,n=n,lower=lo,upper=hi))
            previous=frame;previous_time=t
        save(out/f's{seed}_fraction_bounds.json',rows)
        records=[r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==case and r['arm'] in ('none','vsl')]
        reference_folder=root/'vsl_native_exposure'/('horizon750' if seed==29 else 'h750_s43')
        preds={};truths={}
        for rec in records:
            ref=reference_folder/(rec['arm']+'.json.gz');pins[str(ref)]=sha(ref)
            with gzip.open(ref,'rt') as f:preds[rec['arm']]=json.load(f)
            truths[rec['arm']]=ObservationData(rec['truth'])
            for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):
                path=Path(rec['truth'])/name;pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
        rec=next(r for r in records if r['arm']=='vsl')
        for bound_index,bound in enumerate(('lower','upper')):
            args,kw=read_primitive_capture(rec['input'],rec['sha256']);pins[rec['input']]=rec['sha256']
            assert args[2]==context['parameters'] and kw['horizon_sec']==450
            kw['port_dynamics']['entry_capacity_mode']='storage'
            kw['offramp_inventory']=dict(contract=load(ri/'contract.json'),raw=load(ri/case/'initial_raw.json'))
            count=[-1];original=freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target
            def observe(self,cfg,road,cell,rho,legacy,displayed):
                old=original(self,cfg,road,cell,rho,legacy,displayed)
                assert road=='FW_E'
                if cell==0:count[0]+=1
                if count[0]<30 or not 14<=cell<=26:return old
                t=round(2220.1+count[0],6);left=round(2220.1+5*(count[0]//5),6);right=round(left+5,6)
                fraction=exposure[left,cell][bound_index]*(right-t)/5+exposure[right,cell][bound_index]*(t-left)/5
                net=cfg.network;row=net.freeway_segment_params[road][cell]
                vf=row.get('v_free',net.v_free);critical=row.get('rho_crit',net.rho_crit);shape=row.get('metanet_a_m',net.metanet_a_m)
                nominal=vf*math.exp(-(max(0.,rho)/critical)**shape/shape)
                spec=(getattr(net,'freeway_vsl_fd_response',{}) or {}).get(road)
                restricted=freeway_fd.literature_desired_speed(spec,cfg,road,cell,rho,min(nominal,(1+net.alpha_vsl)*90),90,True)
                unrestricted=freeway_fd.literature_desired_speed(spec,cfg,road,cell,rho,nominal,110,False)
                return fraction*restricted+(1-fraction)*unrestricted
            begin=time.perf_counter();freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target=observe
            try:pred=model.rollout(*args,**kw)
            finally:freeway_fd.VSLExposure.target=original
            assert count[0]==449
            parity={key:json.loads(json.dumps([r for r in pred[key] if r[t]<=2250.1+1e-7]))==
                [r for r in preds['vsl'][key] if r[t]<=2250.1+1e-7]
                for key,t in [('cells','time_s'),('flows','window_end_s'),('ports','time_s'),('ramps','end_sec')]}
            assert all(parity.values()),parity
            with gzip.open(out/f's{seed}_{bound}.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(pred,f,allow_nan=False)
            preds[bound]=pred
            results.append(dict(seed=seed,bound=bound,wall_sec=time.perf_counter()-begin,pre2250_parity=parity,
                diagnostics=pred['diagnostics'],autonomous_prediction=False,future_observation_inputs=True))
        stop=2640.1 if seed==29 else 2490.1
        def metrics(cells,flows):
            by={};through={c:0. for c in range(31)}
            for row in cells:
                if row['road']=='FW_E' and 2220.1<=row['time_s']<=stop:by.setdefault(row['time_s'],0.);by[row['time_s']]+=row['n_veh']
            times=sorted(by);assert times[0]==2220.1 and times[-1]==stop
            ttt=sum((by[a]+by[b])*.5*(b-a)/3600 for a,b in zip(times,times[1:]))
            for row in flows:
                if row['road']=='FW_E' and 2220.1<row['window_end_s']<=stop:through[int(row['cell'])]+=float(row['downstream_crossings'])
            return dict(ttt=ttt,through=through,end_n=by[stop])
        values={}
        for arm in ('none','vsl'):
            truth=truths[arm]
            actual_cells=[r for rows_at_time in truth.cells.values() for r in rows_at_time]
            actual_flows=[dict(r,window_end_s=t,road=road,cell=cell) for (t,road,cell),r in truth.flows.items()]
            values['actual_'+arm]=metrics(actual_cells,actual_flows)
        initial=[dict(row) for row in args[0]]
        for arm,pred in preds.items():values[arm]=metrics(initial+pred['cells'],pred['flows'])
        for mode in ('actual_vsl','vsl','lower','upper'):
            base=values['actual_none' if mode=='actual_vsl' else 'none'];v=values[mode]
            paired.append(dict(seed=seed,mode=mode,end=stop,delta_ttt=v['ttt']-base['ttt'],delta_end_n=v['end_n']-base['end_n'],
                delta_through={str(c):v['through'][c]-base['through'][c] for c in range(31)}))
        print(json.dumps([x for x in paired if x['seed']==seed]),flush=True)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for p,h in pre['unchanged_pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(results=results,paired=paired,pins=pins,new_rollouts=4,new_native=0,fit=0,
        autonomous_prediction=False,future_observation_inputs=True,adopted=False))


def audit_history_discharge_regimes():
    """Bounded NC-only matched-state screen; no capacity fit or model change."""
    from statistics import mean,median
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    out=HERE/'history_discharge_20261001';assert out.is_dir() and not (out/'summary.json').exists()
    pre=load(out/'preflight.json')
    for p,h in pre['pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    modes={
        'density':{'rho':2.},
        'density_speed':{'rho':2.,'speed':5.},
        'local_context':{'rho':2.,'speed':5.,'down_rho':3.,'down_speed':10.,
                         'up_flow_past_vph':1000.,'merge_past_vph':180.,'off_stock':5.},
    }
    protocol=dict(previous_goal_turn='PROGRESS: unchanged-model exposure observation and conditional ablation',
        seeds=[29,43],arms=['none'],cells=list(range(16,26)),window=[300.1,8850.1],
        history_seconds=60,direction_threshold_density=2.,target='Next60s total cell departures(mainline+off+terminal), vph; future values only labels.',
        modes=modes,pairing='Same seed and physical cell; one positive-history and one negative-history state; >=120s separation. Greedy minimum normalized current-state distance; no outcome in matching and no observation reused within a mode.',
        exclusion='Require n>=5/current speed available; all four past/future30s balance windows have no unexplained entry/loss/native removal and exact conservation.',
        support='At least12 pairs in EACH seed at a cell for a cell-specific directional claim; no forced relaxation when support is low.',
        caveats=['Density trend alone is not a causal traffic regime label; alternating waves can change it.',
                 'NC observations only. No controller response or closed-loop accuracy qualification.',
                 'Flow is not capacity: native supply and receiving availability are not experimentally held fixed.',
                 'Extra hysteresis state is not justified if current speed/context accounts for the difference or matched support is insufficient.'],
        new_native=0,fzp_reads=0,new_rollouts=0,fit=0)
    save(out/'protocol.json',protocol);(out/'helper_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    ri=HERE/'baseline_reproduction_20260929/cellwise_calibration/freeway_first/route_inventory'
    compiled=load(ri/'compiled.json');contract=load(ri/'contract.json')
    selected_network=ROOT/contract['network']['path']
    assert sha(selected_network)==contract['network']['sha256']=='64cf5f55fe9990f3e25bc4fedebf4ab1e4634c8138dbcf5697a136c48cb559dc'
    pins={str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__)}
    for path in (ri/'compiled.json',ri/'contract.json',selected_network):pins[str(path)]=sha(path)
    summaries=[];pairs=[];samples=[];quality=[];geometry_checks=[]
    for seed in protocol['seeds']:
        rec=next(r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==f's{seed}_early' and r['arm']=='none')
        folder=Path(rec['truth']);data=ObservationData(folder)
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv','ports_30s.csv','geometry.json'):
            p=folder/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        geometry=data.geometry
        # Historical observer keeps the old baseline file identity. Require
        # equality of physical projection instead of rewriting that metadata.
        assert geometry['bounds']==compiled['bounds']
        for boundary in geometry['boundaries']:
            if boundary['kind']=='ramp':
                target=compiled['merges'][boundary['id']]
                assert (boundary['road'],boundary['to_cell'])==(target['freeway'],target['cell'])
            elif boundary['kind']=='offramp':
                target=compiled['branches'][str(boundary['connector'])]
                assert (boundary['road'],boundary['from_cell'])==(target['freeway'],target['source_cell'])
                assert abs(boundary['chain_pos_m']-target['source_chain_m'])<1e-7
        observation_manifest=load(folder/'manifest.json');pins[str(folder/'manifest.json')]=sha(folder/'manifest.json')
        for name,digest in observation_manifest.get('files',{}).items():assert sha(folder/name)==digest,name
        source=observation_manifest.get('source',observation_manifest.get('source_fzp'))
        assert f'20260924_bottleneck90_full_s{seed}' in source and f'mtfc_s{seed}_none_001.fzp' in source
        geometry_checks.append(dict(seed=seed,observer_geometry_network=geometry['network'],selected_network=contract['network'],
            physical_cell_bounds_exact=True,ramp_exit_cells_and_exit_positions_exact=True,native_source=source,
            scope='Cached physical projection equivalence, not byte equality of historical and selected network files.'))
        cells={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rows in data.cells.items() for r in rows if r['road']=='FW_E'}
        branch={c:[str(b['connector']) for b in geometry['boundaries'] if b['road']=='FW_E' and b['kind']=='offramp' and b['from_cell']==c] for c in protocol['cells']}
        excluded={'insufficient_stock':0,'unresolved_flow':0,'neutral_history':0,'missing_neighbor':0,'port_absence':0};selected=[]
        for (t,c),row in sorted(cells.items()):
            if c not in protocol['cells'] or not 300.1<=t<=8850.1:continue
            hist=cells.get((round(t-60,6),c));down=cells.get((t,c+1))
            if hist is None or down is None or down['v_kmh'] is None:
                excluded['missing_neighbor']+=1;continue
            if row['n_veh']<5 or hist['n_veh']<5 or row['v_kmh'] is None:
                excluded['insufficient_stock']+=1;continue
            flux=[data.flows[round(t+dt,6),'FW_E',c] for dt in (-30,0,30,60)]
            if any(float(r[k])!=0 for r in flux for k in ('unexplained_entries','unexplained_losses','native_removals','conservation_residual_veh')):
                excluded['unresolved_flow']+=1;continue
            ports=[data.ports[t,b] for b in branch[c]]
            if any(float(p['unresolved_absences_veh'])!=0 for p in ports):excluded['port_absence']+=1;continue
            trend=row['rho_veh_per_km_lane']-hist['rho_veh_per_km_lane']
            if abs(trend)<2.:excluded['neutral_history']+=1;continue
            lane_length=data.cell_geometry['FW_E',c]['length_km']
            q0=row['n_veh']*row['v_kmh']/lane_length
            future=sum(float(r[k]) for r in flux[2:] for k in ('downstream_crossings','off_departures','terminal_exits_inferred'))*60.
            selected.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=c,time_s=t,phase='forming' if trend>0 else 'clearing',trend=trend,
                rho=row['rho_veh_per_km_lane'],speed=row['v_kmh'],down_rho=down['rho_veh_per_km_lane'],down_speed=down['v_kmh'],
                up_flow_past_vph=sum(float(r['upstream_crossings']) for r in flux[:2])*60.,
                merge_past_vph=sum(float(r['ramp_merges']) for r in flux[:2])*60.,
                off_stock=sum(float(p['end_n_veh']) for p in ports),q0=q0,q_next60=future,q_next60_residual=future-q0,
                supply_queue_proxy=bool(int(cells[t,c-1].get('stopped_veh',0))>0),
                downstream_open_proxy=down['v_kmh']>=60.,n=row['n_veh']))
        samples.extend(selected);quality.append(dict(seed=seed,selected=len(selected),excluded=excluded))
        for cell in protocol['cells']:
            a=[r for r in selected if r['cell']==cell and r['phase']=='forming'];b=[r for r in selected if r['cell']==cell and r['phase']=='clearing']
            for name,tolerances in modes.items():
                edges=[]
                for i,x in enumerate(a):
                    for j,y in enumerate(b):
                        if abs(x['time_s']-y['time_s'])<120:continue
                        gaps={k:abs(x[k]-y[k])/v for k,v in tolerances.items()}
                        if max(gaps.values())<=1:edges.append((sum(v*v for v in gaps.values()),i,j))
                used_a=set();used_b=set();selected_pairs=[]
                for distance,i,j in sorted(edges):
                    if i in used_a or j in used_b:continue
                    used_a.add(i);used_b.add(j);x=a[i];y=b[j]
                    selected_pairs.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=cell,mode=name,forming=x,clearing=y,distance=distance,
                        delta_q_next60=y['q_next60']-x['q_next60'],delta_q0=y['q0']-x['q0'],
                        delta_speed=y['speed']-x['speed'],delta_residual=y['q_next60_residual']-x['q_next60_residual'],
                        both_queued_and_open=all(z['supply_queue_proxy'] and z['downstream_open_proxy'] for z in (x,y))))
                pairs.extend(selected_pairs)
                keys=('delta_q_next60','delta_q0','delta_speed','delta_residual')
                summaries.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=cell,mode=name,pairs=len(selected_pairs),support_pass=len(selected_pairs)>=12,
                    queued_open_pairs=sum(p['both_queued_and_open'] for p in selected_pairs),
                    means={k:mean(p[k] for p in selected_pairs) for k in keys} if selected_pairs else None,
                    medians={k:median(p[k] for p in selected_pairs) for k in keys} if selected_pairs else None))
        print(json.dumps(quality[-1]),flush=True)
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for p,h in pre['pins'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    with gzip.open(out/'pairs.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(pairs,f,allow_nan=False)
    with gzip.open(out/'samples.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(samples,f,allow_nan=False)
    save(out/'summary.json',dict(quality=quality,groups=summaries,pins=pins,geometry_checks=geometry_checks,new_rollouts=0,fit=0,new_native=0,
        adopted=False,scope='Observed conditional next60s discharge, not autonomous METANET or causally identified capacity.'))


def decompose_history_discharge_pairs():
    """Explain the already matched flow contrast through exact stock balance."""
    from statistics import mean
    from diagnostics.demand_sweep.user_native_20260914.metanet_calibration_v1.boundary_factory import ObservationData
    out=HERE/'history_discharge_20261001';assert not (out/'balance.json').exists()
    summary=load(out/'summary.json')
    with gzip.open(out/'pairs.json.gz','rt') as f:pairs=json.load(f)
    pins={str(out/'summary.json'):sha(out/'summary.json'),str(out/'pairs.json.gz'):sha(out/'pairs.json.gz'),str(Path(__file__)):sha(__file__)}
    save(out/'balance_protocol.json',dict(new_pair_selection=False,new_native=0,fit=0,new_forecasts=0,
        scope='Future inflow and inventory are outcome decomposition only; not model inputs or a causal receiving-capacity estimate.',
        identity='delta_out_vph = delta(upstream+source+ramp)_vph - 60*delta(N(t+60)-N(t))',pins=pins))
    (out/'balance_executed.txt').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    audits=[]
    for seed in (29,43):
        rec=next(r for r in load(DECISIONS/'capture.json')['records'] if r['case']==f's{seed}_early' and r['arm']=='none')
        data=ObservationData(rec['truth']);cells={(t,r['cell']):r for t,rs in data.cells.items() for r in rs if r['road']=='FW_E'}
        for name in ('cells_30s.csv','flows_30s.csv'):
            p=Path(rec['truth'])/name;pins[str(p)]=sha(p)
        for pair in pairs:
            if pair['seed']!=seed:continue
            details={}
            for phase in ('forming','clearing'):
                row=pair[phase];t=row['time_s'];c=row['cell'];end=round(t+60,6)
                fl=[data.flows[round(t+dt,6),'FW_E',c] for dt in (30,60)]
                incoming=sum(float(f[k]) for f in fl for k in ('upstream_crossings','source_admissions','ramp_merges'))*60.
                stock_change=(cells[end,c]['n_veh']-row['n'])*60.
                assert abs(incoming-stock_change-row['q_next60'])<1e-7,(seed,t,c)
                endpoint_speed=cells[end,c]['v_kmh']
                details[phase]=dict(inflow=incoming,stock_rate=stock_change,
                    speed_change=endpoint_speed-row['speed'] if endpoint_speed is not None else None)
            a,b=details['forming'],details['clearing']
            di=b['inflow']-a['inflow'];dn=b['stock_rate']-a['stock_rate']
            assert abs(pair['delta_q_next60']-(di-dn))<1e-7
            audits.append(dict(seed=seed,cell=pair['cell'],mode=pair['mode'],forming_time=pair['forming']['time_s'],clearing_time=pair['clearing']['time_s'],
                delta_out_vph=pair['delta_q_next60'],delta_in_vph=di,minus_delta_stock_rate=-dn,
                delta_speed_change=None if a['speed_change'] is None or b['speed_change'] is None else b['speed_change']-a['speed_change']))
    groups=[]
    for group in summary['groups']:
        rows=[x for x in audits if all(x[k]==group[k] for k in ('seed','cell','mode'))]
        groups.append(dict(seed=group['seed'],cell=group['cell'],mode=group['mode'],pairs=len(rows),
            means={k:mean(x[k] for x in rows) for k in ('delta_out_vph','delta_in_vph','minus_delta_stock_rate')} if rows else None,
            mean_speed_change_difference=mean(x['delta_speed_change'] for x in rows if x['delta_speed_change'] is not None) if any(x['delta_speed_change'] is not None for x in rows) else None))
    for p,h in pins.items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    with gzip.open(out/'balance_pairs.json.gz','wt') as f:json.dump(audits,f,allow_nan=False)
    save(out/'balance.json',dict(groups=groups,exact_pair_balances=len(audits),pins=pins,new_native=0,new_rollouts=0,fit=0))
    print(json.dumps(dict(exact_pair_balances=len(audits),new_rollouts=0)),flush=True)
